Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : FORMA_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : AC
Hangi Tablo için : SUGG05
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set hhh = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK46", "")
	Set S46E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK46E")
	Set S46T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK46T")

	Set S46T_LIST = Doc.Select2("asdfaj", "STOK46T_LIST", "SUGGESTED_TARIH<>'" & Space(10) & "'&ACIKKAPALI='K'", "EVRAKNO", -1)
	For i = 1 to S46T_LIST.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage i & " / " & S46T_LIST.GetRowCount()
			if S46T_LIST.EVRAKNO(i) <> EskiSipNo Then
				if trim(S46E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc1.Save_Voucher
				Doc1.Load_Voucher S46T_LIST.EVRAKNO(i)
			End if 

			Set S46T1 = Doc1.Select2("sdgadfajh", "STOK46T", "TRNUM='" & S46T_LIST.TRNUM(i) & "'", "", -1)
			if S46T1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				satirNo = Doc.CDbl(S46T1.SRNUM(1))
				S46T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				S46T.ACIKKAPALI(satirNo) = " "
			End if
		EskiSipNo = S46T_LIST.EVRAKNO(i)	
	Next
	if trim(S46E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher

	For i = 1 to STOK46T_LIST.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Açýk/Kapalý tekrar okunuyor : " & i & " / " & STOK46T_LIST.GetRowCount()
		Set S46T1 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdgsdfhsj", "SELECT ACIKKAPALI FROM STOK46T WHERE EVRAKNO='" & STOK46T_LIST.EVRAKNO(i) & "' AND TRNUM='" & STOK46T_LIST.TRNUM(i) & "'")
		if S46T1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			STOK46T_LIST.ACIKKAPALI(i) = S46T1.ACIKKAPALI(1)
		End if
	Next
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Call CHStokKodlarindaDegisiklikVarsaMailGonder()
End Sub
Sub CHStokKodlarindaDegisiklikVarsaMailGonder()
	MsgStr = ""
	QStr = "SELECT STOK0GT.STOKCODE, STOK00.AD"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM STOK0GT"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD=STOK0GT.STOKCODE"
	STOK0GT.SafePack
	if STOK0GT.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		QStr = QStr & " WHERE STOK0GT.STOKCODE NOT IN ("
		For i = 1 to STOK0GT.GetRowCount()
			if i > 1 Then QStr = QStr & ","
			QStr = QStr & "'" & STOK0GT.STOKCODE(i) & "'"
		Next
		QStr = QStr & " )"
	End if
	Set S0GT = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdsdfhsj", QStr)
	if S0GT.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		MsgStr = MsgStr & "Silinen Kodlar : " & vbcrlf
		For i = 1 to S0GT.GetRowCount()
			MsgStr = MsgStr & "Stok Kodu : " & trim(S0GT.KOD(i)) & ", Stok Adý : " & trim(S0GT.KOD(i)) & vbCrLf
		Next
	End if
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : FORMA_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : DM_MALIYET
Hangi Tablo için : STOK48
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Fiyat Listesindeki Fiyatlarý Boþ Endeksli Standart Maliyetlere Aktar
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
'	for i = 1 to LISTET.GetRowCount()
'		Doc.Load_Voucher LISTET.EVRAKNO(i)
'		Call Makro2()
'	Next
'End Sub
'Sub Makro2()
'	EndeksKodu = GKYoksaEkle("STMTYP", "  ", "Teklif Maliyet Endeksi")
'	UnsurKodu = GKYoksaEkle("STMMUS", "  ", "Hammadde Maliyeti")

	EndeksKodu = "  "
	UnsurKodu = "  "

	STOK48T.SafePack()

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STDMH0","")
	Set STDMH0E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STDMH0E")
	Set STDMH0T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STDMH0T")
	For i = 1 to STOK48T.GetRecCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & STOK48T.GetRecCount()
		Tarih = STOK48T.KRI_TARIH_1(i)
		if trim(Tarih) = "" Then Tarih = STOK48E.REF_TARIH_1
		if trim(Tarih) = "" Then Tarih = Doc.BuGun
		
		StokKodu = STOK48T.KOD(i)
		Set ST = Doc.Select1("ww", "STDMH0E","KOD='" & StokKodu & "'&STDMALTYPE='" & EndeksKodu & "'", "", -1)
		if ST.GetRowCount() = 0 then
			ret = Doc1.New_Voucher()				
			STDMH0E.STDMALTYPE = EndeksKodu
			STDMH0E.KOD = StokKodu
			STDMH0T.AddRow
			satirNo = STDMH0T.GetRecCount
		else
			ret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(ST.EVRAKNO)
			if ret <> 0 then
				k = Doc.d7MsgBox(trim(StokKodu) & ": Standart Maliyet tablosu yüklenemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir.","dinamo",MB_OK)
				exit sub
			end if
			satirNo = satirBul(STDMH0T, UnsurKodu, Tarih)
			if satirNo = 0 then satirNo = STDMH0T.AddRow
		end if
		Fiyat = STOK48T.PRICE(i)
		IslemBirimi = STOK48T.SF_UNIT(i)
		TemelBirim = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", STOK48T.KOD(i), "")
		TemelFiyat = Doc.Stok00_toUnit(STOK48T.KOD(i), TemelBirim, IslemBirimi, d7c_ErrMsgNo) * Fiyat

		STDMH0T.UNSUR(satirNo) = UnsurKodu
		STDMH0T.TUTAR(satirNo) = TemelFiyat
		STDMH0T.PARABIRIMI(satirNo) = STOK48T.PRICEUNIT(i)
		STDMH0T.VALIDAFTERTARIH(satirNo) = Tarih
		ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
		if ret <> 0 then
			k = Doc.d7MsgBox(trim(StokKodu) & ": Standart Maliyet tablosu kaydedilemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir.","dinamo",MB_OK)
			ret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
			exit sub
		end if
		ret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
	Next
End Sub
Function satirBul(STDMH0T, UnsurKodu, Tarih)
	satirBul = 0
	For i = 1 to STDMH0T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(STDMH0T.UNSUR(i)) = trim(UnsurKodu) and STDMH0T.VALIDAFTERTARIH(i) = Tarih then
			satirBul = i
			exit function
		end if
	Next
End Function
Function GKYoksaEkle(GrupKoduEvrakNo, GrupKodu, Ad)
	Set GST = Doc.Select1("ddee", "GECOUST", "EVRAKNO='" & GrupKoduEvrakNo & "'&KOD='" & GrupKodu & "'", "", -1)
	if GST.GetRowCount = 0 Then
		Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("GECO10", "")
		Set GT = Doc1.GetTableObject("GECOUST")

		Doc1.Load_Voucher GrupKoduEvrakNo
		satirNo = GT.AddRow
		GT.KOD(satirNo) = GrupKodu
		GT.AD(satirNo) = Ad
		Doc1.Save_Voucher
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	End if
	GKYoksaEkle = GrupKodu
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : FORMA_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : SERÝLE-4
Hangi Tablo için : STOK29
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Satýnalma Giriþlerini Serile
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	STOK29T.SafePack()
	For i = STOK29T.GetRowCount() to 1 Step -1
		if trim(STOK29T.SERINO(i)) = "" Then
			Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdffjsaj", "SELECT B_MINPARTIBUYUKLUGU FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & STOK29T.KOD(i) & "'")
			if S00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				PartiBuyuklugu = S00.B_MINPARTIBUYUKLUGU(1)
				if PartiBuyuklugu > 0 Then
					KasaSayisi = Doc.Ceil(STOK29T.SF_MIKTAR(i) / PartiBuyuklugu)
					Miktar = STOK29T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
					For j = 1 to KasaSayisi
						if j < KasaSayisi Then
							KasaIci = PartiBuyuklugu
						Else
							KasaIci = Miktar - (j - 1) * PartiBuyuklugu
						End if
						if j = 1 Then
							STOK29T.SetCurrentRow(i)
							STOK29T.SF_MIKTAR(i) = KasaIci
							STOK29T.SERINO(i) = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
						Else
							satirNo = i + (j-1)
							STOK29T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
							if satirNo > STOK29T.GetRowCount() Then
								STOK29T.AddRow
							Else
								STOK29T.InsertRow(satirNo)
							End if
							STOK29T.CopyRowFromOtherTable satirNo, STOK29T, i
							STOK29T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = KasaIci
							STOK29T.SERINO(satirNo) = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
						End if
					Next
				End if
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set SUGG02T_DETAY_001 = Doc.GetTableObject("SUGG02T_DETAY_001")
	SUGG02T_DETAY_001.AddField2 "CH_ADI", "CHAR", 50,0,L,"",""
	SUGG02T_DETAY_001.Setd7FieldEvent "CHKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "CHKOD_AV"

End Sub
Sub cmdSuz()
	Doc.RunCommand "SUGG_0001_DETAY"
	Call CHAdlariniGetir()
End Sub
Sub CHAdlariniGetir()
	Set SUGG02T_DETAY_001 = Doc.GetTableObject("SUGG02T_DETAY_001")
	For i = 1 to SUGG02T_DETAY_001.GetRowCount()
		SUGG02T_DETAY_001.CH_ADI(i) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", SUGG02T_DETAY_001.CHKOD(i), "")
	Next
End Sub
Sub CHKOD_AV()
	Set SUGG02T_DETAY_001 = Doc.GetTableObject("SUGG02T_DETAY_001")
	SUGG02T_DETAY_001.CH_ADI = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", SUGG02T_DETAY_001.CHKOD, "")
End Sub

Sub Nerede()
	Set MSTCK=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	MSTCK.Addfield2 "MUSTERI","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	
	Set STACK1 = Doc.CreateTableObject("STACK1=STACK","IO")
	STACK1.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	STACK1.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"

	Call StokGoster()

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "NEREDE",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
End Sub
Sub StokGoster()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Call StokGoster2(STOK01_PDA.KOD)
End Sub
Sub StokGoster2(StokKodu)
	Set STOK26E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK26E")
	Set STOK01_PDA_LIST = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST")

	STOK01_PDA_LIST.Empty
	if trim(StokKodu) = "" Then Exit Sub

	QStr = "KOD='" & StokKodu & "'"
	if trim(STOK26E.TARGETAMBCODE) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & "&AMBCODE='" & STOK26E.TARGETAMBCODE & "'"
	End if
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdfw3ff4f4", "STOK01", QStr, "", -1)

	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = STOK01_PDA_LIST.AddRow()
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

		STOK01_PDA_LIST.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
	Next
	STOK01_PDA_LIST.SetCurrentRow(1)
End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set STACK1 = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK1")
	if trim(STACK1.BARKOD) = "" Then Exit Sub
	
	StokKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "SKOD", STACK1.BARKOD, "")
	Call StokGoster2(StokKodu)
End Sub

	EDITTEXT D=LOCATION3 CAPTION="L3" NAVIGATE=0
	EDITTEXT D=LOCATION4 CAPTION="L4"	 NAVIGATE=0
	EDITTEXT D=STOK_MIKTAR NAVIGATE=0
	EDITTEXT D=KOD_STOK00_IUNIT NAVIGATE=0
	EDITTEXT D="ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE" W=90p CAPTION="RzYpEt" NAVIGATE=0
	EDITTEXT D="ALLOC_EVRAKNO" W=90p CAPTION="RzYpEn" NAVIGATE=0
	EDITTEXT D="ALLOC_SIPTYPE" W=90p CAPTION="RzSpT" NAVIGATE=0
	EDITTEXT D="ALLOC_SIPNO" W=90p CAPTION="RzSpNo" NAVIGATE=0
	EDITTEXT D="ALLOC_SIPARTNO" W=90p CAPTION="RzSpAn" NAVIGATE=0
	EDITTEXT D="ALLOC_MPSEVNO" W=90p CAPTION="RzMPSNo" NAVIGATE=0
	EDITTEXT D="ALLOC_MPSARTNO" W=90p CAPTION="RzMPSANo" NAVIGATE=0
	EDITTEXT D=VRI_VR_C NAVIGATE=0
	EDITTEXT D=VRI_VR_R1 NAVIGATE=0
	EDITTEXT D=VRI_VR_R2 NAVIGATE=0
	EDITTEXT D=VRI_VR_R3 NAVIGATE=0
	EDITTEXT D=VRI_NUM1 NAVIGATE=0
	EDITTEXT D=VRI_NUM2 NAVIGATE=0
ENDGRID

ENDFORM

Sub Makro1()
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")

	Set STACK_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STACK_PDA=STACK","IO")
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "PROPT","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "AUTO_ADD","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	Set STOK01_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STOK01_PDA=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "LOCATIONS","CHAR",11,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION1","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION2","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION3","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION4","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_KALAN","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SAYAC","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""
	
	STOK01_PDA.Setd7FieldEvent "SF_PAKETICERIGI","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "SF_PAKETICERIGI_AV"

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPS_BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
		
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "MPS_BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "MPS_BARKOD_AV"

	QStr = "SELECT '0' OKUNDU10, T.EVRAKNO, T.JOBNO, T.R_KAYNAKTYPE, T.R_MAMULKODU, S.AD MAMUL_AD, "
	QStr = QStr & " (R_MIKTART - R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN - R_HM_ODONEMNET - R_HM_IADE_EDILEN) R_HM_BAKIYE, "
	QStr = QStr & " 0 OKUTULAN_MIKTAR,  0 KALAN_MIKTAR"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM MMPS10T T"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S ON S.KOD=T.R_MAMULKODU"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE T.R_STATUS='R'"
	Set MTableT = Doc.RunSQLQuery("MMPS10T_LIST", QStr)

	Set MSTCK=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
End Sub
Sub MalzemeToplaMain()
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "TRACK32_2X2",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	

	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	if Not Doc.IsBlank(STACK_PDA) Then
		STACK_PDA.PROPT = 1
		STACK_PDA.AUTO_ADD = "1"
	End if

End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	STOK01_PDA.D_ID = Barkod

	if STACK_PDA.PROPT = "2" Then
		Call Cikart()
	Else
		LocPrefix = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCPREFX"))
		if Left(MTable.BARKOD, Len(LocPrefix)) = LocPrefix Then
			Call LokasyonBilgileriniDodur()
		Else
			Call MiktarBilgisiniDoldur()
		End if
	End if
	Call HammaddeIhtiyacMPSList()
End Sub
Sub MiktariSifirla()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0
End Sub
Sub Cikart()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")

	Barkod = 	MTable.BARKOD
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

	Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("sdf3gdfhd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Barkod", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set S83T = Doc.Select2("sdfsdfhsj", "STOK83T", "D_ID='" & trim(Barkod) & "'", "", -1)
	if S83T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Barkod daha önce okutulmamýþ", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	satirNo = Doc.Cdbl(S83T.SRNUM(1))
	STOK83T.KOD(satirNo) = ""
	STOK83T.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo) = ""
	STOK83T.SafePack()
	Call OkutulanMiktarlariGuncelle()
End Sub
Sub MiktarBilgisiniDoldur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")


	Barkod = 	MTable.BARKOD
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

	Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("sdf3gdfhd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Barkod", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	Miktar = GetStokMiktar(d7LB.SERINO, L1, L2, L3, L4, Depo)
	STOK01_PDA.KOD = D7LB.SKOD
	STOK01_PDA.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", D7LB.SKOD, "")
	STOK01_PDA.AMBCODE = Depo
	STOK01_PDA.LOCATION1 = L1
	STOK01_PDA.LOCATION2 = L2
	STOK01_PDA.LOCATION3 = L3
	STOK01_PDA.LOCATION4 = L4
	STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER = D7LB.LOTNUMBER
	STOK01_PDA.SERINO = D7LB.SERINO
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R1 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R1
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R2 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R2
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R3 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R3
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM1 = D7LB.VRI_NUM1
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM2 = D7LB.VRI_NUM2
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_C = D7LB.VRI_VR_C
'	STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR = D7LB.SF_BAKIYE
	STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR = Miktar
	STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT = D7LB.SF_UNIT
	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = Miktar
	STOK01_PDA.FOKOD = D7LB.FOKOD
End Sub
Sub EkraniTemizle()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T_LIST")

	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	MTable.Empty
	MTableT.Empty
End Sub
Sub SonIslemIptal()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")

	if trim(MTable.STOK26_EVRAKNO) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Set STOK26T = Doc1.GettableObject("STOK26T")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.STOK26_EVRAKNO
	Doc1.Delete_Vouhcer
End Sub
Sub Nerede()

	Call StokGoster(MTableT.R_KAYNAKKODU)

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "NEREDE",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
End Sub
Sub Nerede2()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Call StokGoster(STOK01_PDA.KOD)

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "NEREDE",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
End Sub
Sub StokGoster(StokKodu)
	Set STOK01_PDA_LIST = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST")
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")

	STOK01_PDA_LIST.Empty

	QStr = "KOD='" & StokKodu & "'"
	if trim(STOK83E.AMBCODE) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & "&AMBCODE='" & STOK83E.AMBCODE & "'"
	End if
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdfw3ff4f4", "STOK01", QStr, "", -1)

	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = STOK01_PDA_LIST.AddRow()
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

		STOK01_PDA_LIST.KOD = S01.KOD(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S01.KOD(i), "")
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
	Next
	STOK01_PDA_LIST.SetCurrentRow(1)
End Sub
Sub cmdKaydet()
	if EtiketleriBol() Then
		if Doc.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
			Call MPSMiktarlariniGuncelle()
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýþlem tamamlandý", "", MB_OK
		End if
	Else
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Etiket bölme iþlemi gerçekleþmediði için evrak kaydedilemedi", "", MB_OK
	End if
End Sub
Function GetTrMiktar(Barkod, EtiketMiktari, M10T)
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK01_PDA) Then Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	TransferBakiyesi = M10T.R_HM_BAKIYE(1)
	Set S83T = Doc.Select2("sdf243gdfgd", "STOK83T", "D_ID='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	if S83T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		EtiketOkutulan = 0
	Else
		EtiketOkutulan = S83T.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")
	End if
	Set S83T = Doc.Select2("sdf243gdfgd", "STOK83T", "JOBNO='" & M10T.JOBNO(1) & "'", "", -1)
	if S83T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		IsOkutulan = 0
	Else
		IsOkutulan = S83T.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")
	End if

	TransferEdilecek = TransferBakiyesi - IsOkutulan
	EtiketMiktari = STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR - EtiketOkutulan

	if TransferEdilecek > EtiketMiktari Then
		GetTrMiktar = EtiketMiktari
	Else
		GetTrMiktar = TransferEdilecek
	End if
End Function
Sub SF_PAKETICERIGI_AV()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR_KALAN = STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR - STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI
End Sub
Sub cmdEkle()
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	if STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Transfer edilecek miktar girilmemiþ", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK83T) Then Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
	STOK83T.SafePack()

	satirNo = STOK83T.AddRow()
	STOK83T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	STOK83T.SetFieldValueNV "D_ID", -1, STOK01_PDA.D_ID

	STOK83T.JOBNO = STOK01_PDA.JOBNO
	Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK83T","JOBNO",STOK83T.GetCurrentRow()	
	STOK83T.ALANAMBARDASREZ = "H"

	STOK83T.KOD = STOK01_PDA.KOD
	Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK83T","KOD",STOK83T.GetCurrentRow()	
	STOK83T.LOTNUMBER = STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER
	STOK83T.SERINO = STOK01_PDA.SERINO
	STOK83T.FOKOD = STOK01_PDA.FOKOD
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_C = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_C
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R1
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R2
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R3
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM1
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM2
	STOK83T.LOCATION1 = STOK01_PDA.LOCATION1
	STOK83T.LOCATION2 = STOK01_PDA.LOCATION2
	STOK83T.LOCATION3 = STOK01_PDA.LOCATION3
	STOK83T.LOCATION4 = STOK01_PDA.LOCATION4
	STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW1 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1
	STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW2 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2
	STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW3 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3
	STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW4 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4
	STOK83T.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS
	STOK83T.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE
	STOK83T.OALLOC_EVRAKNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKNO
	STOK83T.OALLOC_SIPTYPE = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPTYPE
	STOK83T.OALLOC_SIPNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPNO
	STOK83T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPARTNO
	STOK83T.OALLOC_MPSEVNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSEVNO
	STOK83T.OALLOC_MPSARTNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSARTNO

	STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR = STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI
'	STOK83T.AMBCODE_TR = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "AMBCODE1", STOK01_PDA.D_ID, "")
'	STOK83T.LOCATION1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "LOCATION1", STOK01_PDA.D_ID, "")
'	STOK83T.LOCATION2 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "LOCATION2", STOK01_PDA.D_ID, "")
'	STOK83T.LOCATION3 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "LOCATION3", STOK01_PDA.D_ID, "")
'	STOK83T.LOCATION4 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "LOCATION4", STOK01_PDA.D_ID, "")

	Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK83T","SF_MIKTAR",STOK83T.GetCurrentRow()	
	STOK83T.SF_SF_UNIT = STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT
	Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK83T","SF_SF_UNIT",STOK83T.GetCurrentRow()	
'	STOK83T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR = STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = STOK83T.GetRowCount()
	Call OkutulanMiktarlariGuncelle()
	if STACK_PDA.AUTO_ADD <> "1" Then Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub
Sub OkutulanMiktarlariGuncelle()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T_LIST")
	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		Set S83T = Doc.Select2("sdf242fdsfgsd", "STOK83T", "JOBNO='" & MTableT.JOBNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if S83T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Okutulan = 0
		Else
			Okutulan = S83T.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")
		End if
		MTableT.OKUTULAN_MIKTAR(i) = Okutulan
		MTableT.KALAN_MIKTAR(i) = MTableT.R_HM_BAKIYE(i) - Okutulan
		if Doc.DRound(MTableT.KALAN_MIKTAR(i)) = 0 Then
			MTableT.OKUNDU10(i) = "1"
		Else
			MTableT.OKUNDU10(i) = "0"
		End if
	Next
	MTableT.Refresh
End Sub
Sub cmdEvrakSil()
	Set S83T = Doc.Select2("sdf24gfdgds", "STOK83T", "", "", -1)
	if Doc.RunCommand("MT_DELETE") Then
		Call EkraniTemizle
		Call EtiketKartlariniGuncelle(S83T)
	End if
End Sub
Function EtiketleriBol()
	EtiketleriBol = False

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Set S01_PDA = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
	For i = 1 to STOK83T.GetRowCount()
		if Trim(STOK83T.D_ID(i)) <> "" Then
			Set S01 = GetS01(STOK83T.SERINO(i))
			if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
				DepoMiktari = 0
			Else
				DepoMiktari = S01.SF_MIKTAR(1)
			End if
			if DepoMiktari > STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
				if trim(S25E.EVRAKNO) = "" Then
					Doc1.New_Voucher
					S25E.AMBCODE = STOK83E.AMBCODE
					S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = STOK83E.AMBCODE
					'Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_LOAD_FORM"
				End if

				satirNo = S25T.AddRow
				S25T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				S25T.KOD(satirNo) = S01.KOD(1)
				S25T.LOTNUMBER(satirNo) = S01.LOTNUMBER(1)
				S25T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				S25T.OSF_SF_UNIT(satirNo) = S01.SF_UNIT(1)
				S25T.SERINO(satirNo) = S01.SERINO(1)
				S25T.FOKOD(satirNo) = S01.FOKOD(1)
				S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_C(satirNo) = S01.VRI_VR_C(1)
				S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R1(satirNo) = S01.VRI_VR_R1(1)
				S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R2(satirNo) = S01.VRI_VR_R2(1)
				S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R3(satirNo) = S01.VRI_VR_R3(1)
				S25T.OSF_VRI_NUM1(satirNo) = S01.VRI_NUM1(1)
				S25T.OSF_VRI_NUM2(satirNo) = S01.VRI_NUM2(1)
				S25T.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(1)
				S25T.ALLOC_USTATUS(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_USTATUS(1)
				S25T.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(1)
				S25T.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(1)
				S25T.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(1)
				S25T.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(1)
				S25T.ALLOC_SIPNO(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(1)
				S25T.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(1)
				S25T.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(1)
				S25T.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(1)
				S25T.W_APP(satirNo) = "STOK83"
				S25T.W_TABLE(satirNo) = "STOK83T"
				S25T.W_EVRAKTYPE(satirNo) = "UT"
				S25T.W_EVRAKNO(satirNo) = STOK83E.EVRAKNO
				S25T.W_TRNUM(satirNo) = STOK83T.TRNUM(i)

				Doc1.RunCommand "SATIR_ESKIBILGILERI_YENIYEKOPYALA"
				S25T.NEWSERINO(satirNo) = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
				Call BarkodEskiyiYeniyeKopyala(S25T.SERINO(satirNo), S25T.NEWSERINO(satirNo), S25T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo))

				S25T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR = S25T.AMBCODE_TR
			Else
				EtiketleriBol = True
				Exit Function
			End if
		End if
	Next
	if trim(S25E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
		if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
			EtiketleriBol = True
			For i = 1 to STOK83T.GetRowCount()
				Set S25T1 = Doc1.Select2("sdf34gdfgd", "STOK25T", "TRNUM='" & STOK83T.TRNUM(i) & "'", "", -1)
				if S25T1.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
					STOK83T.SetFieldValueNV "SERINO", i, S25T1.NEWSERINO(1)
					STOK83T.SetFieldValueNV "D_ID", i, S25T1.NEWSERINO(1)
					STOK83T.AMBCODE_TR(i) = S25T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR(1)
				End if
			Next
		End if
	End if

	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Sub EtiketleriBol2()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
	For i = 1 to STOK83T.GetRowCount()
		Set d7LB1 = Doc.Select1("sdf2fsdg", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & STOK83T.D_ID(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if d7LB1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			if d7LB1.SF_BAKIYE > STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
				EskiBarkod = STOK83T.D_ID(i)
				Doc1.Load_Voucher EskiBarkod
				Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_COPY"

				YeniBarkod = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
				Doc1.Load_Voucher YeniBarkod
				Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_PASTE"
				d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK = 0
				d7LB.SF_BAKIYE = STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				d7LB.AMBCODE1 = STOK83E.TARGETAMBCODE
				d7LB.LOCATION1 = STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW1(i)
				d7LB.LOCATION2 = STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW2(i)
				d7LB.LOCATION3 = STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW3(i)
				d7LB.LOCATION4 = STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW4(i)
				d7LB.ONCEKETIKET = EskiBarkod
				d7LB.APPKODU = Doc.GetDocType
				d7LB.EVRAKNO = STOK83E.EVRAKNO
				if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
					Doc1.Load_Voucher EskiBarkod
					if d7LB.SF_BAKIYE < STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
						d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK = d7LB.SF_MIKTAR
					Else
						d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK = d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK + STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
					End if
					if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
						STOK83T.SetFieldValueNV "D_ID", i, YeniBarkod
					End if
				End if
			End if
		End if
	Next
	if Doc.IsChanged Then Doc.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Sub EtiketKartlariniGuncelle(S83T)
	For i = 1 to S83T.GetRowCount()
		Set d7LB1 = Doc.Select1("sdf34gfdfg", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & S83T.D_ID(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if d7LB1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			if trim(d7LB1.APPKODU) = trim(Doc.GetDocType) and trim(d7LB1.EVRAKNO) = trim(S83T.EVRAKNO(i)) and trim(d7LB1.ONCEKETIKET) <> "" Then
				Call EtiketIptal(d7LB1)
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function CikartOrIptal(Barkod)
	CikartOrIptal = False
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
	For i = 1 to STOK83T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(STOK83T.D_ID(i)) = trim(Barkod) Then
			ret = Doc.d7MsgBox(trim(Barkod) & " barkodu daha önce okutulmuþ. " & vbcrlf & "Transfer listesinden çýkarmak istiyorsanýz 'Evet', istemiyorsanýz 'Hayýr' tuþuna basýnýz", "Uyarý", MB_YESNO)
			if ret = 6 Then
				Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
				STOK83T.DeleteRow_NoUI i
				Call OkutulanMiktarlariGuncelle()
			End if
			CikartOrIptal = True
			Exit Function
		Else
			Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("dwefddddwsds", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & STOK83T.D_ID(i) & "'", "", -1)
			if d7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				if trim(d7LB.APPKODU) = "STOK83" and trim(d7LB.EVRAKNO) = trim(STOK83E.EVRAKNO) and _
					trim(D7LB.ONCEKETIKET) = trim(Barkod) Then

					ret = Doc.d7MsgBox(trim(Barkod) & " barkodu daha önce okutulmuþ. " & vbcrlf & "Transfer listesinden çýkarmak istiyorsanýz 'Evet', istemiyorsanýz 'Hayýr' tuþuna basýnýz", "Uyarý", MB_YESNO)
					if ret = 6 Then
						Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
						STOK83T.DeleteRow_NoUI i
						Call EtiketIptal(d7LB)
						Call OkutulanMiktarlariGuncelle()
					End if
					CikartOrIptal = True
					Exit Function
				End if
			End if
		End if
 	Next
End Function
Sub EtiketIptal(d7LB1)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Doc1.Load_Voucher d7LB1.ONCEKETIKET
	d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK = d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK - d7LB1.SF_MIKTAR
	if d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK < 0 Then
		d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = d7LB.SF_MIKTAR - d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK
		d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK = 0
	End if
	Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Function GetStokMiktar(SeriNo, Lokasyon1, Lokasyon2, Lokasyon3, Lokasyon4, Depo)
	GetStokMiktar = 0
	Lokasyon1 = ""
	Lokasyon2 = ""
	Lokasyon3 = ""
	Lokasyon4 = ""
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	'Depo = ""

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdf34dfh", "STOK01", "SERINO='" & SeriNo & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(SeriNo) & " seri no.lu malzeme stokta bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
		Exit Function
	End if

	if S01.GetRowCount() > 1 Then
	'*********** OKAN TAKATAÞ  TARAFINDAN EKLENDÝ
		
		Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdf34dfh", "STOK01", "SERINO='" & SeriNo & "'&AMBCODE='"& STOK83E.AMBCODE &"'", "", -1)

		if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox trim(SeriNo) & " seri no.lu malzeme "&STOK83E.AMBCODE & " deposunda bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
			Exit Function
		End if

		'Doc.d7MsgBox trim(SeriNo) & " seri no.lu malzeme stok listesinde birden çok satýrda bulundu", "", MB_OK
		'Exit Function
	'************************

	End if
	GetStokMiktar = S01.SF_MIKTAR(1)
	Lokasyon1 = S01.LOCATION1(1)
	Lokasyon2 = S01.LOCATION2(1)
	Lokasyon3 = S01.LOCATION3(1)
	Lokasyon4 = S01.LOCATION4(1)
	Depo = S01.AMBCODE(1)
End Function
Function GetS01(SeriNo)
	Set GetS01 = Doc.Select1("dfgrfgwsyksrthf", "STOK01", "KOD='XDFGW46RT45'", "", -1)
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdf34dfh", "STOK01", "SERINO='" & SeriNo & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(SeriNo) & " seri no.lu malzeme stokta bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
		Exit Function
	End if

	if S01.GetRowCount() > 1 Then
	'*********** OKAN TAKATAÞ  TARAFINDAN EKLENDÝ
		Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdf34dfh", "STOK01", "SERINO='" & SeriNo & "'&AMBCODE='"& STOK83E.AMBCODE &"'", "", -1)

		if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox trim(SeriNo) & " seri no.lu malzeme "&STOK83E.AMBCODE & " deposunda bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
			Exit Function
		End if

		'Doc.d7MsgBox trim(SeriNo) & " seri no.lu malzeme stok listesinde birden çok satýrda bulundu", "", MB_OK
		'Exit Function
	'************************

	End if
	Set GetS01 = S01
End Function

Function MPSeEkle(MPSNo, StokKodu)
	MPSeEkle = ""
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MMPS10", "")
	Set M10T = Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MPSNo
	satirNo = M10T.AddRow
	M10T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
	M10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(satirNo) = "H"
	M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo) = StokKodu
	JobNo = M10T.JOBNO(satirNo)
	if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
		MPSeEkle = JobNo
		Set ML = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T_LIST")
		ML.SetRowAID 1,1,1
		satirNo1 = ML.AddRow
		ML.SetCurrentRow(satirNo1)
		ML.R_KAYNAKTYPE(satirNo1) = "H"
		ML.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo1) = StokKodu
		ML.JOBNO(satirNo1) = JobNo
		ML.KAYNAK_AD(satirNo1) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, "")
		ML.KAYNAK_BIRIM(satirNo1) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", StokKodu, "")
		ML.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Sub MPSMiktarlariniGuncelle()
	DocYaratildi = False
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set S83T = Doc.Select2G("asdfasdg", "STOK83T", "JOBNO>' '", "", "JOBNO", -1)
	For i = 1 to S83T.GetRowCount()
		if Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_MIKTAR0", S83T.JOBNO(i), "JOBNO") = 0 Then
			if Not DocYaratildi Then
				Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MMPS10", "")
				Set M10T = Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
				ret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(MTable.MPSNO)
				if ret <> 0 Then Exit Sub
				DocYaratildi = True
			End if
			Set M10T1 = Doc1.Select2("sdfeaj", "MMPS10T", "JOBNO='" & S83T.JOBNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
			if M10T1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				satirNo = Doc.Cdbl(M10T1.SRNUM(1))
				M10T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				M10T.R_MIKTAR0(satirNo) = S83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			End if
		End if
	Next
	if DocYaratildi Then
		Doc1.Save_Voucher
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	End if
End Sub
Sub MPS_BARKOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	Barkod = 	MTable.MPS_BARKOD
	MTable.MPS_BARKOD = ""

	If InStr(1, trim(Barkod), "%JB%") = 1 Then
		IsNo = Doc.RightJustify(trim(Mid(Barkod, 5)),16)
		QStr = "SELECT EVRAKNO, JOBNO, R_KAYNAKTYPE, R_KAYNAKKODU, R_SIRANO, "
		QStr = QStr & " (R_MIKTART - R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN - R_HM_ODONEMNET - R_HM_IADE_EDILEN) TR_BAKIYE "
		QStr = QStr & " FROM MMPS10T"
		QStr = QStr & " WHERE JOBNO='" & IsNo & "'"
		Set M10T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfdfgjdk", QStr)
		if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Barkod Geçersiz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
			Exit Sub
		End if
		If M10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(1) = "H" Then
			if M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(1) = STOK01_PDA.KOD Then
				HmdIsNo = M10T.JOBNO(1)
			Else
				Doc.d7MsgBox "Okutulan barkod baþka hammaddeye ait", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
				Exit Sub
			End if
		Else
			MPSNo = M10T.EVRAKNO(1)
		End if
	Else
		if InStr(1, trim(Barkod), "%MP%") = 1 Then
			MPSNo = Doc.RightJustify(trim(Mid(Barkod, 5)),10)
		Else
			MPSNo = Doc.RightJustify(trim(Barkod),10)
		End if
	End if

	if Doc.IsBlank(M10T) Then
		MPSNo1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "EVRAKNO", MPSNo, "") 
		if trim(MPSNo1) = "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "MPS No Geçersiz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
			Exit Sub
		End if
		QStr = "SELECT EVRAKNO, JOBNO, R_KAYNAKTYPE, R_KAYNAKKODU, R_SIRANO, "
		QStr = QStr & " (R_MIKTART - R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN - R_HM_ODONEMNET - R_HM_IADE_EDILEN) R_HM_BAKIYE "
		QStr = QStr & " FROM MMPS10T"
		QStr = QStr & " WHERE EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo1 & "'"
		QStr = QStr & " AND R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'"
		QStr = QStr & " AND R_KAYNAKKODU='" & STOK01_PDA.KOD & "'"
		Set M10T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfdfgjdk", QStr)
		if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu hammadde " & trim(MPSNo1) & " no.lu MPS 'te bulunamadý", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
			Exit Sub
		End if
	End if

	if Doc.IsBlank(M10T) Then Exit Sub

	STOK01_PDA.JOBNO = M10T.JOBNO(1)
	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = GetTrMiktar(STOK01_PDA.D_ID, STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR, M10T)

	if STACK_PDA.PROPT = "1" and STACK_PDA.AUTO_ADD = "1" Then
		if STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu malzemeden transfer edilecek miktar yok", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
			Exit Sub
		End if
		Call cmdEkle()
	Else
		Dim Par
		Set Par=Nothing
		Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "TR_EKLE",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
	End if
End Sub
Sub HammaddeIhtiyacMPSList()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	QStr = "SELECT '0' OKUNDU10, T.EVRAKNO, T.JOBNO, T.R_KAYNAKTYPE, T.R_MAMULKODU, S.AD MAMUL_AD, "
	QStr = QStr & " (R_MIKTART - R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN - R_HM_ODONEMNET - R_HM_IADE_EDILEN) R_HM_BAKIYE, "
	QStr = QStr & " 0 OKUTULAN_MIKTAR,  0 KALAN_MIKTAR"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM MMPS10T T"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S ON S.KOD=T.R_MAMULKODU"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE T.R_STATUS='R'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND T.ACIK_KAPALI=' '"
	QStr = QStr & " AND T.R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND T.R_KAYNAKKODU='" & STOK01_PDA.KOD & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND T.R_ACIK_KAPALI=' '"
	QStr = QStr & " AND (R_MIKTART - R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN - R_HM_ODONEMNET - R_HM_IADE_EDILEN) >0"	
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY T.EVRAKNO"

	Set M10T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sDGE4RTUE546IEdfdfgjdk", QStr)
	Set MPSList = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T_LIST")
	MPSList.Empty
	MPSList.AppendTable(M10T)
End Sub
Sub BarkodEskiyiYeniyeKopyala(EskiBarkod, YeniBarkod, Miktar)
	if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "KOD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(EskiBarkod),30), "")) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher EskiBarkod
	Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_COPY"
	Doc1.Load_Voucher YeniBarkod
	Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_PASTE"
	D7LB.SERINO = YeniBarkod
	if trim(d7LB.ILKETIKET) = "" Then d7LB.ILKETIKET = EskiBarkod
	d7LB.ONCEKETIKET = EskiBarkod
	d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = Miktar
	d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK = 0
	Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Sub Makro1()

	Dim KOD, LOT, SERI, QSTR ,EVRAKNO
	STOK70T.SAFEPACK()

	Dim MTableT

	Set MTableT = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STACK","IO")
	
	MTableT.Addfield2 "KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "KOD_STOK00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "LOTNUMBER", "CHAR",12,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "EVRAKTYPE", "CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SERINO", "CHAR",20,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO", "CHAR",10,0,"L","",""

	for i = 1 To STOK70T.GetRecCount()

		KOD = STOK70T.KOD(i)
		LOT = STOK70T.LOTNUMBER(i)
		SERI =STOK70T.SERINO(i)
		
		stok10a_danal(KOD, LOT, SERI)

		if A_STOK10A.GetRecCount() > 0 Then 		
		
****
				K = A_STOK10A.KOD(j)
				L = A_STOK10A.LOTNUMBER(j) 
				S = A_STOK10A.SERINO(j)
				
				EVRAKNO =  Doc.RightJustify(trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKno(j)),10)
					
				end if 
			next
		end if	
	next 

	Call Yazdir()
End sub

Function MG_EVRAKTYPE(KOD, LOT, SERI)
	
	MG_EVRAKTYPE = ""
	Dim QSTR
	QSTR = ""

	QSTR = " SELECT * FROM STOK10A  WHERE  KOD = '"& KOD &"' AND LOTNUMBER = '"& LOT &"' AND SERINO = '"& SERI &"'  "
	QSTR = QSTR & " AND EVRAKTYPE IN ('MG', 'SI', 'SF', 'FG', 'K2', 'DG', 'DT','0S')  AND SF_MIKTAR > 0 "
	Set A_STOK10A = Doc.RunSqlQuery("SDSDDSD", QSTR)
	
	'MSGBOX KOD & "  "  & LOT & "  "  & SERI & "  "  &A_STOK10A.GetRecCount()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	'MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	
	for j = 1 To A_STOK10A.GetRecCount()
			satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
			MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			MTableT.KOD = A_STOK10A.KOD(j)
			MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", A_STOK10A.KOD(j), "")
			MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK10A.LOTNUMBER(j)
			MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)
			MTableT.SERINO = A_STOK10A.SERINO(j)
			MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK10A.EVRAKNO(j)
	next	

End Function

Sub Yazdir()

	SayfaBoyu = 290
	SayfaEni = 300
	SolMargin = 6
	SagMargin = 1
	UstMargin = 20
	AltMargin = 1


	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Order Confirmation", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("Left")
	res = pe1.SelectFont(Arial, 10)
	
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, 20 , 10 , 20 , "ETYP")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 30 , "EVRAKNO")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , "KOD")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 65 , "AD")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 30 , "LOT")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , "SERÝNO")
	res = pe1.SelectFont(Arial, 8)
	
	for i = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount()
		
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 10, 20 , 10 , 20 , trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)))
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 30 , trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)))
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , trim(MTableT.KOD(i)))
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 65 , trim(MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD(i)))
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 30 , trim(MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)))
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)))
			if pe1.GetTop = 280 Then
			 res = pe1.PageBreak()
				res = pe1.write(UstMargin, 20 , 10 , 20 , "ETYP")
				res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 30 , "EVRAKNO")
				res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , "KOD")
				res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 65 , "AD")
				res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 30 , "LOT")
				res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , "SERÝNO")
			end if
	next
	
End Sub

Function stok10a_danal(sayac_10A, KOD,LOT,SERI)

	QSTR = " SELECT * FROM STOK10A  WHERE  KOD = '"& KOD &"' AND LOTNUMBER = '"& LOT &"' AND SERINO = '"& SERI &"'  "
	QSTR = QSTR & " AND EVRAKTYPE IN ('MG', 'SI', 'SF', 'FG', 'K2', 'DG', 'DT','0S')  AND SF_MIKTAR > 0 "
	Set A_STOK10A = Doc.RunSqlQuery("AAA_" & sayac, QSTR)

	for j = 1 To A_STOK10A.GetRecCount()
		if A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j) = "SI" OR  A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j) = "SF" Then	
			satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
			MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			MTableT.KOD = A_STOK10A.KOD(j)
			MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", A_STOK10A.KOD(j), "")
			MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK10A.LOTNUMBER(j)
			MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)
			MTableT.SERINO = A_STOK10A.SERINO(j)
			MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK10A.EVRAKNO(j)
		elseif 	A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j) = "MG" Then
			*** mg yiii çaðýrrrrrr
		end if
	next		
End Function

Function MG_TABLO(sayac_mg)

		Dim QSTR_MG	
		
		QSTR_MG = " SELECT STOK20TI.KOD, STOK20TI.LOTNUMBER,  STOK20TI.SERINO FROM STOK20T"
		QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & " LEFT JOIN STOK20TI ON STOK20TI.EVRAKNO   = STOK20T.EVRAKNO AND STOK20TI.KARSITRNUM = STOK20T.KARSITRNUM"	
		QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & " WHERE STOK20T.EVRAKNO = '"&EVRAKNO&"' AND STOK20TI.BILGISATIRIE <> 'E' "
		
		Set A_STOK20T = Doc.RunSqlQuery("MG" & sayac_mg , QSTR_MG)	

		if A_STOK20T.GetRecCount()	 > 0 Then 

			for t = 1 To A_STOK20T.GetRecCount()
							
				K = A_STOK20T.KOD(t)
				L = A_STOK20T.LOTNUMBER(t)
				S = A_STOK20T.SERINO(t)						
				SET MG_TABLO = stok10a_danal(sayac_10A, K,L,S)	
			next		
		end if
	End Function

Sub Makro1()
	say =  1
	msgbox say_()
	say =  2
	msgbox say_()

	exit sub

	 dim TabArr(100,2)
	 Dim sayac
	 sayac=0
	 Set AL_1 = TABLO_AL(sayac,"2013/09/09")
	 MSGBOX AL_1.GetRecCount()
	 MSGBOX AL_1.GetAppName()
	 TabArr(sayac,0)=sayac
	 TabArr(sayac,1)=AL_1.GetAppName()

	 sayac=sayac+1
	 Set AL_2 = TABLO_AL(sayac,"2013/09/10")
	 MSGBOX AL_2.GetRecCount()
	 MSGBOX AL_2.GetAppName()
	 TabArr(sayac,0)=sayac
	 TabArr(sayac,1)=AL_2.GetAppName()
    
End Sub


Function TABLO_AL(sayac,T_TARIH)

	 Set a_stok10 = Doc.RunSqlQuery("TTTT_"&sayac, "SELECT * FROM STOK10A WHERE TARIH >'"&T_TARIH&"'")
	 Set TABLO_AL = a_stok10

End Function

Function say_()
	say_ = say * 3
End Function

Sub Makro1()

	on error resume next

	Set STOK70T = Doc.Select1("FASFASDS", "STOK70T", "EVRAKNO = '435703'", "SRNUM", -1)

	'**** FATURADAN OKU	
	for i = 3 To 3 ' STOK70T.GetRecCount()

		KOD = STOK70T.KOD(i)
		LOT = STOK70T.LOTNUMBER(i)
		SERI = STOK70T.SERINO(i)
				
		QSTR = " SELECT KOD, EVRAKNO, EVRAKTYPE, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, SUM(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR FROM STOK10A  WHERE  KOD = '"& KOD &"' AND LOTNUMBER = '"& LOT &"' AND SERINO = '"& SERI &"'  "
		QSTR = QSTR & " AND EVRAKTYPE IN ('MG', 'SI', 'SF', 'FG', 'K2', 'DG', 'DT','0S')  AND SF_MIKTAR > 0 "
		QSTR = QSTR & " GROUP BY KOD, EVRAKNO, EVRAKTYPE, LOTNUMBER, SERINO "
		Set A_STOK10A = Doc.RunSqlQuery("AAA", QSTR)

				MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK10A.EVRAKNO(j)

				MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = "-     " & i&"[" & trim(KOD) & "#- "  & trim(SERI) & " - "  & trim(STOK70T.EVRAKNO) & " - FT (" & STOK70T.SF_MIKTAR(i) &"*"&MTableT.ORAN_M2F2& ")  ]" 
				if trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "DG" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "DT" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "0S" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "SF" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "SI" Then
					MTableT.OKUNDU =  "*"
				end if

					MTableT.SAYAC_K2(i) = SAYAC_1
					MTableT.SAYAC_K2 = SAYAC_1
					SAYAC_1  = SAYAC_1 + 1 
				end if
		next		
	next
	
	'***** 	TABLOYU AÇ
	
	icini_ac = 1 
	while  icini_ac = 1
		
		'**** ÜRETÝM FÝÞÝ ÝÇÝN
		
		for i = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount()
			if trim(MTableT.OKUNDU(i)) =  ""  AND trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "MG"  Then					
					Dim QSTR_MG				
					QSTR_MG = " SELECT ST20TI.EVRAKNO, ST20TI.KOD, ST20TI.LOTNUMBER,  ST20TI.SERINO, ST20TI.SF_MIKTAR FROM STOK20T "
					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & "  LEFT JOIN (SELECT EVRAKNO,KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, SUM(SF_MIKTAR)SF_MIKTAR,KARSITRNUM FROM STOK20TI "
					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & " WHERE STOK20TI.EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"' AND STOK20TI.BILGISATIRIE <> 'E' "

					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & " ON ST20TI.EVRAKNO = STOK20T.EVRAKNO AND ST20TI.KARSITRNUM = STOK20T.KARSITRNUM "

					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & " STOK20T.KOD = '"& MTableT.KOD(i) &"' AND STOK20T.LOTNUMBER = '"& MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i) &"' AND STOK20T.SERINO = '"& MTableT.SERINO(i) &"'  "			 								
														                								
					MTableT.OKUNDU(i) =  "*"			
					Set A_STOK20TI = Doc.RunSqlQuery("MG" , QSTR_MG)	
					
					if A_STOK20TI.GetRecCount()	 > 0 Then 

						for j = 1 To A_STOK20TI.GetRecCount()		
						
							QSTR_MG_COUNT = " SELECT STOK20T.SERINO FROM STOK20TI"
							QSTR_MG_COUNT = QSTR_MG_COUNT & " LEFT JOIN STOK20T ON STOK20T.EVRAKNO = STOK20TI.EVRAKNO AND STOK20TI.KARSITRNUM  = STOK20T.KARSITRNUM "
							QSTR_MG_COUNT = QSTR_MG_COUNT & " WHERE STOK20TI.EVRAKNO ='"&A_STOK20TI.EVRAKNO(j)&"' AND STOK20TI.KOD = '"&A_STOK20TI.KOD(j)&"' AND STOK20TI.LOTNUMBER =  '"&A_STOK20TI.LOTNUMBER(j)&"' AND  STOK20TI.SERINO = '"&A_STOK20TI.SERINO(j)&"' "
							QSTR_MG_COUNT = QSTR_MG_COUNT & " GROUP BY STOK20T.SERINO"
							
							Set D_STOK20TI = Doc.RunSqlQuery("JKJKJMG" , QSTR_MG_COUNT )	
							satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
							MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
							MTableT.ORAN_M2F2 = D_STOK20TI.GetRecCount()

							MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", A_STOK20TI.KOD(j), "")
							MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK20TI.LOTNUMBER(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = "M2"
							MTableT.SERINO = A_STOK20TI.SERINO(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK20TI.EVRAKNO(j)
							MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = A_STOK20TI.SF_MIKTAR(j)
	
							MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) & "[" & trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) &" (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) & "*" & MTableT.ORAN_M2F2 & ") ]"
							if trim(A_STOK20TI.KOD(j))	= trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) Then 
								'Msgbox A_STOK20TI.EVRAKNO(j) & " Nolu Evrakda HARCANAN ile MAMUL  ayný."									
								MTableT.OKUNDU =  "*"			
							end if	
						next	
					end if
			end if
		next		
		'**** FASON ÝÇÝN 
		
		for i = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount()
			if trim(MTableT.OKUNDU(i)) =  ""  AND trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "FG" Then					
					Dim QSTR_FG			
					QSTR_FG = " SELECT ST68TI.EVRAKNO, ST68TI.KOD, ST68TI.LOTNUMBER,  ST68TI.SERINO FROM STOK68T "
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & "  LEFT JOIN (SELECT EVRAKNO,KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, SUM(SF_MIKTAR)SF_MIKTAR,KARSITRNUM FROM STOK68TI "
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " WHERE STOK68TI.EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"' AND STOK68TI.BILGISATIRIE <> 'E' "
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " GROUP BY EVRAKNO,KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO,KARSITRNUM)ST68TI "
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " ON ST68TI.EVRAKNO = STOK68T.EVRAKNO AND ST68TI.KARSITRNUM = STOK68T.KARSITRNUM "
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " WHERE STOK68T.EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"' AND " 
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " STOK68T.KOD = '"& MTableT.KOD(i) &"' AND STOK68T.LOTNUMBER = '"& MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i) &"' AND STOK68T.SERINO = '"& MTableT.SERINO(i) &"'  "			 								
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " AND STOK68T.LOTNUMBER <> ST68TI.LOTNUMBER AND STOK68T.SERINO <> ST68TI.SERINO "
								
					MTableT.OKUNDU(i) =  "*"

							QSTR_fG_COUNT = " SELECT * FROM STOK68TI"
							QSTR_fG_COUNT = QSTR_fG_COUNT & " LEFT JOIN STOK68T ON STOK68T.EVRAKNO = STOK68TI.EVRAKNO AND STOK68TI.KARSITRNUM  = STOK68T.KARSITRNUM "
							QSTR_fG_COUNT = QSTR_fG_COUNT & " WHERE STOK68TI.EVRAKNO = '"&A_STOK68TI.EVRAKNO(j)&"' AND STOK68TI.KOD = '"&A_STOK68TI.KOD(j)&"' AND STOK68TI.LOTNUMBER =  '"&A_STOK68TI.LOTNUMBER(j)&"' AND  STOK68TI.SERINO = '"&A_STOK68TI.SERINO(j)&"' "
							Set Y_STOK20TI = Doc.RunSqlQuery("WEWERTRTVVV" , QSTR_fG_COUNT )

							MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

							MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK68TI.LOTNUMBER(j)

							MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK68TI.EVRAKNO(j)
							MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = A_STOK68TI.SF_MIKTAR(j)
							MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) & "[" & trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & " - " &  trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i))  & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) & " (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) &"*"&MTableT.ORAN_M2F2&  ") ]"
						next	
					end if
			end if
		next	
		'**  K2 ÝÇÝN OKU

		for i = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount()
			KOD = MTableT.KOD(i)
			LOT = MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)

					Dim QSTR_K2

					QSTR_KXX = QSTR_KXX & " WHERE EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"' AND   EVRAKTYPE = 'K2 ' AND KOD = '"& KOD &"' AND LOTNUMBER = '"& LOT &"' AND SERINO = '"& SERI &"' "
					
					Set XX_STOK25T = Doc.RunSqlQuery("SDASDSAXX___" , QSTR_KXX)

					QSTR_K2 = QSTR_K2 & " WHERE EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"'  AND KARSIEVRAKTYPE = 'K2' AND TRNUM = '"&XX_STOK25T.TRNUM(1)&"' "
					
					Set A_STOK25T = Doc.RunSqlQuery("KKKFF___" , QSTR_K2)

					QSTR_K2__2  = QSTR_K2__2  & " WHERE EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"'  AND EVRAKTYPE = 'K2' AND SERINO = '"&A_STOK25T.SERINO(1)&"' "
										
					Set yy_STOK25T = Doc.RunSqlQuery("ZZSSDSDY" , QSTR_K2__2)
					if yy_STOK25T.GetRecCount() > 0 Then 											
						k2_satirsayisi = 0
					end if
					MTableT.OKUNDU(i) =  "*"

						for j = 1 To A_STOK25T.GetRecCount()		
							satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()

							MTableT.KOD = A_STOK25T.KOD(j)

							MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK25T.EVRAKNO(j)
							MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = A_STOK25T.SF_MIKTAR(j) * -1
									
							MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) & "[" & trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i))  & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) & " (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) &"*"&MTableT.ORAN_M2F2&  ") ]"
							MTableT.SAYAC_K2 = MTableT.SAYAC_K2(i)
							QSTR_CAN = "" 
							
							QSTR_CAN  = " SELECT KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, EVRAKTYPE   FROM  STOK10A  "
							QSTR_CAN  = QSTR_CAN & " WHERE KOD = '"& MTableT.KOD &"' AND LOTNUMBER =  ' "& MTableT.LOTNUMBER &"' AND  SERINO =  '"& MTableT.SERINO &"' AND (EVRAKTYPE = 'MG' OR  EVRAKTYPE = 'K2'  ) "
							QSTR_CAN  = QSTR_CAN & "  GROUP BY KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, EVRAKTYPE  "							
							
							Set QSTR_CAN = Doc.RunSqlQuery("HKHKJHKDDDD" , QSTR_CAN)
							if QSTR_CAN.GetRecCount() > 0 Then
								MTableT.OKUNDU =  "*"			
							end if
							
						next	
					end if
			end if
		next

		for i = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount()
			if trim(MTableT.OKUNDU(i)) = ""  Then
				KOD = MTableT.KOD(i)
				LOT = MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)
				SERI = MTableT.SERINO(i)
						
				QSTR_A = " SELECT KOD, EVRAKNO, EVRAKTYPE, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, SUM(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR FROM STOK10A  WHERE  KOD = '"& KOD &"' AND LOTNUMBER = '"& LOT &"' AND SERINO = '"& SERI &"'  "
				QSTR_A = QSTR_A & " AND EVRAKTYPE IN ('MG', 'SI', 'SF', 'FG', 'K2', 'DG', 'DT','0S')  AND SF_MIKTAR > 0 "
				QSTR_A = QSTR_A & " GROUP BY KOD, EVRAKNO, EVRAKTYPE, LOTNUMBER, SERINO "
				
				Set A_STOK10A = Doc.RunSqlQuery("DSADASD_DD", QSTR_A)
									
				MTableT.OKUNDU(i) =  "*"					
				if A_STOK10A.GetRecCount() > 0 Then 
					
					for j = 1 To A_STOK10A.GetRecCount()					
						if trim(KOD) = trim(A_STOK10A.KOD(j)) and trim(LOT) = trim(A_STOK10A.LOTNUMBER(j)) and trim(SERI) = trim(A_STOK10A.SERINO(j)) and trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) Then
							a = 1
						else 	
							satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
							MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
							F_SAY = F_SAY + 1 
							MTableT.F_SAYAC = F_SAY 							
							MTableT.KOD = A_STOK10A.KOD(j)
							MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", A_STOK10A.KOD(j), "")
							MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK10A.LOTNUMBER(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)
							MTableT.SERINO = A_STOK10A.SERINO(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK10A.EVRAKNO(j)
							MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = A_STOK10A.SF_MIKTAR(j)
							
							MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) &  "[" & trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i))  & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) & " (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) &"*"&MTableT.ORAN_M2F2&  ") ]"
									
							if trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "DG" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "DT" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "0S" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "SF" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "SI" Then
								MTableT.OKUNDU =  "*"
							end if 
																		
							if trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "KD"  Then				
									MTableT.SAYAC_K2 = MTableT.SAYAC_K2(i) 							
							end if 
							
							if trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "K2" and trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) <> "KD"Then
									MTableT.SAYAC_K2(i) = SAYAC_1
									MTableT.SAYAC_K2 = SAYAC_1
									SAYAC_1  = SAYAC_1 + 1 
							end if
						end if	
					next	
				end if	
			else
			a = 1
			end if				
		next				
		Set DAS_ = Doc.Select2G( "dsasdrrrr", "MAKROTABLET" , " ", " ", "OKUNDU", -1 )
		ser = ser + 1
		
		if DAS_.GetRecCount() = 1 Then 
			icini_ac = 0		
		end if
		
	Wend

	sayil	 = 0
	for i = 1 TO MTableT.GetRecCount()
		MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i) = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) & "[" & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & "]"  & trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)) &"-"& trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) & " (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) &"*"&MTableT.ORAN_M2F2& ")"
	next
	MTableT.sort("KAYNAK_KOD")
if 1 = 2 Then 
	for i = 1 TO MTableT.GetRecCount()
			if trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "0S" OR trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "SI" Then
				Set KAPP_ = Doc.Select2( "SWSWSSDDFFD", "MAKROTABLET" ,  "KAYNAK_KOD = '"&MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)&"'' ", "", -1 )
				if KAPP_.GetRecCount() > 1  Then				
				sayil	 = sayil	 + 1
					MTableT.AYNI(i) = sayil	
			 end if
			end if	 			
	next
end if	
'Doc.ShowTableDynForm MTableT.GetAppName
Call Yazdir_2()

			exit sub
			
	for k = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount() 
		if MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(k) = "SI" OR  MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(k) = "SF" OR MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(k) = "0S" OR MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(k) =  "DG" OR MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(k) = "DT" Then
			satirNo = MTableT_2.AddRow()
			MTableT_2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			
			MTableT_2.KOD = MTableT.KOD(k)
			MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD = MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(k)
			MTableT_2.SERINO = trim(MTableT.SERINO(k))
			MTableT_2.EVRAKNO = MTableT.EVRAKNO(k)
			MTableT_2.EVRAKTYPE = MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(k)
		end if	
	next

	Dim hareket_syisi, yildiz_syisi

	'' nihai tablo hazýrlanýyor
	for 	xx =1 To MTableT_2.GetREcCount() 
		yildiz_syisi = 0 
		hareket_syisi =  0
		ReDim aray(50,3) ' birinci özellik miktar ikinci  mg/m2 fg/f2 oraný, üçüncü özellik evraktype
		for i = 1 To len(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)))		
			if mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i ,  1) = "*" Then	 
				Dim miktar,oran,e_ty_al
				flag = 1
				flag_2 = 1
				
				for t = 1 To 16
					if mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t , 1 ) <> "(" and flag Then
						if flag = 1 then 
							miktar =  mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t , 1 )	& miktar
						end if 	
					elseif mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t , 1 ) = "(" 	then
						flag = 0
						e_ty_al = mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t - 3  , 1 ) & mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t - 2 , 1 )
						
					end if
					
					if mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i + t , 1 ) <> ")"  Then					
						if flag_2 = 1 then oran = oran &  mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i + t , 1 )
					else	
						flag_2 = 0
					end if
				next	

				aray(yildiz_syisi,0) = miktar
				aray(yildiz_syisi,1) = oran
				aray(yildiz_syisi,2) = e_ty_al
				miktar = ""
				oran = ""
				yildiz_syisi = yildiz_syisi + 1				
			elseif mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i ,  1) = "#" Then
				flaf_ax = 1
				for t = 1 To 30				
					if i - t > 0  Then
						if mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t , 1 ) <> "["  Then
							if flaf_ax = 1 Then fatura_kod = mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t , 1 ) & fatura_kod						
						elseif mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t , 1 ) = "[" Then	
							 flaf_ax = 0
							 fatura_sirasi = mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t - 3 , 1 ) & mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t - 2 , 1 ) & mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t - 1 , 1 )
						end if
					end if	
				next	
			end if
		next
		s_array = 0

		MTableT_2.FAT_KOD(xx) = fatura_kod
		MTableT_2.FAT_SIRA(xx) = fatura_sirasi
		fatura_kod = ""
		fatura_sirasi = ""
	
		for t = 0 TO ubound	(aray,1)
			if trim(aray(t+1,0)) = "" Then Exit For
			if trim(aray(t, 2)) = "K2" OR trim(aray(t, 2)) = "KD" Then	
				aray(t, 0) = 1 
				aray(t, 1) = 1
			end if
		next

		for t = 0 TO ubound	(aray,1)
			if trim(aray(t+1,0)) = "" Then Exit For						
			if Doc.Fmod(t+1, 2) = 1 and round(cdbl(trim(aray(t,0))),4) <> 0 and round(cdbl(trim(aray(t+1,0))),4) <> 0 Then			
				aray(t+1,1) = round(cdbl(round(cdbl(round(cdbl(trim(aray(t+1,0))),4) / round(cdbl(trim(aray(t,0))),4)),4) * round(cdbl(trim(aray(t+1,1))),4)),4)
			elseif round(cdbl(trim(aray(t,1))),4) <> 0 and round(cdbl(trim(aray(t+1,0))),4) <> 0 Then
				aray(t+1 ,0) =  round(cdbl(round(cdbl(trim(aray(t+1,0))),4) / round(cdbl(trim(aray(t,1))),4)),4)
			end if	
			s_array = s_array + 1 
		next

		MTableT_2.SF_MIKTAR_C(xx) = aray(s_array - 1,0)
		
	next

	Set Last_xx = Doc.Select2G("RRRRF" ,"MAKROTABLET_2", "",  "", "EVRAKNO+SERINO+FAT_KOD", -1)

		for k = 1 To Last_xx.GetRecCount()
			satirNo = MTableT_3.AddRow()
			MTableT_3.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				
			MTableT_3.KOD = trim(Last_xx.KOD(k))
			MTableT_3.FAT_KOD = trim(Last_xx.FAT_KOD(k))
			MTableT_3.FAT_SIRA = trim(Last_xx.FAT_SIRA(k))
			MTableT_3.KOD_STOK00_AD = trim(Last_xx.KOD_STOK00_AD(k))
			MTableT_3.KAYNAK_KOD = trim(Last_xx.KAYNAK_KOD(k))
			MTableT_3.SERINO = trim(Last_xx.SERINO(k))
			MTableT_3.EVRAKNO = trim(Last_xx.EVRAKNO(k))
			MTableT_3.EVRAKTYPE = trim(Last_xx.EVRAKTYPE(k))
			MTableT_3.SF_MIKTAR_C = Last_xx.SF_MIKTAR_C(k)
						
			if trim(Last_xx.EVRAKTYPE(k)) = "SI" Then		
				Set SI_STOK29T = Doc.Select1("TYTRHR", "STOK29T", "EVRAKNO = '"&Last_xx.EVRAKNO(k)&"' &KOD = '"&trim(Last_xx.KOD(k))&"' & SERINO = '"&trim(Last_xx.SERINO(k))&"' ", "", -1 )
				if SI_STOK29T.GetRecCount() > 0 Then
					MTableT_3.SF_MIKTAR_G = SI_STOK29T.SF_MIKTAR(1)
					MTableT_3.TL_FIYAT = SI_STOK29T.TL_FIYAT(1)
					MTableT_3.TL_TUTAR = SI_STOK29T.TL_FIYAT(1) *  round(cdbl(trim(Last_xx.SF_MIKTAR_C(k))),2)
					MTableT_3.PRICE = "TL"
				end if	
			elseif	trim(Last_xx.EVRAKTYPE(k)) = "0S" Then						
					Set S_STOK2AT = Doc.Select1("ERETRR", "STOK2AT", "EVRAKNO = '"&Last_xx.EVRAKNO(k)&"' &KOD = '"&trim(Last_xx.KOD(k))&"' & SERINO = '"&trim(Last_xx.SERINO(k))&"' ", "", -1 )					
					if S_STOK2AT.GetRecCount() > 0 Then
						MTableT_3.SF_MIKTAR_G = S_STOK2AT.SF_MIKTAR(1)
						MTableT_3.TL_FIYAT = S_STOK2AT.TL_FIYAT(1)
						MTableT_3.TL_TUTAR = S_STOK2AT.TL_FIYAT(1) *  round(cdbl(trim(Last_xx.SF_MIKTAR_C(k))),2)
						MTableT_3.PRICE = "TL"
					end if	
			elseif	trim(Last_xx.EVRAKTYPE(k)) = "DG" Then						
				
					Set S_STOK21T = Doc.Select1("LKLKLKLRER", "STOK21T", "EVRAKNO = '"&Last_xx.EVRAKNO(k)&"' &KOD = '"&trim(Last_xx.KOD(k))&"' & SERINO = '"&trim(Last_xx.SERINO(k))&"' ", "", -1 )					
					if S_STOK21T.GetRecCount() > 0 Then
						MTableT_3.SF_MIKTAR_G = S_STOK21T.SF_MIKTAR(1)
						MTableT_3.TL_FIYAT = S_STOK21T.TL_FIYAT(1)
						MTableT_3.TL_TUTAR = S_STOK21T.TL_FIYAT(1) *  round(cdbl(trim(Last_xx.SF_MIKTAR_C(k))),2)
						MTableT_3.PRICE = "TL"
					end if					
				elseif	trim(Last_xx.EVRAKTYPE(k)) = "DT" Then								
				
					Set S_STOK24T = Doc.Select1("XXAAADDD", "STOK24T", "EVRAKNO = '"&Last_xx.EVRAKNO(k)&"' &KOD = '"&trim(Last_xx.KOD(k))&"' & SERINO = '"&trim(Last_xx.SERINO(k))&"' ", "", -1 )					
					if S_STOK24T.GetRecCount() > 0 Then
						MTableT_3.SF_MIKTAR_G = S_STOK24T.SF_MIKTAR(1)
						MTableT_3.TL_FIYAT = S_STOK24T.TL_FIYAT(1)
						MTableT_3.TL_TUTAR = S_STOK24T.TL_FIYAT(1) *  round(cdbl(trim(Last_xx.SF_MIKTAR_C(k))),2)
						MTableT_3.PRICE = "TL"
					end if			
			end if
		next

	Call Yazdir_2()
	'Doc.ShowTableDynForm MTableT.GetAppName
End Sub

Sub Yazdir_2()

	SayfaBoyu = 520
	SayfaEni = 2000
	SolMargin = 6
	SagMargin = 1
	UstMargin = 20
	AltMargin = 1

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Order Confirmation", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("Left")
	res = pe1.SelectFont(Arial, 10)
	TOPUTU = 0
	CIKAN = 0
	Set MTableT_3 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	
	MTableT_3.SORT("KOD KAYNAK")
	
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, 10, 10 , 10 , "AYNI")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, 20, 10 , 10 , "LEN")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, 30, 10 , 30 , "KOD KAYNAK")
	msgbox MTableT_3.GetRecCount()
	ayn_i = ""
	for i = 1 To  MTableT_3.GetRecCount()
		if i > 1 Then
			if trim(MTableT_3.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) = trim(MTableT_3.KAYNAK_KOD(i-1)) Then
				ayn_i = ayn_i + 1 
				msgbox ayn_i
			end if
		end if	
		'msgbox i
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 8, 10, 10 , 2000, ayn_i )
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop , 20, 20 , 2000, Len(trim(MTableT_3.KAYNAK_KOD(i))))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop , 30, 30 , 2000, trim(MTableT_3.KAYNAK_KOD(i)))
		
		if pe1.GetTop() > 500 Then

			res = pe1.PageBreak()			 
			res = pe1.write(UstMargin, 10, 10 , 10 , "AYNI")
			res = pe1.write(UstMargin, 20, 10 , 10 , "LEN")
			res = pe1.write(UstMargin, 30, 10 , 30 , "KOD KAYNAK")

		end if

	next
		
End Sub
