Sub Makro1()
	Call kdv_listesi()
End Sub

Sub kdv_listesi()
	on error resume next	
	
	
	F_SAY = 0 	
	Dim MTableT, SAYAC_1
	SAYAC_1 = 1
	SER = 1	
	Set MTableT = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STACK","IO")

	MTableT.Addfield2 "KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "KOD_STOK00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "LOTNUMBER", "CHAR",12,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "EVRAKTYPE", "CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SERINO", "CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OKUNDU", "CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "ORAN_M2F2", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "KAYNAK_KOD", "CHAR",1000,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SAYAC_K2", "DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "F_SAYAC", "DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "AYNI","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""

	Set MTableT_2 = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET_2=STACK","IO")
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "FAT_KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "FAT_SIRA", "CHAR",3,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "KOD_STOK00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "KAYNAK_KOD", "CHAR",1000,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "SERINO", "CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","",""	
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "EVRAKTYPE", "CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_G", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_C", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "TL_FIYAT", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "TL_TUTAR", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "PRICE", "CHAR",2,0,"L","",""

	Set MTableT_3 = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET_3=STACK","IO")
	
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "FAT_KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "FAT_SIRA", "CHAR",3,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "KOD_STOK00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "KAYNAK_KOD", "CHAR",700,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "SERINO", "CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","",""	
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "EVRAKTYPE", "CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_G", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_C", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "TL_FIYAT", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "TL_TUTAR", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "PRICE", "CHAR",2,0,"L","",""

	'Set KTT_KDVIE_A = Doc.GetTableobject("KTT_KDVIE")		
	'evrakno = Doc.RightJustify(trim(KTT_KDVIE_A.FT_EVRAK),10)
	evrakno = "    435599"
	Set A_STOK70T = Doc.Select1("EWRWFS", "STOK70T", "EVRAKNO= '"&evrakno&"'", "", - 1) 

	if A_STOK70T.GetRecCount() = 0 Then
		MSGBOX evrakno & " Nolu Fatura Bulunamýyor."
		Exit Sub
	End if 
	
	'**** FATURADAN OKU	
	for i = 1 To A_STOK70T.GetRecCount()

		KOD = A_STOK70T.KOD(i)
		LOT = A_STOK70T.LOTNUMBER(i)
		SERI = A_STOK70T.SERINO(i)
				
		QSTR = " SELECT KOD, EVRAKNO, EVRAKTYPE, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, SUM(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR FROM STOK10A  WHERE  KOD = '"& KOD &"' AND LOTNUMBER = '"& LOT &"' AND SERINO = '"& SERI &"'  "
		QSTR = QSTR & " AND EVRAKTYPE IN ('MG', 'SI', 'SF', 'FG', 'K2', 'DG', 'DT','0S','DM')  AND SF_MIKTAR > 0 "
		QSTR = QSTR & " GROUP BY KOD, EVRAKNO, EVRAKTYPE, LOTNUMBER, SERINO "
		Set A_STOK10A = Doc.RunSqlQuery("AAA", QSTR)

		for j = 1 To A_STOK10A.GetRecCount()
		
				satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
				MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				MTableT.KOD = A_STOK10A.KOD(j)
				MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", A_STOK10A.KOD(j), "")
				MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK10A.LOTNUMBER(j)
				MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)
				MTableT.SERINO = A_STOK10A.SERINO(j)
				MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK10A.EVRAKNO(j)
				MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = A_STOK10A.SF_MIKTAR(j)
				MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = "-     " & i&"[" & trim(KOD) & "#- "  & trim(SERI) & " - "  & trim(A_STOK70T.EVRAKNO) & " - FT (" & A_STOK70T.SF_MIKTAR(i) &"*"&MTableT.ORAN_M2F2& ")  ]" 
				if trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "DG" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "DT" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "0S" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "SF" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "SI" Then
					MTableT.OKUNDU =  "*"
				end if 
		next		
	next

	'***** 	TABLOYU AÇ
	icini_ac = 1 
	while  icini_ac = 1
		
		'**** ÜRETÝM FÝÞÝ ÝÇÝN
		
		for i = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount()

			if trim(MTableT.OKUNDU(i)) =  ""  AND trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "MG"  Then					
					Dim QSTR_MG	
				
					QSTR_MG = " SELECT ST20TI.EVRAKNO, ST20TI.KOD, ST20TI.LOTNUMBER,  ST20TI.SERINO, ST20TI.SF_MIKTAR FROM STOK20T "
					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & "  LEFT JOIN (SELECT EVRAKNO,KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, SUM(SF_MIKTAR)SF_MIKTAR,KARSITRNUM FROM STOK20TI "
					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & " WHERE STOK20TI.EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"' AND STOK20TI.BILGISATIRIE <> 'E' "
					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & " GROUP BY EVRAKNO,KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO,KARSITRNUM)ST20TI "
					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & " ON ST20TI.EVRAKNO = STOK20T.EVRAKNO AND ST20TI.KARSITRNUM = STOK20T.KARSITRNUM "
					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & " WHERE STOK20T.EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"' AND " 
					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & " STOK20T.KOD = '"& MTableT.KOD(i) &"' AND STOK20T.LOTNUMBER = '"& MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i) &"' AND STOK20T.SERINO = '"& MTableT.SERINO(i) &"'  "			 								
														                								
					MTableT.OKUNDU(i) =  "*"			
					Set A_STOK20TI = Doc.RunSqlQuery("MG" , QSTR_MG)	
					
					if A_STOK20TI.GetRecCount()	 > 0 Then 
						for j = 1 To A_STOK20TI.GetRecCount()		
						
							QSTR_MG_COUNT = " SELECT STOK20T.SERINO FROM STOK20TI"
							QSTR_MG_COUNT = QSTR_MG_COUNT & " LEFT JOIN STOK20T ON STOK20T.EVRAKNO = STOK20TI.EVRAKNO AND STOK20TI.KARSITRNUM  = STOK20T.KARSITRNUM "
							QSTR_MG_COUNT = QSTR_MG_COUNT & " WHERE STOK20TI.EVRAKNO ='"&A_STOK20TI.EVRAKNO(j)&"' AND STOK20TI.KOD = '"&A_STOK20TI.KOD(j)&"' AND STOK20TI.LOTNUMBER =  '"&A_STOK20TI.LOTNUMBER(j)&"' AND  STOK20TI.SERINO = '"&A_STOK20TI.SERINO(j)&"' "
							QSTR_MG_COUNT = QSTR_MG_COUNT & " GROUP BY STOK20T.SERINO"							
							Set D_STOK20TI = Doc.RunSqlQuery("JKJKJMG" , QSTR_MG_COUNT )	
							satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
							MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
							MTableT.ORAN_M2F2 = D_STOK20TI.GetRecCount()	
							MTableT.KOD = A_STOK20TI.KOD(j)
							MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", A_STOK20TI.KOD(j), "")
							MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK20TI.LOTNUMBER(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = "M2"
							MTableT.SERINO = A_STOK20TI.SERINO(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK20TI.EVRAKNO(j)
							MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = A_STOK20TI.SF_MIKTAR(j)
							MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) & "[" & trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) &" (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) & "*" & MTableT.ORAN_M2F2 & ") ]"
						next	
					end if
			end if
		next		
		'**** FASON ÝÇÝN 
		
		for i = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount()
			if trim(MTableT.OKUNDU(i)) =  ""  AND trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "FG" Then					
					Dim QSTR_FG			
					
					QSTR_FG = " SELECT ST68TI.EVRAKNO, ST68TI.KOD, ST68TI.LOTNUMBER,  ST68TI.SERINO FROM STOK68T "
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & "  LEFT JOIN (SELECT EVRAKNO,KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, SUM(SF_MIKTAR)SF_MIKTAR,KARSITRNUM FROM STOK68TI "
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " WHERE STOK68TI.EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"' AND STOK68TI.BILGISATIRIE <> 'E' "
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " GROUP BY EVRAKNO,KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO,KARSITRNUM)ST68TI "
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " ON ST68TI.EVRAKNO = STOK68T.EVRAKNO AND ST68TI.KARSITRNUM = STOK68T.KARSITRNUM "
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " WHERE STOK68T.EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"' AND " 
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " STOK68T.KOD = '"& MTableT.KOD(i) &"' AND STOK68T.LOTNUMBER = '"& MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i) &"' AND STOK68T.SERINO = '"& MTableT.SERINO(i) &"'  "			 								
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " AND STOK68T.LOTNUMBER <> ST68TI.LOTNUMBER AND STOK68T.SERINO <> ST68TI.SERINO "
								
					MTableT.OKUNDU(i) =  "*"			
					Set A_STOK68TI = Doc.RunSqlQuery("FG___" , QSTR_FG)	
					
					if A_STOK68TI.GetRecCount()	 > 0 Then 
						for j = 1 To A_STOK68TI.GetRecCount()								
							QSTR_fG_COUNT = " SELECT * FROM STOK68TI"
							QSTR_fG_COUNT = QSTR_fG_COUNT & " LEFT JOIN STOK68T ON STOK68T.EVRAKNO = STOK68TI.EVRAKNO AND STOK68TI.KARSITRNUM  = STOK68T.KARSITRNUM "
							QSTR_fG_COUNT = QSTR_fG_COUNT & " WHERE STOK68TI.EVRAKNO = '"&A_STOK68TI.EVRAKNO(j)&"' AND STOK68TI.KOD = '"&A_STOK68TI.KOD(j)&"' AND STOK68TI.LOTNUMBER =  '"&A_STOK68TI.LOTNUMBER(j)&"' AND  STOK68TI.SERINO = '"&A_STOK68TI.SERINO(j)&"' "
							Set Y_STOK20TI = Doc.RunSqlQuery("WEWERTRTVVV" , QSTR_fG_COUNT )	
							MTableT.ORAN_M2F2 = Y_STOK20TI.GetRecCount()	
							satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
							MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
							MTableT.KOD = A_STOK68TI.KOD(j)
							MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", A_STOK68TI.KOD(j), "")
							MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK68TI.LOTNUMBER(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = "F2"
							MTableT.SERINO = A_STOK68TI.SERINO(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK68TI.EVRAKNO(j)
							MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = A_STOK68TI.SF_MIKTAR(j)
							MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) & "[" & trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & " - " &  trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i))  & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) & " (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) &"*"&MTableT.ORAN_M2F2&  ") ]"
						next	
					end if
			end if
		next	
		'**  K2 ÝÇÝN OKU

		for i = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount()
			KOD = MTableT.KOD(i)
			LOT = MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)
			SERI = MTableT.SERINO(i)

			if trim(MTableT.OKUNDU(i)) =  ""  AND trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "K2"  Then					
					Dim QSTR_K2			

										 
					QSTR_KXX = " SELECT EVRAKTYPE,  EVRAKNO, KOD, LOTNUMBER,SERINO  , TRNUM  FROM  STOK10A"
					QSTR_KXX = QSTR_KXX & " WHERE EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"' AND   EVRAKTYPE = 'K2 ' AND KOD = '"& KOD &"' AND LOTNUMBER = '"& LOT &"' AND SERINO = '"& SERI &"' "
					
					Set XX_STOK25T = Doc.RunSqlQuery("SDASDSAXX___" , QSTR_KXX)
									
					QSTR_K2 = " SELECT EVRAKTYPE,  EVRAKNO, KOD, LOTNUMBER,SERINO  , TRNUM, SF_MIKTAR FROM  STOK10A"
					QSTR_K2 = QSTR_K2 & " WHERE EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"'  AND KARSIEVRAKTYPE = 'K2' AND TRNUM = '"&XX_STOK25T.TRNUM(1)&"' "
					
					Set A_STOK25T = Doc.RunSqlQuery("KKKFF___" , QSTR_K2)	
					k2_satirsayisi = A_STOK25T.GetRecCount() 
					
					QSTR_K2__2 = " SELECT EVRAKTYPE,  EVRAKNO, KOD, LOTNUMBER,SERINO  , TRNUM FROM  STOK10A"
					QSTR_K2__2  = QSTR_K2__2  & " WHERE EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"'  AND EVRAKTYPE = 'K2' AND SERINO = '"&A_STOK25T.SERINO(1)&"' "
										
					Set yy_STOK25T = Doc.RunSqlQuery("ZZSSDSDY" , QSTR_K2__2)
					if yy_STOK25T.GetRecCount() > 0 Then 											
						k2_satirsayisi = 0
					end if
					MTableT.OKUNDU(i) =  "*"			
					
					if k2_satirsayisi > 0 Then 
					
						for j = 1 To A_STOK25T.GetRecCount()		
							satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
							MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
							MTableT.KOD = A_STOK25T.KOD(j)
							MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", A_STOK25T.KOD(j), "")
							MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK25T.LOTNUMBER(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = "KD"
							MTableT.SERINO = A_STOK25T.SERINO(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK25T.EVRAKNO(j)
							MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = A_STOK25T.SF_MIKTAR(j) * -1
									
							MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) & "[" & trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i))  & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) & " (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) &"*"&MTableT.ORAN_M2F2&  ") ]"
							MTableT.SAYAC_K2 = MTableT.SAYAC_K2(i)
							 								
						next	
					end if
			end if
		next	

'' DEMONTAJ ÝÇÝN OKU
		
		for i = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount()

			if trim(MTableT.OKUNDU(i)) =  ""  AND trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "DM"  Then					
					Dim QSTR_DM
				
					QSTR_DM = " SELECT STOK36T.EVRAKNO, STOK36T.KOD, STOK36T.LOTNUMBER,  STOK36T.SERINO, SUM(STOK36T.SF_MIKTAR) FROM STOK36E "
					QSTR_DM = QSTR_DM & " LEFT JOIN STOK36T ON STOK36T.EVRAKNO =  STOK36E.EVRAKNO "
					QSTR_DM = QSTR_DM & " WHERE STOK36E.EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"' "
					QSTR_DM = QSTR_DM & " GROUP BY STOK36T.EVRAKNO, STOK36T.KOD, STOK36T.LOTNUMBER,  STOK36T.SERINO"


					QSTR_DM = "SELECT STOK36E.EVRAKNO , STOK36E.FORW_CODE , STOK36E.FORW_LOTNUMBER,  STOK36E.FORW_SERINO, SUM(STOK36E.FORW_SF_STOK_MIKTAR) FROM STOK36E "
					QSTR_DM = QSTR_DM & "LEFT JOIN STOK36T ON STOK36T.EVRAKNO = STOK36E.EVRAKNO "
					QSTR_DM = QSTR_DM & "WHERE STOK36T.EVRAKNO = ' "&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"'  "
         								
					MTableT.OKUNDU(i) =  "*"			
					Set A_STOK36T = Doc.RunSqlQuery("DM" , QSTR_DM )	
					
					if A_STOK36T.GetRecCount()	 > 0 Then 
						for j = 1 To 1 
						
							satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
							MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
							MTableT.ORAN_M2F2 = D_STOK20TI.GetRecCount()	
							MTableT.KOD = A_STOK36T.KOD(j)
							MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", A_STOK36T.KOD(j), "")
							MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK36T.LOTNUMBER(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = "D2"
							MTableT.SERINO = A_STOK36T.SERINO(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK36T.EVRAKNO(j)
							MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = A_STOK36T.SF_MIKTAR(j)
							MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) & "[" & trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) &" (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) & "*" & MTableT.ORAN_M2F2 & ") ]"
						next	
					end if
			end if
		next		

	

		
		' *** STOK 10A dan bul	
		' *****
	
		for i = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount()
		
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & MTableT.GetRowCount()
			
			if trim(MTableT.OKUNDU(i)) = ""  Then
				KOD = MTableT.KOD(i)
				LOT = MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)
				SERI = MTableT.SERINO(i)
						
				QSTR_A = " SELECT KOD, EVRAKNO, EVRAKTYPE, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, SUM(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR FROM STOK10A  WHERE  KOD = '"& KOD &"' AND LOTNUMBER = '"& LOT &"' AND SERINO = '"& SERI &"'  "
				QSTR_A = QSTR_A & " AND EVRAKTYPE IN ('MG', 'SI', 'SF', 'FG', 'K2', 'DG', 'DT','0S','DM')  AND SF_MIKTAR > 0 "
				QSTR_A = QSTR_A & " GROUP BY KOD, EVRAKNO, EVRAKTYPE, LOTNUMBER, SERINO "
				
				Set A_STOK10A = Doc.RunSqlQuery("DSADASD_DD", QSTR_A)
									
				MTableT.OKUNDU(i) =  "*"					
				if A_STOK10A.GetRecCount() > 0 Then 
					
					for j = 1 To A_STOK10A.GetRecCount()					
						if trim(KOD) = trim(A_STOK10A.KOD(j)) and trim(LOT) = trim(A_STOK10A.LOTNUMBER(j)) and trim(SERI) = trim(A_STOK10A.SERINO(j)) and trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) Then
							a = 1
						else 	
							satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
							MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
							F_SAY = F_SAY + 1 
							MTableT.F_SAYAC = F_SAY 							
							MTableT.KOD = A_STOK10A.KOD(j)
							MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", A_STOK10A.KOD(j), "")
							MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK10A.LOTNUMBER(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)
							MTableT.SERINO = A_STOK10A.SERINO(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK10A.EVRAKNO(j)
							MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = A_STOK10A.SF_MIKTAR(j)
							
							MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) &  "[" & trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i))  & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) & " (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) &"*"&MTableT.ORAN_M2F2&  ") ]"
									
							if trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "DG" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "DT" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "0S" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "SF" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "SI" Then
								MTableT.OKUNDU =  "*"
							end if 
		
						end if	
					next	
				end if	
			else
			a = 1
			end if				
		next				
		Set DAS_ = Doc.Select2G( "dsasdrrrr", "MAKROTABLET" , " ", " ", "OKUNDU", -1 )
		ser = ser + 1
				
		if DAS_.GetRecCount() = 1 Then 
			icini_ac = 0
		end if
	Wend

	MTableT.sort("KAYNAK_KOD")
	sayil	 = 0
	for i = 1 TO MTableT.GetRecCount()
		MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i) = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) & "[" & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & "]"  & trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)) &"-"& trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) & " (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) &"*"&MTableT.ORAN_M2F2& ")"
	next
	
	Doc.ShowTableDynForm MTableT.GetAppName
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")

	Set STACK_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STACK_PDA=STACK","IO")
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "PROPT","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "AUTO_ADD","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	Set STOK01_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STOK01_PDA=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "LOCATIONS","CHAR",11,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION1","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION2","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION3","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION4","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_KALAN","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SAYAC","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""
	
	STOK01_PDA.Setd7FieldEvent "SF_PAKETICERIGI","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "SF_PAKETICERIGI_AV"

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"
		
	Set MTable2=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLE_MPS=STACK","IO")
	MTable2.Addfield2 "MPSNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')", ""
	MTable2.Setd7FieldEvent "MPSNO","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "MPSNO_AV"

	Set MTableT = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MMPS10T_LIST=MMPS10T(NOFILE)", "IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OKUNDU10","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OKUTULAN_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",11,3,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "KALAN_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",11,3,"R","",""

	Set MSTCK=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
End Sub
Sub MalzemeToplaMain()
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "TRACK32_2X2",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
End Sub
Sub MPSNO_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_MPS")
	if trim(MTable.MPSNO) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Set M10E = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfgshsh", "SELECT STATUS FROM MMPS10E WHERE EVRAKNO='" & Doc.RightJustify(trim(MTable.MPSNO),10) & "'")
	if M10E.GetRowCount() = 0 then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "MPS No Hatalý", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		MTable.MPSNO = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if M10E.STATUS <> "R" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Sadece R statüsündeki MPS 'ler için transfer yapýlabilir", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		MTable.MPSNO = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if
End Sub
Sub cmdHammaddeleriOku()
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK83E) Then Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK83T) Then Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")

	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_MPS")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T_LIST")

	MTableT.Empty

	InStr1 = "'sdfgd__ertuyut'"
	For i = 1 to MTable.GetRowCount()
		if trim(MTable.MPSNO(i)) <> "" Then
			InStr1 = InStr1 & ",'" & Doc.RightJustify(trim(MTable.MPSNO(i)),10) & "'"
		End if
	Next

	QStr = QStr & " SELECT *  FROM MMPS10T "
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN(SELECT KOD, MIN(LOCATION1 || '-' || LOCATION2 || '-' || LOCATION3 || '-' || LOCATION3)LOK FROM STOK01 "
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE  trim(LOCATION1) IS NOT NULL AND trim(LOCATION2) IS NOT NULL AND AMBCODE ='"& STOK83E.AMBCODE & " ' "
	QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY  KOD "
	QStr = QStr & " )ST1  ON MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU = ST1.KOD "
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE MMPS10T.EVRAKNO  IN (" & InStr1 & ") AND  MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE='H' "
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY ST1.LOK NULLS LAST "
	'Set ff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	'Doc.d7msgBox QStr, "", MB_OK
	Set M10T = Doc.RunSqlQuery("gdfgdrr", QStr)

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	For i = 1 to M10T.GetRowCount()
		TrBakiye = M10T.R_MIKTART(i) - M10T.R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN(i) - M10T.R_HM_ODONEMNET(i) - M10T.R_HM_IADE_EDILEN(i)

		if TrBakiye > 0 Then
			satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
			MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			MTableT.JOBNO = M10T.JOBNO(i)
			MTableT.R_KAYNAKTYPE = "H"
			MTableT.R_KAYNAKKODU = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)
			MTableT.KAYNAK_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i), "")
			MTableT.R_HM_BAKIYE = TrBakiye
			MTableT.KAYNAK_BIRIM = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i), "")
			MTableT.KALAN_MIKTAR = TrBakiye
		End if
	Next
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	STOK01_PDA.D_ID = Barkod

	if STACK_PDA.PROPT = "2" Then
		Call Cikart()
	Else
		LocPrefix = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCPREFX"))
		if Left(MTable.BARKOD, Len(LocPrefix)) = LocPrefix Then
			Call LokasyonBilgileriniDodur()
		Else
			Call MiktarBilgisiniDoldur()
		End if
	End if
End Sub
Sub MiktariSifirla()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0
End Sub
Sub Cikart()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")

	Barkod = 	MTable.BARKOD
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

	Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("sdf3gdfhd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Barkod", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set S83T = Doc.Select2("sdfsdfhsj", "STOK83T", "D_ID='" & trim(Barkod) & "'", "", -1)
	if S83T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Barkod daha önce okutulmamýþ", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	satirNo = Doc.Cdbl(S83T.SRNUM(1))
	STOK83T.KOD(satirNo) = ""
	STOK83T.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo) = ""
	STOK83T.SafePack()
	Call OkutulanMiktarlariGuncelle()
End Sub
Sub MiktarBilgisiniDoldur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTable2 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_MPS")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")


	Barkod = 	MTable.BARKOD
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

	Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("sdf3gdfhd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Barkod", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	Set ML = Doc.Select2("sdf2gsdgs", "MMPS10T_LIST", "R_KAYNAKKODU='" & d7LB.SKOD & "'", "", -1)
	if ML.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		ret = Doc.d7MsgBox("Bu malzeme transfer edilecekler listesinde yok" & vbcrlf & "Eklemek Ýster misiniz?", "Dikkat", MB_YESNO)
		if ret <> 6 Then Exit Sub
		JobNo = MPSeEkle(MTable2.MPSNO, d7LB.SKOD)
		if trim(JobNo) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Set ML = Doc.Select2("sdf2gsdgs", "MMPS10T_LIST", "R_KAYNAKKODU='" & d7LB.SKOD & "'", "", -1)
	End if
	
	if CikartOrIptal(Barkod) Then Exit Sub

	Miktar = GetStokMiktar(d7LB.SERINO, L1, L2, L3, L4, Depo)
	'STOK01_PDA.JOBNO = ML.JOBNO
	STOK01_PDA.KOD = D7LB.SKOD
	STOK01_PDA.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", D7LB.SKOD, "")
	STOK01_PDA.AMBCODE = Depo
	STOK01_PDA.LOCATION1 = L1
	STOK01_PDA.LOCATION2 = L2
	STOK01_PDA.LOCATION3 = L3
	STOK01_PDA.LOCATION4 = L4
	STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER = D7LB.LOTNUMBER
	STOK01_PDA.SERINO = D7LB.SERINO
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R1 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R1
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R2 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R2
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R3 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R3
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM1 = D7LB.VRI_NUM1
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM2 = D7LB.VRI_NUM2
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_C = D7LB.VRI_VR_C
'	STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR = D7LB.SF_BAKIYE
	STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR = Miktar
	STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT = D7LB.SF_UNIT
	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = GetTrMiktar(d7LB.SKOD, STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR, ML)
	STOK01_PDA.FOKOD = D7LB.FOKOD

	if STACK_PDA.PROPT = "1" and STACK_PDA.AUTO_ADD = "1" Then
		if STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu malzemeden transfer edilecek miktar yok", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
			Exit Sub
		End if
		Call cmdEkle()		
	Else
		Dim Par
		Set Par=Nothing
		Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "TR_EKLE",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
	End if
End Sub
Sub EkraniTemizle()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T_LIST")

	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	MTable.Empty
	MTableT.Empty
End Sub
Sub SonIslemIptal()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")

	if trim(MTable.STOK26_EVRAKNO) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Set STOK26T = Doc1.GettableObject("STOK26T")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.STOK26_EVRAKNO
	Doc1.Delete_Vouhcer
End Sub
Sub Nerede()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T_LIST")

	Call StokGoster(MTableT.R_KAYNAKKODU)

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "NEREDE",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
End Sub
Sub Nerede2()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Call StokGoster(STOK01_PDA.KOD)

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "NEREDE",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
End Sub
Sub StokGoster(StokKodu)
	Set STOK01_PDA_LIST = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST")
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")

	STOK01_PDA_LIST.Empty

	QStr = "KOD='" & StokKodu & "'"
	if trim(STOK83E.AMBCODE) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & "&AMBCODE='" & STOK83E.AMBCODE & "'"
	End if
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdfw3ff4f4", "STOK01", QStr, "", -1)

	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = STOK01_PDA_LIST.AddRow()
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

		STOK01_PDA_LIST.KOD = S01.KOD(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S01.KOD(i), "")
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
	Next
	STOK01_PDA_LIST.SetCurrentRow(1)
End Sub
Sub cmdKaydet()
	if EtiketleriBol() Then
		if Doc.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
			Call MPSMiktarlariniGuncelle()
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýþlem tamamlandý", "", MB_OK
		End if
	Else
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Etiket bölme iþlemi gerçekleþmediði için evrak kaydedilemedi", "", MB_OK
	End if
End Sub
Function GetTrMiktar(StokKodu, EtiketMiktari, ML)
	TransferBakiyesi = ML.SumOf("R_HM_BAKIYE")
	Set S83T = Doc.Select2("sdf243gdfgd", "STOK83T", "KOD='" & StokKodu & "'", "", -1)
	if S83T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Okutulan = 0
	Else
		Okutulan = S83T.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")
	End if
	TransferEdilecek = TransferBakiyesi - Okutulan
	if TransferEdilecek > EtiketMiktari Then
		GetTrMiktar = EtiketMiktari
	Else
		GetTrMiktar = TransferEdilecek
	End if
End Function
Function GetJobOkutulanMiktar(JobNo)
	Set S83T = Doc.Select2("sdf243gdfgd", "STOK83T", "JOBNO='" & JobNo & "'", "", -1)
	if S83T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		GetJobOkutulanMiktar = 0
	Else
		GetJobOkutulanMiktar = S83T.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")
	End if
End Function

Sub SF_PAKETICERIGI_AV()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR_KALAN = STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR - STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI
End Sub
Sub cmdEkle()
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	if STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Transfer edilecek miktar girilmemiþ", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK83T) Then Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
	STOK83T.SafePack()

	KalanMiktar = STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI
	Set ML = Doc.Select2("sdf2gsdgs", "MMPS10T_LIST", "R_KAYNAKKODU='" & STOK01_PDA.KOD & "'", "", -1)
	if ML.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	For i = 1 to ML.GetRowCount()
		if KalanMiktar = 0 Then Exit For
		OkutulanMiktar = GetJobOkutulanMiktar(ML.JOBNO(i))
		TrMiktar = ML.R_HM_BAKIYE(i) - OkutulanMiktar
		if TrMiktar > 0 Then
			if KalanMiktar < TrMiktar Then
				DagitilacakMiktar = KalanMiktar
			Else
				DagitilacakMiktar = TrMiktar
			End if
			KalanMiktar = KalanMiktar - DagitilacakMiktar
			satirNo = STOK83T.AddRow()
			STOK83T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

			STOK83T.SetFieldValueNV "D_ID", -1, STOK01_PDA.D_ID

			STOK83T.JOBNO = ML.JOBNO(i)
			Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK83T","JOBNO",STOK83T.GetCurrentRow()	
			STOK83T.ALANAMBARDASREZ = "H"

			STOK83T.KOD = STOK01_PDA.KOD
			Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK83T","KOD",STOK83T.GetCurrentRow()	
			STOK83T.LOTNUMBER = STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER
			STOK83T.SERINO = STOK01_PDA.SERINO
			STOK83T.FOKOD = STOK01_PDA.FOKOD
			STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_C = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_C
			STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R1
			STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R2
			STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R3
			STOK83T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM1
			STOK83T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM2
			STOK83T.LOCATION1 = STOK01_PDA.LOCATION1
			STOK83T.LOCATION2 = STOK01_PDA.LOCATION2
			STOK83T.LOCATION3 = STOK01_PDA.LOCATION3
			STOK83T.LOCATION4 = STOK01_PDA.LOCATION4
			STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW1 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1
			STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW2 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2
			STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW3 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3
			STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW4 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4
			STOK83T.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS
			STOK83T.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE
			STOK83T.OALLOC_EVRAKNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKNO
			STOK83T.OALLOC_SIPTYPE = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPTYPE
			STOK83T.OALLOC_SIPNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPNO
			STOK83T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPARTNO
			STOK83T.OALLOC_MPSEVNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSEVNO
			STOK83T.OALLOC_MPSARTNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSARTNO

			STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR = DagitilacakMiktar

			Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK83T","SF_MIKTAR",STOK83T.GetCurrentRow()	
			STOK83T.SF_SF_UNIT = STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT
			Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK83T","SF_SF_UNIT",STOK83T.GetCurrentRow()	
		End if
	Next
'	STOK83T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR = STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = STOK83T.GetRowCount()
	Call OkutulanMiktarlariGuncelle()
	if STACK_PDA.AUTO_ADD <> "1" Then Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub
Sub OkutulanMiktarlariGuncelle()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T_LIST")
	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		Okutulan = GetJobOkutulanMiktar(MTableT.JOBNO(i))

		MTableT.OKUTULAN_MIKTAR(i) = Okutulan
		MTableT.KALAN_MIKTAR(i) = MTableT.R_HM_BAKIYE(i) - Okutulan
		if Doc.DRound(MTableT.KALAN_MIKTAR(i)) = 0 Then
			MTableT.OKUNDU10(i) = "1"
		Else
			MTableT.OKUNDU10(i) = "0"
		End if
	Next
	MTableT.Refresh
End Sub
Sub cmdEvrakSil()
	Set S83T = Doc.Select2("sdf24gfdgds", "STOK83T", "", "", -1)
	if Doc.RunCommand("MT_DELETE") Then
		Call EkraniTemizle
		Call EtiketKartlariniGuncelle(S83T)
	End if
End Sub
Function EtiketleriBol()
	EtiketleriBol = False

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK25", "")
	Set S01_PDA = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
	For i = 1 to STOK83T.GetRowCount()
		if Trim(STOK83T.D_ID(i)) <> "" Then
			Set S01 = GetS01(STOK83T.SERINO(i))
			if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
				DepoMiktari = 0
			Else
				DepoMiktari = S01.SF_MIKTAR(1)
			End if
			if DepoMiktari > STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
				if trim(S25E.EVRAKNO) = "" Then
					Doc1.New_Voucher
					S25E.AMBCODE = STOK83E.AMBCODE
					S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = STOK83E.AMBCODE
					'Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_LOAD_FORM"
				End if

				satirNo = S25T.AddRow
				S25T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				S25T.KOD(satirNo) = S01.KOD(1)
				S25T.LOTNUMBER(satirNo) = S01.LOTNUMBER(1)
				S25T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				S25T.OSF_SF_UNIT(satirNo) = S01.SF_UNIT(1)
				S25T.SERINO(satirNo) = S01.SERINO(1)
				S25T.FOKOD(satirNo) = S01.FOKOD(1)
				S25T.LOCATION1(satirNo) = S01.LOCATION1(1)
				S25T.LOCATION2(satirNo) = S01.LOCATION2(1)
				S25T.LOCATION3(satirNo) = S01.LOCATION3(1)
				S25T.LOCATION4(satirNo) = S01.LOCATION4(1)
				S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_C(satirNo) = S01.VRI_VR_C(1)
				S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R1(satirNo) = S01.VRI_VR_R1(1)
				S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R2(satirNo) = S01.VRI_VR_R2(1)
				S25T.OSF_VRI_VR_R3(satirNo) = S01.VRI_VR_R3(1)
				S25T.OSF_VRI_NUM1(satirNo) = S01.VRI_NUM1(1)
				S25T.OSF_VRI_NUM2(satirNo) = S01.VRI_NUM2(1)
				S25T.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(1)
				S25T.ALLOC_USTATUS(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_USTATUS(1)
				S25T.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_NONMOVABL(1)
				S25T.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(1)
				S25T.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(1)
				S25T.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(1)
				S25T.ALLOC_SIPNO(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(1)
				S25T.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(1)
				S25T.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(1)
				S25T.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(satirNo) = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(1)
				S25T.W_APP(satirNo) = "STOK83"
				S25T.W_TABLE(satirNo) = "STOK83T"
				S25T.W_EVRAKTYPE(satirNo) = "UT"
				S25T.W_EVRAKNO(satirNo) = STOK83E.EVRAKNO
				S25T.W_TRNUM(satirNo) = STOK83T.TRNUM(i)

				Doc1.RunCommand "SATIR_ESKIBILGILERI_YENIYEKOPYALA"
				S25T.NEWSERINO(satirNo) = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
				Call BarkodEskiyiYeniyeKopyala(S25T.SERINO(satirNo), S25T.NEWSERINO(satirNo), S25T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo))

				S25T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR = S25T.AMBCODE_TR
			Else
				EtiketleriBol = True
				Exit Function
			End if
		End if
	Next
	if trim(S25E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
		if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
			EtiketleriBol = True
			For i = 1 to STOK83T.GetRowCount()
				Set S25T1 = Doc1.Select2("sdf34gdfgd", "STOK25T", "W_TRNUM='" & STOK83T.TRNUM(i) & "'", "", -1)
				if S25T1.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
					STOK83T.SetFieldValueNV "SERINO", i, S25T1.NEWSERINO(1)
					STOK83T.SetFieldValueNV "D_ID", i, S25T1.NEWSERINO(1)
					STOK83T.AMBCODE_TR(i) = S25T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR(1)
				End if
			Next
		End if
	End if

	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Function EtiketleriBol2()
	EtiketleriBol = False

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("TRACK251", "")
	Set S01_PDA = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
	For i = 1 to STOK83T.GetRowCount()
		if Trim(STOK83T.D_ID(i)) <> "" Then
			DepoMiktari = GetStokMiktar(STOK83T.SERINO(i), L1, L2, L3, L4, Depo)
			if DepoMiktari > STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
				if trim(S25E.EVRAKNO) = "" Then
					Doc1.New_Voucher
					S25E.AMBCODE = STOK83E.AMBCODE
					S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = STOK83E.AMBCODE
					'Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_LOAD_FORM"
				End if

				S01_PDA.D_ID2 = STOK83T.D_ID(i)
				'Doc1.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK01_PDA", "D_ID2", -1

				S01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				Doc1.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK01_PDA", "SF_PAKETICERIGI", -1

				Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_EKLE"
				S25T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR = S25T.AMBCODE_TR
			Else
				EtiketleriBol = True
				Exit Function
			End if
		End if
	Next
	if trim(S25E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
		if Doc1.RunCommand("MT_SAVE") Then
			EtiketleriBol = True
			For i = 1 to STOK83T.GetRowCount()
				Set S25T1 = Doc1.Select2("sdf34gdfgd", "STOK25T", "SERINO='" & STOK83T.SERINO(i) & "'&SF_MIKTAR='" & STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i) & "'", "", -1)
				if S25T1.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
					STOK83T.SetFieldValueNV "SERINO", i, S25T1.NEWSERINO(1)
					STOK83T.SetFieldValueNV "D_ID", i, S25T1.NEWSERINO(1)
					STOK83T.AMBCODE_TR(i) = S25T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR(1)
				End if
			Next
		End if
	End if

	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Sub EtiketleriBol2()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
	For i = 1 to STOK83T.GetRowCount()
		Set d7LB1 = Doc.Select1("sdf2fsdg", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & STOK83T.D_ID(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if d7LB1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			if d7LB1.SF_BAKIYE > STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
				EskiBarkod = STOK83T.D_ID(i)
				Doc1.Load_Voucher EskiBarkod
				Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_COPY"

				YeniBarkod = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
				Doc1.Load_Voucher YeniBarkod
				Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_PASTE"
				d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK = 0
				d7LB.SF_BAKIYE = STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				d7LB.AMBCODE1 = STOK83E.TARGETAMBCODE
				d7LB.LOCATION1 = STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW1(i)
				d7LB.LOCATION2 = STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW2(i)
				d7LB.LOCATION3 = STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW3(i)
				d7LB.LOCATION4 = STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW4(i)
				d7LB.ONCEKETIKET = EskiBarkod
				d7LB.APPKODU = Doc.GetDocType
				d7LB.EVRAKNO = STOK83E.EVRAKNO
				if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
					Doc1.Load_Voucher EskiBarkod
					if d7LB.SF_BAKIYE < STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
						d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK = d7LB.SF_MIKTAR
					Else
						d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK = d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK + STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
					End if
					if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
						STOK83T.SetFieldValueNV "D_ID", i, YeniBarkod
					End if
				End if
			End if
		End if
	Next
	if Doc.IsChanged Then Doc.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Sub EtiketKartlariniGuncelle(S83T)
	For i = 1 to S83T.GetRowCount()
		Set d7LB1 = Doc.Select1("sdf34gfdfg", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & S83T.D_ID(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if d7LB1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			if trim(d7LB1.APPKODU) = trim(Doc.GetDocType) and trim(d7LB1.EVRAKNO) = trim(S83T.EVRAKNO(i)) and trim(d7LB1.ONCEKETIKET) <> "" Then
				Call EtiketIptal(d7LB1)
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function CikartOrIptal(Barkod)
	CikartOrIptal = False
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
	For i = 1 to STOK83T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(STOK83T.D_ID(i)) = trim(Barkod) Then
			ret = Doc.d7MsgBox(trim(Barkod) & " barkodu daha önce okutulmuþ. " & vbcrlf & "Transfer listesinden çýkarmak istiyorsanýz 'Evet', istemiyorsanýz 'Hayýr' tuþuna basýnýz", "Uyarý", MB_YESNO)
			if ret = 6 Then
				Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
				STOK83T.DeleteRow_NoUI i
				Call OkutulanMiktarlariGuncelle()
			End if
			CikartOrIptal = True
			Exit Function
		Else
			Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("dwefddddwsds", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & STOK83T.D_ID(i) & "'", "", -1)
			if d7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				if trim(d7LB.APPKODU) = "STOK83" and trim(d7LB.EVRAKNO) = trim(STOK83E.EVRAKNO) and _
					trim(D7LB.ONCEKETIKET) = trim(Barkod) Then

					ret = Doc.d7MsgBox(trim(Barkod) & " barkodu daha önce okutulmuþ. " & vbcrlf & "Transfer listesinden çýkarmak istiyorsanýz 'Evet', istemiyorsanýz 'Hayýr' tuþuna basýnýz", "Uyarý", MB_YESNO)
					if ret = 6 Then
						Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
						STOK83T.DeleteRow_NoUI i
						Call EtiketIptal(d7LB)
						Call OkutulanMiktarlariGuncelle()
					End if
					CikartOrIptal = True
					Exit Function
				End if
			End if
		End if
 	Next
End Function
Sub EtiketIptal(d7LB1)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Doc1.Load_Voucher d7LB1.ONCEKETIKET
	d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK = d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK - d7LB1.SF_MIKTAR
	if d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK < 0 Then
		d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = d7LB.SF_MIKTAR - d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK
		d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK = 0
	End if
	Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Function GetStokMiktar(SeriNo, Lokasyon1, Lokasyon2, Lokasyon3, Lokasyon4, Depo)
	GetStokMiktar = 0
	Lokasyon1 = ""
	Lokasyon2 = ""
	Lokasyon3 = ""
	Lokasyon4 = ""
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	'Depo = ""

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdf34dfh", "STOK01", "SERINO='" & SeriNo & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(SeriNo) & " seri no.lu malzeme stokta bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
		Exit Function
	End if

	if S01.GetRowCount() > 1 Then
	'*********** OKAN TAKATAÞ  TARAFINDAN EKLENDÝ
		
		Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdf34dfh", "STOK01", "SERINO='" & SeriNo & "'&AMBCODE='"& STOK83E.AMBCODE &"'", "", -1)

		if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox trim(SeriNo) & " seri no.lu malzeme "&STOK83E.AMBCODE & " deposunda bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
			Exit Function
		End if

		'Doc.d7MsgBox trim(SeriNo) & " seri no.lu malzeme stok listesinde birden çok satýrda bulundu", "", MB_OK
		'Exit Function
	'************************

	End if
	GetStokMiktar = S01.SF_MIKTAR(1)
	Lokasyon1 = S01.LOCATION1(1)
	Lokasyon2 = S01.LOCATION2(1)
	Lokasyon3 = S01.LOCATION3(1)
	Lokasyon4 = S01.LOCATION4(1)
	Depo = S01.AMBCODE(1)
End Function
Function MPSeEkle(MPSNo, StokKodu)
	MPSeEkle = ""
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MMPS10", "")
	Set M10T = Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MPSNo
	satirNo = M10T.AddRow
	M10T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
	M10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(satirNo) = "H"
	M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo) = StokKodu
	JobNo = M10T.JOBNO(satirNo)
	if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
		MPSeEkle = JobNo
		Set ML = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T_LIST")
		ML.SetRowAID 1,1,1
		satirNo1 = ML.AddRow
		ML.SetCurrentRow(satirNo1)
		ML.R_KAYNAKTYPE(satirNo1) = "H"
		ML.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo1) = StokKodu
		ML.JOBNO(satirNo1) = JobNo
		ML.KAYNAK_AD(satirNo1) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, "")
		ML.KAYNAK_BIRIM(satirNo1) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", StokKodu, "")
		ML.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Sub MPSMiktarlariniGuncelle()
	DocYaratildi = False
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set S83T = Doc.Select2G("asdfasdg", "STOK83T", "JOBNO>' '", "", "JOBNO", -1)
	For i = 1 to S83T.GetRowCount()
		if Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_MIKTAR0", S83T.JOBNO(i), "JOBNO") = 0 Then
			if Not DocYaratildi Then
				Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MMPS10", "")
				Set M10T = Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
				ret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(MTable.MPSNO)
				if ret <> 0 Then Exit Sub
				DocYaratildi = True
			End if
			Set M10T1 = Doc1.Select2("sdfeaj", "MMPS10T", "JOBNO='" & S83T.JOBNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
			if M10T1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				satirNo = Doc.Cdbl(M10T1.SRNUM(1))
				M10T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				M10T.R_MIKTAR0(satirNo) = S83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			End if
		End if
	Next
	if DocYaratildi Then
		Doc1.Save_Voucher
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	End if
End Sub
Function GetS01(SeriNo)
	Set GetS01 = Doc.Select1("dfgrfgwsyksrthf", "STOK01", "KOD='XDFGW46RT45'", "", -1)
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdf34dfh", "STOK01", "SERINO='" & SeriNo & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(SeriNo) & " seri no.lu malzeme stokta bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
		Exit Function
	End if

	if S01.GetRowCount() > 1 Then
	'*********** OKAN TAKATAÞ  TARAFINDAN EKLENDÝ
		Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdf34dfh", "STOK01", "SERINO='" & SeriNo & "'&AMBCODE='"& STOK83E.AMBCODE &"'", "", -1)

		if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox trim(SeriNo) & " seri no.lu malzeme "&STOK83E.AMBCODE & " deposunda bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
			Exit Function
		End if

		'Doc.d7MsgBox trim(SeriNo) & " seri no.lu malzeme stok listesinde birden çok satýrda bulundu", "", MB_OK
		'Exit Function
	'************************

	End if
	Set GetS01 = S01
End Function
Sub BarkodEskiyiYeniyeKopyala(EskiBarkod, YeniBarkod, Miktar)
	if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "KOD", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(EskiBarkod),30), "")) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher EskiBarkod
	Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_COPY"
	Doc1.Load_Voucher YeniBarkod
	Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_PASTE"
	D7LB.SERINO = YeniBarkod
	if trim(d7LB.ILKETIKET) = "" Then d7LB.ILKETIKET = EskiBarkod
	d7LB.ONCEKETIKET = EskiBarkod
	d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = Miktar
	d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK = 0
	Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Bu Uygulama için : STOK83
Bu Uygulama için Baþlangýç Ekran Formu : TRACK83U2
Baþlangýçta Bu Makro Çalýþacak : Makro1
Alt Ekranlar Ýçin Ekran Formu
Alt Ekran Form Kodu : 
Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro :

Const adDBTime = 134
Const adDBTimeStamp = 135
Const adBSTR = 8
Const adChar = 129
Const adVarChar = 200
Const adLongVarChar = 201
Const adWChar = 130
Const adVarWChar = 202
Const adLongVarWChar = 203
Const adBinary = 128
Const adVarBinary = 204
Const adLongVarBinary = 205
Const adChapter = 136
Const adFileTime = 64
Const adPropVariant = 138
Const adVarNumeric = 139
Const adArray = &H2000

Const adParamInput = &H1
Const adExecuteNoRecords = &H80

Sub Makro1()
	QStr = "SELECT D.Filename, V.[VariableID], V1.VariableName"
	QStr = QStr & " ,V.[DocumentID]"
	QStr = QStr & " ,[ProjectID]"
	QStr = QStr & " ,[RevisionNo]"
	QStr = QStr & " ,[ConfigurationID]"
	QStr = QStr & " ,[ValueText]"
	QStr = QStr & " ,[ValueInt]"
	QStr = QStr & " ,[ValueFloat]"
	QStr = QStr & " ,[ValueDate]"
	QStr = QStr & " ,[ValueCache]"
	QStr = QStr & " ,[IsLongText]"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM [TEST].[dbo].[VariableValue] V"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN [TEST].[dbo].[Documents] D ON D.DocumentID=V.DocumentID"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN [TEST].[dbo].[Variable] V1 ON V1.VariableID=V.VariableID"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE D.Filename like '%P.000001%' and D.Filename not like '%Copy%'"

	Set Par1 = Doc.CreateSObject("PARAMETERS")
	Par1.SetParameter "REPORT_CONNECTION_STRING", CnnStr
	'Set tbl1 = Doc.RunSQLQuery_Par("_DDDD_2333",QStr,Par1)
'	msgbox tbl1.GetRowCount()


	Set Oconn = CreateObject("ADODB.Connection")
	Oconn.ConnectionString = cnnStr
	Oconn.Open

	StokKodu1 = "P.004732"
'	VariableId = 109
'	Deger = "152"

	QStr = "SELECT KOD, SUPPLIERCODE FROM STOK00 WHERE TRIM(SUPPLIERCODE) IS NOT NULL"
'	QStr = QStr & " AND SUPPLIERCODE='" & StokKodu1 & "'"

	Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("asdhasd", QStr)
	For i = 1 to S00.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & S00.GetRowCount()
		EskiStokKodu = S00.SUPPLIERCODE(i)

		QStr1 = "SELECT d.DocumentID,d.Filename, R.RevNr, R.ActiveConfigurationID"
		QStr1 = QStr1 & " FROM [TEST].[dbo].[Documents] d"
		QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN [TEST].[dbo].[Revisions] R ON R.DocumentID=D.DocumentID"
		QStr1 = QStr1 & " GROUP BY d.DocumentID,d.Filename, R.RevNr, R.ActiveConfigurationID"

		'QStr1 = "SELECT DISTINCT documentid FROM FROM [TEST].[dbo].[Documents] D"
		'QStr1 = QStr1 & " WHERE D.Filename like '%" & trim(EskiStokKodu) & "%' and D.Filename not like '%Copy%')"

		Arr1 = CreateArray(S00.KOD(i))
		For j = 0 to UBound(Arr1, 1)
			VariableId = Arr1(j,0)
			Deger = Arr1(j,1)
			if trim(Deger) <> "" Then
				Call UpdateTable(Oconn, trim(EskiStokKodu), VariableId, Deger)
			End if
		Next
	Next
End Sub
Sub UpdateTable(Oconn, StokKodu, VariableId, Deger)
	Set cmd = CreateObject("ADODB.Command")
	Set cmd.ActiveConnection = Oconn
	cmd.Prepared = True

	QStr = "UPDATE [TEST].[dbo].[VariableValue]"
	QStr = QStr & " SET valuetext=?, valueint=?, valuefloat=?"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE documentid in (SELECT documentid FROM [TEST].[dbo].[Documents] D"
	QStr = QStr & "										WHERE D.Filename like '%" & trim(StokKodu) & "%' and D.Filename not like '%Copy%')"
	QStr = QStr & " and variableid=" & VariableId

	cmd.CommandText = QStr

	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("valuetext", adVarChar, adParamInput, -1, Deger)
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("valueint", adInteger, adParamInput, 10, Doc.Cdbl(Deger))
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("ValueFloat", adDouble, adParamInput, 10, Doc.Cdbl(Deger))

	Set ret = cmd.Execute(, , adExecuteNoRecords)
	Set cmd = Nothing
End Sub
Sub InsertTable(Oconn, StokKodu, VariableId, Deger)
	Set cmd = CreateObject("ADODB.Command")
	Set cmd.ActiveConnection = Oconn
	cmd.Prepared = True

	QStr = "INSERT INTO [TEST].[dbo].[VariableValue] (VariableID,DocumentID,ProjectID,RevisionNo,ConfigurationID,ValueText,ValueInt,ValueFloat,ValueDate,ValueCache,IsLongText)"
	QStr = QStr & " VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)"

	cmd.CommandText = QStr

	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("VariableID", adInteger, adParamInput, 10, VariableID)
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("DocumentID", adInteger, adParamInput, 10, DocumentID)
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("ProjectID", adInteger, adParamInput, 10, ProjectID)
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("RevisionNo", adInteger, adParamInput, 10, RevisionNo)
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("ConfigurationID", adInteger, adParamInput, 10, ConfigurationID)
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("valuetext", adVarChar, adParamInput, -1, Deger)
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("valueint", adDouble, adParamInput, 10, Doc.Cdbl(Deger))
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("ValueFloat", adDouble, adParamInput, 10, Doc.Cdbl(Deger))
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("ValueCache", adVarChar, adParamInput, -1, Deger)
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("IsLongText", adSmallInt, adParamInput, 10, 0)

	Set ret = cmd.Execute(, , adExecuteNoRecords)
	Set cmd = Nothing
End Sub

Function CreateArray(ByVal StokKodu)
	StokKodu = Trim(StokKodu)
  Dim Arr1(3, 1)
  Arr1(0, 0) = 109
  Arr1(1, 0) = 132
  Arr1(2, 0) = 131
  Arr1(3, 0) = 50

  CreateArray = Arr1

  KodArr = Split(StokKodu, " ")
  If UBound(KodArr) <> 2 Then Exit Function
  If Len(Trim(KodArr(2))) <> 5 Then Exit Function
  
  Arr1(0, 1) = KodArr(0)
  Arr1(1, 1) = KodArr(1)
  Arr1(2, 1) = Left(KodArr(2), 3)
  Arr1(3, 1) = Mid(KodArr(2), 4)

  CreateArray = Arr1
End Function

Makro Kodu : PDM-2

Const cnnStr = "Provider=SQLNCLI;Data Source=pdmserver;Initial catalog=test;User Id=erp;Password=Freedom2010;"

Sub Makro1()
	Set ffff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	QStr = "SELECT X.BomDocumentID, X.ROWNO, D.Filename, D.LockPath, MAX(ITEM_NO) ITEM_NO, MAX(PART_NUMBER) PART_NUMBER, MAX(QTY) QTY"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM ("
	QStr = QStr & " SELECT V.BomDocumentID, V.RowNo,"
	QStr = QStr & " 	(CASE WHEN ColumnNo=0 THEN ValueText ELSE ' ' END) ITEM_NO,"
	QStr = QStr & " 	(CASE WHEN ColumnNo=1 THEN ValueText ELSE ' ' END) PART_NUMBER,"
	QStr = QStr & " 	(CASE WHEN ColumnNo=2 THEN ValueText ELSE ' ' END) DESCRIPTION1,"
	QStr = QStr & "     (CASE WHEN ColumnNo=3 THEN ValueText ELSE ' ' END) QTY"
	QStr = QStr & "   FROM [TEST].[dbo].[BomSheetValue] V"
	QStr = QStr & "   LEFT JOIN [TEST].[dbo].[BomSheetRow] R ON R.RowNo=V.RowNo"
	QStr = QStr & "   ) X"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN [TEST].[dbo].[BomSheets] E ON E.BomDocumentID=X.BomDocumentID"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN [TEST].[dbo].[Documents] D ON D.DocumentID=E.SourceDocumentID"
	QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY X.BomDocumentID, X.ROWNO, D.Filename, D.LockPath"
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY X.BomDocumentID, X.ROWNO"

	Set Par1 = Doc.CreateSObject("PARAMETERS")
	Par1.SetParameter "REPORT_CONNECTION_STRING", CnnStr
	Set BomTable = Doc.RunSQLQuery_Par("_DDDD_2333",QStr,Par1)
	For i = 1 to BomTable.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Ürün Aðaçlarý Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & BomTable.GetRowCount()
		if trim(BomTable.FileName(i)) <> trim(EskiDosyaAdi) Then
			if trim(BOMU01E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher

			MamulKodu = GetMamulKoduFromFileName(BomTable.FileName(i))
			Hata = False
			if trim(MamulKodu) = "" Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox "Dosya Adý : " & trim(BomTable.FileName(i)) & " aktarýlmadý", "", MB_OK
			Else
				FrMamulKodu = GetFreedomStokKodu(MamulKodu)
				if FrMamulKodu = "" Then
					Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & trim(MamulKodu) & " Freedom 'da Stok Kodu Bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
				Else
					Call UrunAgaciYoksaYarat(FrMamulKodu)
				End if
			End if
		End if
		EskiDosyaAdi = BomTable.FileName(i)

		if trim(FrMamulKodu) <> "" and not Hata Then
			HammaddeKodu = GetFreedomStokKodu(BomTable.PART_NUMBER(i))
			if HammaddeKodu = "" Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox "Mamul Kodu : " & trim(FrMamulKodu) & ", Hammadde : " & BomTable.PART_NUMBER(i) & " Freedom 'da stok kartý bulunamadýðý için ürün aðacý aktarýlamadý", "", MB_OK
				Hata = True
				BOMU01T.Empty
				Doc.Save_Voucher
				Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher
			Else
				satirNo = BOMU01T.AddRow()
				BOMU01T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(satirNo) = "H"
				BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(satirNo) = HammaddeKodu
				BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(satirNo) = Doc.Cdbl(BomTable.QTY(i))
			End if
		End if
	Next
	if trim(BOMU01E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
End Sub
Function GetMamulKoduFromFileName(ByVal DosyaAdi)
	DosyaAdi = trim(DosyaAdi)
	GetMamulKoduFromFileName = ""
	SonEkPos = InStr(1, trim(DosyaAdi), ".SLD")
	If SonEkPos = 0 Then Exit Function
	GetMamulKoduFromFileName = Left(DosyaAdi, SonEkPos - 1)
End Function
Function GetFreedomStokKodu(MamulKodu)
	GetFreedomStokKodu = ""
	QStr = "SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & MamulKodu & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " UNION ALL"
	QStr = QStr & " SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE SUPPLIERCODE='" & MamulKodu & "'"

	Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dxfhadfs", QStr)
	if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	GetFreedomStokKodu = S00.KOD(1)
End Function
Sub UrunAgaciYoksaYarat(FrMamulKodu)
	Set B01E = Doc.RunSQLQuery("zsdghd", "SELECT EVRAKNO FROM BOMU01E WHERE MAMULCODE='" & FrMamulKodu & "' AND AKTIF_PASIF='*'")
	if B01E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.New_Voucher
		BOMU01E.MAMULCODE = FrMamulKodu
		BOMU01E.MAMULMIKTAR = 1
		BOMU01E.AKTIF_PASIF = "*"
	Else
		Doc.Load_Voucher B01E.EVRAKNO(1)
		For i = 1 to BOMU01T.GetRowCount()
			if BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "H" Then
				BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = ""
				BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) = ""
			End if
		Next
		BOMU01T.SafePack
	End if
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set S21T = Doc.GetTableObject("MULTILEVEL1")
'	Call SendTableToExcel(S21T)
	Call SendGridToExcel(S21T, "MULTILEVEL1", "Envanter Standart Ürün Aðaçlarý Analizi")
End Sub
Sub SendGridToExcel(Tablo, GridName, RaporAciklama)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	Set ReportOutput=Doc.CreateSObject("ReportOutput")

	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 RaporAciklama

	Grid = Doc.GetGridColInfo("", GridName)
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Grid,1)
		if Grid(i,1) > 0 Then
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
			AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
			AlanBasligi = Grid(i,2)

			If AlanTuru = "N" Then
				Align = "RIGHT"
			Else
				Align = "LEFT"
			End if
			pe2.AddColumnDef Grid(i,0), Grid(i,5), Grid(i,6), Align
			pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanBasligi
		End if
	Next
	PE2.LineOut

	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gönderiliyor : " & GridName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues

		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Grid,1)
			if Grid(i,1) > 0 Then
				ncol = ncol+1
				if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
				AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
				AlanTuru = Grid(i,4)
				If AlanTuru = "N" Then
					Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
					pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
				ElseIf AlanTuru = "D" Then
					OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
				Else
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,RTrim(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
				End if
			End if
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	ExcelFile = PE2.GetFileName()
	pe2.Close
	Call ExcelFormat(ExcelFile, uBound(Grid,1) + 1)
End Sub

Sub SendTableToExcel(Tablo)
	Set pe2 = Doc.CreateSObject("ExcelOutput")
	PE2.SetOrientation "P"
	PE2.PageB_ReportHead_std1 Tablo.GetAppName

	Alanlar = Tablo.GetFields()
	ncol = 0
	For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
		AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
		if Cikacak(AlanAdi) Then
			ncol = ncol+1
			if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
			AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
				If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
				Align = "RIGHT"
			Else
				Align = "LEFT"
			End if
			pe2.AddColumnDef Alanlar(i,0), Alanlar(i,3), Alanlar(i,4), Align
			pe2.SetRowValue_String -1, AlanAdi
		End if
	Next
	PE2.LineOut

	For satirNo = 1 to Tablo.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Excel 'e gönderiliyor : " & Tablo.GetAppName & " - " & i & " / " & Tablo.GetRowCount
		PE2.InitRowValues

		ExcelSatir = -1
		ncol = 0
		For i=0 To uBound(Alanlar,1)
			AlanAdi = Alanlar(i,0)
			if Cikacak(AlanAdi) Then
				ncol = ncol+1
				if ncol > 256 Then Exit For
				AlanTuru = Alanlar(i,2)
				If AlanTuru = "INT16" or AlanTuru = "INT32" or AlanTuru = "DOUBLE" Then
					Deger = Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo)
					pe2.SetRowValue_Double ExcelSatir, Deger
				ElseIf AlanTuru = "DATE" Then
					OutputStr=Doc.StrToNorDate(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,OutputStr
				Else
					pe2.SetRowValue_String ExcelSatir,RTrim(Tablo.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,satirNo))
				End if
			End if
		Next
		PE2.LineOut
	Next
	pe2.Show
End Sub
Function Cikacak(AlanAdi)
	Cikacak = True
	If InStr(1, AlanAdi, "REPORT___") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End If
	If InStr(1, AlanAdi, "BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End If
	If InStr(1, AlanAdi, "BOMREC_CODE_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End If
	If InStr(1, AlanAdi, "JOBNO_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End If
	If InStr(1, AlanAdi, "MPSNO_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End If
	If InStr(1, AlanAdi, "BOMREC_CODE_") > 0 Then
		Cikacak = False
	End If
End Function
Sub ExcelFormat(ExcelFile, AlanSayisi)
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open(ExcelFile)
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	A1 = Sh1.Cells(1,1).Value
	B1 = Sh1.Cells(2,1).Value

	Sh1.Cells(1,1).Value = ""
	Sh1.Cells(2,1).Value = ""

	Sh1.Range(Sh1.Cells(3, 	1), Sh1.Cells(3, AlanSayisi)).Select
	Excel.Selection.Font.Bold = True
	Excel.Selection.WrapText = True
	Excel.Selection.Interior.ColorIndex = 3
	Excel.Selection.Font.ColorIndex = 2
	Excel.Selection.EntireColumn.AutoFit

	
	Sh1.Cells(1,1).Value = A1
	Sh1.Cells(2,1).Value = B1

	Excel.Cells(1,1).Select
	Excel.Visible = True
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("d:\ted.xls", False, True)
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets("Þablon")
	Set ggg = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	For i = 3 to 65535
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i
		if Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value = "" Then Exit For
		CHKodu = trim(Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value)
		if trim(CHKodu) <> trim(EskiCHKodu) Then
			if trim(CARI00.KOD) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
			Doc.Load_Voucher CHKodu
			STOK0GT.Empty
		End if
		EskiCHKodu = CHKodu
		QStr = "SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value & "'"
		Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("zsfgadfha", QStr)
		if S00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			satirNo = STOK0GT.AddRow()
			STOK0GT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			STOK0GT.STATU(satirNo) = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value)
			STOK0GT.STOKCODE(satirNo) = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value)
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox "C/H Kodu : " & trim(CHKodu) & ", Hammadde Kodu : " & Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value & " geçersiz", "", MB_OK
		End if
	Next
	if trim(CARI00.KOD) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
  ret = GetEbatArr("PASLANMAZ PLAKALAR 0001,5 x 0025 x 0160")
  'MsgBox UBound(ret)
  For Each Segment In ret
    ebat = CDbl(Segment)
  Next
End Sub

Function GetEbatArr(ByVal AciklamaStr)
  AciklamaStr = Replace(UCase(Trim(AciklamaStr)), " ", "")
  Dim EbatArr(2)
  EbatArr(0) = 0
  EbatArr(1) = 0
  EbatArr(2) = 0
  GetEbatArr = EbatArr
  xPos = InStr(1, AciklamaStr, "X")
  If xPos = 0 Then Exit Function
  If xPos < 2 Then Exit Function
  If Len(AciklamaStr) = xPos Then Exit Function
    
  Arr1 = Split(AciklamaStr, "X")
  If UBound(Arr1) > 2 Then Exit Function
  
  For i = 0 To UBound(Arr1)
    If i = 0 Then
      Segment = LTrim1(Arr1(i))
    Else
      Segment = RTrim1(Arr1(i))
    End If
    If Trim(Segment) <> "" Then
      EbatArr(i) = Segment
    End If
  Next
  GetEbatArr = EbatArr
End Function
Function LTrim1(Str1)
  LTrim1 = ""
  For i = Len(Str1) To 1 Step -1
    Char1 = Mid(Str1, i, 1)
    If Not AktarilacakMi(Char1) Then Exit Function
    LTrim1 = Char1 & LTrim1
  Next
End Function
Function RTrim1(Str1)
  RTrim1 = ""
  For i = 1 To Len(Str1)
    Char1 = Mid(Str1, i, 1)
    If Not AktarilacakMi(Char1) Then Exit Function
    RTrim1 = RTrim1 & Char1
  Next
End Function
Function AktarilacakMi(Char1)
  If (Char1 >= "0" And Char1 <= "9") Or Char1 = "," Or Char1 = "," Then
    AktarilacakMi = True
  Else
    AktarilacakMi = False
  End If
End Function

Sub Makro1()
'	if HammaddeBirimHata() Then Exit Sub

'	if RotaKontrolHata() Then Exit Sub
'	if not TezgahveOpKartlariniYarat() Then Exit Sub
'	Call HammaddeleriAktar()
	Call OperasyonlariAktarAktar()
End Sub
Sub OperasyonlariAktarAktar()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\kesme.xls", false, true)
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	For i = 2 to 65536
		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) = "" Then Exit For
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Rotalar aktarýlýyor : " & i
		StokKodu = Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value
		if trim(StokKodu) <> trim(EskiStokKodu) Then
			if trim(BOMU01E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
				BOMU01T.Sort("BOMREC_SIRANO")
				Doc.Save_Voucher
			End if
			FrMamulKodu = GetYariMamulStokKodu(StokKodu)
			if trim(FrMamulKodu) <> "" Then
				if true Then
					Doc.New_Voucher
					BOMU01E.MAMULCODE = FrMamulKodu
					BOMU01E.AKTIF_PASIF = "*"
					BOMU01E.MAMULMIKTAR = 1
					BOMU01T.Empty
				Else
					Doc.Load_Voucher EvrakNo
					For j = 1 to BOMU01T.GetRowCount()
						if BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(j) = "I" Then
							BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(j) = ""
							BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(j) = ""
						End if
					Next
					BOMU01T.SafePack
				End if
			End if
		End if
		EskiStokKodu = StokKodu
		if trim(FrMamulKodu) <> "" Then

			satirNo = BOMU01T.AddRow()
			BOMU01T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE = "I"
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_SIRANO = Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE = GetTezgahKodu(Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value)
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_OPERASYON = GetTezgahKodu(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value)
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0_BV = Sh1.Cells(i,6).Value
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0_BU = "sn"
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK1 = Sh1.Cells(i,5).Value / 3600
			BOMU01T.OPERATORSAYISI1 = Sh1.Cells(i,10).Value
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_YMAMULPM = BOMU01T.BOMREC_YMAMULPM
		End if
	Next
	if trim(BOMU01E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
		BOMU01T.Sort("BOMREC_SIRANO")
		Doc.Save_Voucher
	End if
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Sub
Function RotaKontrolHata()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\rotalar.xls")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)
	Set fff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	RotaKontrolHata = False
	For i = 2 to 65000
		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) = "" Then Exit For
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Rotalar kontrol ediliyor : " & i
		QStr = "SELECT EVRAKNO FROM STOK0U U WHERE U.UCODE='" & trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) & "'"
		Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdgsdrhs", QStr)
		if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) & " üretici kodlarýnda bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
			RotaKontrolHata = True
		End if
	Next
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Function
Function TezgahveOpKartlariniYarat()
	TezgahveOpKartlariniYarat = False
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\rotalar.xls")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	Set I00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdsdf", "SELECT  KOD, AD FROM IMLT00 WHERE 1=0")
	Set I01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdsdfddd", "SELECT  KOD, AD FROM IMLT01 WHERE 1=0")
	I00.Empty
	I01.Empty

	For i = 2 to 65000
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Tezgah ve Operasyon Kodlarý inceleniyor : " & i
		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) = "" Then Exit For

		StokKodu = Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value
		if trim(StokKodu) <> trim(EskiStokKodu) Then
			FrMamulKodu = GetFreedomStokKodu(StokKodu)
		End if
		EskiStokKodu = StokKodu

'		satirNo = I00.AddRow
		satirNo = I01.AddRow

'		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value) <> "" and trim(Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value) <> "31" Then
'			I00.KOD(satirNo) = GetTezgahKodu(Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value)
'			I00.AD(satirNo) = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,11).Value)
'		End if

		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value) <> "" and trim(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value) <> "09" and FrMamulKodu <> "" Then
			I01.KOD(satirNo) = GetTezgahKodu(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value)
			I01.AD(satirNo) = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,12).Value)
		End if
	Next

'	I00.GroupBy("KOD")
	I01.GroupBy("KOD")

'	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("IMLT00", "")
'	Set I0 = Doc1.GetTableObject("IMLT00")
'	For i = 1 to I00.GetRowCount()
'		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Tezgah Kartlarý Yaratýlýyor : " & i & " / " & I00.GetRowCount()
'		Set I00_D = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sadgsdfh", "SELECT KOD FROM IMLT00 WHERE KOD='" & I00.KOD(i) &"'")
'		if I00_D.GetRowCount() = 0 and trim(I00.KOD(i)) <> "" Then
'			Doc1.Load_Voucher I00.KOD(i)
'			I0.AD = I00.AD(i)
'			if Doc1.Save_Voucher <> 0 Then Exit Function
'		End if
'	Next
'	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
'	Doc.DeleteDocumentNoUI "IMLT00", ""

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("IMLT01", "")
	Set I1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("IMLT01")
	For i = 1 to I01.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Operasyon Kartlarý Yaratýlýyor : " & i & " / " & I01.GetRowCount()
		Set I01_D = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sadgsdfh", "SELECT KOD FROM IMLT01 WHERE KOD='" & I01.KOD(i) &"'")
		if I01_D.GetRowCount() = 0 and trim(I01.KOD(i)) <> "" Then
			Doc1.Load_Voucher I01.KOD(i)
			I1.AD = I01.AD(i)
			if Doc1.Save_Voucher <> 0 Then Exit Function
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher

	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
	TezgahveOpKartlariniYarat = True
End Function
Function GetTezgahKodu(ByVal TezgahKodu)
	TezgahKodu = Replace(TezgahKodu,".", "-")
	TezgahKodu = Replace(TezgahKodu," ", "-")
	GetTezgahKodu = TezgahKodu
End Function
Sub HammaddeleriAktar()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\receteler.xls")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	For i = 2 to 65000
		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) = "" Then Exit For
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Reçeteler aktarýlýyor : " & i
		StokKodu = Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value
		if trim(StokKodu) <> trim(EskiStokKodu) Then
			if trim(BOMU01E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
			EvrakNo = GetUrunAgaciEvrakno(StokKodu)
			if trim(EvrakNo) = "" Then
				Doc.New_Voucher
				BOMU01E.MAMULCODE = StokKodu
				BOMU01E.AKTIF_PASIF = "*"
				BOMU01E.MAMULMIKTAR = Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value
			Else
				Doc.Load_Voucher EvrakNo
			End if
			BOMU01T.Empty
		End if
		EskiStokKodu = StokKodu

		satirNo = BOMU01T.AddRow()
		BOMU01T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE = "H"
		BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE = Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value
		BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0 = Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value
	Next
	if trim(BOMU01E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Sub
Function GetUrunAgaciEvrakno(StokKodu)
	GetUrunAgaciEvrakno = ""
	QStr = "SELECT EVRAKNO FROM BOMU01E WHERE MAMULCODE='" & StokKodu & "' AND AKTIF_PASIF='*'"
	Set B01E = Doc.RunSQLQuery("zsdfgasefhsdh", QStr)
	if B01E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	GetUrunAgaciEvrakno = B01E.EVRAKNO
End Function
Function GetYeniBirim(EskiBirim)
	if trim(EskiBirim) = "ADET" Then
		GetYeniBirim = "AD"
	Elseif trim(EskiBirim) = "KÝLOGRAM" Then
		GetYeniBirim = "KG"
	Elseif trim(EskiBirim) = "METRE" Then
		GetYeniBirim = "MT"
	Elseif trim(EskiBirim) = "TKM" Then
		GetYeniBirim = "TK"
	Elseif trim(EskiBirim) = "TAKIM" Then
		GetYeniBirim = "TK"
	Else
		GetYeniBirim = EskiBirim
	End if
End Function
Function HammaddeBirimHata()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\receteler.xls")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)
	Set fff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	HammaddeBirimHata = False
	For i = 2 to 65536
		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) = "" Then Exit For
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Birim kontrolü yapýlýyor : " & i
		QStr = "SELECT S.KOD, S.IUNIT FROM STOK0U U LEFT JOIN STOK00 S ON S.KOD=U.EVRAKNO WHERE U.UCODE='" & trim(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value) & "'"
		Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdgsdrhs", QStr)

		QStr = "SELECT S.KOD, S.IUNIT FROM STOK0U U LEFT JOIN STOK00 S ON S.KOD=U.EVRAKNO WHERE U.UCODE='" & trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) & "'"
		Set S001 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdgsdrhCCCs", QStr)
		if S00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			YeniBirim = GetYeniBirim(Sh1.Cells(i,7).Value)
			if trim(YeniBirim) <> trim(S00.IUNIT(1)) Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox i & ". satýr, Stok Kodu : " & trim(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value) & ", Tüketim birimi: " & YeniBirim & _
								", Stok Birimi : " & S00.IUNIT(1), "", MB_OK
				HammaddeBirimHata = True
			End if
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & trim(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value) & " üretici kodlarýnda bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
			HammaddeBirimHata = True
		End if
		if S001.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) & " üretici kodlarýnda bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
			HammaddeBirimHata = True
		End if
	Next
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Function
Function GetYariMamulStokKodu(StokKodu)
	GetYariMamulStokKodu = ""
	FreedomStokKodu = GetFreedomStokKodu(StokKodu)
	if trim(FreedomStokKodu) = "" Then Exit Function

	QStr = "SELECT BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE FROM BOMU01T"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE BOMREC_CODE='" & FreedomStokKodu & "' AND AKTIF_PASIF='*' AND BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'"
	Set B01T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfaetjakj", QStr)
	if B01T.GetRowCount <> 1 Then Exit Function

	QStr = "SELECT * FROM BOMU01E WHERE MAMULCODE='" & B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(1) & "' AND AKTIF_PASIF='*'"
	Set B01E = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfgq45dhf", QStr)

	if B01E.GetRowCount > 0 Then Exit Function

	GetYariMamulStokKodu = B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(1)
End Function
Function GetFreedomStokKodu(MamulKodu)
	GetFreedomStokKodu = ""
	QStr = "SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & MamulKodu & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " UNION ALL"
	QStr = QStr & " SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE SUPPLIERCODE='" & MamulKodu & "'"

	Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dxfhadfs", QStr)
	if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	GetFreedomStokKodu = S00.KOD(1)
End Function
