Sub Makro1()
	Set Doc2 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")

	Set STACK_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STACK_PDA=STACK","IO")
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "PROPT","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	STACK_PDA.Addfield2 "AUTO_ADD","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""

	Set STOK01_PDA=Doc.CreateTableObject("STOK01_PDA=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "LOCATIONS","CHAR",11,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION1","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION2","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION3","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "PRX_LOCATION4","CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_PAKETICERIGI","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_KALAN","DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "SAYAC","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	STOK01_PDA.Addfield2 "JOBNO","CHAR",16,0,"R","",""
	
	STOK01_PDA.Setd7FieldEvent "SF_PAKETICERIGI","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "SF_PAKETICERIGI_AV"

	Set MTable=Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "MPSNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10TM','=EVRAKNO','ACIK_KAPALI+EVRAKNO','BRW_MMPS10TM_0','MPSNO','STATUS==""R""')",""

	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "MPSNO","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "MPSNO_AV"

	Set MTableT = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MMPS10T_LIST=MMPS10T(NOFILE)", "IO")
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OKUNDU10","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OKUTULAN_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",11,3,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "KALAN_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",11,3,"R","",""

	Set MSTCK=Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST=STOK01(NOFILE)","IO")
End Sub
Sub MalzemeToplaMain()
	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "TRACK32_2X2",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
End Sub
Sub MPSNO_AV()
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK83E) Then Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK83T) Then Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")

	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T_LIST")
	MTableT.Empty
	
 	mps__no = Doc.RightJustify(trim(MTable.MPSNO),10)
'/////////////
	QSTR = QSTR & " SELECT *  FROM MMPS10T "
	QSTR = QSTR & " LEFT JOIN(SELECT KOD, MIN(LOCATION1 || '-' || LOCATION2 || '-' || LOCATION3 || '-' || LOCATION3)LOK FROM STOK01 "
	QSTR = QSTR & " WHERE  trim(LOCATION1) IS NOT NULL AND trim(LOCATION2) IS NOT NULL AND AMBCODE ='"& STOK83E.AMBCODE & " ' "
	QSTR = QSTR & " GROUP BY  KOD "
	QSTR = QSTR & " )ST1  ON MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU = ST1.KOD "
	QSTR = QSTR & " WHERE MMPS10T.EVRAKNO = '"& mps__no &"' AND  MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE='H' "
	QSTR = QSTR & " ORDER BY ST1.LOK NULLS LAST "
	
	Set M10T = dOC.RunSqlQuery("gdfgdrr", QSTR)

'Set M10T = Doc.Select1("sdf234gdfh", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MTable.MPSNO & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'", "", -1)
'///////

	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "MPS bulunamadý", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if M10T.R_STATUS <> "R" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Sadece R statüsündeki MPS 'ler için transfer yapýlabilir", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	For i = 1 to M10T.GetRowCount()
		TrBakiye = M10T.R_MIKTART(i) - M10T.R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN(i) - M10T.R_HM_ODONEMNET(i) - M10T.R_HM_IADE_EDILEN(i)
		if TrBakiye > 0 Then
			satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
			MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			MTableT.JOBNO = M10T.JOBNO(i)
			MTableT.R_KAYNAKKODU = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)
			MTableT.KAYNAK_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i), "")
			MTableT.R_HM_BAKIYE = TrBakiye
			MTableT.KAYNAK_BIRIM = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i), "")
			MTableT.KALAN_MIKTAR = TrBakiye
		End if
	Next
	MTableT.SetCurrentRow(1)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_STACK")

	Barkod = MTable.BARKOD
	STOK01_PDA.D_ID = Barkod

	if STACK_PDA.PROPT = "2" Then
		Call Cikart()
	Else
		LocPrefix = trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("LOCPREFX"))
		if Left(MTable.BARKOD, Len(LocPrefix)) = LocPrefix Then
			Call LokasyonBilgileriniDodur()
		Else
			Call MiktarBilgisiniDoldur()
		End if
	End if
End Sub
Sub MiktariSifirla()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0
End Sub
Sub Cikart()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")

	Barkod = 	MTable.BARKOD
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

	Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("sdf3gdfhd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Barkod", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set S83T = Doc.Select2("sdfsdfhsj", "STOK83T", "D_ID='" & trim(Barkod) & "'", "", -1)
	if S83T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Barkod daha önce okutulmamýþ", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	satirNo = Doc.Cdbl(S83T.SRNUM(1))
	STOK83T.KOD(satirNo) = ""
	STOK83T.SF_SF_UNIT(satirNo) = ""
	STOK83T.SafePack()
	Call OkutulanMiktarlariGuncelle()
End Sub
Sub MiktarBilgisiniDoldur()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")


	Barkod = 	MTable.BARKOD
	MTable.BARKOD = ""

	Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("sdf3gdfhd", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & Barkod & "'", "", -1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Barkod", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	Set ML = Doc.Select2("sdf2gsdgs", "MMPS10T_LIST", "R_KAYNAKKODU='" & d7LB.SKOD & "'", "", -1)
	if ML.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		ret = Doc.d7MsgBox("Bu malzeme transfer edilecekler listesinde yok" & vbcrlf & "Eklemek Ýster misiniz?", "Dikkat", MB_YESNO)
		if ret <> 6 Then Exit Sub
		JobNo = MPSeEkle(MTable.MPSNO, d7LB.SKOD)
		if trim(JobNo) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Set ML = Doc.Select2("sdf2gsdgs", "MMPS10T_LIST", "R_KAYNAKKODU='" & d7LB.SKOD & "'", "", -1)
	End if
	
	if CikartOrIptal(Barkod) Then Exit Sub

	Miktar = GetStokMiktar(d7LB.SERINO, L1, L2, L3, L4, Depo)
	STOK01_PDA.JOBNO = ML.JOBNO
	STOK01_PDA.KOD = D7LB.SKOD
	STOK01_PDA.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", D7LB.SKOD, "")
	STOK01_PDA.AMBCODE = Depo
	STOK01_PDA.LOCATION1 = L1
	STOK01_PDA.LOCATION2 = L2
	STOK01_PDA.LOCATION3 = L3
	STOK01_PDA.LOCATION4 = L4
	STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER = D7LB.LOTNUMBER
	STOK01_PDA.SERINO = D7LB.SERINO
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R1 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R1
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R2 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R2
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R3 = D7LB.VRI_VR_R3
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM1 = D7LB.VRI_NUM1
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM2 = D7LB.VRI_NUM2
	STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_C = D7LB.VRI_VR_C
'	STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR = D7LB.SF_BAKIYE
	STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR = Miktar
	STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT = D7LB.SF_UNIT
	STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = GetTrMiktar(d7LB.SKOD, STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR, ML)
	STOK01_PDA.FOKOD = D7LB.FOKOD

	if STACK_PDA.PROPT = "1" and STACK_PDA.AUTO_ADD = "1" Then
		if STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu malzemeden transfer edilecek miktar yok", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
			Exit Sub
		End if
		Call cmdEkle()		
	Else
		Dim Par
		Set Par=Nothing
		Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "TR_EKLE",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
	End if
End Sub
Sub EkraniTemizle()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T_LIST")

	STOK01_PDA.Empty
	MTable.Empty
	MTableT.Empty
End Sub
Sub SonIslemIptal()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")

	if trim(MTable.STOK26_EVRAKNO) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Set STOK26T = Doc1.GettableObject("STOK26T")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MTable.STOK26_EVRAKNO
	Doc1.Delete_Vouhcer
End Sub
Sub Nerede()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T_LIST")

	Call StokGoster(MTableT.R_KAYNAKKODU)

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "NEREDE",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
End Sub
Sub Nerede2()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Call StokGoster(STOK01_PDA.KOD)

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadMainForm_Dyn "NEREDE",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
End Sub
Sub StokGoster(StokKodu)
	Set STOK01_PDA_LIST = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA_LIST")
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")

	STOK01_PDA_LIST.Empty

	QStr = "KOD='" & StokKodu & "'"
	if trim(STOK83E.AMBCODE) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & "&AMBCODE='" & STOK83E.AMBCODE & "'"
	End if
	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdfw3ff4f4", "STOK01", QStr, "", -1)

	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	
	For i = 1 to S01.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = STOK01_PDA_LIST.AddRow()
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

		STOK01_PDA_LIST.KOD = S01.KOD(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", S01.KOD(i), "")
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.AMBCODE = S01.AMBCODE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION1 = S01.LOCATION1(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION2 = S01.LOCATION2(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION3 = S01.LOCATION3(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOCATION4 = S01.LOCATION4(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.LOTNUMBER = S01.LOTNUMBER(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SERINO = S01.SERINO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_R1 = S01.VRI_VR_R1(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_R2 = S01.VRI_VR_R2(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_R3 = S01.VRI_VR_R3(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_NUM1 = S01.VRI_NUM1(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_NUM2 = S01.VRI_NUM2(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.VRI_VR_C = S01.VRI_VR_C(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SF_MIKTAR = S01.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.SF_UNIT = S01.SF_UNIT(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.FOKOD = S01.FOKOD(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS = S01.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_EVRAKNO = S01.ALLOC_EVRAKNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SIPTYPE = S01.ALLOC_SIPTYPE(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SIPNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_SIPARTNO = S01.ALLOC_SIPARTNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_MPSEVNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSEVNO(i)
		STOK01_PDA_LIST.ALLOC_MPSARTNO = S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO(i)
	Next
	STOK01_PDA_LIST.SetCurrentRow(1)
End Sub
Sub cmdKaydet()
	if EtiketleriBol() Then
		if Doc.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
			Call MPSMiktarlariniGuncelle()
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýþlem tamamlandý", "", MB_OK
		End if
	Else
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Etiket bölme iþlemi gerçekleþmediði için evrak kaydedilemedi", "", MB_OK
	End if
End Sub
Function GetTrMiktar(StokKodu, EtiketMiktari, ML)
	TransferBakiyesi = ML.R_HM_BAKIYE
	Set S83T = Doc.Select2("sdf243gdfgd", "STOK83T", "KOD='" & StokKodu & "'", "", -1)
	if S83T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Okutulan = 0
	Else
		Okutulan = S83T.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")
	End if
	TransferEdilecek = TransferBakiyesi - Okutulan
	if TransferEdilecek > EtiketMiktari Then
		GetTrMiktar = EtiketMiktari
	Else
		GetTrMiktar = TransferEdilecek
	End if
End Function
Sub SF_PAKETICERIGI_AV()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR_KALAN = STOK01_PDA.SF_MIKTAR - STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI
End Sub
Sub cmdEkle()
	Set STACK_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STACK_PDA")
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	if STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Transfer edilecek miktar girilmemiþ", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK83T) Then Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
	STOK83T.SafePack()

	satirNo = STOK83T.AddRow()
	STOK83T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	STOK83T.SetFieldValueNV "D_ID", -1, STOK01_PDA.D_ID

	STOK83T.JOBNO = STOK01_PDA.JOBNO
	Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK83T","JOBNO",STOK83T.GetCurrentRow()	
	STOK83T.ALANAMBARDASREZ = "H"

	STOK83T.KOD = STOK01_PDA.KOD
	Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK83T","KOD",STOK83T.GetCurrentRow()	
	STOK83T.LOTNUMBER = STOK01_PDA.LOTNUMBER
	STOK83T.SERINO = STOK01_PDA.SERINO
	STOK83T.FOKOD = STOK01_PDA.FOKOD
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_C = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_C
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R1
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R2
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_VR_R3
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_NUM1 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM1
	STOK83T.SF_VRI_NUM2 = STOK01_PDA.VRI_NUM2
	STOK83T.LOCATION1 = STOK01_PDA.LOCATION1
	STOK83T.LOCATION2 = STOK01_PDA.LOCATION2
	STOK83T.LOCATION3 = STOK01_PDA.LOCATION3
	STOK83T.LOCATION4 = STOK01_PDA.LOCATION4
	STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW1 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION1
	STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW2 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION2
	STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW3 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION3
	STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW4 = STOK01_PDA.PRX_LOCATION4
	STOK83T.OALLOC_SYSSTATUS = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SYSSTATUS
	STOK83T.OALLOC_EVRAKTYPE = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKTYPE
	STOK83T.OALLOC_EVRAKNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_EVRAKNO
	STOK83T.OALLOC_SIPTYPE = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPTYPE
	STOK83T.OALLOC_SIPNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPNO
	STOK83T.OALLOC_SIPARTNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_SIPARTNO
	STOK83T.OALLOC_MPSEVNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSEVNO
	STOK83T.OALLOC_MPSARTNO = STOK01_PDA.ALLOC_MPSARTNO

	STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR = STOK01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI
'	STOK83T.AMBCODE_TR = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "AMBCODE1", STOK01_PDA.D_ID, "")
'	STOK83T.LOCATION1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "LOCATION1", STOK01_PDA.D_ID, "")
'	STOK83T.LOCATION2 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "LOCATION2", STOK01_PDA.D_ID, "")
'	STOK83T.LOCATION3 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "LOCATION3", STOK01_PDA.D_ID, "")
'	STOK83T.LOCATION4 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "LOCATION4", STOK01_PDA.D_ID, "")

	Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK83T","SF_MIKTAR",STOK83T.GetCurrentRow()	
	STOK83T.SF_SF_UNIT = STOK01_PDA.SF_UNIT
	Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK83T","SF_SF_UNIT",STOK83T.GetCurrentRow()	
'	STOK83T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR = STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR
	STOK01_PDA.SAYAC = STOK83T.GetRowCount()
	Call OkutulanMiktarlariGuncelle()
	if STACK_PDA.AUTO_ADD <> "1" Then Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL
End Sub
Sub OkutulanMiktarlariGuncelle()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T_LIST")
	For i = 1 to MTableT.GetRowCount()
		Set S83T = Doc.Select2("sdf242fdsfgsd", "STOK83T", "JOBNO='" & MTableT.JOBNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if S83T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Okutulan = 0
		Else
			Okutulan = S83T.SumOf("SF_MIKTAR")
		End if
		MTableT.OKUTULAN_MIKTAR(i) = Okutulan
		MTableT.KALAN_MIKTAR(i) = MTableT.R_HM_BAKIYE(i) - Okutulan
		if Doc.DRound(MTableT.KALAN_MIKTAR(i)) = 0 Then
			MTableT.OKUNDU10(i) = "1"
		Else
			MTableT.OKUNDU10(i) = "0"
		End if
	Next
	MTableT.Refresh
End Sub
Sub cmdEvrakSil()
	Set S83T = Doc.Select2("sdf24gfdgds", "STOK83T", "", "", -1)
	if Doc.RunCommand("MT_DELETE") Then
		Call EkraniTemizle
		Call EtiketKartlariniGuncelle(S83T)
	End if
End Sub
Function EtiketleriBol()
	EtiketleriBol = False

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("TRACK251", "")
	Set S01_PDA = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set S25E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25E")
	Set S25T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK25T")

	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
	For i = 1 to STOK83T.GetRowCount()
		if Trim(STOK83T.D_ID(i)) <> "" Then
			DepoMiktari = GetStokMiktar(STOK83T.SERINO(i), L1, L2, L3, L4, Depo)
			if DepoMiktari > STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
				if trim(S25E.EVRAKNO) = "" Then
					Doc1.New_Voucher
					S25E.AMBCODE = STOK83E.AMBCODE
					S25E.TARGETAMBCODE = STOK83E.AMBCODE
					'Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_LOAD_FORM"
				End if

				S01_PDA.D_ID2 = STOK83T.D_ID(i)
				'Doc1.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK01_PDA", "D_ID2", -1

				S01_PDA.SF_PAKETICERIGI = STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				Doc1.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK01_PDA", "SF_PAKETICERIGI", -1

				Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_EKLE"
				S25T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR = S25T.AMBCODE_TR
			Else
				EtiketleriBol = True
				Exit Function
			End if
		End if
	Next
	if trim(S25E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
		if Doc1.RunCommand("MT_SAVE") Then
			EtiketleriBol = True
			For i = 1 to STOK83T.GetRowCount()
				Set S25T1 = Doc1.Select2("sdf34gdfgd", "STOK25T", "SERINO='" & STOK83T.SERINO(i) & "'&SF_MIKTAR='" & STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i) & "'", "", -1)
				if S25T1.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
					STOK83T.SetFieldValueNV "SERINO", i, S25T1.NEWSERINO(1)
					STOK83T.SetFieldValueNV "D_ID", i, S25T1.NEWSERINO(1)
					STOK83T.AMBCODE_TR(i) = S25T.TARGETAMBCODE_TR(1)
				End if
			Next
		End if
	End if

	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Sub EtiketleriBol2()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
	For i = 1 to STOK83T.GetRowCount()
		Set d7LB1 = Doc.Select1("sdf2fsdg", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & STOK83T.D_ID(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if d7LB1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			if d7LB1.SF_BAKIYE > STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
				EskiBarkod = STOK83T.D_ID(i)
				Doc1.Load_Voucher EskiBarkod
				Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_COPY"

				YeniBarkod = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
				Doc1.Load_Voucher YeniBarkod
				Doc1.RunCommand "MT_DOC_PASTE"
				d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK = 0
				d7LB.SF_BAKIYE = STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				d7LB.AMBCODE1 = STOK83E.TARGETAMBCODE
				d7LB.LOCATION1 = STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW1(i)
				d7LB.LOCATION2 = STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW2(i)
				d7LB.LOCATION3 = STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW3(i)
				d7LB.LOCATION4 = STOK83T.LOCATION_NEW4(i)
				d7LB.ONCEKETIKET = EskiBarkod
				d7LB.APPKODU = Doc.GetDocType
				d7LB.EVRAKNO = STOK83E.EVRAKNO
				if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
					Doc1.Load_Voucher EskiBarkod
					if d7LB.SF_BAKIYE < STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i) Then
						d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK = d7LB.SF_MIKTAR
					Else
						d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK = d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK + STOK83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
					End if
					if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
						STOK83T.SetFieldValueNV "D_ID", i, YeniBarkod
					End if
				End if
			End if
		End if
	Next
	if Doc.IsChanged Then Doc.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Sub EtiketKartlariniGuncelle(S83T)
	For i = 1 to S83T.GetRowCount()
		Set d7LB1 = Doc.Select1("sdf34gfdfg", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & S83T.D_ID(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if d7LB1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			if trim(d7LB1.APPKODU) = trim(Doc.GetDocType) and trim(d7LB1.EVRAKNO) = trim(S83T.EVRAKNO(i)) and trim(d7LB1.ONCEKETIKET) <> "" Then
				Call EtiketIptal(d7LB1)
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function CikartOrIptal(Barkod)
	CikartOrIptal = False
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
	For i = 1 to STOK83T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(STOK83T.D_ID(i)) = trim(Barkod) Then
			ret = Doc.d7MsgBox(trim(Barkod) & " barkodu daha önce okutulmuþ. " & vbcrlf & "Transfer listesinden çýkarmak istiyorsanýz 'Evet', istemiyorsanýz 'Hayýr' tuþuna basýnýz", "Uyarý", MB_YESNO)
			if ret = 6 Then
				Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
				STOK83T.DeleteRow_NoUI i
				Call OkutulanMiktarlariGuncelle()
			End if
			CikartOrIptal = True
			Exit Function
		Else
			Set d7LB = Doc.Select1("dwefddddwsds", "D7KIDSLB", "KOD='" & STOK83T.D_ID(i) & "'", "", -1)
			if d7LB.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				if trim(d7LB.APPKODU) = "STOK83" and trim(d7LB.EVRAKNO) = trim(STOK83E.EVRAKNO) and _
					trim(D7LB.ONCEKETIKET) = trim(Barkod) Then

					ret = Doc.d7MsgBox(trim(Barkod) & " barkodu daha önce okutulmuþ. " & vbcrlf & "Transfer listesinden çýkarmak istiyorsanýz 'Evet', istemiyorsanýz 'Hayýr' tuþuna basýnýz", "Uyarý", MB_YESNO)
					if ret = 6 Then
						Set STOK83T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83T")
						STOK83T.DeleteRow_NoUI i
						Call EtiketIptal(d7LB)
						Call OkutulanMiktarlariGuncelle()
					End if
					CikartOrIptal = True
					Exit Function
				End if
			End if
		End if
 	Next
End Function
Sub EtiketIptal(d7LB1)
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("D7KIDSLB", "")
	Set d7LB = Doc1.GetTableObject("D7KIDSLB")

	Doc1.Load_Voucher d7LB1.ONCEKETIKET
	d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK = d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK - d7LB1.SF_MIKTAR
	if d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK < 0 Then
		d7LB.SF_MIKTAR = d7LB.SF_MIKTAR - d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK
		d7LB.SF_NETKAPANANMIK = 0
	End if
	Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Function GetStokMiktar(SeriNo, Lokasyon1, Lokasyon2, Lokasyon3, Lokasyon4, Depo)
	GetStokMiktar = 0
	Lokasyon1 = ""
	Lokasyon2 = ""
	Lokasyon3 = ""
	Lokasyon4 = ""
	Set STOK83E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	'Depo = ""

	Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdf34dfh", "STOK01", "SERINO='" & SeriNo & "'", "", -1)
	if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(SeriNo) & " seri no.lu malzeme stokta bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
		Exit Function
	End if

	if S01.GetRowCount() > 1 Then
	'*********** OKAN TAKATAÞ  TARAFINDAN EKLENDÝ
		
		Set S01 = Doc.Select1("sdf34dfh", "STOK01", "SERINO='" & SeriNo & "'&AMBCODE='"& STOK83E.AMBCODE &"'", "", -1)

		if S01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox trim(SeriNo) & " seri no.lu malzeme "&STOK83E.AMBCODE & " deposunda bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
			Exit Function
		End if

		'Doc.d7MsgBox trim(SeriNo) & " seri no.lu malzeme stok listesinde birden çok satýrda bulundu", "", MB_OK
		'Exit Function
	'************************

	End if
	GetStokMiktar = S01.SF_MIKTAR(1)
	Lokasyon1 = S01.LOCATION1(1)
	Lokasyon2 = S01.LOCATION2(1)
	Lokasyon3 = S01.LOCATION3(1)
	Lokasyon4 = S01.LOCATION4(1)
	Depo = S01.AMBCODE(1)
End Function
Function MPSeEkle(MPSNo, StokKodu)
	MPSeEkle = ""
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MMPS10", "")
	Set M10T = Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher MPSNo
	satirNo = M10T.AddRow
	M10T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
	M10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(satirNo) = "H"
	M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo) = StokKodu
	JobNo = M10T.JOBNO(satirNo)
	if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
		MPSeEkle = JobNo
		Set ML = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T_LIST")
		ML.SetRowAID 1,1,1
		satirNo1 = ML.AddRow
		ML.SetCurrentRow(satirNo1)
		ML.R_KAYNAKTYPE(satirNo1) = "H"
		ML.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo1) = StokKodu
		ML.JOBNO(satirNo1) = JobNo
		ML.KAYNAK_AD(satirNo1) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, "")
		ML.KAYNAK_BIRIM(satirNo1) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", StokKodu, "")
		ML.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Sub MPSMiktarlariniGuncelle()
	DocYaratildi = False
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_SF")
	Set S83T = Doc.Select2G("asdfasdg", "STOK83T", "JOBNO>' '", "", "JOBNO", -1)
	For i = 1 to S83T.GetRowCount()
		if Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_MIKTAR0", S83T.JOBNO(i), "JOBNO") = 0 Then
			if Not DocYaratildi Then
				Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MMPS10", "")
				Set M10T = Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
				ret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(MTable.MPSNO)
				if ret <> 0 Then Exit Sub
				DocYaratildi = True
			End if
			Set M10T1 = Doc1.Select2("sdfeaj", "MMPS10T", "JOBNO='" & S83T.JOBNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
			if M10T1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				satirNo = Doc.Cdbl(M10T1.SRNUM(1))
				M10T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				M10T.R_MIKTAR0(satirNo) = S83T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			End if
		End if
	Next
	if DocYaratildi Then
		Doc1.Save_Voucher
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	End if
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : FORMA_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : BANKA
Hangi Tablo için : BNK00
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Banka EFT kodlarýný Aktar (xml)
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set objXMLDoc = Nothing
	Set objXMLDoc = CreateObject("Microsoft.XMLDOM")
	objXMLDoc.async = False

	Doc.StatusBarMessage "Merkez Bankasý 'na baðlanýlýyor..."
	objXMLDoc.Load "http://eft.tcmb.gov.tr/bankasubelistesi/cif.xml"
'	objXMLDoc.Load "c:\cif.xml"
'	Set x = objXMLDoc.createProcessingInstruction("xml", "version=""1.0"" encoding=""ISO-8859-9""")
'	objXMLDoc.insertBefore x, objXMLDoc.childNodes(0)
'	msgbox objXMLDoc.xml

	Set Root = objXMLDoc.documentElement
	If Root Is Nothing Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Merkez Bankasý 'na baðlanýlamadý", "Hata", "Ýþlem kesilmiþtir."
		Exit Sub
	End If

	Set RecList = Root.getElementsByTagName("record")
	For Each x In RecList
		BankaKodu = GetNodeValue(x, "BKOD")
		BankaKodu = Right("0000" & BankaKodu, 4)

		SubeKodu = GetNodeValue(x, "SKOD")
		SubeKodu = Right("00000" & SubeKodu, 5)

		SubeAdi = GetNodeValue(x, "NAME")
		SehirKodu = GetNodeValue(x, "CITY")
		if trim(SubeKodu) = "00000" Then BankaAdi = SubeAdi

		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Banka/Þube EFT kodlarý aktarýlýyor : " & BankaKodu & " / " & SubeKodu & " : " & SubeAdi

		if BankaKodu <> EskiBankaKodu Then
			if trim(BNK00.KOD) <> "" Then
				Doc.Save_Voucher
			End if
			Doc.Load_Voucher BankaKodu
			BNK00.AD = BankaAdi
			BNK00T.Empty
		End if
		if trim(SubeKodu) <> "00000" Then
			satirNo = BNK00T.AddRow()
			BNK00T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			BNK00T.SUBE_KODU = SubeKodu
			BNK00T.SUBE_ADI = SubeAdi
			BNK00T.SUBE_ILKODU = SehirKodu
		End if
		EskiBankaKodu = BankaKodu
	Next
	if trim(BNK00.KOD) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
End Sub
Function GetNodeValue(NodeName, AlanAdi)
	Set NodeXml = NodeName.SelectSingleNode(AlanAdi)
	If Not NodeXml Is Nothing Then
		If Not NodeXml.FirstChild Is Nothing Then
			GetNodeValue = NodeXml.FirstChild.Data
		End If
	End If
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : FORMA_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : RECETE
Hangi Tablo için : BOMU01
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Mikrodaki Rotalarý Freedomdaki Ürün Aðaçlarýna Aktar
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
'	if HammaddeBirimHata() Then Exit Sub

'	if RotaKontrolHata() Then Exit Sub
'	if not TezgahveOpKartlariniYarat() Then Exit Sub
'	Call HammaddeleriAktar()
	Call OperasyonlariAktarAktar()
End Sub
Sub OperasyonlariAktarAktar()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\rotalar.xls")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	For i = 2 to 65536
		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) = "" Then Exit For
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Rotalar aktarýlýyor : " & i
		StokKodu = Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value
		if trim(StokKodu) <> trim(EskiStokKodu) Then
			if trim(BOMU01E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
				BOMU01T.Sort("BOMREC_SIRANO")
				Doc.Save_Voucher
			End if
			FrMamulKodu = GetFreedomStokKodu(StokKodu)
			if trim(FrMamulKodu) <> "" Then
				EvrakNo = GetUrunAgaciEvrakno(FrMamulKodu)
				if trim(EvrakNo) = "" Then
					Doc.New_Voucher
					BOMU01E.MAMULCODE = StokKodu
					BOMU01E.AKTIF_PASIF = "*"
					BOMU01E.MAMULMIKTAR = 1
				Else
					Doc.Load_Voucher EvrakNo
					For j = 1 to BOMU01T.GetRowCount()
						if BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(j) = "I" Then
							BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(j) = ""
							BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(j) = ""
						End if
					Next
					BOMU01T.SafePack
				End if
			End if
		End if
		EskiStokKodu = StokKodu
		if trim(FrMamulKodu) <> "" and trim(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value) <> "01" and trim(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value) <> "09" Then
			satirNo = BOMU01T.AddRow()
			BOMU01T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE = "I"
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_SIRANO = Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE = GetTezgahKodu(Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value)
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_OPERASYON = GetTezgahKodu(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value)
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0_BV = Sh1.Cells(i,6).Value
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0_BU = "sn"
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK1 = Sh1.Cells(i,5).Value / 3600
			BOMU01T.OPERATORSAYISI1 = Sh1.Cells(i,10).Value
		End if
	Next
	if trim(BOMU01E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
		BOMU01T.Sort("BOMREC_SIRANO")
		Doc.Save_Voucher
	End if
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Sub
Function RotaKontrolHata()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\rotalar.xls")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)
	Set fff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	RotaKontrolHata = False
	For i = 2 to 65000
		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) = "" Then Exit For
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Rotalar kontrol ediliyor : " & i
		QStr = "SELECT EVRAKNO FROM STOK0U U WHERE U.UCODE='" & trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) & "'"
		Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdgsdrhs", QStr)
		if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) & " üretici kodlarýnda bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
			RotaKontrolHata = True
		End if
	Next
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Function
Function TezgahveOpKartlariniYarat()
	TezgahveOpKartlariniYarat = False
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\rotalar.xls")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	Set I00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdsdf", "SELECT  KOD, AD FROM IMLT00 WHERE 1=0")
	Set I01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdsdfddd", "SELECT  KOD, AD FROM IMLT01 WHERE 1=0")
	I00.Empty
	I01.Empty

	For i = 2 to 65000
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Tezgah ve Operasyon Kodlarý inceleniyor : " & i
		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) = "" Then Exit For

		StokKodu = Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value
		if trim(StokKodu) <> trim(EskiStokKodu) Then
			FrMamulKodu = GetFreedomStokKodu(StokKodu)
		End if
		EskiStokKodu = StokKodu

'		satirNo = I00.AddRow
		satirNo = I01.AddRow

'		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value) <> "" and trim(Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value) <> "31" Then
'			I00.KOD(satirNo) = GetTezgahKodu(Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value)
'			I00.AD(satirNo) = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,11).Value)
'		End if

		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value) <> "" and trim(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value) <> "09" and FrMamulKodu <> "" Then
			I01.KOD(satirNo) = GetTezgahKodu(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value)
			I01.AD(satirNo) = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,12).Value)
		End if
	Next

'	I00.GroupBy("KOD")
	I01.GroupBy("KOD")

'	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("IMLT00", "")
'	Set I0 = Doc1.GetTableObject("IMLT00")
'	For i = 1 to I00.GetRowCount()
'		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Tezgah Kartlarý Yaratýlýyor : " & i & " / " & I00.GetRowCount()
'		Set I00_D = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sadgsdfh", "SELECT KOD FROM IMLT00 WHERE KOD='" & I00.KOD(i) &"'")
'		if I00_D.GetRowCount() = 0 and trim(I00.KOD(i)) <> "" Then
'			Doc1.Load_Voucher I00.KOD(i)
'			I0.AD = I00.AD(i)
'			if Doc1.Save_Voucher <> 0 Then Exit Function
'		End if
'	Next
'	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
'	Doc.DeleteDocumentNoUI "IMLT00", ""

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("IMLT01", "")
	Set I1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("IMLT01")
	For i = 1 to I01.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Operasyon Kartlarý Yaratýlýyor : " & i & " / " & I01.GetRowCount()
		Set I01_D = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sadgsdfh", "SELECT KOD FROM IMLT01 WHERE KOD='" & I01.KOD(i) &"'")
		if I01_D.GetRowCount() = 0 and trim(I01.KOD(i)) <> "" Then
			Doc1.Load_Voucher I01.KOD(i)
			I1.AD = I01.AD(i)
			if Doc1.Save_Voucher <> 0 Then Exit Function
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher

	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
	TezgahveOpKartlariniYarat = True
End Function
Function GetTezgahKodu(ByVal TezgahKodu)
	TezgahKodu = Replace(TezgahKodu,".", "-")
	TezgahKodu = Replace(TezgahKodu," ", "-")
	GetTezgahKodu = TezgahKodu
End Function
Sub HammaddeleriAktar()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\receteler.xls")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	For i = 2 to 65000
		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) = "" Then Exit For
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Reçeteler aktarýlýyor : " & i
		StokKodu = Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value
		if trim(StokKodu) <> trim(EskiStokKodu) Then
			if trim(BOMU01E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
			EvrakNo = GetUrunAgaciEvrakno(StokKodu)
			if trim(EvrakNo) = "" Then
				Doc.New_Voucher
				BOMU01E.MAMULCODE = StokKodu
				BOMU01E.AKTIF_PASIF = "*"
				BOMU01E.MAMULMIKTAR = Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value
			Else
				Doc.Load_Voucher EvrakNo
			End if
			BOMU01T.Empty
		End if
		EskiStokKodu = StokKodu

		satirNo = BOMU01T.AddRow()
		BOMU01T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE = "H"
		BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE = Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value
		BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0 = Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value
	Next
	if trim(BOMU01E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Sub
Function GetUrunAgaciEvrakno(StokKodu)
	GetUrunAgaciEvrakno = ""
	QStr = "SELECT EVRAKNO FROM BOMU01E WHERE MAMULCODE='" & StokKodu & "' AND AKTIF_PASIF='*'"
	Set B01E = Doc.RunSQLQuery("zsdfgasefhsdh", QStr)
	if B01E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	GetUrunAgaciEvrakno = B01E.EVRAKNO
End Function
Function GetYeniBirim(EskiBirim)
	if trim(EskiBirim) = "ADET" Then
		GetYeniBirim = "AD"
	Elseif trim(EskiBirim) = "KÝLOGRAM" Then
		GetYeniBirim = "KG"
	Elseif trim(EskiBirim) = "METRE" Then
		GetYeniBirim = "MT"
	Elseif trim(EskiBirim) = "TKM" Then
		GetYeniBirim = "TK"
	Elseif trim(EskiBirim) = "TAKIM" Then
		GetYeniBirim = "TK"
	Else
		GetYeniBirim = EskiBirim
	End if
End Function
Function HammaddeBirimHata()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\receteler.xls")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)
	Set fff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	HammaddeBirimHata = False
	For i = 2 to 65536
		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) = "" Then Exit For
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Birim kontrolü yapýlýyor : " & i
		QStr = "SELECT S.KOD, S.IUNIT FROM STOK0U U LEFT JOIN STOK00 S ON S.KOD=U.EVRAKNO WHERE U.UCODE='" & trim(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value) & "'"
		Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdgsdrhs", QStr)

		QStr = "SELECT S.KOD, S.IUNIT FROM STOK0U U LEFT JOIN STOK00 S ON S.KOD=U.EVRAKNO WHERE U.UCODE='" & trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) & "'"
		Set S001 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdgsdrhCCCs", QStr)
		if S00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			YeniBirim = GetYeniBirim(Sh1.Cells(i,7).Value)
			if trim(YeniBirim) <> trim(S00.IUNIT(1)) Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox i & ". satýr, Stok Kodu : " & trim(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value) & ", Tüketim birimi: " & YeniBirim & _
								", Stok Birimi : " & S00.IUNIT(1), "", MB_OK
				HammaddeBirimHata = True
			End if
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & trim(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value) & " üretici kodlarýnda bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
			HammaddeBirimHata = True
		End if
		if S001.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) & " üretici kodlarýnda bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
			HammaddeBirimHata = True
		End if
	Next
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Function

Function GetFreedomStokKodu(MamulKodu)
	GetFreedomStokKodu = ""
	QStr = "SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & MamulKodu & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " UNION ALL"
	QStr = QStr & " SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE SUPPLIERCODE='" & MamulKodu & "'"

	Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dxfhadfs", QStr)
	if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	GetFreedomStokKodu = S00.KOD(1)
End Function

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : FORMA_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : URETIMFISI
Hangi Tablo için : SFDC32
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : MAKR7S
Açýklama : Sonraki Operasyon Fason Ýse Üretim Fiþi Kes
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Call SonrakiOpFasonIseUretimFisi()
End Sub
Sub SonrakiOpFasonIseUretimFisi()
	SFDC32T.SafePack
	if SFDC32T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	if SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR(1) = 0 Then Exit Sub
	if trim(SFDC32T.KOD(1)) = "" Then Exit Sub

	if trim(SFDC32E.JOBNO) = "" Then Exit Sub

	QStr = "SELECT EVRAKNO FROM STOK20T"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE W_APP='SFDC32' AND W_TABLE='SFDC32T' AND W_EVRAKTYPE='S4'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND W_EVRAKNO='" & SFDC32E.EVRAKNO & "' AND W_TRNUM='" & SFDC32T.TRNUM(1) & "'" 

	Set S20T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdaehfraj", QStr)
	if S20T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then Exit Sub

	if Not SonrakiOpFason() Then Exit Sub

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK20", "")
	Set S20E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK20E")
	Set S20T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK20T")

	if Doc1.New_Voucher <> 0 Then Exit Sub
	S20T.Empty

	S20E.AMBCODE = "02"
	S20E.IMALATAMBCODE = "02"
	S20E.YANMAMULAMBCODE = "02"

	For i = 1 to SFDC32T.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S20T.AddRow
		S20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S20T.MPSNO = SFDC32E.MPSNO
		S20T.MPSTM_TRNUM = SFDC32T.MPSTM_TRNUM(i)
		S20T.KOD = SFDC32T.KOD(i)
		S20T.SF_MIKTAR = SFDC32T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
		S20T.LOTNUMBER = GetYeniLotNo(SFDC32E.MPSNO)
'		S20T.SERINO = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
		S20T.W_FIRMA = Doc.Getd7FirmCode()
		S20T.W_APP = "SFDC32"
		S20T.W_TABLE = "SFDC32T"
		S20T.W_EVRAKTYPE ="S4"
		S20T.W_EVRAKNO = SFDC32E.EVRAKNO
		S20T.W_TRNUM = SFDC32T.TRNUM(i)
	Next
	Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_SIRALIK_ISLEM_003"
	Doc1.RunCommand "CMD_STOK01DEBULUNDUMU_BILGISATIRIE"
	ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	if ret = 0 Then
		For i = 1 to SFDC32T.GetRowCount()
			SFDC32T.URETIMFISIVAR(i) = "E"
		Next
	End if
End Sub
Function SonrakiOpFason()
	SonrakiOpFason = False
	SRNum = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "SRNUM", SFDC32E.JOBNO, "JOBNO")
	QStr = "SELECT R_OPERASYON FROM MMPS10T"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE EVRAKNO='" & SFDC32E.MPSNO & "' AND R_KAYNAKTYPE='I' AND SRNUM>'" & SRNum & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY SRNUM DESC"
	
	Set M10T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("zsdaerhaj", QStr)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	if trim(M10T.R_OPERASYON(1)) = "013" Then SonrakiOpFason = True
End Function
Function GetYeniLotNo(MPSNo)
	QStr = "SELECT MAX(LOTNUMBER) LOTNUMBER FROM STOK20T"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE MPSNO='" & MPSNo & "' AND LOTNUMBER LIKE '" & trim(MPSNO) & "%'"
	Set S20T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("zsdgaerha", QStr)
	if S20T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		GetYeniLotNo = trim(MPSNo) & "-01"
	Else
		if trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(1)) = "" Then
			GetYeniLotNo = trim(MPSNo) & "-01"
		Else
			GetYeniLotNo = Doc.StrIncrement(trim(S20T.LOTNUMBER(1)))
		End if
	End if
End Function                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   

Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar Ýçin Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Ayarlarý
Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Bu Uygulama için : 
Bu Uygulama için Baþlangýç Ekran Formu : 
Baþlangýçta Bu Makro Çalýþacak : 
Alt Ekranlar Ýçin Ekran Formu
Alt Ekran Form Kodu : 
Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro : 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : FORMA_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : SERILE
Hangi Tablo için : STOK24
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : STOK36
Açýklama : Serile
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	STOK24T.SafePack()
	For i = STOK24T.GetRowCount() to 1 Step -1
		if trim(STOK24T.SERINO(i)) = "" Then
			Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdffjsaj", "SELECT B_MINPARTIBUYUKLUGU FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & STOK24T.KOD(i) & "'")
			if S00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				PartiBuyuklugu = S00.B_MINPARTIBUYUKLUGU(1)
				if PartiBuyuklugu > 0 Then
					KasaSayisi = Doc.Ceil(STOK24T.GIREN_MIKTAR(i) / PartiBuyuklugu)
					Miktar = STOK24T.GIREN_MIKTAR(i)
					For j = 1 to KasaSayisi
						if j < KasaSayisi Then
							KasaIci = PartiBuyuklugu
						Else
							KasaIci = Miktar - (j - 1) * PartiBuyuklugu
						End if
						if j = 1 Then
							STOK24T.SetCurrentRow(i)
							STOK24T.GIREN_MIKTAR(i) = KasaIci
							STOK24T.SERINO(i) = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
						Else
							satirNo = i + (j-1)
							STOK24T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
							if satirNo > STOK24T.GetRowCount() Then
								STOK24T.AddRow
							Else
								STOK24T.InsertRow(satirNo)
							End if
							STOK24T.CopyRowFromOtherTable satirNo, STOK24T, i
							STOK24T.GIREN_MIKTAR(satirNo) = KasaIci
							STOK24T.SERINO(satirNo) = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
						End if
					Next
				End if
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           

Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar Ýçin Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Ayarlarý

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : FORMA_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : RECETE-1
Hangi Tablo için : BOMU01
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Tezgah ve Operasyon Kartlarýný Aktar
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	if not TezgahveOpKartlariniYarat() Then Exit Sub
End Sub
Sub OperasyonlariAktarAktar()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\rotalar.xls")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	For i = 2 to 65536
		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) = "" Then Exit For
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Rotalar aktarýlýyor : " & i
		StokKodu = Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value
		if trim(StokKodu) <> trim(EskiStokKodu) Then
			if trim(BOMU01E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
				BOMU01T.Sort("BOMREC_SIRANO")
				Doc.Save_Voucher
			End if
			FrMamulKodu = GetFreedomStokKodu(StokKodu)
			if trim(FrMamulKodu) <> "" Then
				EvrakNo = GetUrunAgaciEvrakno(FrMamulKodu)
				if trim(EvrakNo) = "" Then
					Doc.New_Voucher
					BOMU01E.MAMULCODE = StokKodu
					BOMU01E.AKTIF_PASIF = "*"
					BOMU01E.MAMULMIKTAR = 1
				Else
					Doc.Load_Voucher EvrakNo
					For j = 1 to BOMU01T.GetRowCount()
						if BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(j) = "I" Then
							BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(j) = ""
							BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(j) = ""
						End if
					Next
					BOMU01T.SafePack
				End if
			End if
		End if
		EskiStokKodu = StokKodu
		if trim(FrMamulKodu) <> "" and trim(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value) <> "01" and trim(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value) <> "09" Then
			satirNo = BOMU01T.AddRow()
			BOMU01T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE = "I"
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_SIRANO = Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE = GetTezgahKodu(Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value)
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_OPERASYON = GetTezgahKodu(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value)
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0_BV = Sh1.Cells(i,6).Value
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0_BU = "sn"
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK1 = Sh1.Cells(i,5).Value / 3600
			BOMU01T.OPERATORSAYISI1 = Sh1.Cells(i,10).Value
		End if
	Next
	if trim(BOMU01E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
		BOMU01T.Sort("BOMREC_SIRANO")
		Doc.Save_Voucher
	End if
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Sub
Function RotaKontrolHata()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\rotalar.xls")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)
	Set fff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	RotaKontrolHata = False
	For i = 2 to 65000
		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) = "" Then Exit For
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Rotalar kontrol ediliyor : " & i
		QStr = "SELECT EVRAKNO FROM STOK0U U WHERE U.UCODE='" & trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) & "'"
		Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdgsdrhs", QStr)
		if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) & " üretici kodlarýnda bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
			RotaKontrolHata = True
		End if
	Next
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Function
Function TezgahveOpKartlariniYarat()
	TezgahveOpKartlariniYarat = False
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\rotalar.xls")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	Set I00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdsdf", "SELECT  KOD, AD FROM IMLT00 WHERE 1=0")
	Set I01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdsdfddd", "SELECT  KOD, AD FROM IMLT01 WHERE 1=0")
	I00.Empty
	I01.Empty

	For i = 2 to 65000
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Tezgah ve Operasyon Kodlarý inceleniyor : " & i
		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) = "" Then Exit For

		StokKodu = Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value
		if trim(StokKodu) <> trim(EskiStokKodu) Then
			FrMamulKodu = GetFreedomStokKodu(StokKodu)
		End if
		EskiStokKodu = StokKodu

		satirNo = I00.AddRow
		satirNo = I01.AddRow

'		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value) <> "" and trim(Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value) <> "31" and FrMamulKodu <> "" Then
'			I00.KOD(satirNo) = GetTezgahKodu(Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value)
'			I00.AD(satirNo) = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,11).Value)
'		End if

		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value) <> "" and trim(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value) <> "09" and FrMamulKodu <> "" Then
			I01.KOD(satirNo) = GetTezgahKodu(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value)
			I01.AD(satirNo) = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,12).Value)

			I00.KOD(satirNo) = GetTezgahKodu(Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value)
			I00.AD(satirNo) = CStr(Sh1.Cells(i,11).Value)

		End if
	Next

	I00.GroupBy("KOD")
	I01.GroupBy("KOD")

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("IMLT00", "")
	Set I0 = Doc1.GetTableObject("IMLT00")
	For i = 1 to I00.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Tezgah Kartlarý Yaratýlýyor : " & i & " / " & I00.GetRowCount()
		Set I00_D = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sadgsdfh", "SELECT KOD FROM IMLT00 WHERE KOD='" & I00.KOD(i) &"'")
		if I00_D.GetRowCount() = 0 and trim(I00.KOD(i)) <> "" Then
			Doc1.Load_Voucher I00.KOD(i)
			I0.AD = I00.AD(i)
			if Doc1.Save_Voucher <> 0 Then Exit Function
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Doc.DeleteDocumentNoUI "IMLT00", ""

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("IMLT01", "")
	Set I1 = Doc1.GetTableObject("IMLT01")
	For i = 1 to I01.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusbarMessage "Operasyon Kartlarý Yaratýlýyor : " & i & " / " & I01.GetRowCount()
		Set I01_D = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sadgsdfh", "SELECT KOD FROM IMLT01 WHERE KOD='" & I01.KOD(i) &"'")
		if I01_D.GetRowCount() = 0 and trim(I01.KOD(i)) <> "" Then
			Doc1.Load_Voucher I01.KOD(i)
			I1.AD = I01.AD(i)
			if Doc1.Save_Voucher <> 0 Then Exit Function
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher

	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
	TezgahveOpKartlariniYarat = True
End Function
Function GetTezgahKodu(ByVal TezgahKodu)
	TezgahKodu = Replace(TezgahKodu,".", "-")
	TezgahKodu = Replace(TezgahKodu," ", "-")
	GetTezgahKodu = TezgahKodu
End Function
Sub HammaddeleriAktar()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\receteler.xls")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)

	For i = 2 to 65000
		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) = "" Then Exit For
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Reçeteler aktarýlýyor : " & i
		StokKodu = Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value
		if trim(StokKodu) <> trim(EskiStokKodu) Then
			if trim(BOMU01E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
			EvrakNo = GetUrunAgaciEvrakno(StokKodu)
			if trim(EvrakNo) = "" Then
				Doc.New_Voucher
				BOMU01E.MAMULCODE = StokKodu
				BOMU01E.AKTIF_PASIF = "*"
				BOMU01E.MAMULMIKTAR = Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value
			Else
				Doc.Load_Voucher EvrakNo
			End if
			BOMU01T.Empty
		End if
		EskiStokKodu = StokKodu

		satirNo = BOMU01T.AddRow()
		BOMU01T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE = "H"
		BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE = Sh1.Cells(i,2).Value
		BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0 = Sh1.Cells(i,4).Value
	Next
	if trim(BOMU01E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Sub
Function GetUrunAgaciEvrakno(StokKodu)
	GetUrunAgaciEvrakno = ""
	QStr = "SELECT EVRAKNO FROM BOMU01E WHERE MAMULCODE='" & StokKodu & "' AND AKTIF_PASIF='*'"
	Set B01E = Doc.RunSQLQuery("zsdfgasefhsdh", QStr)
	if B01E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	GetUrunAgaciEvrakno = B01E.EVRAKNO
End Function
Function GetYeniBirim(EskiBirim)
	if trim(EskiBirim) = "ADET" Then
		GetYeniBirim = "AD"
	Elseif trim(EskiBirim) = "KÝLOGRAM" Then
		GetYeniBirim = "KG"
	Elseif trim(EskiBirim) = "METRE" Then
		GetYeniBirim = "MT"
	Elseif trim(EskiBirim) = "TKM" Then
		GetYeniBirim = "TK"
	Elseif trim(EskiBirim) = "TAKIM" Then
		GetYeniBirim = "TK"
	Else
		GetYeniBirim = EskiBirim
	End if
End Function
Function HammaddeBirimHata()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("c:\receteler.xls")
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)
	Set fff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	HammaddeBirimHata = False
	For i = 2 to 65536
		if trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) = "" Then Exit For
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Birim kontrolü yapýlýyor : " & i
		QStr = "SELECT S.KOD, S.IUNIT FROM STOK0U U LEFT JOIN STOK00 S ON S.KOD=U.EVRAKNO WHERE U.UCODE='" & trim(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value) & "'"
		Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdgsdrhs", QStr)

		QStr = "SELECT S.KOD, S.IUNIT FROM STOK0U U LEFT JOIN STOK00 S ON S.KOD=U.EVRAKNO WHERE U.UCODE='" & trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) & "'"
		Set S001 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdgsdrhCCCs", QStr)
		if S00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			YeniBirim = GetYeniBirim(Sh1.Cells(i,7).Value)
			if trim(YeniBirim) <> trim(S00.IUNIT(1)) Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox i & ". satýr, Stok Kodu : " & trim(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value) & ", Tüketim birimi: " & YeniBirim & _
								", Stok Birimi : " & S00.IUNIT(1), "", MB_OK
				HammaddeBirimHata = True
			End if
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & trim(Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value) & " üretici kodlarýnda bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
			HammaddeBirimHata = True
		End if
		if S001.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & trim(Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value) & " üretici kodlarýnda bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
			HammaddeBirimHata = True
		End if
	Next
	Wb1.Close
	Excel.Quit
End Function

Function GetFreedomStokKodu(MamulKodu)
	GetFreedomStokKodu = ""
	QStr = "SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & MamulKodu & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " UNION ALL"
	QStr = QStr & " SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE SUPPLIERCODE='" & MamulKodu & "'"

	Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dxfhadfs", QStr)
	if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	GetFreedomStokKodu = S00.KOD(1)
End Function

Makro Kodu : MESLEK
Hangi Tablo için : PERSM22
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : MESLEK KODLARININ AÇILMAS.
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()

	Dim May_container
	Set May_container = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLE2=STACK","IO")

	May_container.Addfield2 "DOSYA","CHAR",255,0,"L","F2=DO_SYSBRW_FILENAME()",""
	satirNo = May_container.AddRow()
	May_container.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "MAKROFORMU1",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
	
End Sub

Dim mesaj_array
Redim mesaj_array(20)

SUB Getir()

	Set TABLO_1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE2")

	if trim(TABLO_1.DOSYA(1)) <> "" then

		Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")

		Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open(TABLO_1.DOSYA(1), False, True)

		Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)
	else 
	
		Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL

		exit sub

	end if
	
	Dim k
	
	k = 1

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("PERSM22", "")
	Set P_PERSM22 = Doc1.GetTableObject("PERSM22")
	Set P_PERSM22_B = Doc1.GetTableObject("PERSM22_B")

	for j = 2 to Sh1.UsedRange.Rows.Count
		if	 trim(Sh1.Cells(j ,1)) <> "" then
			nret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(UCase(trim(Sh1.Cells(j ,1))))
			P_PERSM22.MESLEKADI = 	trim(Sh1.Cells(j ,2))
			P_PERSM22.MESLEKTANIMI = trim(Sh1.Cells(j ,3))
			P_PERSM22_B.GOREVISLEMLER = trim(Sh1.Cells(j ,4))

			nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
			nret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
		end if
	Next

	Wb1.Close

	Excel.Quit

	Doc.RUN_APP_CANCEL

End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu

Sub Makro1()
	Set STOK29E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29E")
	STOK29E.AddField2 "TEDARIKCI", "CHAR", 16, 0, "L", "F2=DO_BRW('CARI00')", ""
	STOK29E.Setd7FieldEvent "TEDARIKCI","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "TEDARIKCI_AV"
End Sub
Sub Makro2()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	STOK01_PDA.Setd7FieldEvent "D_ID2","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "D_ID2_AV"
End Sub
Sub TEDARIKCI_AV()
	Set STOK29E = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29E")
	if trim(STOK29E.TEDARIKCI) = "" Then
		STOK29E.TEDARIKCI = ""
		STOK29E.CARIHESAPCODE = ""
		STOK29E.CARIHESAPCODE_CARI00_AD = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set C00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfgqerqjqj", "SELECT KOD, AD FROM CARI00 WHERE KOD='" & STOK29E.TEDARIKCI & "'")
	if C00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		STOK29E.CARIHESAPCODE = C00.KOD(1)
		STOK29E.CARIHESAPCODE_CARI00_AD = C00.AD(1)
		Exit Sub
	End if

	BarkodNo = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(STOK29E.TEDARIKCI), 30)
	QStr = "SELECT D.CH_TEDARIKCI, C.AD FROM D7KIDSLB D"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN CARI00 C ON C.KOD=D.CH_TEDARIKCI"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE D.KOD='" & BarkodNo & "'"

	Set D7LB = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfgsjsjsgj", QStr)
	if D7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz tedarikçi/barkod", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		STOK29E.SetFieldValueNV "TEDARIKCI", 1, ""
		STOK29E.CARIHESAPCODE = ""
		STOK29E.CARIHESAPCODE_CARI00_AD = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if

	STOK29E.SetFieldValueNV "TEDARIKCI", 1, D7LB.CH_TEDARIKCI(1)
	STOK29E.CARIHESAPCODE = D7LB.CH_TEDARIKCI(1)
	STOK29E.CARIHESAPCODE_CARI00_AD = D7LB.AD(1)
End Sub
Sub D_ID2_AV()
	Set STOK01_PDA = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK01_PDA")
	Set STOK29T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK29T")

	STOK29T.SafePack()

	satirNo = STOK29T.GetRowCount()
	if satirNo = 0 Then Exit Sub
	if trim(STOK29T.D_ID(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub

	QStr = "SELECT D7KIDSLB.SF_MIKTAR, D7KIDSLB.SF_UNIT FROM D7KIDSLB "
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD=D7KIDSLB.SKOD"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE D7KIDSLB.KOD='" & STOK29T.D_ID(satirNo) & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND D7KIDSLB.SF_UNIT=STOK00.IUNIT"

	Set d7LB = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfgertherht", QStr)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	STOK29T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
	STOK29T.SF_AUTOCALC(satirNo) = "E"
	STOK29T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(satirNo) = D7LB.SF_MIKTAR(1)

	Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "STOK29T", "SF_STOK_MIKTAR", satirNo
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : FORMA_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : RECETEAKT
Hangi Tablo için : BOMU01
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : MAKR7S
Açýklama : Ürün Aðaçlarýný Mikro 'dan Aktar
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set par1 = Doc.CreateSObject("Parameters")
	par = "Provider=SQLOLEDB;Data Source=dbserver;Initial Catalog=MikroDB_V14_FORMAMAKÝNA2012;Integrated Security=SSPI;"
	par1.SetParameter "REPORT_CONNECTION_STRING", par

	QStr = "select U.URt_RotaUrunKodu,U.URt_ID,U.URt_OpKod, U.URt_IsmerkeziveyaGrupKod, U.URt_SabitHazirlikSuresi,U.URt_DegiskenOperasyonSuresi,"
	QStr = QStr & " U.URt_SabitOperasyonSuresi,U.URt_OperasyonSureCarpani,U.URt_Aciklama,U.URt_CalisanSayisi,I.IsM_Aciklama, O.Op_Aciklama"
	QStr = QStr & " from URUN_ROTALARI U"
	QStr = QStr & " left JOIN IS_MERKEZLERI I ON I.IsM_Kodu=U.URt_IsmerkeziveyaGrupKod"
	QStr = QStr & " left JOIN URETIM_OPERASYONLARI O ON O.Op_Kodu=U.URt_OpKod"
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY U.URt_RotaUrunKodu, U.URt_ID"
	Set B01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery_Par("sdgsdsdgh", QStr, par1)

	msgbox B01.GetRowCount()
End Sub

Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	QStr = "SELECT DISTINCT KOD FROM STOK00 S	"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK0U U ON U.EVRAKNO=S.KOD"


'	QStr = QStr & " WHERE S.KOD = '150 121 05200'"
'	QStr = QStr & " WHERE S.SUPPLIERCODE>' ' AND U.EVRAKNO IS NULL"
'	QStr = QStr & " WHERE  U.EVRAKNO IS NULL"


	Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfgdfgsdh", QStr)
	For i = 1 to S00.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & S00.GetRowCount()
		Doc.Load_Voucher S00.KOD(i)
		STOK0U.SafePack
		if STOK0U.GetRowCount() > 0 then
			STOK0U.UCODE(1) = STOK00.SUPPLIERCODE
		else		
			satirNo = STOK0U.AddRow
			STOK0U.UCODE(satirNo) = STOK00.SUPPLIERCODE
		end if
		STOK00.NAME2 = STOK00.SUPPLIERCODE
		Doc.Save_Voucher
	Next
End Sub

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : FORMA_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : HMD-AKTAR
Hangi Tablo için : BOMU01
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Excel 'den Hammaddeleri Aktar
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Open("C:\Users\Planlama1\Desktop\hammaddeler.xls", False, True)
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets(1)
	For i = 3 to 65536
		MamulKodu = Sh1.Cells(i,1).Value
		if trim(MamulKodu) = "" Then Exit For
		if trim(MamulKodu) <> trim(EskiMamulKodu) Then
			if trim(BOMU01E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
			ret = UrunAgaciYukle(MamulKodu)
			satirNo = 0
		End if
		EskiMamulKodu = MamulKodu

		if ret = 0 Then
			satirNo = satirNo + 1
			if satirNo > BOMU01T.GetRowCount() Then
				satirNo = BOMU01T.AddRow
			Else
				BOMU01T.InsertRow(satirNo)
			End if
			BOMU01T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(satirNo) = "H"
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_SIRANO(satirNo) = Sh1.Cells(i,3).Value
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(satirNo) = Sh1.Cells(i,5).Value
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(satirNo) = Sh1.Cells(i,7).Value
		End if
	Next
	if trim(BOMU01E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
End Sub
Function UrunAgaciYukle(StokKodu)
	UrunAgaciYukle = 1
	Set B01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfhdfjsdj", "SELECT EVRAKNO FROM BOMU01E WHERE MAMULCODE='" & StokKodu & "' AND AKTIF_PASIF='*'")
	if B01.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	Doc.Load_Voucher B01.EVRAKNO(1)
	For i = 1 to BOMU01T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i)) = "H" Then
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = ""
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) = ""
		End if
	Next
	BOMU01T.SafePack
	UrunAgaciYukle = 0
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	Set WZD = Doc.GetTableObject("WIZARD_STACK")
	WZD.Addfield2 "EKLE_CIKAR","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	WZD.Setd7FieldEvent "D_ID","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "D_ID_AV"

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("SFDC20", "")


	Set SFDC32O = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32O")
	SFDC32O.Setd7FieldEvent "RECTARIH1","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "S32O_RECTARIH1_AV"
	SFDC32O.Setd7FieldEvent "RECTIME1","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "S32O_RECTIME1_AV"
	SFDC32O.Setd7FieldEvent "RECTARIH2","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "S32O_RECTARIH2_AV"
	SFDC32O.Setd7FieldEvent "RECTIME2","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "S32O_RECTIME2_AV"

End Sub

Sub OperatorEkleCikarMain()
	Set MTable = Doc.CreateTableObject("MAKROTABLE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "BARKOD","CHAR",30,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Setd7FieldEvent "BARKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "BARKOD_AV"

	Call EkrandakiOperatoruEkle()
End Sub
Sub OperatorCikarMain()
	Set WZD = Doc.GetTableObject("WIZARD_STACK")
	WZD.EKLE_CIKAR = "C"
	Call OperatorEkleCikarMain()
End Sub

Sub BARKOD_AV()
	Set WZD = Doc.GetTableObject("WIZARD_STACK")
	if WZD.EKLE_CIKAR = "E" Then
		Call OperatorEkle()
	Elseif WZD.EKLE_CIKAR = "C" Then
		Call OperatorCikar()
	End if
End Sub
Sub OperatorEkle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set SFDC32O = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32O")
	Set SFDC321E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC321E")

	if Left(MTable.BARKOD, 4) <> "%OP%" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Yalnýzca operatör barkodu okutulabilir", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if

	OperatorKodu = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(Mid(MTable.BARKOD, 5)), 16)
	Set P00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfwewg", "SELECT AD FROM PERS00 WHERE KOD='" & OperatorKodu & "'")
	if P00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Personel Kodu geçersiz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set S32O = Doc.Select2("adf2eg2gdsf", "SFDC32O", "OPERATOR='" & OperatorKodu & "'&RECTARIH2='" & Space(10) & "'", "", -1)
	if S32O.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu operatör daha önce okutulmuþ", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if AcikIsVarMi(OperatorKodu, SFDC321E.JOBNO) Then
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if

	SFDC32O.SafePack
	satirNo = SFDC32O.AddRow
	SFDC32O.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
	SFDC32O.OPERATOR = OperatorKodu
	SFDC32O.OPERATOR_PERS00_AD = P00.AD(1)
	SFDC32O.RECTARIH1 = Doc.BuGun
	SFDC32O.RECTIME1 = Doc.Saat(5)

	MTable.BARKOD = ""
End Sub
Sub OperatorCikar()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set SFDC32O = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32O")
	Set SFDC32E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32E")

	if Left(MTable.BARKOD, 4) <> "%OP%" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Yalnýzca operatör barkodu okutulabilir", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if

	OperatorKodu = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(Mid(MTable.BARKOD, 5)), 16)
	Set P00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfwewg", "SELECT AD FROM PERS00 WHERE KOD='" & OperatorKodu & "'")
	if P00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Personel Kodu geçersiz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if

	SFDC32O.SafePack
	Set S32O = Doc.Select2("adf2eg2gdsf", "SFDC32O", "OPERATOR='" & OperatorKodu & "'", "", -1)
	if S32O.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu operatör daha önce okutulmamýþ", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set S32O = Doc.Select2("adf2eg2gdsf", "SFDC32O", "OPERATOR='" & OperatorKodu & "'&RECTIME2='" & Space(5) & "'", "", -1)
	if S32O.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu operatör daha önce çýkýþ yapmýþ.", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if

	satirNo = Doc.Cdbl(S32O.SRNUM(1))
	SFDC32O.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
	SFDC32O.RECTARIH2 = Doc.BuGun
	SFDC32O.RECTIME2 = Doc.Saat(5)
	Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "SFDC32O", "RECTIME2", SFDC32O.GetCurrentRow

	Set S32O = Doc.Select2("adf2eg2gdsf", "SFDC32O", "RECTARIH2='" & Space(10) & "'", "", -1)
	if S32O.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		SFDC32E.D7_AKSIYON = "C"
		SFDC32E.RECTARIH2 = SFDC32O.RECTARIH2
		SFDC32E.RECTIME2 = SFDC32O.RECTIME2
		Doc.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "SFDC32E", "RECTIME2", 1
	End if
	MTable.BARKOD = ""
End Sub

Sub EkrandakiOperatoruEkle()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
	Set SFDC32O = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32O")
	Set WIZARD_STACK = Doc.GetTableObject("WIZARD_STACK")

	OperatorKodu = WIZARD_STACK.OPERATOR
	if trim(OperatorKodu) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Set S32O = Doc.Select2("adf2eg2gdsf", "SFDC32O", "OPERATOR='" & OperatorKodu & "'", "", -1)
	if S32O.GetRowCount() > 0 Then Exit Sub

	Set P00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfwewg", "SELECT AD FROM PERS00 WHERE KOD='" & OperatorKodu & "'")

	SFDC32O.SafePack
	satirNo = SFDC32O.AddRow
	SFDC32O.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
	SFDC32O.OPERATOR = OperatorKodu
	SFDC32O.OPERATOR_PERS00_AD = P00.AD(1)
	SFDC32O.RECTARIH1 = Doc.BuGun
	SFDC32O.RECTIME1 = Doc.Saat(5)
End Sub
Sub OperatorEkleCikarOK()
	Set WIZARD_INFO = Doc.GetTableObject("WIZARD_INFO")
	Set SFDC32E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32E")
	Set SFDC32O = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32O")

	SFDC32E.OPERATOR_SAYISI = SFDC32O.GetRowCount()

	if trim(SFDC32E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
		Doc.Save_Voucher
		Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher
		Doc.RunCommand "WIZARD_CMD_CLEAR"
		WIZARD_INFO.ERRORMSG = ""
	End if
	Doc.RUN_APP_OK
	Doc.SetFormControlProperty "U_SFDC321_WIZARD_01", "WIZARD_CMD_NEXT_01", "READONLY", 1
End Sub
Sub Baslat()
	Set WIZARD_STACK = Doc.GetTableObject("WIZARD_STACK")
	Set SFDC321E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC321E")
	Set SFDC32O = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32O")
	SFDC32O.SafePack
	if SFDC32O.GetRowCount() <= 1 Then
		Doc.RunCommand "WIZARD_CMD_START"
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set S32O = Doc.Select2("afq2eg2egs", "SFDC32O", "", "", -1)
	OperatorKodu = WIZARD_STACK.OPERATOR
	IsNo = SFDC321E.JOBNO

	Doc.RunCommand "WIZARD_CMD_START"

	QStr = "SELECT SFDC32E.EVRAKNO FROM SFDC32E"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN SFDC32O ON SFDC32O.EVRAKNO=SFDC32E.EVRAKNO"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE SFDC32O.OPERATOR='" & OperatorKodu & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND SFDC32E.JOBNO='" & IsNo & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND SFDC32E.RECTARIH2='" & Space(10) & "'"
	
	Set S32E = Doc.RunSQLQuery("asfegsgs", QStr)
	if S32E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	if trim(S32E.EVRAKNO(1)) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Doc.Load_Voucher S32E.EVRAKNO(1)
	For i = 1 to S32O.GetRowCount()
		if trim(S32O.OPERATOR(i)) <> trim(OperatorKodu) Then
			satirNo = SFDC32O.AddRow
			SFDC32O.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			SFDC32O.OPERATOR = S32O.OPERATOR(i)
		End if
		SFDC32O.RECTARIH1 = S32O.RECTARIH1(i)
		SFDC32O.RECTIME1 = S32O.RECTIME1(i)
	Next
	Doc.Save_Voucher
	Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	Doc.SetFormControlProperty "U_SFDC321_WIZARD_01", "WIZARD_CMD_NEXT_01", "READONLY", 1
End Sub

Sub OP_BARKOD_AV()
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE_OP")
	if trim(MTable.BARKOD) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Set SFDC321E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC321E")
	Set WIZARD_STACK = Doc.GetTableObject("WIZARD_STACK")

	if Left(MTable.BARKOD, 4) <> "%OP%" AND Left(MTable.BARKOD, 4) <> "%TZ%" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Yalnýzca tezgah ve operatör barkodlarý okutulmalý", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		MTable.BARKOD = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if Left(MTable.BARKOD, 4) = "%OP%" Then
		OperatorKodu = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(Mid(MTable.BARKOD, 5)), 16)
		Set P00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfwewg", "SELECT AD FROM PERS00 WHERE KOD='" & OperatorKodu & "'")
		if P00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Personel Kodu geçersiz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
			MTable.BARKOD = ""
			Exit Sub
		End if
		WIZARD_STACK.OPERATOR = OperatorKodu
		WIZARD_STACK.OPERATOR_AD	= P00.AD
	Elseif Left(MTable.BARKOD, 4) = "%TZ%" Then
		TezgahKodu = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(Mid(MTable.BARKOD, 5)), 24)
		Set I00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfwewg", "SELECT AD FROM IMLT00 WHERE KOD='" & TezgahKodu & "'")
		if I00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Tezgah Kodu geçersiz", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
			MTable.BARKOD = ""
			Exit Sub
		End if
		SFDC321E.X_T_ISMERKEZI = TezgahKodu
		SFDC321E.X_T_ISMERKEZI_IMLT00_AD = I00.AD
	End if
	MTable.BARKOD = ""
	if trim(SFDC321E.X_T_ISMERKEZI) <> "" and trim(WIZARD_STACK.OPERATOR) <> "" Then
		Set S32D1 = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("SFDC32D1=SFDC32D(NOFILE)", "IO")
		Dim Par
		Set Par=Nothing
		Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "DURUS_LIST",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par
		Set SFDC32D = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32D")
		SFDC32D.Refresh
	End if
End Sub
Sub cmdTemizle()
	Set SFDC321E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC321E")
	Set WIZARD_STACK = Doc.GetTableObject("WIZARD_STACK")

	SFDC321E.Empty
	WIZARD_STACK.Empty
	Doc.RunCommand "WIZARD_CMD_CLEAR"
End Sub
Sub DurusOk()
	Set SFDC32D1 = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32D1")
	Set SFDC321E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC321E")
	Set WIZARD_STACK = Doc.GetTableObject("WIZARD_STACK")

	if DurusSuresiGirilmemis Then Exit Sub

	For i = 1 to SFDC32D1.GetRowCount()
		if trim(SFDC32D1.DURMA_SEBEBI(i)) <> "" Then
			if Not DocYaratildi Then
				Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("SFDC20", "")
				Set S20T = Doc1.GetTableObject("SFDC20T")
				Doc1.New_Voucher
				S20T.Empty
				DocYaratildi = True
			End if

			satirNo = S20T.AddRow
			S20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			S20T.KOD(satirNo) = SFDC321E.X_T_ISMERKEZI
			S20T.OPERATOR_1(satirNo) = WIZARD_STACK.OPERATOR
			S20T.RECTARIH1(satirNo) = Doc.BuGun
			S20T.RECTIME1(satirNo) = Doc.Saat(5)
			S20T.D7_ISLEM_KODU(satirNo) = "D"
			S20T.DURMA_SEBEBI(satirNo) = SFDC32D1.DURMA_SEBEBI(i)
			S20T.DAKIKA(satirNo) = SFDC32D1.DAKIKA(i)
			Doc1.CallFieldAfterValidate_Row "SFDC20T", "DAKIKA", satirNo
		End if
	Next
	if DocYaratildi Then
		Doc1.Save_Voucher
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	End if
	SFDC321E.Empty
	WIZARD_STACK.Empty
	Doc.RUN_APP_OK
	Doc.RUN_APP_OK
End Sub
Function AcikIsVarMi(OperatorKodu, IsNo)
	AcikIsVarMi = False
	if Doc.GetdocType0 <> "SFDC321" Then Exit Function

	if trim(OperatorKodu) = "" or trim(IsNo) = "" Then Exit Function

	QStr = "RECTARIH1>='2012/07/30'&OPERATOR='" & OperatorKodu & "'&RECTARIH2='" & Space(10) & "'&JOBNO<>'" & Doc.RightJustify(trim(IsNo),16) & "'"
	Set S32O = Doc.Select1("sdgaerhasdh", "SFDC32O", QStr, "", -1)

	if S32O.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Doc.SetDocParametervalue "BATCHERRORMODE_HEADER_MESSAGE", "Bu operatöre ait açýk iþler var : "
		Set dd = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

		For i = 1 to S32O.GetRowCount()
			OpKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10T", "R_OPERASYON", S32O.JOBNO(i), "JOBNO")
			Operasyon = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT01", "AD", OpKodu, "")
			Doc.d7MsgBox "MPS No : " & trim(S32O.MPSNO(i)) & ", Operasyon : " & trim(Operasyon) & ", Fiþ No : " & trim(S32O.EVRAKNO(i)), "", MB_OK
		Next
		'Set dd = Nothing
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Önceki iþler kapatýlmadan bu iþe bildirim yapýlamaz", "", MB_OK
		AcikIsVarMi = True
	End if
End Function
Sub D_ID_AV()
	if Doc.IsBlank(SFDC321E) Then Set SFDC321E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC321E")
	if Doc.IsBlank(WIZARD_STACK) Then Set WIZARD_STACK = Doc.GetTableObject("WIZARD_STACK")

	if AcikIsVarMi(WIZARD_STACK.OPERATOR, SFDC321E.JOBNO) Then
		Doc.RunCommand "WIZARD_CMD_CLEAR"
	End if
End Sub
Function DurusSuresiGirilmemis()
	DurusSuresiGirilmemis = False
	Set S32D = Doc.Select2("sdfqehh", "SFDC32D1", "DURMA_SEBEBI>'" & Space(4) & "'&SURE=0", "", -1)
	if S32D.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Duruþ süresi girilmemiþ", "Ýþlem kesildi", MB_OK
		DurusSuresiGirilmemis = True
	End if
End Function
Sub OperatorSuresiHesapla()
	Set SFDC32O = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32O")
	satirNo = SFDC32O.GetCurrentRow
	SFDC32O.SURE = GetOperatorSure(satirNo)
	SFDC32O.DAKIKA = SFDC32O.SURE
End Sub
Function GetOperatorSure(satirNo)
	Set SFDC32O = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32O")
	MolaSuresi = GetPDurusSure(SFDC32O.RECTARIH1(satirNo), SFDC32O.RECTIME1(satirNo), SFDC32O.RECTARIH2(satirNo), SFDC32O.RECTIME2(satirNo))
	GetOperatorSure = Doc.DTDTDif1(SFDC32O.RECTARIH1(satirNo), SFDC32O.RECTIME1(satirNo), SFDC32O.RECTARIH2(satirNo), SFDC32O.RECTIME2(satirNo)) - MolaSuresi
End Function
Function GetPDurusSure(BTarih, BSaat, ETarih, ESaat)
'	Set KK = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	MolaArray = GetMolaArray()

	GunSayisi = Doc.CDateToInt(ETarih) - Doc.CDateToInt(BTarih)
	MolaSure = 0
	For i = 0 to uBound(MolaArray, 1)
		For j = 0 to GunSayisi
			if j < GunSayisi Then
				BaslangicSaat = BSaat
				BitisSaat = "23:59"
			Elseif j > 0 Then
				BaslangicSaat = "00:00"
				BitisSaat = ESaat
			Else
				BaslangicSaat = BSaat
				BitisSaat = ESaat
			End if
			Gun = Doc.StrDate_Increment(BTarih, j)
'			Doc.d7MsgBox i &  ", " & j &  ", " & Gun &  ", " & BaslangicSaat &  ", " & BitisSaat & ", " & MolaArray(i,0) & _
'					 ", " & MolaArray(i,1), "", MB_OK
			if not(Gun & BitisSaat < Gun & MolaArray(i,0) or Gun & BaslangicSaat > Gun & MolaArray(i,1)) Then
				if Gun & MolaArray(i,0) >= Gun & BaslangicSaat and Gun & MolaArray(i,1) <= Gun & BitisSaat Then
					MolaSure = MolaSure + Doc.DTDTDif1(Gun, MolaArray(i,0), Gun, MolaArray(i,1))
				Elseif Gun & MolaArray(i,0) >= Gun & BaslangicSaat and Gun & MolaArray(i,1) > Gun & BitisSaat Then
					MolaSure = MolaSure + Doc.DTDTDif1(Gun, MolaArray(i,0), Gun, BitisSaat)
				Elseif Gun & MolaArray(i,0) <= Gun & BaslangicSaat and Gun & MolaArray(i,1) <= Gun & BitisSaat Then
					MolaSure = MolaSure + Doc.DTDTDif1(Gun, BaslangicSaat, Gun, MolaArray(i,1))
				End if
			End if
		Next
	Next
	GetPDurusSure = MolaSure
End Function
Function GetMolaArray()
	Set SFDC32E = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32E")
	Gk_1 = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("IMLT00", "GK_1", SFDC32E.X_T_ISMERKEZI, "")
	if trim(Gk_1) = "MNT" Then
		GetMolaArray = GetMontajMolaArray()
	Else
		GetMolaArray = GetTalasliImalatMolaArray()
	End if
End Function
Function GetMontajMolaArray()
	Dim MolaArray(3,1)
	MolaArray(0,0) = "10:00"
	MolaArray(0,1) = "10:15"
	MolaArray(1,0) = "12:00"
	MolaArray(1,1) = "12:30"
	MolaArray(2,0) = "15:00"
	MolaArray(2,1) = "15:15"
	MolaArray(3,0) = "18:00"
	MolaArray(3,1) = "18:30"

	GetMontajMolaArray = MolaArray
End Function
Function GetTalasliImalatMolaArray()
	Dim MolaArray(3,1)
	MolaArray(0,0) = "10:00"
	MolaArray(0,1) = "10:15"
	MolaArray(1,0) = "12:30"
	MolaArray(1,1) = "13:00"
	MolaArray(2,0) = "15:00"
	MolaArray(2,1) = "15:15"
	MolaArray(3,0) = "18:00"
	MolaArray(3,1) = "18:30"

	GetTalasliImalatMolaArray = MolaArray
End Function

Sub DurusBaslat()
	Set S32D = Doc.GetTableObject("SFDC32D")
	S32D.SafePack

	Set S32D1 = Doc.Select2("sdawrash", "SFDC32D", "RECTARIH1<>'" & Space(10) & "'&RECTARIH2='" & Space(10) & "'", "", -1)
	if S32D1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu iþ için " & Doc.StrToNorDate(S32D1.RECTARIH1) & " " & S32D1.RECTIME1 & " 'de " & _
				trim(S32D1.DURMA_SEBEBI) & " kodlu duruþ baþlatýlmýþ.", "Kapatýlmamýþ Duruþ Var", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set MTableD = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLED")

	if trim(SFDC32E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
		S32D.SafePack
		satirNo = S32D.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S32D.DURMA_SEBEBI(satirNo) = MTableD.DURMA_SEBEBI
		S32D.RECTARIH1(satirNo) = MTableD.RECTARIH1
		S32D.RECTIME1(satirNo) = MTableD.RECTIME1
		Doc.Save_Vocher
		Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher

		Doc.RunCommand "WIZARD_CMD_CLEAR"
		WIZARD_INFO.ERRORMSG = ""
	End if
	Doc.RUN_APP_OK
	Doc.SetFormControlProperty "U_SFDC321_WIZARD_01", "WIZARD_CMD_NEXT_01", "READONLY", 1
End Sub
Sub DURMA_SEBEBI_AV()
	Set MTableD = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLED")
	MTableD.DURMA_SEBEBI_AD = Doc.GetGecoValue("DURMAS", "AD", MTableD.DURMA_SEBEBI, -1)
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu

Hangi Tablo için : 
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için :

Sub Makro1()
	Set STOK00_1 = Doc.CreateTableObject("STOK00_1=STOK00(NOFILE)", "IO")
	STOK00_1.Setd7FieldEvent "KOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "KOD_AV"

	Set STOK0P = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("STOK0P=STOK0P(NOFILE)", "IO")
	Set ATCDOC = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("ATCDOC=ATCDOC(NOFILE)", "IO")
End Sub
Sub KOD_AV()
	Set STOK00_1 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK00_1")
	if trim(STOK00_1.KOD) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfgsdfjsj", "SELECT KOD, AD, IUNIT, CODETYPE, NAME2 FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & STOK00_1.KOD & "'")
	if S00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then 
		STOK00_1.KOD = S00.KOD
		STOK00_1.AD = S00.AD
		STOK00_1.CODETYPE = S00.CODETYPE
		STOK00_1.IUNIT = S00.IUNIT
		Call ResimleriYukle()
		Exit Sub
	End if

	StokKodu = GetFreedomStokKodu(STOK00_1.KOD)
	if trim(StokKodu) <> "" Then
		Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfgsdfjsj", "SELECT KOD, AD, IUNIT, CODETYPE, NAME2 FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & StokKodu & "'")
		STOK00_1.KOD = S00.KOD
		STOK00_1.AD = S00.AD
		STOK00_1.CODETYPE = S00.CODETYPE
		STOK00_1.IUNIT = S00.IUNIT
		Call ResimleriYukle()
		Exit Sub
	End if

	QStr = "SELECT S.KOD, S.AD, S.IUNIT, S.CODETYPE, S.NAME2 FROM D7KIDSLB D"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S ON S.KOD=D.SKOD"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE D.KOD='" & STOK00_1.KOD & "'"

	Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("asdgaerhaj", QStr)
	if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Stok Kodu/ Barkod", "", MB_OK
		STOK00_1.KOD = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if

	STOK00_1.KOD = S00.KOD
	STOK00_1.AD = S00.AD
	STOK00_1.CODETYPE = S00.CODETYPE
	STOK00_1.IUNIT = S00.IUNIT
	Call ResimleriYukle()
End Sub
Sub ResimleriYukle()
	Set STOK00_1 = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK00_1")
	Set STOK0P = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK0P")
	Set ATCDOC = Doc.GetTableObject("ATCDOC")
	STOK0P.Empty
	ATCDOC.Empty

	Set S0P = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dsfgsdfjs", "SELECT * FROM STOK0P WHERE EVRAKNO='" & STOK00_1.KOD & "'")
	For i = 1 to S0P.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = STOK0P.AddRow
		satirNo = ATCDOC.AddRow
		'STOK0P.FULLFILENAME1(satirNo) = S0P.FULLFILENAME1(i)
		ATCDOC.FULLFILENAME(satirNo) = S0P.FULLFILENAME1(i)
	Next

	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")

	StokKodu = GetParcaStokKodu(STOK00_1.KOD)
	if trim(StokKodu) = "" Then Exit Sub

	BazKlasor = "\\SERVER\tasarým_ortak\TASARIM_DOKUMANLARI\"
	DosyaAdi = BazKlasor & trim(StokKodu) & ".JPG"

	if fso.FileExists(DosyaAdi) Then
		satirNo = STOK0P.AddRow
		satirNo = ATCDOC.AddRow
'		STOK0P.FULLFILENAME1(satirNo) = DosyaAdi
		ATCDOC.FULLFILENAME(satirNo) = DosyaAdi
	End if

	For i = 0 to 10
		DosyaAdi = BazKlasor & trim(StokKodu) & "_" & i & ".JPG"

		if fso.FileExists(DosyaAdi) Then
			satirNo = STOK0P.AddRow
			satirNo = ATCDOC.AddRow
	'		STOK0P.FULLFILENAME1(satirNo) = DosyaAdi
			ATCDOC.FULLFILENAME(satirNo) = DosyaAdi
		End if
	Next
End Sub

Function GetParcaStokKodu(MamulKodu)
	GetParcaStokKodu = ""
	QStr = "SELECT SUPPLIERCODE FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & MamulKodu & "'"
	Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dxfhadfs", QStr)
	if S00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetParcaStokKodu = S00.SUPPLIERCODE(1)
	Else
		GetParcaStokKodu = MamulStokKodu
	End if
End Function

Function GetFreedomStokKodu(MamulKodu)
	GetFreedomStokKodu = ""
	QStr = "SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & MamulKodu & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " UNION ALL"
	QStr = QStr & " SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE SUPPLIERCODE='" & MamulKodu & "'"

	Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dxfhadfs", QStr)
	if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	GetFreedomStokKodu = S00.KOD(1)
End Function
