----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : FORMA_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : SURE
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Call KaynakTarihleriniHesapla()
End Sub
Sub KaynakTarihleriniHesapla()
	if Left(MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, 3) <> "152" Then Exit Sub
	Call OperasyonTarihleriniHesapla()
	Call HammaddeIhtiyacTarihleriniHesapla()
End Sub
Sub HammaddeIhtiyacTarihleriniHesapla()
	Set M10TH = Doc.Select2("sdsdgash", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'", "SRNUM", -1)
	Call MontajSiralariniHesapla(M10TH)

	MaxMontajSiraNo = M10TH.R_HM_BAKIYE(1)
	For i = 1 to M10TH.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = Doc.Cdbl(M10TH.SRNUM(i))
		if M10TH.R_HM_BAKIYE(i) = MaxMontajSiraNo Then
			BitisTarihi = GetIlkOpBaslangicTarihi()
		Else
			BitisTarihi = GetOncekiMontajBaslanicTarihi(M10TH, i)
		End if
		UretimSuresi = GetYMUretimSuresi(M10TH.R_KAYNAKKODU(i), M10TH.R_MIKTAR0(i))
		BaslangicTarihi = GetBaslangicTarihi(BitisTarihi, UretimSuresi)

		M10TH.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(i) = BaslangicTarihi
		MMPS10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(satirNo) = BaslangicTarihi
'		MMPS10T.R_HM_BAKIYE(satirNo) = M10TH.R_HM_BAKIYE(i)
	Next
	Doc.RunCommand "CMD_HESAPLANMIS_1"
End Sub
Sub MontajSiralariniHesapla(M10TH)
	if M10TH.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	QStr = "SELECT BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE, REFNUM01"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM BOMU01T "
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE BOMREC_CODE='" & M10TH.R_MAMULKODU(1) & "' AND AKTIF_PASIF='*' AND BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'"
	Set B01T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dferhshsh", QStr)

	For i = 1 to M10TH.GetRowCount()
		Set B01T1 = Doc.Select2("sfasdfhad", B01T.GetAppName, "BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE='" & M10TH.R_KAYNAKKODU(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if B01T1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			M10TH.R_HM_BAKIYE(i) = B01T1.REFNUM01(1)
		Else
			M10TH.R_HM_BAKIYE(i) = 0
		End if
	Next
	M10TH.Sort("<R_HM_BAKIYE")
End Sub
Function GetIhtiyacTarihi(HammaddeKodu, MPSTeslimTarihi)
	GetIhtiyacTarihi = MPSTeslimTarihi
	Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfsds", "SELECT B_HAZIRLIKSURESI FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & HammaddeKodu & "'")
	if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	if S00.B_HAZIRLIKSURESI(1) = 0 Then Exit Function

	GetIhtiyacTarihi = GetIsGunu(Doc.StrDate_Increment(MPSTeslimTarihi, -1 * S00.B_HAZIRLIKSURESI(1)))
End Function
Function GetIsGunu(Tarih)
	GetIsGunu = Tarih
	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	d7Date.SetDate Tarih
	if d7Date.GetDOW > 5 Then
		d7Date.Increment (d7Date.GetDOW - 7)
		GetIsGunu = d7Date.GetRevDate
	End if
End Function
Sub OperasyonTarihleriniHesapla()
	Set M10TI = Doc.Select2("sdsdgash", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "<SRNUM", -1)
	For i = 1 to M10TI.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = Doc.Cdbl(M10TI.SRNUM(i))
		if i = 1 Then
			MMPS10T.R_LATEST_E_TARIH(satirNo) = MMPS10E.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH
		Else
			MMPS10T.R_LATEST_E_TARIH(satirNo) = M10TI.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(i-1)
		End if
		IslemSuresiGun = GetSureGun(M10TI.R_KAYNAKKODU(i), M10TI.R_MIKTART(i))
		M10TI.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(i) = GetBaslangicTarihi(MMPS10T.R_LATEST_E_TARIH(satirNo), IslemSuresiGun)
		MMPS10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(satirNo) = M10TI.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(i)
	Next
End Sub
Function GetBaslangicTarihi(BitisTarihi, UretimSuresi)
	BaslangicTarihi = Doc.StrDate_Increment(BitisTarihi, -1 * UretimSuresi)
	TatilGunSayisi = GetTatilGunSayisi(BaslangicTarihi, BitisTarihi)

	GetBaslangicTarihi = GetIsGunu(Doc.StrDate_Increment(BaslangicTarihi, -1 * TatilGunSayisi))
End Function
Function GetSureGun(TezgahKodu, SureSaat)
	GetSureGun = 0
	Set I00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfgsdfgs", "SELECT B_KAPASITE, B_KAPASITE_PERMPS FROM IMLT00 WHERE KOD='" & TezgahKodu & "'")
	if I00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	GunlukKapasite = I00.B_KAPASITE_PERMPS
	if GunlukKapasite = 0 Then GunlukKapasite = I00.B_KAPASITE
	if GunlukKapasite = 0 Then Exit Function

	GetSureGun = Doc.Ceil(Doc.Div(SureSaat, GunlukKapasite))
End Function
Function GetTatilGunSayisi(BaslangicTarihi, ByVal BitisTarihi)
	GetTatilGunSayisi = 0
	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	d7Date.SetDate BitisTarihi
	if Not d7Date.IsValid Then Exit Function
	BitisTarihi = d7Date.GetRevDate

	d7Date.SetDate BaslangicTarihi
	if Not d7Date.IsValid Then Exit Function

	While d7Date.GetRevDate <= BitisTarihi
		if d7Date.GetDOW > 5 Then
			GetTatilGunSayisi = GetTatilGunSayisi + 1
		End if
		d7Date.Increment 1
	Wend
End Function
Function GetIlkOpBaslangicTarihi()
	Set M10TI1 = Doc.Select2("sdfgsdfhsh", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='I'", "SRNUM", -1)
	if M10TI1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetIlkOpBaslangicTarihi = M10TI1.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(1)
	Else
		GetIlkOpBaslangicTarihi = MMPS10E.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH
	End if
End Function
Function GetYMUretimSuresi(YMKodu, Miktar)
	GetYMUretimSuresi = 0
	if trim(YMKodu) = "" Then Exit Function
	if Doc.Cdbl(Miktar) = 0 Then Exit Function

	QStr = "SELECT BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE, (" & Doc.Cdbl(Miktar) & "*BOMREC_KAYNAK0/BOMREC_MAMULMIKTAR + BOMREC_KAYNAK1) SURE"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM BOMU01T"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE BOMREC_CODE='" & YMKodu & "' AND BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='I' AND BOMREC_ALTTURU<'1' AND AKTIF_PASIF='*'"
	Set B01T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("asdferuqsgsj", QStr)
	For i = 1 to B01T.GetRowCount()
		GetYMUretimSuresi = GetYMUretimSuresi + GetSureGun(B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), B01T.SURE(i))
	Next
End Function
Function GetOncekiMontajBaslanicTarihi(M10TH, satirNo)
	GetOncekiMontajBaslanicTarihi = ""
	MontajSirasi = M10TH.R_HM_BAKIYE(satirNo)

	Set M10TH1 = Doc.Select2("sdfsdfhsh", M10TH.GetAppName, "R_HM_BAKIYE>" & MontajSirasi, "R_HM_BAKIYE+R_LATEST_B_TARIH", -1)
	if M10TH1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetOncekiMontajBaslanicTarihi = M10TH1.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(1)
	End if
End Function

Const cnnStr = "Provider=SQLNCLI;Data Source=pdmserver;Initial catalog=test;User Id=erp;Password=Freedom2010;"

Sub Makro1()
	Set ffff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	QStr = "SELECT X.BomDocumentID, DK.MAMUL_DOC_ID, X.ROWNO, D.Filename, D.LockPath, MAX(ITEM_NO) ITEM_NO, (MAX(K1) + ' ' + MAX(K2) + ' ' + MAX(K3)+MAX(K4)) PART_NUMBER, "
	QStr = QStr & " MAX(DESCRIPTION1) DESCRIPTION1, MAX(QTY) QTY, DK.STOK_KODU"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM ("
	QStr = QStr & " SELECT V.BomDocumentID, V.RowNo,"
	QStr = QStr & " 	(CASE WHEN ColumnNo=0 THEN ValueText ELSE ' ' END) ITEM_NO,"
	QStr = QStr & " 	(CASE WHEN ColumnNo=4 THEN ValueText ELSE ' ' END) K1,"
	QStr = QStr & " 	(CASE WHEN ColumnNo=5 THEN ValueText ELSE ' ' END) K2,"
	QStr = QStr & " 	(CASE WHEN ColumnNo=6 THEN ValueText ELSE ' ' END) K3,"
	QStr = QStr & " 	(CASE WHEN ColumnNo=7 THEN ValueText ELSE ' ' END) K4,"
	QStr = QStr & " 	(CASE WHEN ColumnNo=2 THEN ValueText ELSE ' ' END) DESCRIPTION1,"
	QStr = QStr & "     (CASE WHEN ColumnNo=1 THEN ValueText ELSE ' ' END) QTY"
	QStr = QStr & "   FROM [TEST].[dbo].[BomSheetValue] V"
	QStr = QStr & "   LEFT JOIN [TEST].[dbo].[BomSheetRow] R ON R.RowNo=V.RowNo"
	QStr = QStr & "   ) X"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN [TEST].[dbo].[BomSheets] E ON E.BomDocumentID=X.BomDocumentID"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN [TEST].[dbo].[Documents] D ON D.DocumentID=E.SourceDocumentID AND E.SourceDocumentVersion=D.LatestRevisionNo"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN [TEST].[dbo].DOC_KOD2 DK ON D.DocumentID=DK.MAMUL_DOC_ID"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE 1=1 "
	QStr = QStr & " AND D.Deleted<>1"
	QStr = QStr & " AND DK.STOK_KODU LIKE '15%'"
	QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY X.BomDocumentID, X.ROWNO, D.Filename, D.LockPath, DK.STOK_KODU, DK.MAMUL_DOC_ID"
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY DK.STOK_KODU"

	Set Par1 = Doc.CreateSObject("PARAMETERS")
	Par1.SetParameter "REPORT_CONNECTION_STRING", CnnStr
	Set BomMamul = Doc.RunSQLQuery_Par("_DDDD_2333",QStr,Par1)
	if BomTableKontrolHata(BomMamul) Then
		Doc.RunMacro1 "MAKR7S.EXCELOUT", "sendTableToExcel", BomMamul
		exit sub
	End if
'	Set BomMamul = Doc.Select2G("sdgaerhaj", BomTable.GetAppName, "STOK_KODU>'15'&STOK_KODU<'15zzzzz'", "", "STOK_KODU", -1)

	For i = 1 to BomMamul.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Ürün Aðaçlarý Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & BomMamul.GetRowCount()
		if trim(BOMU01E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
			Doc.Save_Voucher
			Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher
		End if
		Set B1 = Doc.Select2("sdgehaj", BomTable.GetAppName, "STOK_KODU='" & BomMamul.STOK_KODU(i) & "'", "", -1)
'		if Aktarilabilir(B1) Then
			Call UrunAgaciYoksaYarat(BomMamul.STOK_KODU(i))
			For j = 1 to B1.GetRowCount()
				satirNo = BOMU01T.AddRow()
				BOMU01T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(satirNo) = "H"
				BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(satirNo) = B1.PART_NUMBER(j)
				BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(satirNo) = Doc.Cdbl(B1.QTY(j))
			Next
'		End if
	Next
	if trim(BOMU01E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
End Sub
Function GetMamulKoduFromFileName(ByVal DosyaAdi)
	DosyaAdi = trim(DosyaAdi)
	GetMamulKoduFromFileName = ""
	SonEkPos = InStr(1, trim(DosyaAdi), ".SLD")
	If SonEkPos = 0 Then Exit Function
	GetMamulKoduFromFileName = Left(DosyaAdi, SonEkPos - 1)
End Function
Function GetFreedomStokKodu(MamulKodu)
	GetFreedomStokKodu = ""
	QStr = "SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & MamulKodu & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " UNION ALL"
	QStr = QStr & " SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE SUPPLIERCODE='" & MamulKodu & "'"

	Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dxfhadfs", QStr)
	if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	GetFreedomStokKodu = S00.KOD(1)
End Function
Sub UrunAgaciYoksaYarat(FrMamulKodu)
	Set B01E = Doc.RunSQLQuery("zsdghd", "SELECT EVRAKNO FROM BOMU01E WHERE MAMULCODE='" & FrMamulKodu & "' AND AKTIF_PASIF='*'")
	if B01E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.New_Voucher
		BOMU01E.MAMULCODE = FrMamulKodu
		BOMU01E.MAMULMIKTAR = 1
		BOMU01E.AKTIF_PASIF = "*"
	Else
		Doc.Load_Voucher B01E.EVRAKNO(1)
		For i = 1 to BOMU01T.GetRowCount()
			if BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "H" Then
				BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = ""
				BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) = ""
			End if
		Next
		BOMU01T.SafePack
	End if
End Sub
Function Aktarilabilir(B1)
	Aktarilabilir = False
	MamulKodu = B1.STOK_KODU(1)
	Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdaefha", "SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & MamulKodu & "'")
	if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & trim(MamulKodu) & " stok kartý tanýmsýz.", "", MB_OK
		Exit Function
	End if

	For i = 1 to B1.GetRowCount()
		Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdaefha", "SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & B1.PART_NUMBER(i) & "'")
		if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Stok Kodu : " & trim(B1.PART_NUMBER(i)) & " stok kartý tanýmsýz.", "", MB_OK
			Exit Function
		End if
	Next
	Aktarilabilir = True
End Function
Function BomTableKontrolHata(BomTable)
	BomTableKontrolHata = False
	For i = 1 to BomTable.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Stok Kartlarý Kontrol Ediliyor : " & i & " / " & BomTable.GetRowCount()
		if BomTable.STOK_KODU(i) <> EskiMamulKodu Then
			if trim(BomTable.STOK_KODU(i)) = "" Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox "Dosya Adý : " & trim(BomTable.Filename(i)) & ", PDM 'de Custom Properties alanlarý doldurulmamýþ", "", MB_OK
				BomTableKontrolHata = True
			Else
				QStr = "SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & BomTable.STOK_KODU(i) & "'"
				Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfsadfadj", QStr)
				if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
					Doc.d7MsgBox "Dosya Adý : " & trim(BomTable.Filename(i)) &  ", Stok Kodu : " & trim(BomTable.STOK_KODU(i)) & ", stok kodu bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
					BomTableKontrolHata = True
				End if
			End if
		End if
		EskiMamulKodu = BomTable.STOK_KODU(i)
		if trim(BomTable.PART_NUMBER(i)) = "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Dosya Adý : " & trim(BomTable.Filename(i)) &  ", Mamul Kodu : " & trim(BomTable.STOK_KODU(i)) & ", Parça Tanýmý : " & trim(BomTable.DESCRIPTION1(i)) & ", PDM 'de Stok kodu girilmemiþ.", "", MB_OK
			BomTableKontrolHata = True
		Else
			QStr = "SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & BomTable.STOK_KODU(i) & "'"
			Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfsadfadj", QStr)
			if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox "Dosya Adý : " & trim(BomTable.Filename(i)) &  ", Mamul Kodu : " & trim(BomTable.STOK_KODU(i)) & ", Parça No : " & trim(BomTable.PART_NUMBER(i)) & " stok kodu bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
				BomTableKontrolHata = True
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Function

Const cnnStr = "Provider=SQLNCLI;Data Source=pdmserver;Initial catalog=PDMWE_2010;User Id=erp;Password=Freedom2010;"

Sub Makro1()
	QStr = "SELECT D.Filename, V.[VariableID], V1.VariableName"
	QStr = QStr & " ,V.[DocumentID]"
	QStr = QStr & " ,[ProjectID]"
	QStr = QStr & " ,[RevisionNo]"
	QStr = QStr & " ,[ConfigurationID]"
	QStr = QStr & " ,[ValueText]"
	QStr = QStr & " ,[ValueInt]"
	QStr = QStr & " ,[ValueFloat]"
	QStr = QStr & " ,[ValueDate]"
	QStr = QStr & " ,[ValueCache]"
	QStr = QStr & " ,[IsLongText]"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM [PDMWE_2010].[dbo].[VariableValue] V"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN [PDMWE_2010].[dbo].[Documents] D ON D.DocumentID=V.DocumentID"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN [PDMWE_2010].[dbo].[Variable] V1 ON V1.VariableID=V.VariableID"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE D.Filename like '%P.000001%' and D.Filename not like '%Copy%'"

	Set Par1 = Doc.CreateSObject("PARAMETERS")
	Par1.SetParameter "REPORT_CONNECTION_STRING", CnnStr
	'Set tbl1 = Doc.RunSQLQuery_Par("_DDDD_2333",QStr,Par1)
'	msgbox tbl1.GetRowCount()


	Set Oconn = CreateObject("ADODB.Connection")
	Oconn.ConnectionString = cnnStr
	Oconn.Open

	StokKodu1 = "P.004732"
'	VariableId = 109
'	Deger = "152"

	QStr = "SELECT KOD, SUPPLIERCODE FROM STOK00 WHERE TRIM(SUPPLIERCODE) IS NOT NULL"
'	QStr = QStr & " AND SUPPLIERCODE='" & StokKodu1 & "'"

	Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("asdhasd", QStr)
	For i = 1 to S00.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & S00.GetRowCount()
		EskiStokKodu = S00.SUPPLIERCODE(i)

		QStr1 = "SELECT d.DocumentID,d.Filename, R.RevNr, R.ActiveConfigurationID"
		QStr1 = QStr1 & " FROM [PDMWE_2010].[dbo].[Documents] d"
		QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN [PDMWE_2010].[dbo].[Revisions] R ON R.DocumentID=D.DocumentID"
		QStr1 = QStr1 & " GROUP BY d.DocumentID,d.Filename, R.RevNr, R.ActiveConfigurationID"

		'QStr1 = "SELECT DISTINCT documentid FROM FROM [PDMWE_2010].[dbo].[Documents] D"
		'QStr1 = QStr1 & " WHERE D.Filename like '%" & trim(EskiStokKodu) & "%' and D.Filename not like '%Copy%')"

		Arr1 = CreateArray(S00.KOD(i))
		For j = 0 to UBound(Arr1, 1)
			VariableId = Arr1(j,0)
			Deger = Arr1(j,1)
			if trim(Deger) <> "" Then
				Call UpdateTable(Oconn, trim(EskiStokKodu), VariableId, Deger)
			End if
		Next
	Next
End Sub
Sub UpdateTable(Oconn, StokKodu, VariableId, Deger)
	Set cmd = CreateObject("ADODB.Command")
	Set cmd.ActiveConnection = Oconn
	cmd.Prepared = True

	QStr = "UPDATE [PDMWE_2010].[dbo].[VariableValue]"
	QStr = QStr & " SET valuetext=?, valueint=?, valuefloat=?"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE documentid in (SELECT documentid FROM [PDMWE_2010].[dbo].[Documents] D"
	QStr = QStr & "										WHERE D.Filename like '%" & trim(StokKodu) & "%' and D.Filename not like '%Copy%')"
	QStr = QStr & " and variableid=" & VariableId

	cmd.CommandText = QStr

	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("valuetext", adVarChar, adParamInput, -1, Deger)
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("valueint", adInteger, adParamInput, 10, Doc.Cdbl(Deger))
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("ValueFloat", adDouble, adParamInput, 10, Doc.Cdbl(Deger))

	Set ret = cmd.Execute(, , adExecuteNoRecords)
	Set cmd = Nothing
End Sub
Sub InsertTable(Oconn, StokKodu, VariableId, Deger)
	Set cmd = CreateObject("ADODB.Command")
	Set cmd.ActiveConnection = Oconn
	cmd.Prepared = True

	QStr = "INSERT INTO [PDMWE_2010].[dbo].[VariableValue] (VariableID,DocumentID,ProjectID,RevisionNo,ConfigurationID,ValueText,ValueInt,ValueFloat,ValueDate,ValueCache,IsLongText)"
	QStr = QStr & " VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)"

	cmd.CommandText = QStr

	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("VariableID", adInteger, adParamInput, 10, VariableID)
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("DocumentID", adInteger, adParamInput, 10, DocumentID)
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("ProjectID", adInteger, adParamInput, 10, ProjectID)
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("RevisionNo", adInteger, adParamInput, 10, RevisionNo)
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("ConfigurationID", adInteger, adParamInput, 10, ConfigurationID)
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("valuetext", adVarChar, adParamInput, -1, Deger)
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("valueint", adDouble, adParamInput, 10, Doc.Cdbl(Deger))
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("ValueFloat", adDouble, adParamInput, 10, Doc.Cdbl(Deger))
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("ValueCache", adVarChar, adParamInput, -1, Deger)
	cmd.Parameters.Append cmd.CreateParameter("IsLongText", adSmallInt, adParamInput, 10, 0)

	Set ret = cmd.Execute(, , adExecuteNoRecords)
	Set cmd = Nothing
End Sub

Function CreateArray(ByVal StokKodu)
	StokKodu = Trim(StokKodu)
  Dim Arr1(3, 1)
  Arr1(0, 0) = 109
  Arr1(1, 0) = 132
  Arr1(2, 0) = 131
  Arr1(3, 0) = 50

  CreateArray = Arr1

  KodArr = Split(StokKodu, " ")
  If UBound(KodArr) <> 2 Then Exit Function
  If Len(Trim(KodArr(2))) <> 5 Then Exit Function
  
  Arr1(0, 1) = KodArr(0)
  Arr1(1, 1) = KodArr(1)
  Arr1(2, 1) = Left(KodArr(2), 3)
  Arr1(3, 1) = Mid(KodArr(2), 4)

  CreateArray = Arr1
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	Dim KriterTbl
	Set KriterTbl = Doc.CreateTableObject("KRITERLER=STACK","IO")

	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "GK_10E","CHAR",10,0,"L","","STKGKA"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "MPS_STATU1","CHAR",1,0,"L","","|MPSST"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "MPS_STATU2","CHAR",1,0,"L","","|MPSST"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "MPS_STATU3","CHAR",1,0,"L","","|MPSST"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "MPS_STATU4","CHAR",1,0,"L","","|MPSST"
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "R_LATEST_B_TARIHB","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "R_LATEST_B_TARIHE","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "REVURETIMDENTESTARIHB","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	KriterTbl.Addfield2 "REVURETIMDENTESTARIHE","DATE",10,0,"L","",""


	Dim HOZET
	Set HOZET = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("HMD_OZET=STACK","IO")
	HOZET.Addfield2 "R_KAYNAKKODU","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "KAYNAK_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "IUNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_IHTIYAC","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "TOPLAM_STOK","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "REZERVE_STOK","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "SERBEST_STOK","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "KARSILANAMAYAN_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "IHTIYAC_MPS_ADEDI","DOUBLE",10,0,"R","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "KARSILANAN_MPS_ADEDI","DOUBLE",10,0,"R","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "HMD_EKSIK_MPS_ADEDI","DOUBLE",10,0,"R","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "ILK_IHTIYAC_TARIHI","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	HOZET.Addfield2 "ILK_TALEP_TARIHI","DATE",10,0,"L","",""

	Dim HLIST
	Set HLIST = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("HMD_LIST=STACK","IO")
	HLIST.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "TRNUM","CHAR",6,0,"R","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "R_KAYNAKKODU","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "KAYNAK_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "IUNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "R_BAKIYEKAYNAK","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "R_LATEST_B_TARIH","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "REZERVE_STOK","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "REZ_BAKIYE","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "STOKLA_KARSILANAN","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "KARSILANAMAYAN_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "R_MAMULKODU","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "REVURETIMDENTESTARIH","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "MAMUL_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "MAMUL_UNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "MAMUL_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "R_STATUS","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "STOKTA_KALAN","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "ALTERNATIFKODU","CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	HLIST.Addfield2 "ALT_ADET","DOUBLE",10,0,"L","",""

	Dim MPSLIST
	Set MPSLIST = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MPS_LIST=STACK","IO")
	MPSLIST.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')",""
	MPSLIST.Addfield2 "R_MAMULKODU","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MPSLIST.Addfield2 "MAMUL_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MPSLIST.Addfield2 "MAMUL_UNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	MPSLIST.Addfield2 "MAMUL_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	MPSLIST.Addfield2 "R_STATUS","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MPSLIST.Addfield2 "REVURETIMDENTESTARIH","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	MPSLIST.Addfield2 "URT_MIKTAR","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	MPSLIST.Addfield2 "URT_BAKIYE","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	MPSLIST.Addfield2 "URT_STATU","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	MPSLIST.Addfield2 "ALT_VARMI","CHAR",3,0,"L","",""

	Dim MPSDetayList, MPSSubList

	Set MPSSubList = Doc.Select2("MPS_SUB_LST", "MPS_LIST", "", "", -1)

End Sub
Sub Hesapla()
	Call DetayListHesapla()
End Sub
Sub DetayListHesapla()
	Dim gg, KRT, QStr
	Set gg = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Set KRT = Doc.GetTableObject("KRITERLER")

	QStr = "SELECT MMPS10T.EVRAKNO, MMPS10T.TRNUM, MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU, STOK00.AD KAYNAK_AD, STOK00.IUNIT IUNIT, MMPS10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK,"
	QStr = QStr & " MMPS10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH, S01.STOK_MIKTAR REZERVE_STOK, (MMPS10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK - COALESCE(S01.STOK_MIKTAR,0)) REZ_BAKIYE,"
	QStr = QStr & " (MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR - MMPS10E.SF_URETILEN) MAMUL_MIKTAR, S00.AD MAMUL_AD, MMPS10E.SF_SF_UNIT MAMUL_UNIT, "
	QStr = QStr & " MMPS10T.R_STATUS, MMPS10T.R_MAMULKODU, MMPS10T.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH, MMPS10T.ALTERNATIFKODU, COALESCE(B01T.ALT_ADET,0) ALT_ADET,"
	QStr = QStr & " (CASE " & _
								"WHEN MMPS10T.BASLAMISMPS10='1' THEN 0 " & _
								"WHEN MMPS10T.R_STATUS='R' THEN 1 " & _
								"WHEN MMPS10T.R_STATUS='P' THEN 2 " & _
								"WHEN MMPS10T.R_STATUS='C' THEN 3 " & _
								"WHEN MMPS10T.R_STATUS='Q' THEN 4 " & _
								"ELSE 5 END) MPS_SIRA"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM MMPS10T"
	QStr = QStr & " INNER JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD=MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN (SELECT ALLOC_MPSARTNO, Sum(STOK_MIKTAR) STOK_MIKTAR FROM STOK01 WHERE ALLOC_MPSARTNO<>'" & Space(16) & "' GROUP BY ALLOC_MPSARTNO) S01 ON MMPS10T.JOBNO=S01.ALLOC_MPSARTNO"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN MMPS10E ON MMPS10E.EVRAKNO=MMPS10T.EVRAKNO"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S00 ON S00.KOD=MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN ("
	QStr = QStr & " 		SELECT EVRAKNO, BOMREC_CODE, BOMREC_INPUTTYPE, BOMREC_ALTKODU, (Count(*) - 1) ALT_ADET"
	QStr = QStr & " 		FROM BOMU01T"
	QStr = QStr & " 		WHERE AKTIF_PASIF='*' AND BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H' AND BOMU01T.BOMREC_ALTKODU<>'          '"
	QStr = QStr & " 		GROUP BY EVRAKNO, BOMREC_CODE, BOMREC_INPUTTYPE, BOMREC_ALTKODU) B01T"
	QStr = QStr & " 		ON B01T.BOMREC_CODE=MMPS10T.R_MAMULKODU AND B01T.BOMREC_ALTKODU=MMPS10T.ALTERNATIFKODU"
	if not Doc.Is_EY1(KRT.YM_KONTROL) Then
		QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN BOMU01TM ON BOMU01TM.MAMULCODE=MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU AND BOMU01TM.AKTIF_PASIF='*' AND BOMU01TM.VARSAYILAN10='1'"
	End if
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE MMPS10T.ACIK_KAPALI=' ' AND MMPS10T.R_ACIK_KAPALI=' ' AND MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'"
	if not Doc.Is_EY1(KRT.YM_KONTROL) Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND (BOMU01TM.EVRAKNO IS NULL OR STOK00.KART_TIPI='F')"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU>='" & KRT.SKODB & "'"
	if trim(KRT.SKODE) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU<='" & KRT.SKODE & "'"
	End if

	if trim(KRT.MPS_STATU1 & KRT.MPS_STATU2 & KRT.MPS_STATU3 & KRT.MPS_STATU4) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND (1=0"
		if trim(KRT.MPS_STATU1) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & " OR MMPS10T.R_STATUS='" & KRT.MPS_STATU1 & "'"
		End if
		if trim(KRT.MPS_STATU2) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & " OR MMPS10T.R_STATUS='" & KRT.MPS_STATU2 & "'"
		End if
		if trim(KRT.MPS_STATU3) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & " OR MMPS10T.R_STATUS='" & KRT.MPS_STATU3 & "'"
		End if
		if trim(KRT.MPS_STATU4) <> "" Then
			QStr = QStr & " OR MMPS10T.R_STATUS='" & KRT.MPS_STATU4 & "'"
		End if
		QStr = QStr & " )"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND MMPS10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH>='" & KRT.R_LATEST_B_TARIHB & "'"
	if trim(KRT.R_LATEST_B_TARIHE) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND MMPS10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH<='" & KRT.R_LATEST_B_TARIHE & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND MMPS10T.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH>='" & KRT.REVURETIMDENTESTARIHB & "'"
	if trim(KRT.REVURETIMDENTESTARIHE) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND MMPS10T.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH<='" & KRT.REVURETIMDENTESTARIHE & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND " & GetGrupKoduQStr
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU, MPS_SIRA, MMPS10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH"
	Dim M10T
	Set M10T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfw3efsssd", QStr)


	QStr = "SELECT STOK01.KOD, Sum(STOK01.STOK_MIKTAR) STOK_MIKTAR"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM STOK01"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN GDEF00 ON GDEF00.KOD=STOK01.AMBCODE"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD=STOK01.KOD"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE GDEF00.MRPDEYOK10<>'1' AND ALLOC_SYSSTATUS=' '"
	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK01.KOD>='" & KRT.SKODB & "'"
	if trim(KRT.SKODE) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK01.KOD<='" & KRT.SKODE & "'"
	End if
	QStr = QStr & " AND " & GetGrupKoduQStr
	QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY STOK01.KOD"
	Dim S01S
	Set S01S = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdf34fdfd", QStr)

	Dim HLIST, HOZET
	Set HLIST = Doc.GetTableObject("HMD_LIST")
	Set HOZET = Doc.GetTableObject("HMD_OZET")

	HLIST.Empty
	HOZET.Empty

	HLIST.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	HOZET.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	Dim EskiStokKodu, satirNo1, i, StokMiktari, KalanMiktar, satirNo, Karsilanan, S01S1
	EskiStokKodu = ""
	satirNo1 = 0
	For i = 1 to M10T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & M10T.GetRowCount()
		if M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i) <> EskiStokKodu Then
			Set S01S1 = Doc.Select2("sdf3wefsdf", S01S.GetAppName, "KOD='" & M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i) & "'", "", -1)
			if S01S1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				StokMiktari = S01S1.STOK_MIKTAR(1)
			Else
				StokMiktari = 0
			End if
			KalanMiktar = StokMiktari
			satirNo1 = HOZET.AddRow
			HOZET.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo1) = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)
			HOZET.KAYNAK_AD(satirNo1) = M10T.KAYNAK_AD(i)
			HOZET.IUNIT(satirNo1) = M10T.IUNIT(i)
			HOZET.TOPLAM_STOK(satirNo1) = StokMiktari
			HOZET.SERBEST_STOK(satirNo1) = StokMiktari
			HOZET.ILK_IHTIYAC_TARIHI(satirNo1) = M10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(i)
		End if
		EskiStokKodu = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)

		satirNo = HLIST.AddRow
		HLIST.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = M10T.EVRAKNO(i)
		HLIST.TRNUM(satirNo) = M10T.TRNUM(i)
		HLIST.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo) = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)
		HLIST.KAYNAK_AD(satirNo) = M10T.KAYNAK_AD(i)
		HLIST.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo) = M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(i)
		HLIST.IUNIT(satirNo) = M10T.IUNIT(i)
		HLIST.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(satirNo) = M10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(i)
		HLIST.REZERVE_STOK(satirNo) = M10T.REZERVE_STOK(i)
		HLIST.REZ_BAKIYE(satirNo) = M10T.REZ_BAKIYE(i)
		HLIST.R_MAMULKODU(satirNo) = M10T.R_MAMULKODU(i)
		HLIST.MAMUL_AD(satirNo) = M10T.MAMUL_AD(i)
		HLIST.MAMUL_UNIT(satirNo) = M10T.MAMUL_UNIT(i)
		HLIST.MAMUL_MIKTAR(satirNo) = M10T.MAMUL_MIKTAR(i)
		HLIST.R_STATUS(satirNo) = M10T.R_STATUS(i)
		HLIST.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(satirNo) = M10T.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(i)
		HLIST.ALTERNATIFKODU(satirNo) = M10T.ALTERNATIFKODU(i)
		HLIST.ALT_ADET(satirNo) = M10T.ALT_ADET(i)

		if M10T.REZ_BAKIYE(i) > 0 and KalanMiktar > 0 Then
			if KalanMiktar < M10T.REZ_BAKIYE(i) Then
				Karsilanan = KalanMiktar
			Else
				Karsilanan = M10T.REZ_BAKIYE(i)
			End if
			KalanMiktar = KalanMiktar - Karsilanan
		Else
			Karsilanan = 0
		End if
		HLIST.STOKLA_KARSILANAN(satirNo) = Karsilanan
		HLIST.KARSILANAMAYAN_MIKTAR(satirNo) = M10T.REZ_BAKIYE(i) - Karsilanan
		HLIST.STOKTA_KALAN(satirNo) = KalanMiktar

		HOZET.TOPLAM_IHTIYAC(satirNo1) = HOZET.TOPLAM_IHTIYAC(satirNo1) + M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(i)
		HOZET.TOPLAM_STOK(satirNo1) = HOZET.TOPLAM_STOK(satirNo1) + M10T.REZERVE_STOK(i)
		HOZET.REZERVE_STOK(satirNo1) = HOZET.REZERVE_STOK(satirNo1) + M10T.REZERVE_STOK(i)
		HOZET.KARSILANAMAYAN_MIKTAR(satirNo1) = HOZET.KARSILANAMAYAN_MIKTAR(satirNo1) + HLIST.KARSILANAMAYAN_MIKTAR(satirNo)
		HOZET.IHTIYAC_MPS_ADEDI(satirNo1) = HOZET.IHTIYAC_MPS_ADEDI(satirNo1) + 1
		if HLIST.KARSILANAMAYAN_MIKTAR(satirNo) = 0 Then
			HOZET.KARSILANAN_MPS_ADEDI(satirNo1) = HOZET.KARSILANAN_MPS_ADEDI(satirNo1) + 1
		Else
			HOZET.HMD_EKSIK_MPS_ADEDI(satirNo1) = HOZET.HMD_EKSIK_MPS_ADEDI(satirNo1) + 1
			if trim(HOZET.ILK_TALEP_TARIHI(satirNo1)) = "" Then
				HOZET.ILK_TALEP_TARIHI(satirNo1) = M10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(i)
			End if
		End if
	Next

	Call GetTumMPSList()

	HLIST.SetRowAID 0,0,0
	HOZET.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub

	Dim MDLIST

Sub GetTumMPSList()
	Dim HL, MLIST, i, satirNo, EskiMPSNo, UretilebilecekMik, AlternatifSayisi
	Set HL = Doc.Select2("asdfd2wfwsdf", "HMD_LIST", "", "EVRAKNO", -1)
	Set MLIST = Doc.GetTableObject("MPS_LIST")
	MLIST.SetRowAID 1,1,1

	MLIST.Empty

	For i = 1 to HL.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "MPS 'lerin Üretilebilirlik Durumlarý Hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & HL.GetRowCount()
		if HL.EVRAKNO(i) <> EskiMPSNo Then
			if trim(EskiMPSNo) <> "" Then
				if AlternatifSayisi > 0 Then
					MLIST.ALT_VARMI(satirNo) = "VAR"
				End if
			End if
			AlternatifSayisi = 0
			satirNo = MLIST.AddRow
			MLIST.EVRAKNO(satirNo) = HL.EVRAKNO(i)
			MLIST.R_MAMULKODU(satirNo) = HL.R_MAMULKODU(i)
			MLIST.MAMUL_AD(satirNo) = HL.MAMUL_AD(i)
			MLIST.MAMUL_UNIT(satirNo) = HL.MAMUL_UNIT(i)
			MLIST.MAMUL_MIKTAR(satirNo) = HL.MAMUL_MIKTAR(i)
			MLIST.R_STATUS(satirNo) = HL.R_STATUS(i)
			MLIST.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(satirNo) = HL.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH(i)
			MLIST.URT_MIKTAR(satirNo) = HL.MAMUL_MIKTAR(i)
			MLIST.URT_BAKIYE(satirNo) = 0
			MLIST.URT_STATU(satirNo) = "E"
		End if
		EskiMPSNo = HL.EVRAKNO(i)
		UretilebilecekMik = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(HL.REZERVE_STOK(i) + HL.STOKLA_KARSILANAN(i), HL.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(i)) * HL.MAMUL_MIKTAR(i), 3)
		if UretilebilecekMik < MLIST.URT_MIKTAR(satirNo) Then
			MLIST.URT_MIKTAR(satirNo) = UretilebilecekMik
			MLIST.URT_BAKIYE(satirNo) = MLIST.MAMUL_MIKTAR(satirNo) - UretilebilecekMik
			if MLIST.URT_MIKTAR(satirNo) = 0 Then
				MLIST.URT_STATU(satirNo) = "H"
			Else
				MLIST.URT_STATU(satirNo) = "K"
			End if
		End if
		if HL.ALT_ADET(i) > 0 Then
			AlternatifSayisi = AlternatifSayisi + 1
		End if
	Next
	if AlternatifSayisi > 0 Then
		MLIST.ALT_VARMI(satirNo) = "VAR"
	Else
		MLIST.ALT_VARMI(satirNo) = "YOK"
	End if
	MLIST.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub

	Set MDLIST = Doc.Select2("MPS_HMD_LST", "HMD_LIST", "EVRAKNO='" & ML.EVRAKNO & "'", "", -1)

Function GetGrupKoduQStr()
	Dim KRT, QStr
	Set KRT = Doc.GetTableObject("KRITERLER")
	QStr = ""
	QStr = QStr & " STOK00.GK_1>='" & KRT.GK_1B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_1E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_1<='" & KRT.GK_1E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_2>='" & KRT.GK_2B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_2E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_2<='" & KRT.GK_2E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_3>='" & KRT.GK_3B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_3E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_3<='" & KRT.GK_3E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_4>='" & KRT.GK_4B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_4E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_4<='" & KRT.GK_4E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_5>='" & KRT.GK_5B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_5E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_5<='" & KRT.GK_5E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_6>='" & KRT.GK_6B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_6E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_6<='" & KRT.GK_6E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_7>='" & KRT.GK_7B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_7E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_7<='" & KRT.GK_7E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_8>='" & KRT.GK_8B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_8E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_8<='" & KRT.GK_8E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_9>='" & KRT.GK_9B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_9E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_9<='" & KRT.GK_9E & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_10>='" & KRT.GK_10B & "'"
	if trim(KRT.GK_10E) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND STOK00.GK_10<='" & KRT.GK_10E & "'"
	End if
	GetGrupKoduQStr = QStr
End Function
Sub GetAlternatifListMain()
	Dim ALT_TABLE
	Set ALT_TABLE = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("ALTERNATIF_HMD=STACK","IO")
	ALT_TABLE.Addfield2 "SELECTED","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
	ALT_TABLE.Addfield2 "FOYNO","CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('BOMU01E')",""
	ALT_TABLE.Addfield2 "BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	ALT_TABLE.Addfield2 "KAYNAK_AD","CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	ALT_TABLE.Addfield2 "IUNIT","CHAR",6,0,"L","",""
	ALT_TABLE.Addfield2 "ALT_IHTIYAC","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	ALT_TABLE.Addfield2 "ALT_STOK","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	ALT_TABLE.Addfield2 "BU_ANALIZ_KULL","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	ALT_TABLE.Addfield2 "ALT_KARSILANAN","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""
	ALT_TABLE.Addfield2 "ALT_KARSILANAMAYAN","DOUBLE",16,3,"R","",""

	Call AlternatifleriHesapla()

	Dim Par
	Set Par=Nothing
	Doc.LoadSubForm_Dyn "ALTERNATIFLER",SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME,Par	
End Sub
Sub AlternatifleriHesapla()
	Dim MLST, B01T, QStr, satirNo, ALT_TABLE, B01T1, AnaHmdKullMik, i
	Set MLST = Doc.GetTableObject("MPS_HMD_LST")
	Set ALT_TABLE = Doc.GetTableObject("ALTERNATIF_HMD")

	if trim(MLST.ALTERNATIFKODU) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ürün aðacýnda bu hammadde için alternatif belirtilmemiþ", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	QStr = "BOMREC_CODE='" & MLST.R_MAMULKODU & "'&AKTIF_PASIF='*'&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'&BOMREC_ALTKODU='" & MLST.ALTERNATIFKODU & "'"
	Set B01T = Doc.Select1("sdf34terdr", "BOMU01T", QStr, "", -1)
	if B01T.GetRowCount() < 2 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Alternatif Hammadde Bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

 	Set B01T1 = Doc.Select2("sdffff44df34terdr", B01T.GetAppname, "BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE='" & MLST.R_KAYNAKKODU & "'", "", -1)
	if B01T1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	if B01T1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		AnaHmdKullMik = B01T1.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(1)
	End if

	ALT_TABLE.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	ALT_TABLE.Empty
	For i = 1 to B01T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i)) <> Trim(MLST.R_KAYNAKKODU) Then
			satirNo = ALT_TABLE.AddRow
			ALT_TABLE.FOYNO(satirNo) = B01T.EVRAKNO(i)
			ALT_TABLE.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(satirNo) = B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i)
			ALT_TABLE.KAYNAK_AD(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), "")
			ALT_TABLE.IUNIT(satirNo) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "IUNIT", B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), "")
			ALT_TABLE.ALT_IHTIYAC(satirNo) = Doc.Div(B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i), AnaHmdKullMik) * MLST.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK
			ALT_TABLE.ALT_STOK(satirNo) = GetStok(B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i))
'			ALT_TABLE.BU_ANALIZ_KULL(satirNo) = 
			if ALT_TABLE.ALT_IHTIYAC(satirNo) > ALT_TABLE.ALT_STOK(satirNo) Then
				ALT_TABLE.ALT_KARSILANAN(satirNo) = ALT_TABLE.ALT_STOK(satirNo)
			Else
				ALT_TABLE.ALT_KARSILANAN(satirNo) = ALT_TABLE.ALT_IHTIYAC(satirNo)
			End if
			ALT_TABLE.ALT_KARSILANAMAYAN(satirNo) = ALT_TABLE.ALT_IHTIYAC(satirNo) - ALT_TABLE.ALT_KARSILANAN(satirNo)
		End if
	Next
	ALT_TABLE.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub AlternatifHmdMPSeYaz()
	Dim MLST, ALT_TBL, Doc1, M10E, M10T, M10T1
	Set MLST = Doc.GetTableObject("MPS_HMD_LST")
	Set ALT_TBL = Doc.Select2("sdf3rgg", "ALTERNATIF_HMD", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if ALT_TBL.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Alternatif Seçmelisiniz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", "MB_OK"
		Exit Sub
	End if

	if ALT_TBL.GetRowCount() > 1 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Yanlýzca 1 adet alternatif seçebilirsiniz", "Ýþlem Kesildi", "MB_OK"
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	if trim(MLST.EVRAKNO) = "" Then Exit Sub
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MMPS10", "")
	Set M10E = Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	Set M10T = Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")

	Doc1.Load_Voucher MLST.EVRAKNO
	Set M10T1 = Doc1.Select2("sdf24gsdfgs", "MMPS10T", "TRNUM='" & MLST.TRNUM & "'&R_KAYNAKKODU='" & MLST.R_KAYNAKKODU & "'", "", -1)
	if M10T1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MLST.EVRAKNO) & " no.lu MPS 'de " & trim(MLST.R_KAYNAKKODU) & " stok kodlu hammadde bulunamadý.", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
		Exit Sub
	End if

	M10T.SetCurrentRow(Doc.Cdbl(M10T1.SRNUM(1)))
	M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU = ALT_TBL.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE
	M10T.R_MIKTAR0 = ALT_TBL.ALT_IHTIYAC

	if M10E.STATUS <> "R" Then M10E.FIXEDRECETE = "E"

	if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(M10T.EVRAKNO) & " no.lu MPS Güncellendi", "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", MB_OK
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub
Function GetStok(StokKodu)
	Dim HOzet
	Set HOzet = Doc.Select2("sdf24grgdfhd", "HMD_OZET", "R_KAYNAKKODU='" & StokKodu & "'", "", -1)
	if HOzet.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		GetStok = HOzet.TOPLAM_STOK - HOzet.TOPLAM_IHTIYAC
	Else
		GetStok = Doc.Stok00c_SonSerbest(StokKodu, "KRITER:MRPDEYOK10<>'1'","")
	End if
End Function

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : FORMA_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : PDM-22
Hangi Tablo için : BOMU01
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : MAKR7S
Açýklama : PDM 'den Ürün Aðaçlarýný Aktar
Kod : 
Const cnnStr = "Provider=SQLNCLI;Data Source=pdm-server;Initial catalog=FORMAPDM;User Id=erp;Password=Freedom2010;"
Public TumunuAktar
Sub Makro1()
	Set ffff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	TumunuAktar = False

	QStr = "SELECT X.BomDocumentID, DK.MAMUL_DOC_ID, X.ROWNO, D.Filename, D.LatestRevisionNo REV_NO, D.LockPath, X.ITEM_NO, "
	QStr = QStr & " X.PART_NUMBER, X.DESCRIPTION1, X.QTY, DK.STOK_KODU"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM BOM_LIST X"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN [dbo].[BomSheets] E ON E.BomDocumentID=X.BomDocumentID"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN [dbo].[Documents] D ON D.DocumentID=E.SourceDocumentID AND E.SourceDocumentVersion=D.LatestRevisionNo"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN [dbo].DOC_KOD2 DK ON D.DocumentID=DK.MAMUL_DOC_ID"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE 1=1 "
	QStr = QStr & " AND D.Deleted<>1"
	QStr = QStr & " AND DK.STOK_KODU LIKE '15%' AND DK.STOK_KODU NOT LIKE '152%'"
'	QStr = QStr & " AND DK.STOK_KODU='151 006 02500'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND X.BomDocumentID IN"
	QStr = QStr & " ("
	QStr = QStr & " 		SELECT MAX(X.BomDocumentID) doc_id"
	QStr = QStr & " 		FROM BOM_LIST X"
	QStr = QStr & " 		LEFT JOIN [dbo].[BomSheets] E ON E.BomDocumentID=X.BomDocumentID"
	QStr = QStr & " 		LEFT JOIN [dbo].[Documents] D ON D.DocumentID=E.SourceDocumentID AND E.SourceDocumentVersion=D.LatestRevisionNo"
	QStr = QStr & " 		LEFT JOIN [dbo].DOC_KOD2 DK ON D.DocumentID=DK.MAMUL_DOC_ID"
	QStr = QStr & " 		WHERE 1=1 "
	QStr = QStr & " 		AND D.Deleted<>1"
	QStr = QStr & " 		AND DK.STOK_KODU LIKE '15%'"
	QStr = QStr & " 		GROUP BY DK.STOK_KODU"
	QStr = QStr & " 	)"
	QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY X.BomDocumentID, X.ROWNO, D.Filename, D.LatestRevisionNo, D.LockPath, DK.STOK_KODU, DK.MAMUL_DOC_ID,"
	QStr = QStr & " X.ITEM_NO, X.PART_NUMBER, X.DESCRIPTION1, X.QTY"
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY DK.STOK_KODU"

	Set Par1 = Doc.CreateSObject("PARAMETERS")
	Par1.SetParameter "REPORT_CONNECTION_STRING", CnnStr
	Set BomTable = Doc.RunSQLQuery_Par("_DDDD_2333",QStr,Par1)

	Set BomHata = Doc.Select2("dgsdsj", BomTable.GetAppName, "STOK_KODU='df234gdfh'", "", -1)
	
	QStr = "STOK_KODU>'15'&STOK_KODU<'15zzzzz'"
	'Qtr = "STOK_KODU='152 002 00300'"
	Set BomMamul = Doc.Select2G("sdgaerhaj", BomTable.GetAppName, QStr, "", "STOK_KODU+PART_NUMBER", -1)
	Set AKT_TABLE = Doc.Select2("ddf3g3dgsdsj", BomMamul.GetAppName, "STOK_KODU='df234gdfh'", "", -1)
	AKT_TABLE.Empty

	For i = 1 to BomMamul.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Ürün Aðaçlarý Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & BomMamul.GetRowCount()
		Set B1 = Doc.Select2("sdgehaj", BomTable.GetAppName, "STOK_KODU='" & BomMamul.STOK_KODU(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if Aktarilabilir(B1, BomHata) Then
			Call UrunAgaciYoksaYarat(BomMamul.STOK_KODU(i))
			For j = 1 to B1.GetRowCount()
				satirNo = BOMU01T.AddRow()
				BOMU01T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				Call StokKoduKontrol(B1, j)
				BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(satirNo) = "H"
				BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(satirNo) = B1.PART_NUMBER(j)
				BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(satirNo) = Doc.Cdbl(B1.QTY(j))
				'Call MontajSirasiYaz(satirNo)
				'Call FOKodEkle(BOMU01T, satirNo)
			Next
			BOMU01T.Sort("BOMREC_INPUTTYPE+REFNUM01+FEATURENO+BOMREC_FO_OPTION+BOMREC_SIRANO+BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE")
			if Doc.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
				Call KTAYaz(B1.REV_NO(1))
				sNo = AKT_TABLE.AddRow
				AKT_TABLE.CopyRowFromOtherTable sNo, BomMamul, i
			End if
			Doc.LoadEmpty_Voucher
		End if
	Next

	if BomHata.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Doc.RunMacro1 "MAKR7S.EXCELOUT", "sendTableToExcel", BomHata
'		exit sub
	End if
	Set ffff = Nothing
	
	Call MPSGuncelle(AKT_TABLE)
End Sub
Function GetMamulKoduFromFileName(ByVal DosyaAdi)
	DosyaAdi = trim(DosyaAdi)
	GetMamulKoduFromFileName = ""
	SonEkPos = InStr(1, trim(DosyaAdi), ".SLD")
	If SonEkPos = 0 Then Exit Function
	GetMamulKoduFromFileName = Left(DosyaAdi, SonEkPos - 1)
End Function
Function GetFreedomStokKodu(MamulKodu)
	GetFreedomStokKodu = ""
	QStr = "SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & MamulKodu & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " UNION ALL"
	QStr = QStr & " SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE SUPPLIERCODE='" & MamulKodu & "'"

	Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dxfhadfs", QStr)
	if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	GetFreedomStokKodu = S00.KOD(1)
End Function
Sub UrunAgaciYoksaYarat(FrMamulKodu)
	Doc.DeleteTableObject "BOMU01T_OLD_00"
	Set B01E = Doc.RunSQLQuery("zsdghd", "SELECT EVRAKNO FROM BOMU01E WHERE MAMULCODE='" & FrMamulKodu & "' AND AKTIF_PASIF='*'")
	if B01E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.New_Voucher
		BOMU01E.MAMULCODE = FrMamulKodu
		BOMU01E.MAMULMIKTAR = 1
		BOMU01E.AKTIF_PASIF = "*"
	Else
		Doc.Load_Voucher B01E.EVRAKNO(1)
		Set B01TOld = Doc.Select2("BOMU01T_OLD_00", "BOMU01T", "", "", -1)
		For i = 1 to BOMU01T.GetRowCount()
			if BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = "H" Then
				if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "GK_9", BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i), "")) <> "E" Then
					BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) = ""
					BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) = ""
				End if
			End if
		Next
		BOMU01T.SafePack
	End if
End Sub
Function Aktarilabilir(B1, BomHata)
	Aktarilabilir = True
	MamulKodu = B1.STOK_KODU(1)
	if trim(MamulKodu) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Dosya Adý : " & trim(B1.Filename(1)) & ", PDM 'de Custom Properties alanlarý doldurulmamýþ", "", MB_OK
		Call HataYaz(B1, BomHata, 1)
		Aktarilabilir = False
		Exit Function
	Else
		QStr = "SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & MamulKodu & "'"
		Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfsadfadj", QStr)
		if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Dosya Adý : " & trim(B1.Filename(1)) &  ", Stok Kodu : " & trim(MamulKodu) & ", stok kodu bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
			Call HataYaz(B1, BomHata, 1)
			Aktarilabilir = False
			Exit Function
		End if
	End if

	if Not TumunuAktar Then
		QStr = "SELECT K.PDM_REVNO FROM BOMU01E E"
		QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN KTA_BOMU01E K ON K.EVRAKNO=E.EVRAKNO"
		QStr = QStr & " WHERE E.MAMULCODE='" & MamulKodu & "' AND AKTIF_PASIF='*'"
		Set B01E = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdrgeraj", QStr)
		if B01E.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			if B01E.PDM_REVNO = B1.REV_NO(1) Then
				Aktarilabilir = False
				Exit Function
			End if
		End if
	End if

	For i = 1 to B1.GetRowCount()
		if trim(B1.PART_NUMBER(i)) = "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Dosya Adý : " & trim(B1.Filename(1)) &  ", Mamul Kodu : " & trim(MamulKodu) & ", Parça Tanýmý : " & trim(B1.DESCRIPTION1(i)) & ", PDM 'de Stok kodu girilmemiþ.", "", MB_OK
			Call HataYaz(B1, BomHata, i)
			Aktarilabilir = False
		Else
			Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdaefha", "SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & B1.PART_NUMBER(i) & "'")
			if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox "Dosya Adý : " & trim(B1.Filename(i)) &  ", Mamul Kodu : " & trim(B1.STOK_KODU(i)) & ", Parça No : " & trim(B1.PART_NUMBER(i)) & " stok kodu bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
				Call HataYaz(B1, BomHata, i)
				Aktarilabilir = False
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Function
Function BomTableKontrolHata(BomTable)
	BomTableKontrolHata = False
	For i = 1 to BomTable.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Stok Kartlarý Kontrol Ediliyor : " & i & " / " & BomTable.GetRowCount()
		if BomTable.STOK_KODU(i) <> EskiMamulKodu Then
			if trim(BomTable.STOK_KODU(i)) = "" Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox "Dosya Adý : " & trim(BomTable.Filename(i)) & ", PDM 'de Custom Properties alanlarý doldurulmamýþ", "", MB_OK
				BomTableKontrolHata = True
			Else
				QStr = "SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & BomTable.STOK_KODU(i) & "'"
				Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfsadfadj", QStr)
				if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
					Doc.d7MsgBox "Dosya Adý : " & trim(BomTable.Filename(i)) &  ", Stok Kodu : " & trim(BomTable.STOK_KODU(i)) & ", stok kodu bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
					BomTableKontrolHata = True
				End if
			End if
		End if
		EskiMamulKodu = BomTable.STOK_KODU(i)
		if trim(BomTable.PART_NUMBER(i)) = "" Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Dosya Adý : " & trim(BomTable.Filename(i)) &  ", Mamul Kodu : " & trim(BomTable.STOK_KODU(i)) & ", Parça Tanýmý : " & trim(BomTable.DESCRIPTION1(i)) & ", PDM 'de Stok kodu girilmemiþ.", "", MB_OK
			BomTableKontrolHata = True
		Else
			QStr = "SELECT KOD FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & BomTable.STOK_KODU(i) & "'"
			Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfsadfadj", QStr)
			if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox "Dosya Adý : " & trim(BomTable.Filename(i)) &  ", Mamul Kodu : " & trim(BomTable.STOK_KODU(i)) & ", Parça No : " & trim(BomTable.PART_NUMBER(i)) & " stok kodu bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
				BomTableKontrolHata = True
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Function
Sub HataYaz(B1, BomHata, satir)
	satirNo = BomHata.AddRow()
	BomHata.CopyRowFromOtherTable satirNo, B1, satir
End Sub
Sub KTAYaz(RevizyonNo)
	ret = Doc.UFLD00_CreateEditTable("xxx","BOMU01E", BOMU01E.EVRAKNO)
	Set KTA = Doc.GetTableObject("xxx")   
	KTA.PDM_REVNO = RevizyonNo
	KTA.Exec "SAVE"   
End Sub
Sub MontajSirasiYaz(satirNo)
	Set B01TOld1 = Doc.Select2("asdfdsah", "BOMU01T_OLD_00", "BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'&BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE='" & BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(satirNo) & "'", "", -1)
	if B01TOld1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	BOMU01T.REFNUM01(satirNo) = B01TOld1.REFNUM01(1)
End Sub
Sub FOKodEkle(BOMU01T, satirNo)
	Set B01TOld1 = Doc.Select2("asdfdsah", "BOMU01T_OLD_00", "BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'&BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE='" & BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(satirNo) & "'&BOMREC_FO_OPTION>' '", "", -1)
	if B01TOld1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	BOMU01T.BOMREC_FO_OPTION(satirNo) = B01TOld1.BOMREC_FO_OPTION(1)
	BOMU01T.FEATURENO(satirNo) = B01TOld1.FEATURENO(1)
	BOMU01T.BOMREC_FO_OPTION_DEF(satirNo) = B01TOld1.BOMREC_FO_OPTION_DEF(1)
	BOMU01T.BOMREC_FO_FEATU_MUST(satirNo) = B01TOld1.BOMREC_FO_FEATU_MUST(1)

	Set B01TOld2 = Doc.Select2("asdfdsah", "BOMU01T_OLD_00", "BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'&BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE<>'" & BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(satirNo) & "'&FEATURENO='" & BOMU01T.FEATURENO(satirNo) & "'&BOMREC_FO_OPTION<>'" & BOMU01T.BOMREC_FO_OPTION(satirNo) & "'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to B01TOld2.GetRowCount()
		satirNo1 = BOMU01T.AddRow
		BOMU01T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo1)
		BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(satirNo1) = "H"
		BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(satirNo1) = B01TOld2.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i)
		BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(satirNo1) = B01TOld2.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
		BOMU01T.BOMREC_FO_OPTION(satirNo1) = B01TOld2.BOMREC_FO_OPTION(i)
		BOMU01T.FEATURENO(satirNo1) = B01TOld2.FEATURENO(i)
		BOMU01T.BOMREC_FO_OPTION_DEF(satirNo1) = B01TOld2.BOMREC_FO_OPTION_DEF(i)
		BOMU01T.BOMREC_FO_FEATU_MUST(satirNo1) = B01TOld2.BOMREC_FO_FEATU_MUST(i)
		Call MontajSirasiYaz(satirNo1)
	Next
End Sub

Sub StokKoduKontrol(B1, satirNo)
	UreticiKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "SUPPLIERCODE", Doc.LeftJustify(trim(B1.PART_NUMBER(satirNo)), 24), "")
	if trim(UreticiKodu) <> trim(B1.DESCRIPTION1(satirNo)) Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Mamul Stok Kodu : " & trim(B1.STOK_KODU(satirNo)) & ", Malzeme Kodu : " & trim(B1.PART_NUMBER(satirNo)) & _
			", PDM 'deki açýklama : " & trim(B1.DESCRIPTION1(satirNo)) & ", Freedom Üretici Kodu : " & trim(UreticiKodu), "", MB_OK
	End if
End Sub
Sub MPSGuncelle(AKT_TABLE)
	if AKT_TABLE.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	if Doc.d7MsgBox("MPS 'ler tekrar hesaplansýn mý", "", MB_YESNO) <> 6 Then Exit Sub

	For i = 1 to AKT_TABLE.GetRowCount()
		if i > 1 Then InStr1 = InStr1 & ","
		InStr1 = InStr1 & "'" & AKT_TABLE.STOK_KODU(i) & "'"
	Next

	QStr = "SELECT EVRAKNO "
	QStr = QStr & " FROM MMPS10TM"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE ACIK_KAPALI<>'K' AND STATUS<>'R' "
	QStr = QStr & " AND MAMULSTOKKODU>='151 001' AND MAMULSTOKKODU<='151 050' "
	QStr = QStr & " AND MAMULSTOKKODU IN (" & InStr1 & ")"
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY EVRAKNO"

	Set M10TM = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfsdfhs", QStr)
	if M10TM.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	Set ffff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MMPS10", "")
	For i = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "MPS 'ler tekrar hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & M10TM.GetRowCount()
		Doc1.Load_Voucher M10TM.EVRAKNO(i)
		Doc1.RunMacro0 "MAKR7S.MPS-HESAP", "Makro1"
		if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox trim(M10TM.EVRAKNO(i)) & " no.lu MPS tekrar hesaplandý", "", MB_OK
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

'CREATE VIEW [dbo].[DOC_KOD2] AS
'SELECT D.BOM_DOC_ID, D.MAMUL_DOC_ID, (MAX(D.K1) + ' ' + MAX(D.K2) + ' ' + MAX(D.K3)+ MAX(D.K4)) AS STOK_KODU
'FROM (
'select V.DocumentID, DX.MAMUL_DOC_ID, DX.BOM_DOC_ID, V.VariableID, MAX(V.ConfigurationID) CONF_ID, V.ValueText,
'(CASE WHEN V.VariableID=109 THEN MAX(V.ValueText) ELSE ' ' END) K1,
'(CASE WHEN V.VariableID=132 THEN MAX(V.ValueText) ELSE ' ' END) K2,
'(CASE WHEN V.VariableID=131 THEN MAX(V.ValueText) ELSE ' ' END) K3,
'(CASE WHEN V.VariableID=50 THEN MAX(V.ValueText) ELSE ' ' END) K4
'from [dbo].[VariableValue] V
'INNER JOIN (
'	select DocumentID,VariableID,max(RevisionNo) max_rev
'	from [dbo].[VariableValue]
'	group by DocumentID,VariableID
') M ON M.DocumentID=V.DocumentID AND M.VariableID=V.VariableID and V.RevisionNo=M.max_rev
'INNER JOIN DOC_LIST DX ON DX.MAMUL_DOC_ID=V.DocumentID
'WHERE V.VariableID in (109,132,131,50)
'group by V.DocumentID, DX.BOM_DOC_ID, V.VariableID, V.ValueText, DX.MAMUL_DOC_ID
') AS D
'GROUP BY D.BOM_DOC_ID, MAMUL_DOC_ID

'CREATE VIEW BOM_LIST AS
'SELECT BomDocumentID, RowNo, RowDocumentID, RowDocVersion, MAX(ITEM_NO) ITEM_NO, 
'(MAX(K1) + ' ' + MAX(K2) + ' ' + MAX(K3) + MAX(K4)) PART_NUMBER, MAX(DESCRIPTION1) DESCRIPTION1, MAX(QTY) QTY
'FROM (
'SELECT V.BomDocumentID, V.RowNo, R.RowDocumentID, R.RowDocVersion,
'	(CASE WHEN ColumnNo=0 THEN ValueText ELSE ' ' END) ITEM_NO,
'	(CASE WHEN ColumnNo=4 THEN ValueText ELSE ' ' END) K1,
'	(CASE WHEN ColumnNo=5 THEN ValueText ELSE ' ' END) K2,
'	(CASE WHEN ColumnNo=6 THEN ValueText ELSE ' ' END) K3,
'	(CASE WHEN ColumnNo=7 THEN ValueText ELSE ' ' END) K4,
'	(CASE WHEN ColumnNo=2 THEN ValueText ELSE ' ' END) DESCRIPTION1,
'    (CASE WHEN ColumnNo=1 THEN ValueText ELSE ' ' END) QTY
' FROM [dbo].[BomSheetValue] V
'  LEFT JOIN [dbo].[BomSheetRow] R ON R.BomDocumentID=V.BomDocumentID AND R.RowNo=V.RowNo
'  )X
' GROUP BY BomDocumentID, RowNo, RowDocumentID, RowDocVersion

'CREATE VIEW [dbo].[DOC_LIST] AS
'SELECT     D.DocumentID AS MAMUL_DOC_ID, X.DocumentID AS BOM_DOC_ID, D.*
'FROM         dbo.Documents AS D INNER JOIN
'                         (SELECT     DocumentID, 
'                          (CASE WHEN CHARINDEX (':', Filename) > 1 THEN Left(Filename, CHARINDEX (':', Filename) -1) ELSE Filename END) AS Filename
'                            FROM          dbo.Documents
'                            ) AS X ON X.Filename = D.Filename
'WHERE     (D.Filename NOT LIKE '%:%')
'AND D.Deleted<>1
'AND D.ExtensionID=3

Sub MusteriHesapla()
	if MMPS10TM.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	if trim(MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE) <> "UP" Then Exit Sub

	QStr = "SELECT M.MUSTERIKODU, M.MUSTERISIPINO, M.MUSTERISIPIARTNO, C.AD"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM MMPS10TM M"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN CARI00 C ON C.KOD=M.MUSTERIKODU"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE M.EVRAKNO='" & MMPS10TM.KAYNAKMPS & "'"
	Set M10TM = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfgsdfhsdfj", QStr)
	if M10TM.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	MMPS10TM.MUSTERIKODU(1) = M10TM.MUSTERIKODU(1)
	MMPS10TM.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD(1) = M10TM.AD(1)
	MMPS10TM.MUSTERISIPINO(1) = M10TM.MUSTERISIPINO(1)
	MMPS10TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(1) = M10TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(1)
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	if LISTEE.HCODE1 <> LISTEE.HCODE2 or trim(LISTEE.HCODE1 & LISTEE.HCODE2) = "" Then
		doc.d7MsgBox "Tek bir hammadde için süz yapýlmalý", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	HammaddeKodu = LISTEE.HCODE1
	For i = 1 to LISTET.GetRowCount()
		Doc.Load_Voucher LISTET.EVRAKNO(i)
		For j = 1 to STOK20TI.GetRowCount()
			if trim(STOK20TI.KOD(j)) = trim(HammaddeKodu) Then
				STOK20TI.BILGISATIRIE(j) = "E"
			End if
		Next
		Doc.Save_Voucher
	Next
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	MailFrom = "erp@formamakina.com" 
	MailTo = "cengizucar@temelteknoloji.com,ccengizucar@gmail.com"
	MailHeader = "deneme"
	MailMessage = "deneme mesajý"
	DosyaAdi = ""

	Call SendMail(MailFrom, MailTo,MailHeader,MailMessage, DosyaAdi)
End Sub
Sub SendMail(MailFrom, MailTo,MailHeader,MailMessage, DosyaAdi)
	'On Error Resume Next
	cdoSendUsingPickup = 1 
	cdoSendUsingPort = 2 
	cdoAnonymous = 0
	cdoBasic = 1
	cdoNTLM = 2 'NTLM
'	Kullanici = Doc.Getd7UserName
'	Kullanici = Doc.LeftJustify(Kullanici, 8)
'	mailserver = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "EMAILSERVER", Kullanici, "")
'	mailUser = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "EMAILKULLANICIADI", Kullanici, "")
'	mailPass = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "DATAMODEMNO", Kullanici, "")
'	mailFrom = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "EMAIL", Kullanici, "")

	mailserver = "mail.formamakina.com"
	mailUser = "erp@formamakina.com"
	mailPass = "369*369*"
	SMTPPort = 587

	Set CDO = CreateObject("CDO.Message")
	'msgbox MailFrom & ", " & MailTo & "," & MailHeader & "," & MailMessage
	CDO.From = trim(MailFrom)
	CDO.To = MailTo
	CDO.Subject = MailHeader
	CDO.TextBody = MailMessage
	
	if trim(DosyaAdi) <> "" Then
		CDO.AddAttachment("file://" & DosyaAdi)
	End if
	CDO.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendusing") = cdoSendUsingPort 
	
	CDO.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpserver") = trim(mailserver)
	if Err.Number > 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Mail Gönderilemedi", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	CDO.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpauthenticate") = cdoBasic
	
	CDO.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendusername") = trim(mailUser)
	if Err.Number > 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Mail Gönderilemedi", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	CDO.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendpassword") = trim(mailPass)
	if Err.Number > 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Mail Gönderilemedi", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	CDO.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpserverport") = SMTPPort
	if Err.Number > 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Mail Gönderilemedi", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	CDO.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpusessl") = False
	
	CDO.Configuration.Fields.Item _
	("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpconnectiontimeout") = 60
	
	CDO.Configuration.Fields.Update
	CDO.Send
	if Err.Number > 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Mail Gönderilemedi", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MUHA10","")
	Set MH20E = Doc1.GetTableObject("MUHA20E")
	Set MH20T = Doc1.GetTableObject("MUHA20T")
	Set K = Doc1.CreateSObject("BATCHUPDATEMODE")

	nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
	Tarih = Inputbox("Mahsup Fiþi tarihi")
	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	d7Date.SetDate(trim(Tarih))
	if not d7Date.IsValid Then
		Doc.d7msgBox "Geçersiz Tarih", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	Tarih = d7Date.GetRevDate
	MH20E.TARIH = Tarih
	MH20E.ACIKLAMA = "DEMIRBAS"
	MH20E.GK_1 = "MALÝYET"

	MH20T.Empty

	For m=1 to LISTET.getreccount()
		demirbasno = LISTET.EVRAKNO(m)
		Set DMRB = Doc.Select1("_DMRB60","DMRB60E","EVRAKNO='"+demirbasno+"'","",-1)
		for k=1 to DMRB.getreccount
			
				aktif_kod=DMRB.MUHASEBEKOD(k)
				amortisman_kod=DMRB.MUHASEBEKODG(k)
				aktif_kod2=DMRB.MUHASEBEKOD2(k)			
		next
		Set DMRB2 = Doc.Select1("_DMRB60T2","DMRB60T2","EVRAKNO='"+demirbasno+"'&YIL='" & Doc.StrDate_Year(Tarih) & "'","",-1)

	if DMRB2.GetRowCount > 0 and Doc.Cdbl(DMRB.AMT_SONHESAP_YIL)>= Doc.StrDate_Year(Tarih) then
		amortismandegeri = DMRB2.CS_AMT_AY(1)
		amortismandegeri1 = doc.div(amortismandegeri, 12) '/* farklý yýl ise 12 ye böl
			satin_alma_tarih = DMRB.SATINALMA_TARIH(1)
			if Trim(Mid(satin_alma_tarih,1,4)) =  Trim(Year(date)) Then		
					AY = 13 - int(Mid(satin_alma_tarih,6,2)) '/* ayný yýl ise kaç aylýk hesaplanmalý 
					amortismandegeri1 = doc.div(amortismandegeri, AY )		
			End if
		For i=1 to 2
			MH20T.AddRow
			satirNo = MH20T.GetRecCount()
			MH20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
	
			if i=1 then
				MH20T.KOD(satirNo) = Aktif_kod
				MH20T.CODE2(satirNo) = Aktif_kod2
				MH20T.ACIKLAMA(satirno)=demirbasno
				MH20T.BORCFIYAT(satirNo) = amortismandegeri1
			else 
				MH20T.KOD(satirNo) = amortisman_kod
				MH20T.ALACAKFIYAT(satirNo) = amortismandegeri1
				MH20T.ACIKLAMA(satirno)=demirbasno
				amortismandegeri=0
			end if
		Next
	end if
Next
	if Doc1.Save_Voucher() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MH20E.EVRAKNO) & " no.lu mahsup fiþi yaratýldý", "", MB_OK
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 

Ekran Formu
Uygulamalar Ýçin Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Ayarlarý
Baþlangýç Ekran Formu Bu Uygulama için : 
Bu Uygulama için Baþlangýç Ekran Formu : 
Baþlangýçta Bu Makro Çalýþacak : 
Alt Ekranlar Ýçin Ekran Formu
Alt Ekran Form Kodu : 
Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro : 


----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : FORMA_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : SERILE-2
Hangi Tablo için : STOK36
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : DEMONTAJ SERÝLE
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	STOK36T.SafePack()
	For i = STOK36T.GetRowCount() to 1 Step -1
		if trim(STOK36T.SERINO(i)) = "" Then
			Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdffjsaj", "SELECT B_MINPARTIBUYUKLUGU FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & STOK36T.KOD(i) & "'")
			if S00.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
				PartiBuyuklugu = S00.B_MINPARTIBUYUKLUGU(1)
				if PartiBuyuklugu > 0 Then
					KasaSayisi = Doc.Ceil(STOK36T.SF_MIKTAR(i) / PartiBuyuklugu)
					Miktar = STOK36T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
					For j = 1 to KasaSayisi
						if j < KasaSayisi Then
							KasaIci = PartiBuyuklugu
						Else
							KasaIci = Miktar - (j - 1) * PartiBuyuklugu
						End if
						if j = 1 Then
							STOK36T.SetCurrentRow(i)
							STOK36T.SF_MIKTAR(i) = KasaIci
							STOK36T.SERINO(i) = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
						Else
							satirNo = i + (j-1)
							STOK36T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
							if satirNo > STOK36T.GetRowCount() Then
								STOK36T.AddRow
							Else
								STOK36T.InsertRow(satirNo)
							End if
							STOK36T.CopyRowFromOtherTable satirNo, STOK36T, i
							STOK36T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = KasaIci
							STOK36T.SERINO(satirNo) = Doc.Sayac0danAl("SERINO")
						End if
					Next
				End if
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Sub

Makro Kodu : YM-URETIM

Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için :

Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	if trim(STOK20E.AMBCODE) <> "ML" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "ML Deposu Seçilmelidir", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	if trim(STOK20E.EVRAKNO) = "" Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Yeni Evrak Yaratýlmalýdýr", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	d7Date.SetDate STOK20E.TARIH
	d7Date.AyinSonGununeGit
	if d7Date.GetRevDate <> STOK20E.TARIH Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Üretim Fiþinin Tarihi Ayýn Son Günü Olmalýdýr", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	QStr = "SELECT DISTINCT MPSNO FROM SFDC20T"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN MMPS10E ON MMPS10E.EVRAKNO=SFDC20T.MPSNO"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE RECTARIH1>='" & Left(STOK20E.TARIH,8) & "01'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND RECTARIH1<='" & STOK20E.TARIH & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND MPSNO NOT IN ("
	QStr = QStr & " 	SELECT DISTINCT MPSNO FROM STOK20T"
	QStr = QStr & " 	WHERE TARIH>='" & Left(STOK20E.TARIH,8) & "01'"
	QStr = QStr & " 	AND TARIH<='" & STOK20E.TARIH & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " )"
	QStr = QStr & " AND MPSNO>'" & Space(10) & "'"				' AND MMPS10E.ACIK_KAPALI<>'K'"
	if Left(Doc.Getd7FirmCode,3) <> "YIL" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND (SFDC20T.MAMULKOD LIKE '152%' OR  (SFDC20T.MAMULKOD>='151' AND SFDC20T.MAMULKOD<='151 050'))"
	End if
	Set S32E = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dsfgdfjsk", QStr)

	if S32E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then 
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýþlem yapýlacak MPS bulunamadý.", "", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set s = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	ret = YMUretimFisiniYarat(S32E)
	if ret <> 0 Then
		MsgBox "Üretim Fiþi Kaydedilemedi. Ýþlem Kesilmiþtir."
		Exit Sub
	End if

	
	Call MamulleriKendiMPSlerineHammaddeOlarakEkle()

	'ret = FirmaParametresiniDegistir(SonGun)
	'if ret <> 0 Then
	'	MsgBox "Firma Parametresi Kaydedilemedi. Ýþlem Kesilmiþtir."
	'	Exit Sub
	'End if
End Sub
Function YMUretimFisiniYarat(S32E)
	YMUretimFisiniYarat = -1

	STOK20T.SafePack()
	For i = 1 to S32E.GetRowCount()
'		if Trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "ACIK_KAPALI", S32E.MPSNO(i), "")) = "" Then
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "MPS 'ler ekleniyor : " & i & " / " & S32E.GetRowCount() & " : " & trim(S32E.MPSNO(i))
			satirNo = STOK20T.AddRow()
			STOK20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			STOK20T.MPSNO = S32E.MPSNO(i)
			STOK20T.SERINO = trim(S32E.MPSNO(i))
			STOK20T.SF_MIKTAR = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", S32E.MPSNO(i), "")
			STOK20T.MPSKAPATMAENGEL = "E"
'		Else
'			Doc.d7MsgBox S32E.MPSNO(i) & " no.lu MPS kapalý olduðu için iþlem yapýlmayacak.", "", MB_OK
'			Eklenmeyen = Eklenmeyen + 1
'		End if
	Next
	'Doc.RunCommand "RECETE_HESAPLA_MPSTRANSFERININ_TUMUNU_KULLAN"
	YMUretimFisiniYarat = Doc.Save_Voucher()
End Function
Sub MPSIcinTransferYap()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STOK83", "")	
	Set S83E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK83E")
	Set S83T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK83T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher()
	S83E.AMBCODE = STOK20E.AMBCODE
	S83E.TARGETAMBCODE = "KIM"
	S83E.TARIH = Doc.StrDate_Increment(STOK20E.TARIH, 1)
	For i = 1 to STOK20T.GetRowCount()
		IsNo = GetJobNo(STOK20T.MPSNO(i))
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "MPS Ýçin Transfer Fiþi Yaratýlýyor : " & i & " / " & STOK20T.GetRowCount() & " : " & trim(STOK20T.MPSNO(i))
		if trim(IsNo) <> "" Then
			satirNo = S83T.AddRow()
			S83T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			S83T.MPSNO = STOK20T.MPSNO(i)
			S83T.JOBNO = IsNo
			S83T.KOD = STOK20T.KOD(i)
			S83T.LOTNUMBER = STOK20T.LOTNUMBER(i)
			S83T.SERINO = STOK20T.SERINO(i)
			S83T.SF_VRI_VR_R1 = STOK20T.SF_VRI_VR_R1(i)
			S83T.SF_VRI_VR_R2 = STOK20T.SF_VRI_VR_R2(i)
			S83T.SF_VRI_VR_R3 = STOK20T.SF_VRI_VR_R3(i)
			S83T.SF_MIKTAR = STOK20T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
			S83T.SF_SF_UNIT = STOK20T.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
		End if
	Next
	nret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
	if nret = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox S83E.EVRAKNO & " no.lu MPS Ýçin Hammadde Transfer Ýade Fiþi yaratýldý", "", MB_OK
		MsgBox "Ýþlem Baþarýyla Tamamlandý"
	Else
		MsgBox "MPS Ýçin Transfer Fiþi kaydedilemedi"
	End if
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Sub
Function GetJobNo(MPSNo)
	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("sde34333", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MPSNo & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'", "", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	GetJobNo = M10T.JOBNO
End Function
Function FirmaParametresiniDegistir(Tarih)
	FirmaParametresiniDegistir = -1
	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	d7Date.SetDate Tarih	
	d7Date.Increment 1
	d7Date.AyinSonGununeGit
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("CUSTOM", "")
	Set CST = Doc1.GetTableObject("CUSTOM")
	Doc1.Load_Voucher "!LAT_KL"
	CST.D_TEXT = d7Date.GetNorDate
	FirmaParametresiniDegistir = Doc1.Save_Voucher
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Function
Sub MamulleriKendiMPSlerineHammaddeOlarakEkle()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MMPS10", "")
	Set MMPS10T = Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")

	For i = 1 to STOK20T.GetRowCount()
		if trim(STOK20T.MPSNO(i)) <> "" Then
			Doc1.Load_Voucher STOK20T.MPSNO(i)
			Set M10T = Doc1.Select2("sdfgsdfhsdj", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKKODU='" & STOK20T.KOD(i) & "'&RECETENOTU='" & STOK20E.TARIH & "'", "", -1)
			if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
				satirNo = MMPS10T.AddRow()
				MMPS10T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(satirNo) = "H"
				MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo) = STOK20T.KOD(i)
				MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR0(satirNo) = STOK20T.SF_MIKTAR(i)
				MMPS10T.RECETENOTU(satirNo) = STOK20E.TARIH
				Doc1.Save_Voucher
			End if
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Sub Makro1()

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : FORMA_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : BIRLESTIR
Hangi Tablo için : BOMU01
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Alanlar = BOMU01T.GetFieldNames("N")

	QStr = "SELECT * FROM BOMU01T"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H' AND BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE IN "
	QStr = QStr & " ("
 	QStr = QStr & " SELECT BOMREC_CODE FROM BOMU01T"
 	QStr = QStr & " WHERE BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='I' AND BOMREC_OPERASYON='009' AND BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE IN ('017','020')"
	QStr = QStr & "   AND AKTIF_PASIF='*' AND EVRAKNO IN ("
	QStr = QStr & "     SELECT EVRAKNO FROM BOMU01T"
	QStr = QStr & "     WHERE BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='I'"
	QStr = QStr & "     GROUP BY EVRAKNO"
	QStr = QStr & "     HAVING COUNT(EVRAKNO)=1"
	QStr = QStr & "   )"
	QStr = QStr & " )"
	QStr = QStr & " AND NOT (BOMREC_CODE>='151 001' AND BOMREC_CODE<='151 050')"
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY EVRAKNO,SRNUM"

	Set B01T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfgsdfhsdfj", QStr)
	EskiEvrakNo = ""
	For i = 1 to B01T.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Ürün aðaçlarý oluþturuluyor : " & i & " / " & B01T.GetRowCount()
		if B01T.EVRAKNO(i) <> EskiEvrakNo Then
			if trim(BOMU01E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
				Doc.Save_Voucher
			End if
			Doc.Load_Voucher B01T.EVRAKNO(i)
		End if
		EskiEvrakNo = B01T.EVRAKNO(i)

		Set B01T1 = Doc.Select2("ssdfsdfgsfsj", "BOMU01T", "TRNUM='" & B01T.TRNUM(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if B01T1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			satirNo = Doc.CDbl(B01T1.SRNUM(1))
			BOMU01T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			ret=Doc.Calc_Bom("1","M", BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(satirNo),1, BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(satirNo),"","","","","","","","")		
			Set Tbmrsys =Doc.GetTableObject("_WBMRSYS_SELECT")			
			satirNo1 = satirNo
			For j = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
				satirNo1 = satirNo1 + 1
				if satirNo1 > BOMU01T.GetRowCount() Then
					BOMU01T.AddRow
				Else
					BOMU01T.InsertRow(satirNo1)
				End if
				BOMU01T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo1)
				For each AlanAdi in Alanlar
					if AlanAdi <> "EVRAKNO" and AlanAdi <> "TRNUM" and AlanAdi <> "SRNUM" Then
						AlanDegeri = Tbmrsys.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi, j)
						BOMU01T.SetFieldValue AlanAdi, satirNo1, AlanDegeri
					End if
				Next
				BOMU01T.BOMREC_SIRANO = "01"
			Next
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(satirNo) = ""
			BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(satirNo) = ""
			BOMU01T.SafePack
		End if
	Next
	if trim(BOMU01E.EVRAKNO) <> "" Then Doc.Save_Voucher
End Sub
