Sub Makro1()
	Set MTable = Doc.CreateTableObject("MPS_KRITER=STACK", "IO")
	MTable.AddField2 "B_EVRAKNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')",""
	MTable.AddField2 "E_EVRAKNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')",""
	MTable.AddField2 "B_MUSTERISIPINO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('STOK40E')",""
	MTable.AddField2 "E_MUSTERISIPINO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('STOK40E')",""
	MTable.AddField2 "R_STATUS1", "CHAR",1,0,"L","","|MPSST"
	MTable.AddField2 "R_STATUS2", "CHAR",1,0,"L","","|MPSST"
	MTable.AddField2 "R_STATUS3", "CHAR",1,0,"L","","|MPSST"
	MTable.AddField2 "B_R_KAYNAKKODU", "CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTable.AddField2 "E_R_KAYNAKKODU", "CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""	
	MTable.AddField2 "B_MUSTERIKODU", "CHAR",16,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('CARI00')",""
	MTable.AddField2 "E_MUSTERIKODU", "CHAR",16,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('CARI00')",""	
	MTable.AddField2 "B_R_LATEST_B_TARIH", "DATE",10,0,"L","",""	
	MTable.AddField2 "E_R_LATEST_B_TARIH", "DATE",10,0,"L","",""		
	MTable.AddField2 "B_R_MAMULKODU", "CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTable.AddField2 "E_R_MAMULKODU", "CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""	
	MTable.AddField2 "IHTIYAC", "CHAR",1,0,"L","",""	
	MTable.AddField2 "KART_TURU_1", "CHAR",1,0,"L","","|KARTTIPI"	
	MTable.AddField2 "KART_TURU_2", "CHAR",1,0,"L","","|KARTTIPI"
	MTable.AddField2 "KART_TURU_3", "CHAR",1,0,"L","","|KARTTIPI"
	
	MTable.IHTIYAC = "E"
	Call GetQStr123(QStr1, QStr2, QStr3)
	QStr = QStr1 & QStr2 & QStr3 & " AND 1=0"

	Set M10T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("MPS_HMD_LIST1", QStr)

	Call F2leriDegistir()
End Sub
Sub cmdMPSHammaddeListesiHazirla()
	Set ggg = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MPS_KRITER")
	Call GetQStr123(QStr1, QStr2, QStr3)
	if trim(MTable.B_EVRAKNO) = trim(MTable.E_EVRAKNO) and trim(MTable.B_EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
		QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T1.EVRAKNO IN (SELECT EVRAKNO FROM TABLE(BAGLANTI.GetBaglantiliMPSList('UP', '" & MTable.B_EVRAKNO & "',' ')))"
	Else
		QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T1.EVRAKNO>='" & MTable.B_EVRAKNO & "'"
		if trim(MTable.E_EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
			QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T1.EVRAKNO<='" & MTable.E_EVRAKNO & "'"
		End if
	End if
	
	QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND E.MUSTERISIPINO>='" & MTable.B_MUSTERISIPINO & "'"
	if trim(MTable.E_MUSTERISIPINO) <> "" Then
		QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND E.MUSTERISIPINO<='" & MTable.E_MUSTERISIPINO & "'"
	End if

	QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND E.MUSTERIKODU>='" & MTable.B_MUSTERIKODU & "'"
	if trim(MTable.E_MUSTERIKODU) <> "" Then
		QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND E.MUSTERIKODU<='" & MTable.E_MUSTERIKODU & "'"
	End if

	QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T1.R_KAYNAKKODU>='" & MTable.B_R_KAYNAKKODU & "'"
	if trim(MTable.E_R_KAYNAKKODU) <> "" Then
		QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T1.R_KAYNAKKODU<='" & MTable.E_R_KAYNAKKODU & "'"
	End if
	
	QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T1.R_LATEST_B_TARIH>='" & MTable.B_R_LATEST_B_TARIH & "'"
	if trim(MTable.E_R_LATEST_B_TARIH) <> "" Then
		QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T1.R_LATEST_B_TARIH<='" & MTable.E_R_LATEST_B_TARIH & "'"
	End if

	QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T1.R_MAMULKODU>='" & MTable.B_R_MAMULKODU & "'"
	if trim(MTable.E_R_MAMULKODU) <> "" Then
		QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T1.R_MAMULKODU<='" & MTable.E_R_MAMULKODU & "'"
	End if

	InStr1 = ""
	For i = 1 to 3
		KartTuru = MTable.GetFieldValue("KART_TURU_" & i, 1)
		if trim(KartTuru) <> "" Then 
			if trim(InStr1) <> "" Then InStr1 = InStr1 & ","
			InStr1 = InStr1 & "'" & KartTuru & "'"
		End if
	Next
	if trim(InStr1) <> "" Then
		QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND S00.KART_TIPI IN (" & InStr1 & ")"
	End if
	

	InStr1 = ""
	For i = 1 to 3
		Statu = MTable.GetFieldValue("R_STATUS" & i, 1)
		if trim(Statu) <> "" Then 
			if trim(InStr1) <> "" Then InStr1 = InStr1 & ","
			InStr1 = InStr1 & "'" & Statu & "'"
		End if
	Next
	if trim(InStr1) <> "" Then
		QStr3 = QStr3 & " AND (CASE WHEN T.ALT_STATUS IS NOT NULL THEN T.ALT_STATUS ELSE M10TM1.STATUS END) IN (" & InStr1 & ")"
	End if

'Doc.d7MsgBox QStr1 & QStr2 & QStr3, "", MB_OK
	Set MTSTable = Doc.RunSQLQuery("fgsdfjsj", QStr1 & QStr2 & QStr3)

	Set M10T = Doc.GetTableObject("MPS_HMD_LIST1")
	M10T.Empty

	if UCase(trim(MTable.IHTIYAC)) = "E" Then
		Set MTSTable = Doc.Select2("sdfdsd", MTSTable.GetAppName, "DEPO_HARIC='E'", "", -1)
	End if

	M10T.AppendTable(MTSTable)
End Sub
Sub GetQStr123(QStr1, QStr2, QStr3)
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MPS_KRITER")
	QStr1 = "SELECT 'H' SELECTED, T.R_KAYNAKKODU, SH.AD HAMMADDE_AD, T.R_MIKTAR0, "
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " (T.R_HM_ODONEMNET + T.R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN) R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN, (T.R_MIKTAR0 - T.R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN - T.R_HM_ODONEMNET) BAKIYE_HMD, T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK, SH.IUNIT HMD_UNIT, SH.NAME2 HMD_AD2,"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " S01_MERKEZ.SF_MIKTAR MERKEZ_STOK, S01_MERKEZ.LOKASYON MERKEZ_LOKASYON, S01_IMLT.SF_MIKTAR IMALAT_STOK, S01_IMLT.LOKASYON IMALAT_LOKASYON, "
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " S01_KK.SF_MIKTAR KK_STOK, S47T.EVRAKNO TALEPNO, S47T.RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH TALEP_TESTARIH, S46T.REV_RTESTARIH ST_TESTARIH, "
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " S46T.EVRAKNO ST_SIP_NO, T.MAMUL_MPS_LIST,"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " (CASE WHEN T.ALT_MPS IS NOT NULL THEN T.ALT_MPS ELSE M10TM1.EVRAKNO END) MPS,"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " (CASE WHEN T.ALT_STATUS IS NOT NULL THEN T.ALT_STATUS ELSE M10TM1.STATUS END) MPS_STATUS, M10TH.MPS_IHTIYAC,"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " (CASE WHEN T.ALT_TESTARIH IS NOT NULL THEN T.ALT_TESTARIH ELSE M10TM1.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH END) MPS_TESTARIH,"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " (CASE WHEN COALESCE(S01_MERKEZ.SF_MIKTAR,0) + COALESCE(S01_KK.SF_MIKTAR,0) - (T.R_MIKTAR0 - T.R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN - T.R_HM_ODONEMNET) >= 0 THEN 'H' ELSE 'E' END) DEPO_HARIC"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " FROM ("
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 		SELECT T1.R_KAYNAKKODU, SUM(T1.R_MIKTAR0) R_MIKTAR0, SUM(T1.R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN+T1.R_HM_ODONEMNET) R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN, "
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 		SUM(T1.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK) R_BAKIYEKAYNAK, TM.EVRAKNO ALT_MPS, TM.STATUS ALT_STATUS, TM.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH ALT_TESTARIH,"
'	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 		(LISTAGG(TRIM(T1.EVRAKNO), ',') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY T1.EVRAKNO)  WHERE ) MAMUL_MPS_LIST"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " TAB_TO_STRING(cast (collect(DISTINCT TRIM(T1.EVRAKNO)) as eno_type)) MAMUL_MPS_LIST"
	'QStr1 = QStr1 & " 		' ' MAMUL_MPS_LIST"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 		FROM MMPS10T T1"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 		LEFT JOIN STOK00 S00 ON S00.KOD=T1.R_KAYNAKKODU"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 		LEFT JOIN MMPS10E E ON E.EVRAKNO=T1.EVRAKNO"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 		LEFT JOIN MMPS10TM TM ON TM.KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE='UP' AND TM.KAYNAKMPS=T1.EVRAKNO AND TM.KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM=T1.TRNUM"
	
	QStr2 = " 		WHERE T1.R_ACIK_KAPALI=' ' AND T1.ACIK_KAPALI=' ' AND T1.R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'"
	QStr2 = QStr2 & " 		AND (T1.R_MIKTAR0 - T1.R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN - T1.R_HM_ODONEMNET) > 0	"	

	QStr3 = " 		GROUP BY T1.R_KAYNAKKODU, TM.EVRAKNO,  TM.STATUS, TM.REVURETIMDENTESTARIH"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " 	) T"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 SH ON SH.KOD=T.R_KAYNAKKODU"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " LEFT JOIN ("
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   SELECT KOD, Sum(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR, "
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   LISTAGG(trim(LOCATION1) || '-' || trim(LOCATION2) || '-' || trim(LOCATION3) || '-' || trim(LOCATION4), ',') WITHIN GROUP "
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   (ORDER BY LOCATION1,LOCATION2,LOCATION3,LOCATION4) LOKASYON"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   FROM ("
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   SELECT KOD, Sum(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR, LOCATION1, LOCATION2, LOCATION3, LOCATION4"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   FROM STOK01"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   WHERE AMBCODE='02'"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   GROUP BY KOD, LOCATION1, LOCATION2, LOCATION3, LOCATION4"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   )"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   GROUP BY KOD"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " ) S01_IMLT on S01_IMLT.KOD=T.R_KAYNAKKODU"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " LEFT JOIN ("
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   SELECT KOD, Sum(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR, "
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   LISTAGG(trim(LOCATION1) || '-' || trim(LOCATION2) || '-' || trim(LOCATION3) || '-' || trim(LOCATION4), ',') WITHIN GROUP "
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   (ORDER BY LOCATION1,LOCATION2,LOCATION3,LOCATION4) LOKASYON"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   FROM ("
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   SELECT KOD, Sum(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR, LOCATION1, LOCATION2, LOCATION3, LOCATION4"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   FROM STOK01"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   WHERE AMBCODE='01'"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   GROUP BY KOD, LOCATION1, LOCATION2, LOCATION3, LOCATION4"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   )"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   GROUP BY KOD"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " ) S01_MERKEZ on S01_MERKEZ.KOD=T.R_KAYNAKKODU"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " LEFT JOIN ("
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   SELECT KOD, Sum(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   FROM STOK01"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   WHERE AMBCODE='00'"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   GROUP BY KOD"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " ) S01_KK on S01_KK.KOD=T.R_KAYNAKKODU"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " LEFT JOIN (SELECT KOD,  TO_CHAR(TO_DATE(SUBSTR(EVRAKNO,1,10),'YYYY/MM/DD'),'DD/MM/YYYY') RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH, SUBSTR(EVRAKNO,11,10) EVRAKNO"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " 	FROM ("
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " 	SELECT KOD, MIN(RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH || EVRAKNO) EVRAKNO "
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   FROM STOK47T "
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   WHERE ACIKKAPALI<>'K'"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   GROUP BY KOD"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " )) S47T ON S47T.KOD=T.R_KAYNAKKODU"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " LEFT JOIN (SELECT KOD,  TO_CHAR(TO_DATE(SUBSTR(EVRAKNO,1,10),'YYYY/MM/DD'),'DD/MM/YYYY') REV_RTESTARIH, SUBSTR(EVRAKNO,11,10) EVRAKNO"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   FROM ("
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   SELECT KOD, MIN(REV_RTESTARIH || EVRAKNO) EVRAKNO "
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   FROM STOK46T "
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   WHERE ACIKKAPALI<>'K'"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   GROUP BY KOD"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " )) S46T ON S46T.KOD=T.R_KAYNAKKODU"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " LEFT JOIN (SELECT MAMULSTOKKODU, TO_CHAR(TO_DATE(SUBSTR(ET, 1,10),'YYYY/MM/DD'),'DD/MM/YYYY') REVURETIMDENTESTARIH, "
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " 	SUBSTR(ET, 11,10) EVRAKNO, SUBSTR(ET, 21,1) STATUS"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " 	FROM ("
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   	SELECT MAMULSTOKKODU, MIN(REVURETIMDENTESTARIH||EVRAKNO||STATUS) ET"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   	FROM MMPS10TM"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   	WHERE SF_BAKIYEMIKTAR > 0 AND ACIK_KAPALI<>'K'"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & "   	GROUP BY MAMULSTOKKODU"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " )) M10TM1 ON M10TM1.MAMULSTOKKODU=T.R_KAYNAKKODU"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " LEFT JOIN (SELECT R_KAYNAKKODU, Sum(R_MIKTAR0-R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN-R_HM_ODONEMNET) MPS_IHTIYAC2, Sum(R_BAKIYEKAYNAK) MPS_IHTIYAC"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " 		FROM MMPS10T"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " 		WHERE R_KAYNAKTYPE='H' AND ACIK_KAPALI<>'K' AND R_ACIK_KAPALI<>'K'"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " 		AND R_LATEST_B_TARIH>='" & MTable.B_R_LATEST_B_TARIH & "'"
	if trim(MTable.E_R_LATEST_B_TARIH) <> "" Then
		QStr3 = QStr3 & " 		AND R_LATEST_B_TARIH<='" & MTable.E_R_LATEST_B_TARIH & "'"
	End if
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " 		GROUP BY R_KAYNAKKODU"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " ) M10TH ON M10TH.R_KAYNAKKODU=T.R_KAYNAKKODU"
	QStr3 = QStr3 & " WHERE 1=1"
End Sub

Sub cmdSecilenMPSleriYazdir()
	Set UTY = Doc.Select1("asdfasdg", "UDEFTY", "KOD='" & Doc.Getd7UserName & "'&YAZICITURU='A4'", "", -1)
	if UTY.GetRowCount = 0 Then
		Doc.d7msgbox Doc.Getd7UserName & " kullanýcýsý için kullanýcý tanýmlarýnda A4 yazýcýsý seçilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set UTY = Doc.Select1("asdfasdg", "UDEFTY", "KOD='" & Doc.Getd7UserName & "'&YAZICITURU='BARKOD'", "", -1)
	if UTY.GetRowCount = 0 Then
		Doc.d7msgbox Doc.Getd7UserName & " kullanýcýsý için kullanýcý tanýmlarýnda BARKOD yazýcýsý seçilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set MPSList1 = Doc.Select2G("sdgsdfhsjs", "MPS_HMD_LIST1", "SELECTED='E'", "", "MPS", -1)
	if MPSList1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7msgBox "Hiçbir MPS seçilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set kkkk = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MMPS10", "")
	Set M10E = Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")

	For i = 1 to MPSList1.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "MPS 'ler yazdýrýlýyor : " & i & " / " & MPSList1.GetRowCount()
		Doc1.Load_Voucher MPSList1.MPS(i)
		M10E.STATUS = "R"
		M10E.NOTLAR_1 = GetMPSNotu(trim(MPSList1.MAMUL_MPS_LIST(i)))
		if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
			Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.MMPS10", "PrintForm"
			Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-YM", "PrintLabel"
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MPSList1.MPS(i)) & " no.lu MPS kaydedilemedi", "", MB_OK
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function GetMPSNotu(MPSList)
	GetMPSNotu = ""
	MPSArr = Split(MPSList, ",")
	if uBound(MPSArr) < 0 Then Exit Function
	QStr = "SELECT C.AD MUSTERI, S.AD MAMUL"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM MMPS10TM TM"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN CARI00 C ON C.KOD=TM.MUSTERIKODU"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S ON S.KOD=TM.MAMULSTOKKODU"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE TM.EVRAKNO='" & Doc.RightJustify(trim(MPSArr(0)),10) & "'"
	Set M10TM = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfgsdfsj", QStr)
	if M10TM.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	GetMPSNotu = trim(Left(M10TM.MUSTERI(1), 20)) & " - " & trim(M10TM.MAMUL(1))
End Function
Sub F2leriDegistir()
	Call F2Degistir("MPS_HMD_LIST1", "MPS", "MMPS10E")
	Call F2Degistir("MPS_HMD_LIST1", "TALEPNO", "STOK47E")
	Call F2Degistir("MPS_HMD_LIST1", "ST_SIP_NO", "STOK46E")
	Call F2Degistir("MPS_HMD_LIST1", "R_KAYNAKKODU", "STOK00")
'	Call F2Degistir("MPS_HMD_LIST1", "R_MAMULKODU", "STOK00")
End Sub
Sub F2Degistir(TabloStr, AlanAdi, ByVal BrwTable)
	BrwTable = "'" & BrwTable & "'"
	Set tbl=doc.GetRowsetObject(TabloStr)
	Set fd=tbl.GetFieldDef_E(AlanAdi)
	fd.F2Fnc="DO_BRW(" & Replace(BrwTable,"'", """") & ")"
	tbl.SetFieldProperties1 AlanAdi,fd 
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : FORMA_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : RUNSQL
Hangi Tablo için : 
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Function RunSQLQuery2(QStr)
	Set Oconn = CreateFirmaOLEDBConnection()
	Oconn.Open

	Set cmd = CreateObject("ADODB.Command")
	Set cmd.ActiveConnection = Oconn
		
	cmd.CommandText = QStr

	Set RunSQLQuery2 = cmd.Execute(, , adExecuteNoRecords)
	Set cmd = Nothing
	Oconn.Close
End Function
Function CreateFirmaOLEDBConnection()
	Set Oconn = CreateObject("ADODB.Connection")
	cnnStr = GetFirmaOLEDBConnectionStr()
	Oconn.ConnectionString = cnnStr
	Set CreateFirmaOLEDBConnection = Oconn
End Function
Function GetFirmaOLEDBConnectionStr()
	GetFirmaOLEDBConnectionStr = ""

	Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
	if Doc.IsSQL Then
		ConnStrHeader = "ConnectionString="
		Dosya = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & "\Entprd.ini"
	Else
		Dosya = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataRoot() & "\freedom.ini"
		ConnStrHeader = "DefaultConnectionString="
	End if
	If Not fso.FileExists(Dosya) Then Exit Function

	Set TFile = fso.OpenTextFile(Dosya, 1)
	SettingsBulundu = False
	While Not TFile.AtEndOfStream
		satirStr = TFile.ReadLine
		If UCase(Trim(satirStr)) = "[SETTINGS]" Then
			SettingsBulundu = True
		End If
		If SettingsBulundu And InStr(1, satirStr, ConnStrHeader) = 1 Then
			ConnStr = Mid(satirStr, Len(ConnStrHeader) + 1)
			if Doc.IsSQL Then
		    	FirmaDonem = Doc.Getd7FirmCode
		    	Arr1 = Split(FirmaDonem, "_")
				if uBound(Arr1) = 1 Then
		     	Firma = LCase(Arr1(0))
     			Donem = Arr1(1)
					ConnStr = Replace(ConnStr, "@@@FIRMA@@@", Firma)
					ConnStr = Replace(ConnStr, "@@@DONEM@@@", Donem)
				End if
			Else
				ConnStr = Replace(ConnStr, "@@@DBNAME@@@", Doc.Getd7FirmCode)
			End if
		End If
	Wend
	GetFirmaOLEDBConnectionStr = ConnStr
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	Call KesinOngoruIsaretle()
End Sub
Sub KesinOngoruIsaretle()
	Set C00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdafhah", "SELECT B_B_TECH_1 FROM CARI00 WHERE KOD='" & STOK46E.CH_KODU & "'")
	if C00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	KesinSiparisPeriyodu = C00.B_B_TECH_1(1)
	if KesinSiparisPeriyodu = 0 Then Exit Sub

	SonTarih = Doc.StrDate_Increment(STOK46E.TARIH, KesinSiparisPeriyodu)
	For i = 1 to STOK46T.GetRowCount()
		if STOK46T.REV_RTESTARIH(i) <= SonTarih Then
			STOK46T.SPSTATUSU(i) = "K"
		Else
			STOK46T.SPSTATUSU(i) = "T"
		End if
	Next
End Sub

Sub CHAdlariniGetir()
	Set SUGG02TAS = Doc.GetTableObject("SUGG02TAS")

		SUGG02TAS.CH_ADI(i) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", SUGG02TAS.CHKOD(i), "")
	Next

Açýklama : Ekrandaki Stok Kartýnýn MRP Analiz Bilgileri ve Alýþveriþ Kütüphanesi Sil
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	if Doc.d7MsgBox(trim(STOK00.KOD) & " stok koduna ait analiz sonuçlarý ve Aliþveriþ Kütüphanesi bilgilerini silmek istediðinize emin misiniz", "", MB_YESNO) <> 6 Then Exit Sub

	QStr = "DELETE FROM SUGG02T WHERE SKOD='" & STOK00.KOD & "'"
	Doc.RunMacro1 "MAKR7S.RUNSQL", "RunSQLQuery2", QStr

	QStr = "DELETE FROM STOK7XT WHERE KOD='" & STOK00.KOD & "'"
	Doc.RunMacro1 "MAKR7S.RUNSQL", "RunSQLQuery2", QStr

	QStr2 = "SELECT * FROM SUGG02T WHERE SKOD='" & STOK00.KOD & "'"
	Set S2T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfgsdfjsj", QStr2)

	QStr2 = "SELECT * FROM STOK7XT WHERE KOD='" & STOK00.KOD & "'"
	Set S7XT = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfgsdfjsj", QStr2)

	if S2T.GetRowCount() = 0 and S7XT.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Ýþlem Tamamlandý", "", MB_OK
	End if
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set LISTEE = Doc.CreateTableObject("LISTEE=STACK", "IO")
	LISTEE.AddField2 "B_EVRAKNO", "CHAR", 10, 0, "R", "F2=DO_BRW('KTT_KDV_IADE')", ""
	LISTEE.AddField2 "E_EVRAKNO", "CHAR", 10, 0, "R", "F2=DO_BRW('KTT_KDV_IADE')", ""
	LISTEE.AddField2 "B_TARIH", "DATE", 10, 0, "L", "", ""
	LISTEE.AddField2 "E_TARIH", "DATE", 10, 0, "L", "", ""

	Set LISTET = Doc.RunSQLQuery("LISTET", "SELECT EVRAKNO, TARIH, NOTES FROM KTT_KDV_IADE WHERE 1=0")
End Sub
Sub cmdFaturaHammaddeListesi()
	Set KTT_KDV_IADE = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_KDV_IADE")
	Set KTT_KDV_IADT = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_KDV_IADT")	

	QStr = "SELECT T.EVRAKNO, T.TARIH, T.KOD, S.AD KOD_STOK00_AD, T.FOKOD, Sum(T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR) SF_STOK_MIKTAR, T.CH_KODU, C.AD CH_KODU_CARI00_AD "
	QStr = QStr & " FROM STOK70T T"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN CARI00 C ON C.KOD=T.CH_KODU"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S ON S.KOD=T.KOD"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE IADEMI<>'1'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND TARIH>='" & KTT_KDV_IADE.B_TARIH & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND TARIH<='" & KTT_KDV_IADE.E_TARIH & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY T.EVRAKNO, T.TARIH, T.KOD, S.AD,T.FOKOD, T.CH_KODU, C.AD"

	Set S70T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("asdfawdqw4fesr", QStr)
	Set Tbl1 = GetFaturaUrunAgaciList(S70T)

	KTT_KDV_IADT.Empty
	For i = 1 to Tbl1.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = KTT_KDV_IADT.AddRow

		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & Tbl1.GetRowCount()
		KTT_KDV_IADT.FATURA_NO(satirNo) = Tbl1.FATURA_NO(i)
		KTT_KDV_IADT.FATURA_TRN(satirNo) = Tbl1.FATURA_TRN(i)
		KTT_KDV_IADT.FAT_TARIH(satirNo) = Tbl1.FAT_TARIH(i)
		KTT_KDV_IADT.MAMUL_KOD(satirNo) = Tbl1.MAMUL_KOD(i)
		KTT_KDV_IADT.MAMUL_AD(satirNo) = Tbl1.MAMUL_AD(i)
		KTT_KDV_IADT.FATURA_MIK(satirNo) = Tbl1.FATURA_MIK(i)
		KTT_KDV_IADT.CH_KODU(satirNo) = Tbl1.CH_KODU(i)
		KTT_KDV_IADT.CH_ADI(satirNo) = Tbl1.CH_ADI(i)
		KTT_KDV_IADT.FOKOD(satirNo) = Tbl1.FOKOD(i)
		KTT_KDV_IADT.HMD_KODU(satirNo) = Tbl1.HMD_KODU(i)
		KTT_KDV_IADT.HMD_ADI(satirNo) = Tbl1.HMD_ADI(i)
		KTT_KDV_IADT.HMD_MIKTAR(satirNo) = Tbl1.HMD_MIKTAR(i)
		KTT_KDV_IADT.HMD_BIRIM(satirNo) = Tbl1.HMD_BIRIM(i)
	Next
	Doc.DeleteTableObject Tbl1.GetAppName
	Call SatinalmaFaturaBaglantilariniHesapla()
End Sub
Function GetFaturaUrunAgaciList(FaturaTable)
	sET GHHHH = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	FaturaNo = ""
	FaturaTrNum = ""
	StokKodu = "asdfqq4tsege"
	StokAdi = ""
	FOKod = ""
	FaturaMiktari = 1
	CHKodu = "asd"
	CHAdi = "sdsdf"
	Tarih = ""
	Set GetFaturaUrunAgaciList = GetFaturaUrunAgaciListTr("FATURA_HMD_LIST2", FaturaNo, FaturaTrNum, Tarih, StokKodu, StokAdi, FOKod, FaturaMiktari, CHKodu, CHAdi)

	GetFaturaUrunAgaciList.Empty
	For i = 1 to FaturaTable.GetRowCount()
		'if i = 122 Then Doc.d7MsgBox i & " , " & StokKodu & ", " & StokAdi, "", MB_OK
		
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Ürün Aðaçlarý Hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & FaturaTable.GetRowCount()
		FaturaNo = FaturaTable.EVRAKNO(i)
		'FaturaTrNum = FaturaTable.TRNUM(i)
		FaturaTrNum = ""
		Tarih = FaturaTable.TARIH(i)
		StokKodu = FaturaTable.KOD(i)
		StokAdi = GetStokAdi(FaturaTable.KOD_STOK00_AD(i))
		FOKod = FaturaTable.FOKOD(i)
		FaturaMiktari = FaturaTable.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(i)
		CHKodu = FaturaTable.CH_KODU(i)
		CHAdi = FaturaTable.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD(i)

		Set BOMList = GetFaturaUrunAgaciListTr("sdfaeasg", FaturaNo, FaturaTrNum, Tarih, StokKodu, StokAdi, FOKod, FaturaMiktari, CHKodu, CHAdi)
'		if i = 122 Then 
'			'msgbox BOMList.GetRowCount()
'			Exit Function
'		End if
		GetFaturaUrunAgaciList.AppendTable(BOMList)
	Next
End Function
Function GetFaturaUrunAgaciListTr(TblStr, ByVal FaturaNo, ByVal FaturaTrNum, ByVal Tarih, ByVal StokKodu, ByVal StokAdi, ByVal FOKod, ByVal FaturaMiktari, ByVal CHKodu, ByVal CHAdi)	
	FaturaNo = Doc.RightJustify(trim(FaturaNo), 10)
	FaturaTrNum = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(FaturaTrNum),6)
	Tarih = Doc.RightJustify(trim(Tarih), 10)	
	StokKodu = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(StokKodu),24)
	StokAdi = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(StokAdi),50)
	FOKod = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(FOKod),6)
	CHKodu = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(CHKodu),16)
	CHAdi = Doc.LeftJustify(trim(CHAdi),50)
	
	QStr = "SELECT '" & FaturaNo & "' FATURA_NO, '" & FaturaTrNum & "' FATURA_TRN, '" & Tarih & "' FAT_TARIH, "
	QStr = QStr & " TOP_BOMREC_CODE MAMUL_KOD, '" & StokAdi & "' MAMUL_AD, Avg(TOP_BOMREC_CODE_MIK) FATURA_MIK, "
	QStr = QStr & "'" & CHKodu & "' CH_KODU, '" & CHAdi & "' CH_ADI, '" & FOKod & "' FOKOD," 
	QStr = QStr & " T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE HMD_KODU, S0.AD HMD_ADI, ROUND(Sum(T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0),3) HMD_MIKTAR, S0.IUNIT HMD_BIRIM"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM TABLE(CALC_BOM_PKG.CALC_BOM('" & StokKodu & "','" & FOKod & "'," & FaturaMiktari & ")) T"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S0 ON S0.KOD=T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND T.IALTSEVIYEBULUNDU=0"
	QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY TOP_BOMREC_CODE, T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE,S0.AD,S0.IUNIT"
'if trim(StokKodu) = "150 141 02500" Then
'	Doc.d7MsgBox QStr, "", MB_OK
'End if
	Set GetFaturaUrunAgaciListTr = Doc.RunSQLQuery(TblStr, QStr)
End Function
Sub SatinalmaFaturaBaglantilariniHesapla()
	Set ggg = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")	
	if Doc.IsBlank(KTT_KDV_IAST) Then Set KTT_KDV_IAST = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_KDV_IAST")
	if Doc.IsBlank(KTT_KDV_IADT) Then Set KTT_KDV_IADT = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_KDV_IADT")
	
	Set FatList = Doc.Select2("sdfsdah", "KTT_KDV_IADT", "", "HMD_KODU+FAT_TARIH", -1)

	KTT_KDV_IAST.Empty
	i = 1
	EskiHmdKodu = ""
	Do While i <= FatList.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Satýnalma Fatura Baðlantýlarý Hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & FatList.GetRowCount()
		if FatList.HMD_KODU(i) <> EskiHmdKodu Then
			Set SFTable = GetSFTable(FatList.HMD_KODU(i))
			j = 1
			if SFTable.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
				Doc.d7msgBox "Satýþ Fatura No : " & trim(FatList.EVRAKNO(i)) & ", Hammadde Kodu : " & trim(FatList.HMD_KODU(i)) & _
						" için satýnalma faturasý bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
			End if
		End if
		EskiHmdKodu = FatList.HMD_KODU(i)
		FaturaHammaddeMiktari = FatList.HMD_MIKTAR(i)
		Do While j <= SFTable.GetRowCount()

			if SFTable.EVRAKNO(j) <> EskiFaturaNo Then
				SFKDVTutari = SFTable.TL_KDVTUT_EFEKTIF(j)
				SFMiktari = SFTable.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(j)
			End if
			EskiFaturaNo = SFTable.EVRAKNO(j)
			if SFTable.TARIH(j) <= FatList.FAT_TARIH(i) Then
				if SFTable.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(j) < FatList.HMD_MIKTAR(i) Then
					DagitilacakMiktar = SFTable.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(j)
				Else
					DagitilacakMiktar = FatList.HMD_MIKTAR(i)
				End if

				satirNo = KTT_KDV_IAST.AddRow
				KTT_KDV_IAST.FATURA_NO(satirNo) = FatList.FATURA_NO(i)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.FATURA_TRN(satirNo) = FatList.FATURA_TRN(i)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.FAT_TARIH(satirNo) = FatList.FAT_TARIH(i)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.MAMUL_KOD(satirNo) = FatList.MAMUL_KOD(i)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.MAMUL_AD(satirNo) = FatList.MAMUL_AD(i)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.FATURA_MIK(satirNo) = FatList.FATURA_MIK(i)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.CH_KODU(satirNo) = FatList.CH_KODU(i)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.CH_ADI(satirNo) = FatList.CH_ADI(i)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.FOKOD(satirNo) = FatList.FOKOD(i)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.FIRMA(satirNo) = SFTable.FIRMA(j)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.SF_EVNO(satirNo) = SFTable.EVRAKNO(j)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.SF_EXTEVNO(satirNo) = SFTable.OR_FATURASIRANO(j)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.SF_TRNUM(satirNo) = SFTable.TRNUM(j)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.SF_TARIH(satirNo) = SFTable.TARIH(j)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.SF_CH_KODU(satirNo) = SFTable.CH_KODU(j)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.SF_CH_ADI(satirNo) = SFTable.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD(j)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.HMD_KODU(satirNo) = FatList.HMD_KODU(i)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.HMD_ADI(satirNo) = FatList.HMD_ADI(i)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.HMD_MIKTAR(satirNo) = DagitilacakMiktar
				KTT_KDV_IAST.HMD_BIRIM(satirNo) = FatList.HMD_BIRIM(i)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.SF_TLFIYAT(satirNo) = SFTable.TL_FIYAT(j)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.SF_ORFIYAT(satirNo) = SFTable.OR_FIYAT(j)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.SF_PUNIT(satirNo) = SFTable.PRICEUNIT(j)
				KTT_KDV_IAST.KDV_ORANI(satirNo) = SFTable.KDV_ORANI(j)
				if DagitilacakMiktar = SFTable.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(j) Then
					KDVTutari = SFTable.TL_KDVTUT_EFEKTIF(j)
				Else
					KDVTutari = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(DagitilacakMiktar, SFMiktari) * SFKDVTutari,2)
				End if
				KTT_KDV_IAST.KDV_TUTARI(satirNo) = KDVTutari

				FatList.HMD_MIKTAR(i) = FatList.HMD_MIKTAR(i) - DagitilacakMiktar
				SFTable.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(j) = SFTable.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(j) - DagitilacakMiktar
				SFTable.TL_KDVTUT_EFEKTIF(j) = SFTable.TL_KDVTUT_EFEKTIF(j) - KDVTutari
				
				if SFTable.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(j) = 0 Then j = j + 1
				if j = SFTable.GetRowCount() and FatList.HMD_MIKTAR(i) > 0 Then
					Doc.d7msgBox "Satýþ Fatura No : " & trim(FatList.EVRAKNO(i)) & ", Hammadde Kodu : " & trim(FatList.HMD_KODU(i)) & _
							", Hammadde Miktarý :  " & FaturaHammaddeMiktari & " " &  trim(FatList.HMD_BIRIM(i)) & _
							", satýnalma faturasýna baðlanamayan miktar : " & FatList.HMD_MIKTAR(i) & " " &  trim(FatList.HMD_BIRIM(i)), "", MB_OK
				End if
				if FatList.HMD_MIKTAR(i) = 0 Then Exit Do
			Else
				j = j + 1
			End if
		Loop
		i = i + 1
	Loop

	KTT_KDV_IAST.Sort("FATURA_NO+MAMUL_KOD")
End Sub
Function GetSFTable(HammaddeKodu)
'Set ggg = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")	
	if Doc.IsBlank(KTT_KDV_IADE) Then Set KTT_KDV_IADE = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_KDV_IADE")
	QStr = "SELECT FIRMA, EVRAKNO, OR_FATURASIRANO, TRNUM, TARIH, CH_KODU, CH_KODU_CARI00_AD,"
	QStr = QStr & " Sum(SF_STOK_MIKTAR) SF_STOK_MIKTAR, Sum(TL_FIYAT) TL_FIYAT, Sum(OR_FIYAT) OR_FIYAT, PRICEUNIT,"
	QStr = QStr & " Sum(TL_KDVTUT_EFEKTIF) TL_KDVTUT_EFEKTIF, Sum(KDV_ORANI) KDV_ORANI"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM ("
	QStr = QStr & " 	SELECT USER FIRMA, T.EVRAKNO, E.OR_FATURASIRANO, T.TRNUM, T.TARIH, T.CH_KODU, C.AD CH_KODU_CARI00_AD, "
	QStr = QStr & " 	T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR, T.SF_MIKTAR / T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR *T.TL_FIYAT TL_FIYAT , T.TL_KDVTUT_EFEKTIF, T.KDV_ORANI, T.SF_MIKTAR / T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR *T.OR_FIYAT OR_FIYAT, T.PRICEUNIT"
	QStr = QStr & " 	FROM STOK74T T "
	QStr = QStr & " 	LEFT JOIN STOK74E E ON E.EVRAKNO=T.EVRAKNO"
	QStr = QStr & " 	LEFT JOIN CARI00 C ON C.KOD=T.CH_KODU"
	QStr = QStr & " 	WHERE T.KOD='" & HammaddeKodu & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " 	AND T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR>0"
	QStr = QStr & GetOncekiYilSatinalmaStr()
	QStr = QStr & " 	UNION ALL"
	QStr = QStr & " 	SELECT USER FIRMA, T.SF_EVNO, T.SF_EXTEVNO, T.SF_TRNUM, T.SF_TARIH, T.SF_CH_KODU, T.SF_CH_ADI,"
	QStr = QStr & " 	T.HMD_MIKTAR * -1, 0 TL_FIYAT, 0 KDV_ORANI, -1 * T.KDV_TUTARI KDV_TUTARI, T.SF_ORFIYAT, T.SF_PUNIT"
	QStr = QStr & " 	FROM KTT_KDV_IAST T "
	QStr = QStr & " 	WHERE T.HMD_KODU='" & HammaddeKodu & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " 	AND EVRAKNO<>'" & KTT_KDV_IADE.EVRAKNO & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " )"
	QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY FIRMA, EVRAKNO, OR_FATURASIRANO, TRNUM, TARIH, CH_KODU, CH_KODU_CARI00_AD, PRICEUNIT"
	QStr = QStr & " HAVING SUM(SF_STOK_MIKTAR)>0"
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY TARIH, EVRAKNO"

'Doc.d7msgBox QStr, "", MB_OK
	Set GetSFTable = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfwdargase", QStr)
End Function
Function GetStokAdi(StokAdi)
	GetStokAdi = StokAdi
	GetStokAdi = Replace(GetStokAdi, "'", "''")	
End Function
Function GetOncekiYilSatinalmaStr()
	GetOncekiYilSatinalmaStr = ""
	OncekiYil = Doc.Customp_Gets("_DV_OFR")
	if trim(OncekiYil) = "" Then Exit Function

	QStr = "SELECT TABLE_NAME FROM ALL_TABLES WHERE OWNER='" & trim(OncekiYil) & "' AND TABLE_NAME='STOK74T'"
	Set Tbl = Doc.RunSQLQuery("asdfsfhs", QStr)
	if Tbl.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	
	QStr = " 	UNION ALL"
	QStr = QStr & " 	SELECT  '" & trim(OncekiYil) & "' FIRMA, T.EVRAKNO, E.OR_FATURASIRANO, T.TRNUM, T.TARIH, T.CH_KODU, C.AD CH_KODU_CARI00_AD, "
	QStr = QStr & " 	T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR, T.SF_MIKTAR / T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR *T.TL_FIYAT TL_FIYAT , T.TL_KDVTUT_EFEKTIF, T.KDV_ORANI, T.SF_MIKTAR / T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR *T.OR_FIYAT OR_FIYAT, T.PRICEUNIT"
	QStr = QStr & " 	FROM " & OncekiYil & ".STOK74T T "
	QStr = QStr & " 	LEFT JOIN STOK74E E ON E.EVRAKNO=T.EVRAKNO"
	QStr = QStr & " 	LEFT JOIN CARI00 C ON C.KOD=T.CH_KODU"
	QStr = QStr & " 	WHERE T.KOD='" & HammaddeKodu & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " 	AND T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR>0"
	GetOncekiYilSatinalmaStr = QStr
End Function

  EDITTEXT D="KOD"  CD=1 CDF="%6"  BROWSEBUTTON=1
  EDITTEXT D="KOD_STOK00_AD"  CD=1 CDF="%6"  BROWSEBUTTON=1
  EDITTEXT D="PRICE"  CD=1 CDF="%6" 
  COMBOBOX D="PRICEUNIT" CD=1 CDF="%6" 
  COMBOBOX D="SF_UNIT" CD=1 CDF="%6" 
  COMBOBOX D="VR_C" CD=1 CDF="%6" 
  EDITTEXT D="VR_R1" CD=1 CDF="%6" BROWSEBUTTON=1
  EDITTEXT D="VR_R2" CD=1 CDF="%6" BROWSEBUTTON=1
  EDITTEXT D="VR_R3" CD=1 CDF="%6" BROWSEBUTTON=1

  EDITTEXT D="REF_CHKODU" CD=1 CDF="%6" BROWSEBUTTON=1

  COMBOBOX D="GK_1" CD=1 CDF="%4" 
  COMBOBOX D="GK_2" CD=1 CDF="%4" 
  COMBOBOX D="GK_3" CD=1 CDF="%4" 

  COMBOBOX D="REF_TARIH_1" CD=1 CDF="%6" 
  COMBOBOX D="REF_TARIH_2" CD=1 CDF="%6" 
  COMBOBOX D="REF_TARIH_3" CD=1 CDF="%6" 

  EDITTEXT D="REF_NUMBER_1" CD=1 CDF="%6" 
  EDITTEXT D="REF_NUMBER_2" CD=1 CDF="%6" 
  EDITTEXT D="REF_NUMBER_3" CD=1 CDF="%6" 

  EDITTEXT D="REF_NOTES" CD=1 CDF="%6" 

  COMBOBOX D=KRI_TARIH_1
  COMBOBOX D=KRI_TARIH_2
  EDITTEXT D=KRI_FOKOD RBUTTON_POPUPMENU=FOEDIT

  EDITTEXT D="D_ID" CD=1 BROWSEBUTTON=1
  EDITTEXT D="D_ID_KAYNAK" W=40p CD=1

 ENDGRID
ENDTAB



ENDFORM

Sub kdv_listesi()
	on error resume next		
	F_SAY = 0 	
	Dim MTableT, SAYAC_1
	SAYAC_1 = 1
	SER = 1	
	Set MTableT = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STACK","IO")

	MTableT.Addfield2 "KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "KOD_STOK00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "LOTNUMBER", "CHAR",12,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "EVRAKTYPE", "CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SERINO", "CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OKUNDU", "CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "ORAN_M2F2", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "KAYNAK_KOD", "CHAR",500,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SAYAC_K2", "DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "F_SAYAC", "DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "AYNI","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""

	Set MTableT_2 = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET_2=STACK","IO")
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "FAT_KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "FAT_SIRA", "CHAR",3,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "KOD_STOK00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "KAYNAK_KOD", "CHAR",500,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "SERINO", "CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","",""	
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "EVRAKTYPE", "CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_G", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_C", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "TL_FIYAT", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "TL_TUTAR", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "PRICE", "CHAR",2,0,"L","",""

	Set MTableT_3 = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET_3=STACK","IO")
	
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "FAT_KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "FAT_SIRA", "CHAR",3,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "KOD_STOK00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "KAYNAK_KOD", "CHAR",500,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "SERINO", "CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","",""	
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "EVRAKTYPE", "CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_G", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_C", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "TL_FIYAT", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "TL_TUTAR", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "PRICE", "CHAR",2,0,"L","",""

	
	Set KTT_KDVIE_A = Doc.GetTableobject("KTT_KDVIE")		
	evrakno = Doc.RightJustify(trim(KTT_KDVIE_A.FT_EVRAK),10)
	
	Set A_STOK70T = Doc.Select1("EWRWFS", "STOK70T", "EVRAKNO= '"&evrakno&"'", "SRNUM", - 1) 

	if A_STOK70T.GetRecCount() = 0 Then
		MSGBOX evrakno & " Nolu Fatura Bulunamýyor."
		Exit Sub
	End if 
	
	'**** FATURADAN OKU	
	for i = 4 To 4 'A_STOK70T.GetRecCount()
MSGBOX A_STOK70T.KOD(i)
		KOD = A_STOK70T.KOD(i)
		LOT = A_STOK70T.LOTNUMBER(i)
		SERI = A_STOK70T.SERINO(i)
				
		QSTR = " SELECT KOD, EVRAKNO, EVRAKTYPE, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, SUM(STOK_MIKTAR) STOK_MIKTAR FROM STOK10A  WHERE  KOD = '"& KOD &"' AND LOTNUMBER = '"& LOT &"' AND SERINO = '"& SERI &"'  "
		QSTR = QSTR & " AND EVRAKTYPE IN ('MG', 'SI', 'SF', 'FG', 'K2', 'DG', 'DT','0S')  AND STOK_MIKTAR > 0 "
		QSTR = QSTR & " GROUP BY KOD, EVRAKNO, EVRAKTYPE, LOTNUMBER, SERINO "
		Set A_STOK10A = Doc.RunSqlQuery("AAA", QSTR)

		for j = 1 To A_STOK10A.GetRecCount()
		
				satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
				MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				MTableT.KOD = A_STOK10A.KOD(j)
				MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", A_STOK10A.KOD(j), "")
				MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK10A.LOTNUMBER(j)
				MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)
				MTableT.SERINO = A_STOK10A.SERINO(j)
				MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK10A.EVRAKNO(j)
				MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = A_STOK10A.STOK_MIKTAR(j)
				MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = "-     " & i&"[" & trim(KOD) & "#- "  & trim(SERI) & " - "  & trim(A_STOK70T.EVRAKNO) & " - FT (" & A_STOK70T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(i) &"*"&MTableT.ORAN_M2F2& ")  ]" 
				if trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "DG" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "DT" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "0S" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "SF" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "SI" Then
					MTableT.OKUNDU =  "*"
				end if 
		next		
	next

	'***** 	TABLOYU AÇ
	icini_ac = 1 
	while  icini_ac = 1
		
		'**** ÜRETÝM FÝÞÝ ÝÇÝN
		
		for i = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount()

			if trim(MTableT.OKUNDU(i)) =  ""  AND trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "MG"  Then					
					Dim QSTR_MG	
				
					QSTR_MG = " SELECT ST20TI.EVRAKNO, ST20TI.KOD, ST20TI.LOTNUMBER,  ST20TI.SERINO, ST20TI.SF_STOK_MIKTAR FROM STOK20T "
					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & "  LEFT JOIN (SELECT EVRAKNO,KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, SUM(SF_STOK_MIKTAR)SF_STOK_MIKTAR,KARSITRNUM FROM STOK20TI "
					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & " WHERE STOK20TI.EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"' AND STOK20TI.BILGISATIRIE <> 'E' "
					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & " GROUP BY EVRAKNO,KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO,KARSITRNUM)ST20TI "
					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & " ON ST20TI.EVRAKNO = STOK20T.EVRAKNO AND ST20TI.KARSITRNUM = STOK20T.KARSITRNUM "
					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & " WHERE STOK20T.EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"' AND " 
					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & " STOK20T.KOD = '"& MTableT.KOD(i) &"' AND STOK20T.LOTNUMBER = '"& MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i) &"' AND STOK20T.SERINO = '"& MTableT.SERINO(i) &"'  "			 								
														                								
					MTableT.OKUNDU(i) =  "*"			
					Set A_STOK20TI = Doc.RunSqlQuery("MG" , QSTR_MG)	
					
					if A_STOK20TI.GetRecCount()	 > 0 Then 

						for j = 1 To A_STOK20TI.GetRecCount()		
						
							QSTR_MG_COUNT = " SELECT STOK20T.SERINO FROM STOK20TI"
							QSTR_MG_COUNT = QSTR_MG_COUNT & " LEFT JOIN STOK20T ON STOK20T.EVRAKNO = STOK20TI.EVRAKNO AND STOK20TI.KARSITRNUM  = STOK20T.KARSITRNUM "
							QSTR_MG_COUNT = QSTR_MG_COUNT & " WHERE STOK20TI.EVRAKNO ='"&A_STOK20TI.EVRAKNO(j)&"' AND STOK20TI.KOD = '"&A_STOK20TI.KOD(j)&"' AND STOK20TI.LOTNUMBER =  '"&A_STOK20TI.LOTNUMBER(j)&"' AND  STOK20TI.SERINO = '"&A_STOK20TI.SERINO(j)&"' "
							QSTR_MG_COUNT = QSTR_MG_COUNT & " GROUP BY STOK20T.SERINO"
							
							Set D_STOK20TI = Doc.RunSqlQuery("JKJKJMG" , QSTR_MG_COUNT )	
							satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
							MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
							MTableT.ORAN_M2F2 = D_STOK20TI.GetRecCount()	
							MTableT.KOD = A_STOK20TI.KOD(j)
							MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", A_STOK20TI.KOD(j), "")
							MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK20TI.LOTNUMBER(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = "M2"
							MTableT.SERINO = A_STOK20TI.SERINO(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK20TI.EVRAKNO(j)
							MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = A_STOK20TI.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(j)
	
							MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) & "[" & trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) &" (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) & "*" & MTableT.ORAN_M2F2 & ") ]"
								if trim(A_STOK20TI.KOD(j))	= trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) Then 
									Msgbox A_STOK20TI.EVRAKNO(j) & " Nolu Evrakda HARCANAN ile MAMUL  ayný."
									'MTableT.OKUNDU =  "*"			
								end if																				
						next	
					end if
			end if
		next		
		'**** FASON ÝÇÝN 
		
		for i = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount()
			if trim(MTableT.OKUNDU(i)) =  ""  AND trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "FG" Then					
					Dim QSTR_FG			
					
					QSTR_FG = " SELECT ST68TI.EVRAKNO, ST68TI.KOD, ST68TI.LOTNUMBER,  ST68TI.SERINO, ST68TI.SF_STOK_MIKTAR FROM STOK68T "
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & "  LEFT JOIN (SELECT EVRAKNO,KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, SUM(SF_STOK_MIKTAR)SF_STOK_MIKTAR,KARSITRNUM FROM STOK68TI "
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " WHERE STOK68TI.EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"' AND STOK68TI.BILGISATIRIE <> 'E' "
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " GROUP BY EVRAKNO,KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO,KARSITRNUM)ST68TI "
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " ON ST68TI.EVRAKNO = STOK68T.EVRAKNO AND ST68TI.KARSITRNUM = STOK68T.KARSITRNUM "
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " WHERE STOK68T.EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"' AND " 
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " STOK68T.KOD = '"& MTableT.KOD(i) &"' AND STOK68T.LOTNUMBER = '"& MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i) &"' AND STOK68T.SERINO = '"& MTableT.SERINO(i) &"'  "			 								
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " AND STOK68T.LOTNUMBER <> ST68TI.LOTNUMBER AND STOK68T.SERINO <> ST68TI.SERINO "
								
					MTableT.OKUNDU(i) =  "*"			
					Set A_STOK68TI = Doc.RunSqlQuery("FG___" , QSTR_FG)	
					
					if A_STOK68TI.GetRecCount()	 > 0 Then 
						for j = 1 To A_STOK68TI.GetRecCount()								
							QSTR_fG_COUNT = " SELECT * FROM STOK68TI"
							QSTR_fG_COUNT = QSTR_fG_COUNT & " LEFT JOIN STOK68T ON STOK68T.EVRAKNO = STOK68TI.EVRAKNO AND STOK68TI.KARSITRNUM  = STOK68T.KARSITRNUM "
							QSTR_fG_COUNT = QSTR_fG_COUNT & " WHERE STOK68TI.EVRAKNO = '"&A_STOK68TI.EVRAKNO(j)&"' AND STOK68TI.KOD = '"&A_STOK68TI.KOD(j)&"' AND STOK68TI.LOTNUMBER =  '"&A_STOK68TI.LOTNUMBER(j)&"' AND  STOK68TI.SERINO = '"&A_STOK68TI.SERINO(j)&"' "
							Set Y_STOK20TI = Doc.RunSqlQuery("WEWERTRTVVV" , QSTR_fG_COUNT )	
							MTableT.ORAN_M2F2 = Y_STOK20TI.GetRecCount()	
							satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
							MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
							MTableT.KOD = A_STOK68TI.KOD(j)
							MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", A_STOK68TI.KOD(j), "")
							MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK68TI.LOTNUMBER(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = "F2"
							MTableT.SERINO = A_STOK68TI.SERINO(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK68TI.EVRAKNO(j)
							MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = A_STOK68TI.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(j)
							MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) & "[" & trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & " - " &  trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i))  & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) & " (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) &"*"&MTableT.ORAN_M2F2&  ") ]"
						next	
					end if
			end if
		next	
		'**  K2 ÝÇÝN OKU

		for i = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount()
			KOD = MTableT.KOD(i)
			LOT = MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)
			SERI = MTableT.SERINO(i)

			if trim(MTableT.OKUNDU(i)) =  ""  AND trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "K2"  Then					
					Dim QSTR_K2			

										 
					QSTR_KXX = " SELECT EVRAKTYPE,  EVRAKNO, KOD, LOTNUMBER,SERINO  , TRNUM  FROM  STOK10A"
					QSTR_KXX = QSTR_KXX & " WHERE EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"' AND   EVRAKTYPE = 'K2 ' AND KOD = '"& KOD &"' AND LOTNUMBER = '"& LOT &"' AND SERINO = '"& SERI &"' "
					
					Set XX_STOK25T = Doc.RunSqlQuery("SDASDSAXX___" , QSTR_KXX)
									
					QSTR_K2 = " SELECT EVRAKTYPE,  EVRAKNO, KOD, LOTNUMBER,SERINO  , TRNUM, STOK_MIKTAR FROM  STOK10A"
					QSTR_K2 = QSTR_K2 & " WHERE EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"'  AND KARSIEVRAKTYPE = 'K2' AND TRNUM = '"&XX_STOK25T.TRNUM(1)&"' "
					
					Set A_STOK25T = Doc.RunSqlQuery("KKKFF___" , QSTR_K2)	
					k2_satirsayisi = A_STOK25T.GetRecCount() 
					
					QSTR_K2__2 = " SELECT EVRAKTYPE,  EVRAKNO, KOD, LOTNUMBER,SERINO  , TRNUM FROM  STOK10A"
					QSTR_K2__2  = QSTR_K2__2  & " WHERE EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"'  AND EVRAKTYPE = 'K2' AND SERINO = '"&A_STOK25T.SERINO(1)&"' "
										
					Set yy_STOK25T = Doc.RunSqlQuery("ZZSSDSDY" , QSTR_K2__2)
					if yy_STOK25T.GetRecCount() > 0 Then 											
						k2_satirsayisi = 0
					end if
					MTableT.OKUNDU(i) =  "*"			
					
					if k2_satirsayisi > 0 Then 
					
						for j = 1 To A_STOK25T.GetRecCount()		
							satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
							MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
							MTableT.KOD = A_STOK25T.KOD(j)
							MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", A_STOK25T.KOD(j), "")
							MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK25T.LOTNUMBER(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = "KD"
							MTableT.SERINO = A_STOK25T.SERINO(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK25T.EVRAKNO(j)
							MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = A_STOK25T.STOK_MIKTAR(j) * -1
									
							MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) & "[" & trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i))  & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) & " (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) &"*"&MTableT.ORAN_M2F2&  ") ]"
							MTableT.SAYAC_K2 = MTableT.SAYAC_K2(i)
							
							QSTR_CAN = "" 
							
							QSTR_CAN  = " SELECT KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, EVRAKTYPE   FROM  STOK10A  "
							QSTR_CAN  = QSTR_CAN & " WHERE KOD = '"& MTableT.KOD &"' AND LOTNUMBER =  ' "& MTableT.LOTNUMBER &"' AND  SERINO =  '"& MTableT.SERINO &"' AND (EVRAKTYPE = 'MG' OR  EVRAKTYPE = 'K2'  ) "
							QSTR_CAN  = QSTR_CAN & "  GROUP BY KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, EVRAKTYPE  "							
							
							Set QSTR_CAN = Doc.RunSqlQuery("HKHKJHKDDDD" , QSTR_CAN)
							if QSTR_CAN.GetRecCount() > 0 Then
								MTableT.OKUNDU =  "*"			
							end if
							 								
						next	
					end if
			end if
		next

'' DEMONTAJ ÝÇÝN OKU
		
		for i = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount()

			if trim(MTableT.OKUNDU(i)) =  ""  AND trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "DM"  Then					
					Dim QSTR_DM
				
					QSTR_DM = " SELECT STOK36T.EVRAKNO, STOK36T.KOD, STOK36T.LOTNUMBER,  STOK36T.SERINO, SUM(STOK36T.SF_MIKTAR) FROM STOK36E "
					QSTR_DM = QSTR_DM & " LEFT JOIN STOK36T ON STOK36T.EVRAKNO =  STOK36E.EVRAKNO "
					QSTR_DM = QSTR_DM & " WHERE STOK36E.EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"' "
					QSTR_DM = QSTR_DM & " GROUP BY STOK36T.EVRAKNO, STOK36T.KOD, STOK36T.LOTNUMBER,  STOK36T.SERINO"


					QSTR_DM = "SELECT STOK36E.EVRAKNO , STOK36E.FORW_CODE , STOK36E.FORW_LOTNUMBER,  STOK36E.FORW_SERINO, SUM(STOK36E.FORW_SF_STOK_MIKTAR) FROM STOK36E "
					QSTR_DM = QSTR_DM & "LEFT JOIN STOK36T ON STOK36T.EVRAKNO = STOK36E.EVRAKNO "
					QSTR_DM = QSTR_DM & "WHERE STOK36T.EVRAKNO = ' "&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"'  "
         								
					MTableT.OKUNDU(i) =  "*"			
					Set A_STOK36T = Doc.RunSqlQuery("DM" , QSTR_DM )	
					
					if A_STOK36T.GetRecCount()	 > 0 Then 
						for j = 1 To 1 
						
							satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
							MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
							MTableT.ORAN_M2F2 = D_STOK20TI.GetRecCount()	
							MTableT.KOD = A_STOK36T.KOD(j)
							MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", A_STOK36T.KOD(j), "")
							MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK36T.LOTNUMBER(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = "D2"
							MTableT.SERINO = A_STOK36T.SERINO(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK36T.EVRAKNO(j)
							MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = A_STOK36T.SF_MIKTAR(j)
							MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) & "[" & trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) &" (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) & "*" & MTableT.ORAN_M2F2 & ") ]"
						next	
					end if
			end if
		next		

		
			
		
		' *** STOK 10A dan bul	
		' *****
	
		for i = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount()
		
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & MTableT.GetRowCount()
			
			if trim(MTableT.OKUNDU(i)) = ""  Then
				KOD = MTableT.KOD(i)
				LOT = MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)
				SERI = MTableT.SERINO(i)
						
				QSTR_A = " SELECT KOD, EVRAKNO, EVRAKTYPE, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, SUM(STOK_MIKTAR) STOK_MIKTAR FROM STOK10A  WHERE  KOD = '"& KOD &"' AND LOTNUMBER = '"& LOT &"' AND SERINO = '"& SERI &"'  "
				QSTR_A = QSTR_A & " AND EVRAKTYPE IN ('MG', 'SI', 'SF', 'FG', 'K2', 'DG', 'DT','0S')  AND STOK_MIKTAR > 0 "
				QSTR_A = QSTR_A & " GROUP BY KOD, EVRAKNO, EVRAKTYPE, LOTNUMBER, SERINO "
				
				Set A_STOK10A = Doc.RunSqlQuery("DSADASD_DD", QSTR_A)
									
				MTableT.OKUNDU(i) =  "*"					
				if A_STOK10A.GetRecCount() > 0 Then 
					
					for j = 1 To A_STOK10A.GetRecCount()					
						if trim(KOD) = trim(A_STOK10A.KOD(j)) and trim(LOT) = trim(A_STOK10A.LOTNUMBER(j)) and trim(SERI) = trim(A_STOK10A.SERINO(j)) and trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) Then
							a = 1
						else 	
							satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
							MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
							F_SAY = F_SAY + 1 
							MTableT.F_SAYAC = F_SAY 							
							MTableT.KOD = A_STOK10A.KOD(j)
							MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", A_STOK10A.KOD(j), "")
							MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK10A.LOTNUMBER(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)
							MTableT.SERINO = A_STOK10A.SERINO(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK10A.EVRAKNO(j)
							MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = A_STOK10A.STOK_MIKTAR(j)
							
							MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) &  "[" & trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i))  & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) & " (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) &"*"&MTableT.ORAN_M2F2&  ") ]"
									
							if trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "DG" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "DT" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "0S" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "SF" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "SI" Then
								MTableT.OKUNDU =  "*"
							end if 
		
						end if	
					next	
				end if	
			else
			a = 1
			end if				
		next				
		Set DAS_ = Doc.Select2G( "dsasdrrrr", "MAKROTABLET" , " ", " ", "OKUNDU", -1 )
		ser = ser + 1
				
		if DAS_.GetRecCount() = 1 Then 
			icini_ac = 0
		end if
	Wend
	
'	Doc.ShowTableDynForm MTableT.GetAppName
'	Call Yazdir_2()
	MTableT.sort("KAYNAK_KOD")
	sayil	 = 0
	for i = 1 TO MTableT.GetRecCount()
		MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i) = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) & "[" & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & "]"  & trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)) &"-"& trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) & " (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) &"*"&MTableT.ORAN_M2F2& ")"
	next

	for i = 1 TO MTableT.GetRecCount()
			if trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "0S" OR trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "SI" Then
				Set KAPP_ = Doc.Select2( "SWSWSSDDFFD", "MAKROTABLET" ,  "KAYNAK_KOD = '"&MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)&"'' ", "", -1 )
				if KAPP_.GetRecCount() > 1  Then				
					sayil	 = sayil	 + 1
					MTableT.AYNI(i) = sayil	
			 end if
			end if	 			
	next

	for k = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & MTableT.GetRowCount() 
		if MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(k) = "SI" OR  MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(k) = "SF" OR MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(k) = "0S" OR MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(k) =  "DG" OR MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(k) = "DT" Then
			satirNo = MTableT_2.AddRow()
			MTableT_2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			
			MTableT_2.KOD = MTableT.KOD(k)
			MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD = MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(k)
			MTableT_2.SERINO = trim(MTableT.SERINO(k))
			MTableT_2.EVRAKNO = MTableT.EVRAKNO(k)
			MTableT_2.EVRAKTYPE = MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(k)
		end if	
	next

	Dim hareket_syisi, yildiz_syisi

	'' nihai tablo hazýrlanýyor
	for 	xx =1 To MTableT_2.GetREcCount() 
		yildiz_syisi = 0 
		hareket_syisi =  0
		ReDim aray(50,3) ' birinci özellik miktar ikinci  mg/m2 fg/f2 oraný, üçüncü özellik evraktype
		for i = 1 To len(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)))		
			if mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i ,  1) = "*" Then	 
				Dim miktar,oran,e_ty_al
				flag = 1
				flag_2 = 1
				
				for t = 1 To 16
					if mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t , 1 ) <> "(" and flag Then
						if flag = 1 then 
							miktar =  mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t , 1 )	& miktar
						end if 	
					elseif mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t , 1 ) = "(" 	then
						flag = 0
						e_ty_al = mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t - 3  , 1 ) & mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t - 2 , 1 )
						
					end if
					
					if mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i + t , 1 ) <> ")"  Then					
						if flag_2 = 1 then oran = oran &  mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i + t , 1 )
					else	
						flag_2 = 0
					end if
				next	

				aray(yildiz_syisi,0) = miktar
				aray(yildiz_syisi,1) = oran
				aray(yildiz_syisi,2) = e_ty_al
				miktar = ""
				oran = ""
				yildiz_syisi = yildiz_syisi + 1				
			elseif mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i ,  1) = "#" Then
				flaf_ax = 1
				for t = 1 To 30				
					if i - t > 0  Then
						if mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t , 1 ) <> "["  Then
							if flaf_ax = 1 Then fatura_kod = mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t , 1 ) & fatura_kod						
						elseif mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t , 1 ) = "[" Then	
							 flaf_ax = 0
							 fatura_sirasi = mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t - 3 , 1 ) & mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t - 2 , 1 ) & mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t - 1 , 1 )
						end if
					end if	
				next	
			end if
		next
		s_array = 0

		MTableT_2.FAT_KOD(xx) = fatura_kod
		MTableT_2.FAT_SIRA(xx) = fatura_sirasi
		fatura_kod = ""
		fatura_sirasi = ""
	
		for t = 0 TO ubound	(aray,1)
			if trim(aray(t+1,0)) = "" Then Exit For
			if trim(aray(t, 2)) = "K2" Then	
				aray(t, 0) = Doc.Dround(aray(t - 1, 0)) * Doc.Dround(aray(t - 1 , 0)) 
				aray(t, 1) = 1
				aray(t + 1, 0) = Doc.Dround(aray(t - 1, 0)) * Doc.Dround(aray(t - 1 , 0)) 
				aray(t + 1, 1) = 1
			end if
		next
		
		for t = 0 TO ubound	(aray,1)
			if trim(aray(t+1,0)) = "" Then Exit For						
			if Doc.Fmod(t+1, 2) = 1 and round(cdbl(trim(aray(t,0))),4) <> 0 and round(cdbl(trim(aray(t+1,0))),4) <> 0 Then			
				aray(t+1,1) = round(cdbl(round(cdbl(round(cdbl(trim(aray(t+1,0))),4) / round(cdbl(trim(aray(t,0))),4)),4) * round(cdbl(trim(aray(t+1,1))),4)),4)
			elseif round(cdbl(trim(aray(t,1))),4) <> 0 and round(cdbl(trim(aray(t+1,0))),4) <> 0 Then
				aray(t+1 ,0) =  round(cdbl(round(cdbl(trim(aray(t+1,0))),4) / round(cdbl(trim(aray(t,1))),4)),4)
			end if	
			s_array = s_array + 1 
		next
		
		MTableT_2.SF_MIKTAR_C(xx) = aray(s_array - 1,0)
		
	next
	
	Set Last_xx = Doc.Select2G("RRRRF" ,"MAKROTABLET_2", "",  "SERINO", "EVRAKNO+SERINO+FAT_KOD+FAT_SIRA", -1)
	
		for k = 1 To Last_xx.GetRecCount()
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Tablo Hazýrlanýyor : " & i & " / " & Last_xx.GetRecCount()
			satirNo = MTableT_3.AddRow()
			
			MTableT_3.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				
			MTableT_3.KOD = trim(Last_xx.KOD(k))
			MTableT_3.FAT_KOD = trim(Last_xx.FAT_KOD(k))
			MTableT_3.FAT_SIRA = trim(Last_xx.FAT_SIRA(k))
			MTableT_3.KOD_STOK00_AD = trim(Last_xx.KOD_STOK00_AD(k))
			MTableT_3.KAYNAK_KOD = trim(Last_xx.KAYNAK_KOD(k))
			MTableT_3.SERINO = trim(Last_xx.SERINO(k))
			MTableT_3.EVRAKNO = trim(Last_xx.EVRAKNO(k))
			MTableT_3.EVRAKTYPE = trim(Last_xx.EVRAKTYPE(k))
			MTableT_3.SF_MIKTAR_C = Last_xx.SF_MIKTAR_C(k)
			
			if trim(Last_xx.EVRAKTYPE(k)) = "SI" Then		
				Set SI_STOK29T = Doc.Select1("TYTRHR", "STOK29T", "EVRAKNO = '"&Last_xx.EVRAKNO(k)&"' &KOD = '"&trim(Last_xx.KOD(k))&"' & SERINO = '"&trim(Last_xx.SERINO(k))&"' ", "", -1 )				
				if SI_STOK29T.GetRecCount() > 0 Then
					MTableT_3.SF_MIKTAR_G = SI_STOK29T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(1)
					MTableT_3.TL_FIYAT = Doc.Div(SI_STOK29T.TL_TUTAR(1), SI_STOK29T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(1))
					MTableT_3.TL_TUTAR = Doc.Div(SI_STOK29T.TL_TUTAR(1), SI_STOK29T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(1)) *  round(cdbl(trim(Last_xx.SF_MIKTAR_C(k))),2)
					MTableT_3.PRICE = "TL"
				end if	
			elseif	trim(Last_xx.EVRAKTYPE(k)) = "0S" Then						
					Set S_STOK2AT = Doc.Select1("ERETRR", "STOK2AT", "EVRAKNO = '"&Last_xx.EVRAKNO(k)&"' &KOD = '"&trim(Last_xx.KOD(k))&"' & SERINO = '"&trim(Last_xx.SERINO(k))&"' ", "", -1 )					
					if S_STOK2AT.GetRecCount() > 0 Then
						MTableT_3.SF_MIKTAR_G = S_STOK2AT.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(1)
						MTableT_3.TL_FIYAT = Doc.Div(S_STOK2AT.TL_TUTAR(1), S_STOK2AT.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(1))
						MTableT_3.TL_TUTAR = Doc.Div(S_STOK2AT.TL_TUTAR(1), S_STOK2AT.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(1)) *  round(cdbl(trim(Last_xx.SF_MIKTAR_C(k))),2)
						MTableT_3.PRICE = "TL"
					end if	
			elseif	trim(Last_xx.EVRAKTYPE(k)) = "DG" Then						
				Set S_STOK21T = Doc.Select1("LKLKLKLRER", "STOK21T", "EVRAKNO = '"&Last_xx.EVRAKNO(k)&"' &KOD = '"&trim(Last_xx.KOD(k))&"' & SERINO = '"&trim(Last_xx.SERINO(k))&"' ", "", -1 )					
				if S_STOK21T.GetRecCount() > 0 Then
					MTableT_3.SF_MIKTAR_G = S_STOK21T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(1)
					MTableT_3.TL_FIYAT = Doc.Div(S_STOK21T.TL_TUTAR(1), S_STOK21T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(1))
					MTableT_3.TL_TUTAR = Doc.Div(S_STOK21T.TL_TUTAR(1), S_STOK21T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(1)) *  round(cdbl(trim(Last_xx.SF_MIKTAR_C(k))),2)
					MTableT_3.PRICE = "TL"
				end if					
			elseif	trim(Last_xx.EVRAKTYPE(k)) = "DT" Then								
				Set S_STOK24T = Doc.Select1("XXAAADDD", "STOK24T", "EVRAKNO = '"&Last_xx.EVRAKNO(k)&"' &KOD = '"&trim(Last_xx.KOD(k))&"' & SERINO = '"&trim(Last_xx.SERINO(k))&"' ", "", -1 )					
				if S_STOK24T.GetRecCount() > 0 Then
					MTableT_3.SF_MIKTAR_G = S_STOK24T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(1)
					MTableT_3.TL_FIYAT = Doc.Div(S_STOK24T.TL_TUTAR(1), S_STOK24T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(1))
					MTableT_3.TL_TUTAR = Doc.Div(S_STOK24T.TL_TUTAR(1), S_STOK24T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(1)) *  round(cdbl(trim(Last_xx.SF_MIKTAR_C(k))),2)
					MTableT_3.PRICE = "TL"
				end if			
			end if
		next

		Set KTT_KDVIT_A = Doc.GetTableobject("KTT_KDVIT")		
		KTT_KDVIT_A.SafePack()
		top_tut = 0
		for i = 1 To MTableT_3.GetRecCount()
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Tablo Hazýrlanýyor : " & i & " / " & MTableT_3.GetRecCount()			
			satirNo = KTT_KDVIT_A.AddRow()
			KTT_KDVIT_A.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)

			KTT_KDVIT_A.FT_SR_NO = MTableT_3.FAT_SIRA(i)			
			KTT_KDVIT_A.FT_KOD = MTableT_3.FAT_KOD(i)
			KTT_KDVIT_A.SAD_ = Doc.GetdbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", MTableT_3.KOD(i) , "")
			KTT_KDVIT_A.EVRAKTY_ = MTableT_3.EVRAKTYPE(i)
			KTT_KDVIT_A.EVRAKNO_ = trim(MTableT_3.EVRAKNO(i))
			KTT_KDVIT_A.KOD_ = MTableT_3.KOD(i)
			KTT_KDVIT_A.GR_MIK  = MTableT_3.SF_MIKTAR_G(i)
			KTT_KDVIT_A.GR_FIY = MTableT_3.TL_FIYAT(i)
			KTT_KDVIT_A.CK_MIK = MTableT_3.SF_MIKTAR_C(i)
			KTT_KDVIT_A.CK_TUT = MTableT_3.TL_TUTAR(i)
			KTT_KDVIT_A.SERINO = MTableT_3.SERINO(i)
			top_tut = top_tut + MTableT_3.TL_TUTAR(i)
		next
		
		Set KTT_KDVIE_A = Doc.GetTableobject("KTT_KDVIE")
		 	
		KTT_KDVIE_A.TUTAR =  Doc.NumFormat(top_tut,18,3)		
	'Doc.ShowTableDynForm MTableT_3.GetAppName
End Sub

Sub Yazdir_2()

	SayfaBoyu = 290
	SayfaEni = 2000
	SolMargin = 6
	SagMargin = 1
	UstMargin = 20
	AltMargin = 1

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Order Confirmation", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("Left")
	res = pe1.SelectFont(Arial, 10)
	TOPUTU = 0
	CIKAN = 0
	Set MTableT_3 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	'MTableT.SORT("EVRAKTYPE + EVRAKNO+SERINO+ KOD +KAYNAK KOD")
	MTableT_3.SORT("KAYNAK_KOD")
	
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, 10, 10 , 30 , "KOD")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight, 10 , 40 , "EVRAKTYPE")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight, 10 , 20 , "SERINO")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 30 , "EVRAKNO")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , "EVRAKTYPE")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 1000 , "KAYNAK_KOD")
	res = pe1.SelectFont(Arial, 8)
	
	for i = 1 To  MTableT_3.GetRecCount()
		if trim(MTableT_3.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "SI" Then
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 8, 10, 10 , 30 , trim(MTableT_3.KOD(i)))
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 40 , 30 , trim(MTableT_3.EVRAKTYPE(i)))
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 20 , 30 , trim(MTableT_3.SERINO(i)))
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 30 , trim(MTableT_3.EVRAKNO(i)))
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , trim(MTableT_3.EVRAKTYPE(i)))
			res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 2000 , trim(MTableT_3.KAYNAK_KOD(i)))
			
			if pe1.GetTop() > 280 Then
				res = pe1.PageBreak()			 					
				res = pe1.write(UstMargin, 10, 10 , 30 , "KOD")
				res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight, 10 , 40 , "EVRAKTYPE")
				res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight, 10 , 20 , "SERINO")
				res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 30 , "EVRAKNO")
				res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , "EVRAKTYPE")
				res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 400 , "KAYNAK_KOD")
			end if
		end if
	next
		
End Sub

Bu Uygulama için Baþlangýç Ekran Formu : _GNPRG_001

Alt Ekranlar Ýçin Ekran Formu

Yeni Alt Ekra Form Kodu: 
Alt Ekran Açýlmadan Önce Çalýþtýrýlacak Makro :

Açýklama : MAHSUP FÝÞ YARATMA - ÖZET

Sub Makro1()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MUHA10","")
	Set MH20E = Doc1.GetTableObject("MUHA20E")
	Set MH20T = Doc1.GetTableObject("MUHA20T")
	Set K = Doc1.CreateSObject("BATCHUPDATEMODE")

	nret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
	Tarih = Inputbox("Mahsup Fiþi tarihi")
	Set d7Date = Doc.CreateSObject("d7Date")
	d7Date.SetDate(trim(Tarih))
	if not d7Date.IsValid Then
		Doc.d7msgBox "Geçersiz Tarih", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	Tarih = d7Date.GetRevDate
	MH20E.TARIH = Tarih
	MH20E.ACIKLAMA = "DEMIRBAS"
	MH20E.GK_1 = "MALÝYET"

	MH20T.Empty

	For m=1 to LISTET.getreccount()
		demirbasno = LISTET.EVRAKNO(m)
		Set DMRB = Doc.Select1("_DMRB60","DMRB60E","EVRAKNO='"+demirbasno+"'","",-1)
		for k=1 to DMRB.getreccount
			
				aktif_kod=DMRB.MUHASEBEKOD(k)
				amortisman_kod=DMRB.MUHASEBEKODG(k)
				aktif_kod2=DMRB.MUHASEBEKOD2(k)			
		next
		Set DMRB2 = Doc.Select1("_DMRB60T2","DMRB60T2","EVRAKNO='"+demirbasno+"'&YIL='" & Doc.StrDate_Year(Tarih) & "'","",-1)


	if DMRB2.GetRowCount > 0 and Doc.Cdbl(DMRB.AMT_SONHESAP_YIL)>= Doc.StrDate_Year(Tarih) then
		amortismandegeri = DMRB2.CS_AMT_AY(1)
		amortismandegeri1 = doc.div(amortismandegeri, 12) '/* farklý yýl ise 12 ye böl
			satin_alma_tarih = DMRB.SATINALMA_TARIH(1)


			if Trim(Mid(satin_alma_tarih,1,4)) =  Trim(Year(date)) Then

						AY = int(Mid(satin_alma_tarih,6,2))
						AY1=	int(Mid(Tarih,6,2)) 	
					If int(Mid(Tarih,6,2)) = int(Mid(satin_alma_tarih,6,2)) then
						amortismandegeri1 = doc.div(amortismandegeri, 12)* Ay
					Else		
						amortismandegeri1 = doc.div(amortismandegeri, AY)
					End If
		
			End if
		For i=1 to 2
			MH20T.AddRow
			satirNo = MH20T.GetRecCount()
			MH20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
	
			if i=1 then
				MH20T.KOD(satirNo) = Aktif_kod
				MH20T.CODE2(satirNo) = Aktif_kod2
				MH20T.ACIKLAMA(satirno)=demirbasno
				MH20T.BORCFIYAT(satirNo) = amortismandegeri1
			else 
				MH20T.KOD(satirNo) = amortisman_kod
				MH20T.ALACAKFIYAT(satirNo) = amortismandegeri1
				MH20T.ACIKLAMA(satirno)=demirbasno
				amortismandegeri=0
			end if
		Next
	end if
Next
	Set M20T1 = Doc1.Select2G("sdfwsgjs", "MUHA20T", "", "", "KOD+CODE2", -1)
	Set M20T = Doc1.getTableObject("MUHA20T")
	M20T.Empty()
	For i = 1 to M20T1.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = M20T.AddRow
		M20T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		M20T.KOD(satirNo) = M20T1.KOD(i)
		M20T.CODE2(satirNo) = M20T1.CODE2(i)
		if M20T1.BORCFIYAT(i) > 0 Then
			M20T.BORCFIYAT(satirNo) = M20T1.BORCFIYAT(i)
		Else
			M20T.ALACAKFIYAT(satirNo) = M20T1.ALACAKFIYAT(i)
		End if
	Next
	if Doc1.Save_Voucher() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MH20E.EVRAKNO) & " no.lu mahsup fiþi yaratýldý", "", MB_OK
	End if
	M20T.Sort("<BORCFIYAT")
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Call UyariMailGonder()
End Sub
Sub UyariMailGonder()
	if Doc.GetDocType0 = "TRACK29" Then Exit Sub
'	Set ddd = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	STOK29T.SafePack
	Set S29T = Doc.Select2G("sdfgsefhsj", "STOK29T", "", "", "KOD", -1)

	MsgStr = ""
	For i = 1 to S29T.GetRowCount()
		QStr = "SELECT M10T.EVRAKNO, M10T.R_MAMULKODU, STOK00.AD MAMUL_ADI, S00.AD HMD_ADI, S00.IUNIT HMD_UNIT, "
		QStr = QStr & " M10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH, M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK "
		QStr = QStr & " FROM MMPS10T M10T"
		QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD=M10T.R_MAMULKODU"
		QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S00 ON S00.KOD=M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU"
		QStr = QStr & " WHERE M10T.ACIK_KAPALI<>'K' AND M10T.R_STATUS='R' AND M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK>0 "
		QStr = QStr & " AND M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU='" & S29T.KOD(i) & "'"
		QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY M10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH, M10T.EVRAKNO"

		Set M10T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfgdsfhs", QStr)

		QStr = "SELECT Sum(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR"
		QStr = QStr & " FROM STOK01 "
		QStr = QStr & " WHERE KOD='" & S29T.KOD(i) & "'"
		QStr = QStr & " AND AMBCODE IN ('01', '02')"
		Set S01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfgddsdfhs", QStr)
		if S01.GetRowCOunt() > 0 Then 
			DepoStok = S01.SF_MIKTAR(1)
		Else
			DepoStok = 0
		End if
		satirNo = 1

		Do
			if DepoStok = 0 Then Exit Do
			if satirNo > M10T.GetRowCount() Then Exit Do

			if DepoStok > M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo) Then
				Dagitilan = M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo)
			Else
				Dagitilan = DepoStok
			End if
			DepoStok = DepoStok - Dagitilan
			M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo) = M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo) - Dagitilan
			if M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo) <= 0 Then satirNo = SatirNo + 1
		Loop
		if satirNo <= M10T.GetRowCount() Then
			MsgStr = MsgStr & vbcrlf & "Hammadde Kodu : " & trim(S29T.KOD(i)) & " " & trim(M10T.HMD_ADI(satirNo)) & _
							", Giriþ Miktarý : " & S29T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(i) & " " & M10T.HMD_UNIT(satirNo)
			SIMiktar = S29T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(i)
			Do
				if SIMiktar = 0 Then Exit Do
				if satirNo > M10T.GetRowCount() Then Exit Do

				if SIMiktar > M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo) Then
					Dagitilan = M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo)
				Else
					Dagitilan = SIMiktar
				End if
				SIMiktar = SIMiktar - Dagitilan
				M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo) = M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo) - Dagitilan
				MsgStr = MsgStr & vbcrlf & "    MPS No : " & trim(M10T.EVRAKNO(satirNo)) & _
							", Mamul : " & trim(M10T.R_MAMULKODU(satirNo)) & " " & trim(M10T.MAMUL_ADI(satirNo)) & _
							", Ýhtiyaç Miktarý : " & Dagitilan & _
							", Ýhtiyaç Tarihi : " & Doc.StrToNorDate(M10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(satirNo))

				if M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo) <= 0 Then satirNo = SatirNo + 1
			Loop
		End if
	Next

	if trim(MsgStr) = "" Then Exit Sub

	MailFrom = "erp@formamakina.com" 
	MailTo = GetMailReceiver("SIMAIL")
	if MailTo = "" Then Exit Sub
	MailHeader = "Satýnalma Giriþi Bilgilendirme Mesajý - Ýrsaliye No : " & trim(STOK29E.EVRAKNO) & ", Tedarikçi : " & trim(STOK29E.CARIHESAPCODE_CARI00_AD)
	MailMessage = MsgStr
	DosyaAdi = ""

	Doc.RunMacro5 "MAKR7S.FREEMAIL", "SendMail", MailFrom, MailTo,MailHeader,MailMessage, DosyaAdi

End Sub
Function GetMailReceiver(KullaniciGrubu)
	MailStr = ""
	QStr = "SELECT EMAIL FROM UDEF"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE KOD IN (SELECT KOD FROM UDEFT12 WHERE OZEL_HAK_KODU='" & trim(KullaniciGrubu) & "')"
	QStr = QStr & " AND TRIM(UDEF.EMAIL) IS NOT NULL"
	
	Set UDF = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfsdfhsdh", QStr)
	For i = 1 to UDF.GetRowCount()
		if trim(MailStr) <> "" Then MailStr = MailStr & ","
		MailStr = MailStr & trim(UDF.EMAIL(i))
	Next
	GetMailReceiver = MailStr
End Function

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : FORMA_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : KODDUZELT
Hangi Tablo için : PFRM7S
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : MAKR7S,MAKR8S
Açýklama : Kod Düzelt
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	if Doc.GetDocType = "PFRM7S" Then
		AnaTabloStr = "PFRM7SE_B"
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "MAKR7S" Then
		AnaTabloStr = "MAKR7SE_B"
	Elseif Doc.GetDocType = "MAKR8S" Then
		AnaTabloStr = "MAKR8SE_B"
	End if
	Set AnaTablo = Doc.GetTableObject(AnaTabloStr)
	
	Kod = AnaTablo.GetFieldValue("SCRIPTTEXT",1)
	KodArray = Split(Kod, vbcrlf)
	AnaTablo.SetFieldValue "SCRIPTTEXT",1, ""
	
	Girinti = 0
	For i = 0 to uBound(KodArray)
		Doc.StatusBarMessage i & " / " & uBound(KodArray)
		satir = KodArray(i)
		satir = Replace(satir, Chr(9), " ")
		satir = trim(Satir)
		
		if uCase(Left(satir, 7)) = "ELSEIF " Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "ELSE" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END SUB" Then
			Girinti = 0
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END FUNCTION" Then
			Girinti = 0
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END IF" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "NEXT" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "WEND" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "LOOP" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 5)) = "CASE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "END SELECT" Then
			Girinti = Girinti - 2
		End if
		YeniSatir = Girintilisatir(satir, Girinti)
		
		kodScript = AnaTablo.GetFieldValue("SCRIPTTEXT",1)
		AnaTablo.SetFieldValue "SCRIPTTEXT",1, trim(kodScript) & YeniSatir & vbcrlf
		
		if Ucase(Left(satir, 4)) = "SUB " Then
			Girinti = 1
		Elseif Ucase(Left(satir, 9)) = "FUNCTION " Then
			Girinti = 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 3)) = "IF " and Not TekSatirIf(satir) Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 5)) = "CASE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 12)) = "SELECT CASE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 2
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 7)) = "ELSEIF " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(satir) = "ELSE" Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 4)) = "FOR " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 6)) = "WHILE " Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		Elseif uCase(Left(satir, 3)) = "DO " or trim(uCase(satir)) = "DO" Then
			Girinti = Girinti  + 1
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function Girintilisatir(satir, Girinti)
	Girintilisatir = satir
	For i = 1 to Girinti
		Girintilisatir = Chr(9) & Girintilisatir
	Next
End Function
Function TekSatirIf(satir)
	TekSatirIf = False
	k = InStr(1, uCase(satir), "THEN ")
	if k = 0 Then Exit Function
	if trim(Mid(satir, k + 5)) <> "" Then
		TekSatirIf = True
	End if
End Function
Sub TabTrim(satir)
	While Left(satir, 1) = Chr(9)
		satir = Mid(satir, 2)
	Wend
	While Right(satir, 1) = Chr(9)
		satir = Left(satir, Len(satir) - 1)
	Wend
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : FORMA_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : MPS-HESAP2
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için :

Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set ggg = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")

	QStr = "SELECT EVRAKNO "
	QStr = QStr & " FROM MMPS10TM"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE ACIK_KAPALI=' ' AND MAMULSTOKKODU IN "
	QStr = QStr & " ("
	QStr = QStr & "   SELECT R_MAMULKODU FROM "
	QStr = QStr & "   ("
	QStr = QStr & "     SELECT MMPS10T.EVRAKNO, MMPS10T.R_MAMULKODU, MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE, MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU, "
	QStr = QStr & "     (CASE WHEN COALESCE(BOMU01T.BOMREC_MAMULMIKTAR,0) > 0 THEN BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0 / BOMU01T.BOMREC_MAMULMIKTAR ELSE 0 END) BOM_BIRIM_HMD, "
	QStr = QStr & "     COALESCE(BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK1,0) BOM_SETUP, (MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR0 / MMPS10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR) MPS_BIRIM_HMD, MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR1 MPS_SETUP"
	QStr = QStr & "     FROM MMPS10T"
	QStr = QStr & "     LEFT JOIN  MMPS10TM ON MMPS10TM.EVRAKNO=MMPS10T.EVRAKNO"
	QStr = QStr & "     LEFT JOIN BOMU01T ON BOMU01T.AKTIF_PASIF='*' AND BOMU01T.BOMREC_CODE=MMPS10T.R_MAMULKODU"
	QStr = QStr & "     AND BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE=MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE AND BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE=MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU AND BOMU01T.BOMREC_OPERASYON=MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON"
	QStr = QStr & "     WHERE MMPS10T.ACIK_KAPALI=' ' AND MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE='H' AND MMPS10T.R_MAMULKODU>='151 001' AND MMPS10T.R_MAMULKODU<='151 050zzzzz'"
	QStr = QStr & "     UNION ALL"
	QStr = QStr & "     SELECT MMPS10T.EVRAKNO, BOMU01T.BOMREC_CODE, BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE, BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE, "
	QStr = QStr & "     (BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0 / BOMU01T.BOMREC_MAMULMIKTAR) BOM_BIRIM_HMD, BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK1 BOM_SETUP,"
	QStr = QStr & "     (CASE WHEN COALESCE(MMPS10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR,0)>0 THEN MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR0 / MMPS10TM.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR ELSE 0 END) MPS_BIRIM_HMD, "
	QStr = QStr & "     COALESCE(MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR1,0) MPS_SETUP"
	QStr = QStr & "     FROM BOMU01T"
	QStr = QStr & "     LEFT JOIN MMPS10T ON BOMU01T.AKTIF_PASIF='*' AND BOMU01T.BOMREC_CODE=MMPS10T.R_MAMULKODU"
	QStr = QStr & "     AND BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE=MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE AND BOMU01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE=MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU AND BOMU01T.BOMREC_OPERASYON=MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON"
	QStr = QStr & "     LEFT JOIN MMPS10TM ON MMPS10TM.EVRAKNO=MMPS10T.EVRAKNO"
	QStr = QStr & "     WHERE BOMU01T.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H' AND "
	QStr = QStr & "     BOMREC_CODE IN (SELECT MAMULSTOKKODU FROM MMPS10TM WHERE ACIK_KAPALI=' ' AND MAMULSTOKKODU>='151 001' AND MAMULSTOKKODU<='151 050zzzzz')"
	QStr = QStr & "     AND MMPS10T.EVRAKNO IS NULL"
	QStr = QStr & "   ) M10T "
	QStr = QStr & "   WHERE (Round(BOM_BIRIM_HMD,3) <> Round(MPS_BIRIM_HMD,3) or Round(BOM_SETUP,3) <> Round(MPS_SETUP,3))"
	QStr = QStr & "   )"
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY EVRAKNO"

	Set M10TM = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfsdfhs", QStr)
	For i = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "MPS 'ler tekrar hesaplanýyor : " & i & " / " & M10TM.GetRowCount()
		Doc.Load_Voucher M10TM.EVRAKNO(i)
		Doc.RunMacro0 "MAKR7S.MPS-HESAP", "Makro1"
		if Doc.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
			Doc.d7MsgBox trim(M10TM.EVRAKNO(i)) & " no.lu MPS tekrar hesaplandý", "", MB_OK
		End if
	Next
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : FORMA_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : UYARI_M2
Hangi Tablo için : STOK20
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Call UyariMailGonder()
End Sub
Sub UyariMailGonder()
	if Doc.GetLastLoadType <> "N" Then Exit Sub

	STOK20T.SafePack
	Set S20T = Doc.Select2G("sdfgsefhsj", "STOK20T", "", "", "KOD", -1)

	MsgStr = ""
	For i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCount()
		QStr = "SELECT M10T.EVRAKNO, M10T.R_MAMULKODU, STOK00.AD MAMUL_ADI, S00.AD HMD_ADI, S00.IUNIT HMD_UNIT, "
		QStr = QStr & " M10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH, M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK "
		QStr = QStr & " FROM MMPS10T M10T"
		QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD=M10T.R_MAMULKODU"
		QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S00 ON S00.KOD=M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU"
		QStr = QStr & " WHERE M10T.ACIK_KAPALI<>'K' AND M10T.R_STATUS='R' AND M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK>0 "
		QStr = QStr & " AND M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU='" & S20T.KOD(i) & "'"
		QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY M10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH, M10T.EVRAKNO"

		Set M10T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfgdsfhs", QStr)

		QStr = "SELECT Sum(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR"
		QStr = QStr & " FROM STOK01 "
		QStr = QStr & " WHERE KOD='" & S20T.KOD(i) & "'"
		QStr = QStr & " AND AMBCODE IN ('01', '02')"
		Set S01 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfgddsdfhs", QStr)
		if S01.GetRowCOunt() > 0 Then 
			DepoStok = S01.SF_MIKTAR(1)
		Else
			DepoStok = 0
		End if
		satirNo = 1

		Do
			if DepoStok = 0 Then Exit Do
			if satirNo > M10T.GetRowCount() Then Exit Do

			if DepoStok > M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo) Then
				Dagitilan = M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo)
			Else
				Dagitilan = DepoStok
			End if
			DepoStok = DepoStok - Dagitilan
			M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo) = M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo) - Dagitilan
			if M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo) <= 0 Then satirNo = SatirNo + 1
		Loop
		if satirNo <= M10T.GetRowCount() Then
			MsgStr = MsgStr & vbcrlf & "Hammadde Kodu : " & trim(S20T.KOD(i)) & " " & trim(M10T.HMD_ADI(satirNo)) & _
							", Giriþ Miktarý : " & S20T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(i) & " " & M10T.HMD_UNIT(satirNo)
			MGMiktar = S20T.SF_STOK_MIKTAR(i)
			Do
				if MGMiktar = 0 Then Exit Do
				if satirNo > M10T.GetRowCount() Then Exit Do

				if MGMiktar > M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo) Then
					Dagitilan = M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo)
				Else
					Dagitilan = MGMiktar
				End if
				MGMiktar = MGMiktar - Dagitilan
				M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo) = M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo) - Dagitilan
				MsgStr = MsgStr & vbcrlf & "    MPS No : " & trim(M10T.EVRAKNO(satirNo)) & _
							", Mamul : " & trim(M10T.R_MAMULKODU(satirNo)) & " " & trim(M10T.MAMUL_ADI(satirNo)) & _
							", Ýhtiyaç Miktarý : " & Dagitilan & _
							", Ýhtiyaç Tarihi : " & Doc.StrToNorDate(M10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(satirNo))

				if M10T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK(satirNo) <= 0 Then satirNo = SatirNo + 1
			Loop
		End if
	Next

	if trim(MsgStr) = "" Then Exit Sub
msgbox MsgStr

	MailFrom = "erp@formamakina.com" 
	MailTo = GetMailReceiver("MGMAIL")
	if MailTo = "" Then Exit Sub
	MailHeader = "Üretim Giriþi Bilgilendirme Mesajý - Fiþ No : " & trim(STOK20E.EVRAKNO)
	MailMessage = MsgStr
	DosyaAdi = ""

	Doc.RunMacro5 "MAKR7S.FREEMAIL", "SendMail", MailFrom, MailTo,MailHeader,MailMessage, DosyaAdi

End Sub
Function GetMailReceiver(KullaniciGrubu)
	MailStr = ""
	QStr = "SELECT EMAIL FROM UDEF"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE KOD IN (SELECT KOD FROM UDEFT12 WHERE OZEL_HAK_KODU='" & trim(KullaniciGrubu) & "')"
	QStr = QStr & " AND TRIM(UDEF.EMAIL) IS NOT NULL"
	
	Set UDF = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfsdfhsdh", QStr)
	For i = 1 to UDF.GetRowCount()
		if trim(MailStr) <> "" Then MailStr = MailStr & ","
		MailStr = MailStr & trim(UDF.EMAIL(i))
	Next
	GetMailReceiver = MailStr
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	Set JOBLIST = Doc.GetTableobject("JOBLIST")
	JOBLIST.AddField2 "SELECTED","CHAR",1,0,"L","",""
End Sub
Sub SecilenleriRyeDonustur()
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MMPS10", "")
	Set M10E = Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")

	Set JOBLIST = Doc.GetTableobject("JOBLIST")
	For i = 1 to JOBLIST.GetRowCount()
		if trim(JOBLIST.SELECTED(i)) = "E" and trim(JOBLIST.R_STATUS(i)) <> "R" Then
			Doc1.Load_Voucher JOBLIST.EVRAKNO(i)
			M10E.STATUS = "R"
			if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
				JOBLIST.R_STATUS(i) = "R"
				JOBLIST.JOBNO(i) = JOBLIST.EVRAKNO(i) & JOBLIST.TRNUM(i)
			End if
		End if
	Next
	Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher
	JOBLIST.Refresh
End Sub
