Sub Makro1()


	on error resume next
	
	F_SAY = 0 	
	Dim MTableT, SAYAC_1
	SAYAC_1 = 1
	SER = 1
	
	Set MTableT = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET=STACK","IO")

	MTableT.Addfield2 "KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "KOD_STOK00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "LOTNUMBER", "CHAR",12,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "EVRAKTYPE", "CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SERINO", "CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "OKUNDU", "CHAR",10,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "ORAN_M2F2", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "KAYNAK_KOD", "CHAR",500,0,"L","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "SAYAC_K2", "DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "F_SAYAC", "DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""
	MTableT.Addfield2 "AYNI","DOUBLE",12,0,"R","",""

	Set MTableT_2 = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET_2=STACK","IO")
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "FAT_KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "FAT_SIRA", "CHAR",3,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "KOD_STOK00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "KAYNAK_KOD", "CHAR",700,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "SERINO", "CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","",""	
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "EVRAKTYPE", "CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_G", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_C", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "TL_FIYAT", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "TL_TUTAR", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_2.Addfield2 "PRICE", "CHAR",2,0,"L","",""

	Set MTableT_3 = Doc.CreateTransactionalTableObject("MAKROTABLET_3=STACK","IO")
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "FAT_KOD", "CHAR",24,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "FAT_SIRA", "CHAR",3,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "KOD_STOK00_AD", "CHAR",50,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "KAYNAK_KOD", "CHAR",700,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "SERINO", "CHAR",20,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "EVRAKNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","",""	
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "EVRAKTYPE", "CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_G", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "SF_MIKTAR_C", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "TL_FIYAT", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "TL_TUTAR", "DOUBLE",12,3,"R","",""
	MTableT_3.Addfield2 "PRICE", "CHAR",2,0,"L","",""
	
	Set STOK70T = Doc.Select1("FASFASDS", "STOK70T", "EVRAKNO = '435703'", "SRNUM", -1)

	'**** FATURADAN OKU	
	for i = 22 To  23 ' STOK70T.GetRecCount()

		KOD = STOK70T.KOD(i)
		LOT = STOK70T.LOTNUMBER(i)
		SERI = STOK70T.SERINO(i)
				
		QSTR = " SELECT KOD, EVRAKNO, EVRAKTYPE, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, SUM(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR FROM STOK10A  WHERE  KOD = '"& KOD &"' AND LOTNUMBER = '"& LOT &"' AND SERINO = '"& SERI &"'  "
		QSTR = QSTR & " AND EVRAKTYPE IN ('MG', 'SI', 'SF', 'FG', 'K2', 'DG', 'DT','0S')  AND SF_MIKTAR > 0 "
		QSTR = QSTR & " GROUP BY KOD, EVRAKNO, EVRAKTYPE, LOTNUMBER, SERINO "
		Set A_STOK10A = Doc.RunSqlQuery("AAA", QSTR)

		for j = 1 To A_STOK10A.GetRecCount()
		
				satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
				MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				MTableT.KOD = A_STOK10A.KOD(j)
				MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", A_STOK10A.KOD(j), "")
				MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK10A.LOTNUMBER(j)
				MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)
				MTableT.SERINO = A_STOK10A.SERINO(j)
				MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK10A.EVRAKNO(j)
				MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = A_STOK10A.SF_MIKTAR(j)
				MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = "-     " & i&"[" & trim(KOD) & "#- "  & trim(SERI) & " - "  & trim(STOK70T.EVRAKNO) & " - FT (" & STOK70T.SF_MIKTAR(i) &"*"&MTableT.ORAN_M2F2& ")  ]" 
				if trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "DG" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "DT" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "0S" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "SF" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "SI" Then
					MTableT.OKUNDU =  "*"
				end if 

				if trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "K2"Then
					MTableT.SAYAC_K2(i) = SAYAC_1
					MTableT.SAYAC_K2 = SAYAC_1
					SAYAC_1  = SAYAC_1 + 1 
				end if
		next		
	next
	
	'***** 	TABLOYU AÇ
	icini_ac = 1 
	while  icini_ac = 1
		
		'**** ÜRETÝM FÝÞÝ ÝÇÝN
		
		for i = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount()
			if trim(MTableT.OKUNDU(i)) =  ""  AND trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "MG"  Then					
					Dim QSTR_MG	
				
					QSTR_MG = " SELECT ST20TI.EVRAKNO, ST20TI.KOD, ST20TI.LOTNUMBER,  ST20TI.SERINO, ST20TI.SF_MIKTAR FROM STOK20T "
					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & "  LEFT JOIN (SELECT EVRAKNO,KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, SUM(SF_MIKTAR)SF_MIKTAR,KARSITRNUM FROM STOK20TI "
					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & " WHERE STOK20TI.EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"' AND STOK20TI.BILGISATIRIE <> 'E' "
					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & " GROUP BY EVRAKNO,KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO,KARSITRNUM)ST20TI "
					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & " ON ST20TI.EVRAKNO = STOK20T.EVRAKNO AND ST20TI.KARSITRNUM = STOK20T.KARSITRNUM "
					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & " WHERE STOK20T.EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"' AND " 
					QSTR_MG = QSTR_MG & " STOK20T.KOD = '"& MTableT.KOD(i) &"' AND STOK20T.LOTNUMBER = '"& MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i) &"' AND STOK20T.SERINO = '"& MTableT.SERINO(i) &"'  "			 								
														                								
					MTableT.OKUNDU(i) =  "*"			
					Set A_STOK20TI = Doc.RunSqlQuery("MG" , QSTR_MG)	
					
					if A_STOK20TI.GetRecCount()	 > 0 Then 

						for j = 1 To A_STOK20TI.GetRecCount()		
						
							QSTR_MG_COUNT = " SELECT STOK20T.SERINO FROM STOK20TI"
							QSTR_MG_COUNT = QSTR_MG_COUNT & " LEFT JOIN STOK20T ON STOK20T.EVRAKNO = STOK20TI.EVRAKNO AND STOK20TI.KARSITRNUM  = STOK20T.KARSITRNUM "
							QSTR_MG_COUNT = QSTR_MG_COUNT & " WHERE STOK20TI.EVRAKNO ='"&A_STOK20TI.EVRAKNO(j)&"' AND STOK20TI.KOD = '"&A_STOK20TI.KOD(j)&"' AND STOK20TI.LOTNUMBER =  '"&A_STOK20TI.LOTNUMBER(j)&"' AND  STOK20TI.SERINO = '"&A_STOK20TI.SERINO(j)&"' "
							QSTR_MG_COUNT = QSTR_MG_COUNT & " GROUP BY STOK20T.SERINO"
							
							Set D_STOK20TI = Doc.RunSqlQuery("JKJKJMG" , QSTR_MG_COUNT )	
							satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
							MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
							MTableT.ORAN_M2F2 = D_STOK20TI.GetRecCount()	
							MTableT.KOD = A_STOK20TI.KOD(j)
							MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", A_STOK20TI.KOD(j), "")
							MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK20TI.LOTNUMBER(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = "M2"
							MTableT.SERINO = A_STOK20TI.SERINO(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK20TI.EVRAKNO(j)
							MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = A_STOK20TI.SF_MIKTAR(j)
	
							MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) & "[" & trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) &" (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) & "*" & MTableT.ORAN_M2F2 & ") ]"
																	
						next	
					end if
			end if
		next		
		'**** FASON ÝÇÝN 
		
		for i = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount()
			if trim(MTableT.OKUNDU(i)) =  ""  AND trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "FG" Then					
					Dim QSTR_FG			
					
					QSTR_FG = " SELECT ST68TI.EVRAKNO, ST68TI.KOD, ST68TI.LOTNUMBER,  ST68TI.SERINO FROM STOK68T "
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & "  LEFT JOIN (SELECT EVRAKNO,KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, SUM(SF_MIKTAR)SF_MIKTAR,KARSITRNUM FROM STOK68TI "
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " WHERE STOK68TI.EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"' AND STOK68TI.BILGISATIRIE <> 'E' "
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " GROUP BY EVRAKNO,KOD, LOTNUMBER, SERINO,KARSITRNUM)ST68TI "
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " ON ST68TI.EVRAKNO = STOK68T.EVRAKNO AND ST68TI.KARSITRNUM = STOK68T.KARSITRNUM "
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " WHERE STOK68T.EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"' AND " 
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " STOK68T.KOD = '"& MTableT.KOD(i) &"' AND STOK68T.LOTNUMBER = '"& MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i) &"' AND STOK68T.SERINO = '"& MTableT.SERINO(i) &"'  "			 								
					QSTR_FG = QSTR_FG & " AND STOK68T.LOTNUMBER <> ST68TI.LOTNUMBER AND STOK68T.SERINO <> ST68TI.SERINO "
								
					MTableT.OKUNDU(i) =  "*"			
					Set A_STOK68TI = Doc.RunSqlQuery("FG___" , QSTR_FG)	
					
					if A_STOK68TI.GetRecCount()	 > 0 Then 
						for j = 1 To A_STOK68TI.GetRecCount()								
							QSTR_fG_COUNT = " SELECT * FROM STOK68TI"
							QSTR_fG_COUNT = QSTR_fG_COUNT & " LEFT JOIN STOK68T ON STOK68T.EVRAKNO = STOK68TI.EVRAKNO AND STOK68TI.KARSITRNUM  = STOK68T.KARSITRNUM "
							QSTR_fG_COUNT = QSTR_fG_COUNT & " WHERE STOK68TI.EVRAKNO = '"&A_STOK68TI.EVRAKNO(j)&"' AND STOK68TI.KOD = '"&A_STOK68TI.KOD(j)&"' AND STOK68TI.LOTNUMBER =  '"&A_STOK68TI.LOTNUMBER(j)&"' AND  STOK68TI.SERINO = '"&A_STOK68TI.SERINO(j)&"' "
							Set Y_STOK20TI = Doc.RunSqlQuery("WEWERTRTVVV" , QSTR_fG_COUNT )	
							MTableT.ORAN_M2F2 = Y_STOK20TI.GetRecCount()	
							satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
							MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
							MTableT.KOD = A_STOK68TI.KOD(j)
							MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", A_STOK68TI.KOD(j), "")
							MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK68TI.LOTNUMBER(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = "F2"
							MTableT.SERINO = A_STOK68TI.SERINO(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK68TI.EVRAKNO(j)
							MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = A_STOK68TI.SF_MIKTAR(j)
							MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) & "[" & trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & " - " &  trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i))  & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) & " (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) &"*"&MTableT.ORAN_M2F2&  ") ]"
						next	
					end if
			end if
		next	
		'**  K2 ÝÇÝN OKU

		for i = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount()
			KOD = MTableT.KOD(i)
			LOT = MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)
			SERI = MTableT.SERINO(i)

			if trim(MTableT.OKUNDU(i)) =  ""  AND trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "K2"  Then					
					Dim QSTR_K2			

										 
					QSTR_KXX = " SELECT EVRAKTYPE,  EVRAKNO, KOD, LOTNUMBER,SERINO  , TRNUM  FROM  STOK10A"
					QSTR_KXX = QSTR_KXX & " WHERE EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"' AND   EVRAKTYPE = 'K2 ' AND KOD = '"& KOD &"' AND LOTNUMBER = '"& LOT &"' AND SERINO = '"& SERI &"' "
					
					Set XX_STOK25T = Doc.RunSqlQuery("SDASDSAXX___" , QSTR_KXX)
									
					QSTR_K2 = " SELECT EVRAKTYPE,  EVRAKNO, KOD, LOTNUMBER,SERINO  , TRNUM, SF_MIKTAR FROM  STOK10A"
					QSTR_K2 = QSTR_K2 & " WHERE EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"'  AND KARSIEVRAKTYPE = 'K2' AND TRNUM = '"&XX_STOK25T.TRNUM(1)&"' "
					
					Set A_STOK25T = Doc.RunSqlQuery("KKKFF___" , QSTR_K2)	
					k2_satirsayisi = A_STOK25T.GetRecCount() 
					
					QSTR_K2__2 = " SELECT EVRAKTYPE,  EVRAKNO, KOD, LOTNUMBER,SERINO  , TRNUM FROM  STOK10A"
					QSTR_K2__2  = QSTR_K2__2  & " WHERE EVRAKNO = '"&MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)&"'  AND EVRAKTYPE = 'K2' AND SERINO = '"&A_STOK25T.SERINO(1)&"' "
										
					Set yy_STOK25T = Doc.RunSqlQuery("ZZSSDSDY" , QSTR_K2__2)
					if yy_STOK25T.GetRecCount() > 0 Then 											
						k2_satirsayisi = 0
					end if
					MTableT.OKUNDU(i) =  "*"			
					
					if k2_satirsayisi > 0 Then 
					
						for j = 1 To A_STOK25T.GetRecCount()		
							satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
							MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
							MTableT.KOD = A_STOK25T.KOD(j)
							MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", A_STOK25T.KOD(j), "")
							MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK25T.LOTNUMBER(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = "KD"
							MTableT.SERINO = A_STOK25T.SERINO(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK25T.EVRAKNO(j)
							MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = A_STOK25T.SF_MIKTAR(j) * -1
									
							MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) & "[" & trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i))  & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) & " (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) &"*"&MTableT.ORAN_M2F2&  ") ]"
							MTableT.SAYAC_K2 = MTableT.SAYAC_K2(i)
							 								
						next	
					end if
			end if
		next	
		
		' *** STOK 10A dan bul	
		' *****
	
		for i = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount()
			if trim(MTableT.OKUNDU(i)) = ""  Then
				KOD = MTableT.KOD(i)
				LOT = MTableT.LOTNUMBER(i)
				SERI = MTableT.SERINO(i)
						
				QSTR_A = " SELECT KOD, EVRAKNO, EVRAKTYPE, LOTNUMBER, SERINO, SUM(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR FROM STOK10A  WHERE  KOD = '"& KOD &"' AND LOTNUMBER = '"& LOT &"' AND SERINO = '"& SERI &"'  "
				QSTR_A = QSTR_A & " AND EVRAKTYPE IN ('MG', 'SI', 'SF', 'FG', 'K2', 'DG', 'DT','0S')  AND SF_MIKTAR > 0 "
				QSTR_A = QSTR_A & " GROUP BY KOD, EVRAKNO, EVRAKTYPE, LOTNUMBER, SERINO "
				
				Set A_STOK10A = Doc.RunSqlQuery("DSADASD_DD", QSTR_A)
									
				MTableT.OKUNDU(i) =  "*"					
				if A_STOK10A.GetRecCount() > 0 Then 
					
					for j = 1 To A_STOK10A.GetRecCount()					
						if trim(KOD) = trim(A_STOK10A.KOD(j)) and trim(LOT) = trim(A_STOK10A.LOTNUMBER(j)) and trim(SERI) = trim(A_STOK10A.SERINO(j)) and trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) Then
							a = 1
						else 	
							satirNo = MTableT.AddRow()
							MTableT.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
							F_SAY = F_SAY + 1 
							MTableT.F_SAYAC = F_SAY 							
							MTableT.KOD = A_STOK10A.KOD(j)
							MTableT.KOD_STOK00_AD = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", A_STOK10A.KOD(j), "")
							MTableT.LOTNUMBER = A_STOK10A.LOTNUMBER(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKTYPE = A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)
							MTableT.SERINO = A_STOK10A.SERINO(j)
							MTableT.EVRAKNO =  A_STOK10A.EVRAKNO(j)
							MTableT.SF_MIKTAR = A_STOK10A.SF_MIKTAR(j)
							
							MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) &  "[" & trim(MTableT.KOD(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i))  & " - " & trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) & " (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) &"*"&MTableT.ORAN_M2F2&  ") ]"
									
							if trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "DG" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "DT" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "0S" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "SF" OR trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "SI" Then
								MTableT.OKUNDU =  "*"
							end if 
																		
							if trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "KD"  Then				
									MTableT.SAYAC_K2 = MTableT.SAYAC_K2(i) 							
							end if 
							
							if trim(A_STOK10A.EVRAKTYPE(j)) = "K2" and trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) <> "KD"Then
									MTableT.SAYAC_K2(i) = SAYAC_1
									MTableT.SAYAC_K2 = SAYAC_1
									SAYAC_1  = SAYAC_1 + 1 
							end if
						end if	
					next	
				end if	
			else
			a = 1
			end if				
		next				
		Set DAS_ = Doc.Select2G( "dsasdrrrr", "MAKROTABLET" , " ", " ", "OKUNDU", -1 )
		ser = ser + 1
				
		if DAS_.GetRecCount() = 1 Then 
			icini_ac = 0
			'MSGBOX icini_ac
		end if
	Wend
	MTableT.sort("KAYNAK_KOD")
	sayil	 = 0
	for i = 1 TO MTableT.GetRecCount()
		MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i) = trim(MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)) & "[" & trim(MTableT.SERINO(i)) & "]"  & trim(MTableT.EVRAKNO(i)) &"-"& trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) & " (" & MTableT.SF_MIKTAR(i) &"*"&MTableT.ORAN_M2F2& ")"
	next

	for i = 1 TO MTableT.GetRecCount()
			if trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "0S" OR trim(MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(i)) = "SI" Then
				Set KAPP_ = Doc.Select2( "SWSWSSDDFFD", "MAKROTABLET" ,  "KAYNAK_KOD = '"&MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(i)&"'' ", "", -1 )
				if KAPP_.GetRecCount() > 1  Then				
				sayil	 = sayil	 + 1
					MTableT.AYNI(i) = sayil	
			 end if
			end if	 			
	next

	for k = 1 To MTableT.GetRecCount() 
		if MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(k) = "SI" OR  MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(k) = "SF" OR MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(k) = "0S" OR MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(k) =  "DG" OR MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(k) = "DT" Then
			satirNo = MTableT_2.AddRow()
			MTableT_2.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
			
			MTableT_2.KOD = MTableT.KOD(k)
			MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD = MTableT.KAYNAK_KOD(k)
			MTableT_2.SERINO = trim(MTableT.SERINO(k))
			MTableT_2.EVRAKNO = MTableT.EVRAKNO(k)
			MTableT_2.EVRAKTYPE = MTableT.EVRAKTYPE(k)
		end if	
	next

	Dim hareket_syisi, yildiz_syisi

	'' nihai tablo hazýrlanýyor
	for 	xx =1 To MTableT_2.GetREcCount() 
		yildiz_syisi = 0 
		hareket_syisi =  0
		ReDim aray(50,3) ' birinci özellik miktar ikinci  mg/m2 fg/f2 oraný, üçüncü özellik evraktype
		for i = 1 To len(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)))		
			if mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i ,  1) = "*" Then	 
				Dim miktar,oran,e_ty_al
				flag = 1
				flag_2 = 1
				
				for t = 1 To 16
					if mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t , 1 ) <> "(" and flag Then
						if flag = 1 then 
							miktar =  mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t , 1 )	& miktar
						end if 	
					elseif mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t , 1 ) = "(" 	then
						flag = 0
						e_ty_al = mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t - 3  , 1 ) & mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t - 2 , 1 )
						
					end if
					
					if mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i + t , 1 ) <> ")"  Then					
						if flag_2 = 1 then oran = oran &  mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i + t , 1 )
					else	
						flag_2 = 0
					end if
				next	

				aray(yildiz_syisi,0) = miktar
				aray(yildiz_syisi,1) = oran
				aray(yildiz_syisi,2) = e_ty_al
				miktar = ""
				oran = ""
				yildiz_syisi = yildiz_syisi + 1				
			elseif mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i ,  1) = "#" Then
				flaf_ax = 1
				for t = 1 To 30				
					if i - t > 0  Then
						if mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t , 1 ) <> "["  Then
							if flaf_ax = 1 Then fatura_kod = mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t , 1 ) & fatura_kod						
						elseif mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t , 1 ) = "[" Then	
							 flaf_ax = 0
							 fatura_sirasi = mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t - 3 , 1 ) & mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t - 2 , 1 ) & mid(trim(MTableT_2.KAYNAK_KOD(xx)), i - t - 1 , 1 )
						end if
					end if	
				next	
			end if
		next
		s_array = 0

		MTableT_2.FAT_KOD(xx) = fatura_kod
		MTableT_2.FAT_SIRA(xx) = fatura_sirasi
		fatura_kod = ""
		fatura_sirasi = ""
	
		for t = 0 TO ubound	(aray,1)
			if trim(aray(t+1,0)) = "" Then Exit For
			if trim(aray(t, 2)) = "K2" OR trim(aray(t, 2)) = "KD" Then	
				aray(t, 0) = 1 
				aray(t, 1) = 1
			end if
		next

		for t = 0 TO ubound	(aray,1)
			if trim(aray(t+1,0)) = "" Then Exit For						
			if Doc.Fmod(t+1, 2) = 1 and round(cdbl(trim(aray(t,0))),4) <> 0 and round(cdbl(trim(aray(t+1,0))),4) <> 0 Then			
				aray(t+1,1) = round(cdbl(round(cdbl(round(cdbl(trim(aray(t+1,0))),4) / round(cdbl(trim(aray(t,0))),4)),4) * round(cdbl(trim(aray(t+1,1))),4)),4)
			elseif round(cdbl(trim(aray(t,1))),4) <> 0 and round(cdbl(trim(aray(t+1,0))),4) <> 0 Then
				aray(t+1 ,0) =  round(cdbl(round(cdbl(trim(aray(t+1,0))),4) / round(cdbl(trim(aray(t,1))),4)),4)
			end if	
			s_array = s_array + 1 
		next

		MTableT_2.SF_MIKTAR_C(xx) = aray(s_array - 1,0)
		
	next

	Set Last_xx = Doc.Select2G("RRRRF" ,"MAKROTABLET_2", "",  "", "EVRAKNO+SERINO+FAT_KOD", -1)

		for k = 1 To Last_xx.GetRecCount()
			satirNo = MTableT_3.AddRow()
			MTableT_3.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				
			MTableT_3.KOD = trim(Last_xx.KOD(k))
			MTableT_3.FAT_KOD = trim(Last_xx.FAT_KOD(k))
			MTableT_3.FAT_SIRA = trim(Last_xx.FAT_SIRA(k))
			MTableT_3.KOD_STOK00_AD = trim(Last_xx.KOD_STOK00_AD(k))
			MTableT_3.KAYNAK_KOD = trim(Last_xx.KAYNAK_KOD(k))
			MTableT_3.SERINO = trim(Last_xx.SERINO(k))
			MTableT_3.EVRAKNO = trim(Last_xx.EVRAKNO(k))
			MTableT_3.EVRAKTYPE = trim(Last_xx.EVRAKTYPE(k))
			MTableT_3.SF_MIKTAR_C = Last_xx.SF_MIKTAR_C(k)
						
			if trim(Last_xx.EVRAKTYPE(k)) = "SI" Then		
				Set SI_STOK29T = Doc.Select1("TYTRHR", "STOK29T", "EVRAKNO = '"&Last_xx.EVRAKNO(k)&"' &KOD = '"&trim(Last_xx.KOD(k))&"' & SERINO = '"&trim(Last_xx.SERINO(k))&"' ", "", -1 )
				if SI_STOK29T.GetRecCount() > 0 Then
					MTableT_3.SF_MIKTAR_G = SI_STOK29T.SF_MIKTAR(1)
					MTableT_3.TL_FIYAT = SI_STOK29T.TL_FIYAT(1)
					MTableT_3.TL_TUTAR = SI_STOK29T.TL_FIYAT(1) *  round(cdbl(trim(Last_xx.SF_MIKTAR_C(k))),2)
					MTableT_3.PRICE = "TL"
				end if	
			elseif	trim(Last_xx.EVRAKTYPE(k)) = "0S" Then						
					Set S_STOK2AT = Doc.Select1("ERETRR", "STOK2AT", "EVRAKNO = '"&Last_xx.EVRAKNO(k)&"' &KOD = '"&trim(Last_xx.KOD(k))&"' & SERINO = '"&trim(Last_xx.SERINO(k))&"' ", "", -1 )					
					if S_STOK2AT.GetRecCount() > 0 Then
						MTableT_3.SF_MIKTAR_G = S_STOK2AT.SF_MIKTAR(1)
						MTableT_3.TL_FIYAT = S_STOK2AT.TL_FIYAT(1)
						MTableT_3.TL_TUTAR = S_STOK2AT.TL_FIYAT(1) *  round(cdbl(trim(Last_xx.SF_MIKTAR_C(k))),2)
						MTableT_3.PRICE = "TL"
					end if	
			elseif	trim(Last_xx.EVRAKTYPE(k)) = "DG" Then						
				'MSGBOX "B"
					Set S_STOK21T = Doc.Select1("LKLKLKLRER", "STOK21T", "EVRAKNO = '"&Last_xx.EVRAKNO(k)&"' &KOD = '"&trim(Last_xx.KOD(k))&"' & SERINO = '"&trim(Last_xx.SERINO(k))&"' ", "", -1 )					
					if S_STOK21T.GetRecCount() > 0 Then
						MTableT_3.SF_MIKTAR_G = S_STOK21T.SF_MIKTAR(1)
						MTableT_3.TL_FIYAT = S_STOK21T.TL_FIYAT(1)
						MTableT_3.TL_TUTAR = S_STOK21T.TL_FIYAT(1) *  round(cdbl(trim(Last_xx.SF_MIKTAR_C(k))),2)
						MTableT_3.PRICE = "TL"
					end if					
				elseif	trim(Last_xx.EVRAKTYPE(k)) = "DT" Then								
				'MSGBOX "A"
					Set S_STOK24T = Doc.Select1("XXAAADDD", "STOK24T", "EVRAKNO = '"&Last_xx.EVRAKNO(k)&"' &KOD = '"&trim(Last_xx.KOD(k))&"' & SERINO = '"&trim(Last_xx.SERINO(k))&"' ", "", -1 )					
					if S_STOK24T.GetRecCount() > 0 Then
						MTableT_3.SF_MIKTAR_G = S_STOK24T.SF_MIKTAR(1)
						MTableT_3.TL_FIYAT = S_STOK24T.TL_FIYAT(1)
						MTableT_3.TL_TUTAR = S_STOK24T.TL_FIYAT(1) *  round(cdbl(trim(Last_xx.SF_MIKTAR_C(k))),2)
						MTableT_3.PRICE = "TL"
					end if			
			end if
		next

	Call Yazdir_2()
	'Doc.ShowTableDynForm MTableT.GetAppName
End Sub

Sub Yazdir_2()

	SayfaBoyu = 290
	SayfaEni = 899
	SolMargin = 6
	SagMargin = 1
	UstMargin = 20
	AltMargin = 1

	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0,"Order Confirmation", SayfaBoyu, SayfaEni, "")

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("Left")
	res = pe1.SelectFont(Arial, 10)
	TOPUTU = 0
	CIKAN = 0
	Set MTableT_3 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET_3")
	'MTableT.SORT("EVRAKTYPE + EVRAKNO+SERINO+ KOD +KAYNAK KOD")
	MTableT_3.SORT("SERINO")
	
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, 10, 10 , 30 , "FT SIRA NO")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight, 10 , 40 , "FATURA KOD")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight, 10 , 20 , "ETYP")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 30 , "EVRAKNO")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , "KOD")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , "SERÝNO")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , "GÝRÝÞ MÝKTAR")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , "GÝRÝÞ FÝYATI")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , "ÇIKIÞ MÝKTAR")
	res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , "ÇIKIÞ TUTARI")	
	res = pe1.SelectFont(Arial, 8)
	
	for i = 1 To  MTableT_3.GetRecCount()
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 8, 10, 10 , 30 , trim(MTableT_3.FAT_SIRA(i)))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 40 , 30 , trim(MTableT_3.FAT_KOD(i)))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 20 , 30 , trim(MTableT_3.EVRAKTYPE(i)))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 30 , trim(MTableT_3.EVRAKNO(i)))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , trim(MTableT_3.KOD(i)))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , trim(MTableT_3.SERINO(i)))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , trim(MTableT_3.SF_MIKTAR_G(i)))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , trim(MTableT_3.TL_FIYAT(i)))		
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , trim(MTableT_3.SF_MIKTAR_C(i)))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , trim(MTableT_3.TL_TUTAR(i)))
		
		TOPUTU = TOPUTU +  MTableT_3.TL_TUTAR(i)
		CIKAN = CIKAN + MTableT_3.SF_MIKTAR_C(i)
		if i =  MTableT_3.GetRecCount() Then
		 res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 10 , pe1.GetLeft , 10 , 40 ,  	Doc.NumFormat0(TOPUTU,12,3))
		 res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + 10 , pe1.GetLeft , 10 , 40 ,  	Doc.NumFormat0(CIKAN,12,3))
		end if 
		
		if pe1.GetTop() > 280 Then
			res = pe1.PageBreak()			 
			res = pe1.write(UstMargin, 10, 10 , 30 , "FT SIRA NO")
			res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight, 10 , 40 , "FATURA KOD")
			res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight, 10 , 20 , "ETYP")
			res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 30 , "EVRAKNO")
			res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , "KOD")
			res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , "SERÝNO")
			res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , "GÝRÝÞ MÝKTAR")
			res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , "GÝRÝÞ FÝYATI")
			res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , "ÇIKIÞ MÝKTAR")
			res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 40 , "ÇIKIÞ TUTARI")
			'res = pe1.write(UstMargin, pe1.GetRight , 10 , 600 , "KAYNAK KOD")				
		end if
	next
		MSGBOX "C__________  "  &  Time()
End Sub                  

Ekran Formu

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : FORMA_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : PDM-22R
Hangi Tablo için : MAKR7S
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : PDM 'den Ürün Aðaçlarýný Aktar
Kod : 
Const cnnStr = "Provider=SQLNCLI;Data Source=pdm-server;Initial catalog=FORMAPDM;User Id=erp;Password=Freedom2010;"
Public TumunuAktar
Sub Makro1()
	Set ffff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	TumunuAktar = False

	QStr = "SELECT X.BomDocumentID, DK.MAMUL_DOC_ID, X.ROWNO, D.Filename, D.LatestRevisionNo REV_NO, D.LockPath, X.ITEM_NO, "
	QStr = QStr & " X.PART_NUMBER, X.DESCRIPTION1, X.QTY, DK.STOK_KODU"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM BOM_LIST X"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN [dbo].[BomSheets] E ON E.BomDocumentID=X.BomDocumentID"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN [dbo].[Documents] D ON D.DocumentID=E.SourceDocumentID AND E.SourceDocumentVersion=D.LatestRevisionNo"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN [dbo].DOC_KOD2 DK ON D.DocumentID=DK.MAMUL_DOC_ID"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE 1=1 "
	QStr = QStr & " AND D.Deleted<>1"
	QStr = QStr & " AND DK.STOK_KODU LIKE '15%' AND DK.STOK_KODU NOT LIKE '152%'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND DK.STOK_KODU='151 001 01500'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND X.BomDocumentID IN"
	QStr = QStr & " ("
	QStr = QStr & " 		SELECT MAX(X.BomDocumentID) doc_id"
	QStr = QStr & " 		FROM BOM_LIST X"
	QStr = QStr & " 		LEFT JOIN [dbo].[BomSheets] E ON E.BomDocumentID=X.BomDocumentID"
	QStr = QStr & " 		LEFT JOIN [dbo].[Documents] D ON D.DocumentID=E.SourceDocumentID AND E.SourceDocumentVersion=D.LatestRevisionNo"
	QStr = QStr & " 		LEFT JOIN [dbo].DOC_KOD2 DK ON D.DocumentID=DK.MAMUL_DOC_ID"
	QStr = QStr & " 		WHERE 1=1 "
	QStr = QStr & " 		AND D.Deleted<>1"
	QStr = QStr & " 		AND DK.STOK_KODU LIKE '15%'"
	QStr = QStr & " 		GROUP BY DK.STOK_KODU"
	QStr = QStr & " 	)"
	QStr = QStr & " GROUP BY X.BomDocumentID, X.ROWNO, D.Filename, D.LatestRevisionNo, D.LockPath, DK.STOK_KODU, DK.MAMUL_DOC_ID,"
	QStr = QStr & " X.ITEM_NO, X.PART_NUMBER, X.DESCRIPTION1, X.QTY"
	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY DK.STOK_KODU"

	Set Par1 = Doc.CreateSObject("PARAMETERS")
	Par1.SetParameter "REPORT_CONNECTION_STRING", CnnStr
	Set BomTable = Doc.RunSQLQuery_Par("_DDDD_2333",QStr,Par1)

	Doc.ShowTableDynForm2 BomTable.GetAppName, "BomDocumentID, MAMUL_DOC_ID, ROWNO, Filename, REV_NO, LockPath, ITEM_NO, PART_NUMBER, DESCRIPTION1, QTY, STOK_KODU"
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : FORMA_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : STOK46
Hangi Tablo için : STOK46
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	Set KTT = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_S46TONG")
	KTT.Setd7FieldEvent "SKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "SKOD_AV"

	Set STOK46T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK46T")
	STOK46T.AddField2 "MPSNO", "CHAR", 10, 0, "R", "F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')", ""
End Sub
Sub SKOD_AV()
	Set KTT = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_S46TONG")
	KTT.SKOD_AD = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", KTT.SKOD, "")
End Sub
Sub AfterLoad2()
	Set KTT = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_S46TONG")
	For i = 1 to KTT.GetRowCount()
		KTT.SKOD_AD(i) = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", KTT.SKOD(i), "")
	Next
	Call KTAlariYukle()
End Sub
Sub KTAlariYukle()
	if Doc.IsBlank(STOK46T) Then Set STOK46T = Doc.GetTableObject("STOK46T")
	For i = 1 to STOK46T.GetRowCount()
		STOK46T.MPSNO(i) = CStr(Doc.GetUDFValue("S46T", STOK46T.ARTNO(i), "MPSNO", 0))
	Next
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set S20T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("asdfasdf", "SELECT DISTINCT EVRAKNO FROM SFDC20T WHERE SURE>0 AND TOPLAM_OPRTR_S_ST=0")
	For i = 1 to S20T.GetRowCOunt()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "% " & Doc.Round(Doc.Div(i, S20T.GetRowCount()) * 100,1)
		Doc.Load_Voucher S20T.EVRAKNO(i)
		Doc.Save_Voucher
	Next
End Sub

Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : FORMA_2014 / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : MPS-HESAP3
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : Ürün aðacýna yeni eklenen satýrlarý MPS 'e ekle - Liste Sayfasý
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	For i = 1 to LISTET.GetRowCount()
		Doc.Load_Voucher LISTET.EVRAKNO(i)
		ret = MPSHesapla()
		Doc.Save_Voucher
	Next
End Sub
Function MPSHesapla()
	if Doc.IsBlank(MMPS10T) Then Set MMPS10T = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T")
	if Doc.IsBlank(MMPS10E) Then Set MMPS10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	if Doc.IsBlank(MMPS10TM) Then Set MMPS10TM = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")

	Set ddd = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	MPSHesapla = False
	MMPS10T.SafePack()
	ret = Doc.Calc_Bom("1","M", MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU,MMPS10E.SF_PAKETSAYISI,MMPS10E.SF_PAKETICERIGI,MMPS10E.FOKOD_MAMULKODU,"","","","","","","")
	Set Tbmrsys = Doc.Select2("asfewrajaj", "_WBMRSYS_SELECT", "BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'", "", -1)

	For i = 1 to Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
'		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Ürün Aðacý Kontrol Ediliyor (1) : " & i & " / " & Tbmrsys.GetRowCount()
		QStr = "R_KAYNAKTYPE='" & Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) & "'&R_KAYNAKKODU='" & Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) & "'"
		QStr = QStr & "&R_OPERASYON='" & Tbmrsys.BOMREC_OPERASYON(i) & "'"

		Set M10T2 = Doc.Select2("asdfefhad", "MMPS10T", QStr, "", -1)

		if M10T2.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
			satirNo = MMPS10T.AddRow
		Elseif M10T2.GetRowCount() = 1 Then
			satirNo = Doc.Cdbl(M10T2.SRNUM(1))

		Else
			satirNo = 0
			Doc.d7MsgBox "Kaynak Türü : " & Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i) & ", Kaynak Kodu : " & Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i) & " MPS 'de birden fazla satýrda bulundu", "", MB_OK
		End if
		if satirNo > 0 Then
			MPSStr = MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR0(satirNo) & MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR1(satirNo) & MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR2(satirNo) & _ 
						MMPS10T.R_YMK_YMPAKET(satirNo) & MMPS10T.R_YMK_YMPAKETICERIGI(satirNo) & MMPS10T.RECETENOTU(satirNo) & _
						MMPS10T.FOKOD(satirNo)
			BomStr = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i) & Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK1(i) & Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK2(i) & _
							Tbmrsys.BOMREC_YMAMULPS(i) & Tbmrsys.BOMREC_YMAMULPM(i) & Tbmrsys.BOMREC_NOT(i) & Tbmrsys.FOKOD(i)
			if MPSStr <> BomStr Then
				'Doc.d7MsgBox "Deðiþti", "", MB_OK
				MMPS10T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
				MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_INPUTTYPE(i)
				MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i)
				MMPS10T.R_SIRANO(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_SIRANO(i)
				MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_OPERASYON(i)
				MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR0(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
				MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR1(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK1(i)
				MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR2(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK2(i)
				MMPS10T.R_MIKTART(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i) + Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK1(i) + Tbmrsys.BOMREC_KAYNAK2(i)

				MMPS10T.R_YMK_MPAKET(satirNo) = MMPS10E.SF_PAKETSAYISI
				MMPS10T.R_YMK_YMPAKET(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_YMAMULPS(i)
				MMPS10T.R_YMK_YMPAKETICERIGI(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_YMAMULPM(i)
				MMPS10T.RECETENOTU(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.BOMREC_NOT(i)
				MMPS10T.FOKOD(satirNo) = Tbmrsys.FOKOD(i)
			End if
		End if
	Next
	For i = 1 to MMPS10T.GetRowCount()
'		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Ürün Aðacý Kontrol Ediliyor (1) : " & i & " / " & MMPS10T.GetRowCount()

		if MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i) = "H" Then
			QStr = "BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='" & MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i) & "'&BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE='" & MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i) & "'"
			QStr = QStr & "&BOMREC_OPERASYON='" & MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON(i) & "'"
			Set BOM = Doc.Select2("asdfefhad", "_WBMRSYS_SELECT", QStr, "", -1)
			if BOM.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
				if trim(GetBaglantiliMPSNo(MMPS10T.TRNUM(i))) <> "" Then
					MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR0(i) = 0
					MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR1(i) = 0
					MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR2(i) = 0
					MMPS10T.R_MIKTART(i) = 0
				Else
					MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i) = ""
					MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(i) = ""
					MMPS10T.R_SIRANO(i) = ""
					MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON(i) = ""
				End if
			End if
		End if
	Next
	MMPS10T.SafePack
	MMPS10T.Sort("R_KAYNAKTYPE+R_KAYNAKKODU")
End Function
Function BaglantiliMPSSorun2()
	BaglantiliMPSSorun2 = False
	QStr = "SELECT MMPS10TM.EVRAKNO, MMPS10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU, STOK00.AD FROM MMPS10TM"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 ON STOK00.KOD=MMPS10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE='UP' and KAYNAKMPS='" & MMPS10E.EVRAKNO & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM IN (SELECT TRNUM FROM MMPS10T WHERE EVRAKNO='" & MMPS10E.EVRAKNO & "')"
	Set M10TM = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfgsfhs", QStr)
	if M10TM.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	
	BaglantiliMPSSorun2 = False
	Set ggg = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	Doc.d7MsgBox "Bu MPS 'e ait baðlantýlý MPS 'ler var, hammadde satýrlarý silinemez. Baðlantýlý MPS 'ler : ", "", MB_OK
	
	For i = 1 to M10TM.GetRowCount()
		Doc.d7MsgBox "MPS No : " & trim(M10TM.EVRAKNO(i)) & ", Stok Kodu : " & Trim(M10TM.MAMULSTOKKODU(i)) & " - " & Trim(M10TM.AD(i)), "", MB_OK
	Next
End Function
Function GetBaglantiliMPSNo(TrNum)
	if Doc.IsBlank(MMPS10E) Then Set MMPS10E = Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")
	GetBaglantiliMPSNo = ""
	QStr = "SELECT MMPS10TM.EVRAKNO FROM MMPS10TM"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE KAYNAKMPS_EVRAKTYPE='UP' and KAYNAKMPS='" & MMPS10E.EVRAKNO & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND KAYNAKMPS_TRNUM='" & TrNum & "'"
	Set M10TM = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfgsfhs", QStr)
	if M10TM.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	GetBaglantiliMPSNo = M10TM.EVRAKNO(1)
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	if FaturalananSatirdaDegisiklikVar() Then
	End if

Function FaturalananSatirdaDegisiklikVar()
	Set fff = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	FaturalananSatirdaDegisiklikVar = False

	QStr = "SELECT ARTNO, OR_FIYAT, PRICEUNIT, SF_MIKTAR, SF_SF_UNIT"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM STOK40T"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE EVRAKNO='" & STOK40E.EVRAKNO & "'"
	QStr = QStr & " AND (SF_FATURALANANMIKTAR>0 OR SF_NETKAPANANMIK>0)"
	
	Set S40T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("asdasdga", QStr)
	if S40T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function

	For i = 1 to STOK40T.GetRowCount()
		Set S40T1 = Doc.Select2("sdfasdga", S40T.GetAppName, "ARTNO='" & STOK40T.ARTNO(i) & "'", "", -1)
		if S40T1.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			if S40T1.SF_MIKTAR(1) & S40T1.SF_SF_UNIT(1) & S40T1.OR_FIYAT(1) & S40T1.PRICEUNIT(1) <> _
				STOK40T.SF_MIKTAR(i) & STOK40T.SF_SF_UNIT(i) & STOK40T.OR_FIYAT(i) & STOK40T.PRICEUNIT(i) Then
				Doc.d7MsgBox i & " satýr, sevk edilen/faturalanan satýrlarda miktar ve fiyat deðiþikliði yapýlamaz", "", MB_OK
		
				FaturalananSatirdaDegisiklikVar = True
			End if
		End if
	Next
End Function

Sub Makro1()
	if Doc.IsBlank(KTT_HATA00) Then Set KTT_HATA00 = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_HATA00")

	KTT_HATA00.Setd7FieldEvent "SKOD","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "SKOD_AV"
	KTT_HATA00.Setd7FieldEvent "MPSNO","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "MPSNO_AV"
	KTT_HATA00.Setd7FieldEvent "CH_KODU","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "CH_KODU_AV"
	KTT_HATA00.Setd7FieldEvent "TEZGAH","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "TEZGAH_AV"
	KTT_HATA00.Setd7FieldEvent "KNT_NOKT","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "KNT_NOKT_AV"
	KTT_HATA00.Setd7FieldEvent "QC_OP_KODU","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "QC_OP_KODU_AV"
	KTT_HATA00.Setd7FieldEvent "HATA_KODU","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "HATA_KODU_AV"
	KTT_HATA00.Setd7FieldEvent "HATA_KY","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "HATA_KY_AV"
	KTT_HATA00.Setd7FieldEvent "OP_KODU","AFTERVALIDATE","MAKR7S." & SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME, "OP_KODU_AV"


	Set MTable = Doc.CreateTableObject("LISTEE=STACK","IO")
	MTable.Addfield2 "EVRAKNOB","CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('KTT_HATA00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "EVRAKNOE","CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('KTT_HATA00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TARIHB","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TARIHE","DATE",10,0,"L","",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "KNT_NOKTB","CHAR",10,0,"L","","!KNT_NOKT"
	MTable.Addfield2 "KNT_NOKTE","CHAR",10,0,"L","","!KNT_NOKT"
	MTable.Addfield2 "TEZGAHB","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('IMLT00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "TEZGAHE","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('IMLT00')","" 
	MTable.Addfield2 "SKODB","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "SKODE","CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')","" 
	MTable.Addfield2 "OP_KODUB","CHAR",16,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('PERS00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "OP_KODUE","CHAR",16,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('PERS00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "CH_KODUB","CHAR",16,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('CARI00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "CH_KODUE","CHAR",16,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('CARI00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "QC_OP_KODUB","CHAR",16,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('PERS00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "QC_OP_KODUE","CHAR",16,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('PERS00')",""
	MTable.Addfield2 "HATA_KODUB","CHAR",10,0,"L","","!HATA_KODU"
	MTable.Addfield2 "HATA_KODUE","CHAR",10,0,"L","","!HATA_KODU"
	MTable.Addfield2 "HATA_KYB","CHAR",10,0,"L","","-!HATA_KYN"
	MTable.Addfield2 "HATA_KYE","CHAR",10,0,"L","","!HATA_KYN"
	MTable.Addfield2 "ACIKKAPALI","CHAR",1,0,"L","","|AKTYPE2"

	QStr1 = GetQStr1()
	
	QStr = QStr1 & " WHERE 1=0"

	Set MTableT = Doc.RunSQLQuery("LISTET", QStr)
	MTableT.SetFieldToDate("TARIH") 

	Call F2Degistir("LISTET", "SKOD", "STOK00")
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub MPSNO_AV()
	if Doc.IsBlank(KTT_HATA00) Then Set KTT_HATA00 = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_HATA00")
	if trim(KTT_HATA00.MPSNO) = "" Then
		KTT_HATA00.SKOD = ""
		KTT_HATA00.SKOD_AD = ""
		KTT_HATA00.SF_SF_UNIT = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if

	QStr = "SELECT M.MAMULSTOKKODU, S.AD, S.IUNIT FROM MMPS10E M"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S ON S.KOD=M.MAMULSTOKKODU"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE M.EVRAKNO='" & KTT_HATA00.MPSNO & "'"
	Set M10E = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfasdga", QStr)
	if M10E.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "MPS No bulunamadý", "", MB_OK
		KTT_HATA00.MPSNO = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	KTT_HATA00.SKOD = M10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(1)
	KTT_HATA00.SKOD_AD = M10E.AD(1)
	KTT_HATA00.SF_SF_UNIT = M10E.IUNIT(1)

End Sub
Sub SKOD_AV()
	if Doc.IsBlank(KTT_HATA00) Then Set KTT_HATA00 = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_HATA00")
	if trim(KTT_HATA00.SKOD) = "" Then
		KTT_HATA00.SKOD_AD = ""
		KTT_HATA00.SF_SF_UNIT = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if

	QStr = "SELECT AD, IUNIT FROM STOK00 WHERE KOD='" & KTT_HATA00.SKOD & "'"
	Set S00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfsdg", QStr)
	if S00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Stok Kodu", "", MB_OK
		KTT_HATA00.SKOD = ""
		KTT_HATA00.SKOD_AD = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if
	KTT_HATA00.SKOD_AD = S00.AD(1)
	KTT_HATA00.SF_SF_UNIT = S00.IUNIT(1)
End Sub
Sub CH_KODU_AV()
	if Doc.IsBlank(KTT_HATA00) Then Set KTT_HATA00 = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_HATA00")
	if trim(KTT_HATA00.CH_KODU) = "" Then
		KTT_HATA00.CH_ADI = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if

	QStr = "SELECT AD FROM CARI00 WHERE KOD='" & KTT_HATA00.CH_KODU & "'"
	Set C00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfsdg", QStr)
	if C00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Cari Hesap Kodu", "", MB_OK
		KTT_HATA00.CH_KODU = ""
		KTT_HATA00.CH_ADI = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if
	KTT_HATA00.CH_ADI = C00.AD(1)
End Sub
Sub OP_KODU_AV()
	if Doc.IsBlank(KTT_HATA00) Then Set KTT_HATA00 = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_HATA00")
	if trim(KTT_HATA00.OP_KODU) = "" Then
		KTT_HATA00.OP_ADI = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if

	QStr = "SELECT AD FROM PERS00 WHERE KOD='" & KTT_HATA00.OP_KODU & "'"
	Set P00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfsdg", QStr)
	if P00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Personel Kodu", "", MB_OK
		KTT_HATA00.OP_KODU = ""
		KTT_HATA00.OP_ADI = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if
	KTT_HATA00.OP_ADI = P00.AD(1)
End Sub
Sub TEZGAH_AV()
	if Doc.IsBlank(KTT_HATA00) Then Set KTT_HATA00 = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_HATA00")
	if trim(KTT_HATA00.TEZGAH) = "" Then
		KTT_HATA00.TEZGAH_ADI = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if

	QStr = "SELECT AD FROM IMLT00 WHERE KOD='" & KTT_HATA00.TEZGAH & "'"
	Set I00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfsdg", QStr)
	if I00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Tezgah Kodu", "", MB_OK
		KTT_HATA00.TEZGAH = ""
		KTT_HATA00.TEZGAH_ADI = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if
	KTT_HATA00.TEZGAH_ADI = I00.AD(1)
End Sub
Sub KNT_NOKT_AV()
	if Doc.IsBlank(KTT_HATA00) Then Set KTT_HATA00 = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_HATA00")
	KTT_HATA00.KNT_NKT_AD = Doc.GetGecoValue("!KNT_NOKT", "AD", KTT_HATA00.KNT_NOKT, -1)
End Sub
Sub QC_OP_KODU_AV()
	if Doc.IsBlank(KTT_HATA00) Then Set KTT_HATA00 = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_HATA00")
	if trim(KTT_HATA00.QC_OP_KODU) = "" Then
		KTT_HATA00.QC_OP_ADI = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if

	QStr = "SELECT AD FROM PERS00 WHERE KOD='" & KTT_HATA00.QC_OP_KODU & "'"
	Set P00 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("sdfsdg", QStr)
	if P00.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Geçersiz Personel Kodu", "", MB_OK
		KTT_HATA00.QC_OP_KODU = ""
		KTT_HATA00.QC_OP_ADI = ""
		Exit Sub
	End if
	KTT_HATA00.QC_OP_ADI = P00.AD(1)
End Sub
Sub HATA_KODU_AV()
	if Doc.IsBlank(KTT_HATA00) Then Set KTT_HATA00 = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_HATA00")
	KTT_HATA00.HATA_ADI = Doc.GetGecoValue("!HATA_KODU", "AD", KTT_HATA00.HATA_KODU, -1)
End Sub
Sub HATA_KY_AV()
	if Doc.IsBlank(KTT_HATA00) Then Set KTT_HATA00 = Doc.GetTableObject("KTT_HATA00")
	KTT_HATA00.HATA_KY_AD = Doc.GetGecoValue("!HATA_KYN", "AD", KTT_HATA00.HATA_KY, -1)
End Sub
Sub AfterLoad2() 'AfterLoad Çaðýrýyor
	Call SKOD_AV()
	Call CH_KODU_AV()
	Call TEZGAH_AV()
	Call KNT_NOKT_AV()
	Call QC_OP_KODU_AV()
	Call HATA_KODU_AV()
	Call HATA_KY_AV()
	Call OP_KODU_AV()
End Sub
Sub cmdSuz()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("LISTET")
	Set LISTEE = Doc.GetTableObject("LISTEE")

	QStr1 = GetQStr1
	QStr = QStr1 & " WHERE 1=1"

	QStr = QStr & GetQStr(LISTEE, "EVRAKNOB", "EVRAKNOE", "KTT", "EVRAKNO")
	QStr = QStr & GetQStr(LISTEE, "TARIHB", "TARIHE", "KTT", "TARIH")
	QStr = QStr & GetQStr(LISTEE, "KNT_NOKTB", "KNT_NOKTE", "KTT", "KNT_NOKT")
	QStr = QStr & GetQStr(LISTEE, "TEZGAHB", "TEZGAHE", "KTT", "TEZGAH")
	QStr = QStr & GetQStr(LISTEE, "SKODB", "SKODE", "KTT", "SKOD")
	QStr = QStr & GetQStr(LISTEE, "OP_KODUB", "OP_KODUE", "KTT", "OP_KODU")
	QStr = QStr & GetQStr(LISTEE, "CH_KODUB", "CH_KODUE", "KTT", "CH_KODU")
	QStr = QStr & GetQStr(LISTEE, "QC_OP_KODUB", "QC_OP_KODUB", "KTT", "QC_OP_KODU")
	QStr = QStr & GetQStr(LISTEE, "HATA_KODUB", "HATA_KODUE", "KTT", "HATA_KODU")
	QStr = QStr & GetQStr(LISTEE, "HATA_KYB", "HATA_KYE", "KTT", "HATA_KY")
	if trim(LISTEE.ACIKKAPALI) <> "" Then
		QStr = QStr & " AND KTT.ACIKKAPALI='" & LISTEE.ACIKKAPALI & "'"
	End if

	QStr = QStr & " ORDER BY KTT.EVRAKNO"
	Set Tbl1 = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfgstjsk", QStr)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	MTableT.Empty
	MTableT.AppendTable(Tbl1)
	MTableT.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub cmdSec()
	Set MTableT = Doc.GetTableObject("LISTET")
	Doc.Load_Voucher MTableT.EVRAKNO
End Sub
Function GetQStr(KriterTbl, AlanB, AlanE, Tablo, Alan)
	GetQStr = ""
	GetQStr = GetQStr & " AND " & Tablo & "." & Alan & ">='" & KriterTbl.GetFieldValue(AlanB, 1) & "'"
	if trim(KriterTbl.GetFieldValue(AlanE, 1)) <> "" Then
		GetQStr = GetQStr & " AND " & Tablo & "." & Alan & "<='" & KriterTbl.GetFieldValue(AlanE, 1) & "'"
	End if
End Function
Function GetQStr1()
	QStr1 = "SELECT KTT.*, C.AD CH_ADI, S.AD SKOD_AD, GHATA_KODU.AD HATA_ADI, GKNT_NOKT.AD KNT_NKT_AD,"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " GHATA_KY.AD HATA_KY_AD, I.AD TEZGAH_ADI, P.AD OP_ADI, GQC_OP_KODU.AD QC_OP_ADI, M.SF_URETILEN"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " FROM KTT_HATA00 KTT"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S ON S.KOD=KTT.SKOD"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN CARI00 C ON C.KOD=KTT.CH_KODU"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN PERS00 P ON P.KOD=KTT.OP_KODU"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN IMLT00 I ON I.KOD=KTT.TEZGAH"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN MMPS10TM M ON M.EVRAKNO=KTT.MPSNO"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN GECOUST GHATA_KODU ON GHATA_KODU.KOD=KTT.HATA_KODU AND GHATA_KODU.EVRAKNO='!HATA_KODU'"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN GECOUST GKNT_NOKT ON GKNT_NOKT.KOD=KTT.KNT_NOKT AND GKNT_NOKT.EVRAKNO='!KNT_NOKT'"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN PERS00 GQC_OP_KODU ON GQC_OP_KODU.KOD=KTT.QC_OP_KODU"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN GECOUST GHATA_KY ON GHATA_KY.KOD=KTT.HATA_KY AND GHATA_KY.EVRAKNO='!HATA_KYN'"
	GetQStr1 = QStr1
End Function
Sub F2Degistir(TabloStr, AlanAdi, ByVal BrwTable)
    BrwTable = "'" & BrwTable & "'"
    Set tbl=doc.GetRowsetObject(TabloStr)
    Set fd=tbl.GetFieldDef_E(AlanAdi)
    fd.F2Fnc="DO_BRW(" & Replace(BrwTable,"'", """") & ")"
    tbl.SetFieldProperties1 AlanAdi,fd
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
	Set MTable = Doc.CreateTableObject("MPS_KRITER=STACK", "IO")
	MTable.AddField2 "B_EVRAKNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')",""
	MTable.AddField2 "E_EVRAKNO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('MMPS10E')",""
	MTable.AddField2 "B_MUSTERISIPINO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('STOK40E')",""
	MTable.AddField2 "E_MUSTERISIPINO", "CHAR",10,0,"R","F2=DO_BRW('STOK40E')",""
	MTable.AddField2 "R_STATUS1", "CHAR",1,0,"L","","|MPSST"
	MTable.AddField2 "R_STATUS2", "CHAR",1,0,"L","","|MPSST"
	MTable.AddField2 "R_STATUS3", "CHAR",1,0,"L","","|MPSST"
	MTable.AddField2 "B_R_KAYNAKKODU", "CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTable.AddField2 "E_R_KAYNAKKODU", "CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""	
	MTable.AddField2 "B_MUSTERIKODU", "CHAR",16,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('CARI00')",""
	MTable.AddField2 "E_MUSTERIKODU", "CHAR",16,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('CARI00')",""	
	MTable.AddField2 "B_R_LATEST_B_TARIH", "DATE",10,0,"L","",""	
	MTable.AddField2 "E_R_LATEST_B_TARIH", "DATE",10,0,"L","",""		
	MTable.AddField2 "B_R_MAMULKODU", "CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""
	MTable.AddField2 "E_R_MAMULKODU", "CHAR",24,0,"L","F2=DO_BRW('STOK00')",""	
	MTable.AddField2 "IHTIYAC", "CHAR",1,0,"L","",""	
	MTable.AddField2 "TALEP", "CHAR",1,0,"L","",""	
	MTable.AddField2 "KART_TURU_1", "CHAR",1,0,"L","","|KARTTIPI"	
	MTable.AddField2 "KART_TURU_2", "CHAR",1,0,"L","","|KARTTIPI"
	MTable.AddField2 "KART_TURU_3", "CHAR",1,0,"L","","|KARTTIPI"
	
	MTable.IHTIYAC = "E"
	MTable.TALEP = "E"
	MTable.KART_TURU_1="S"
	MTable.KART_TURU_2="F"

	Call GetQStr123(QStr1, QStr2, QStr3)
	QStr = QStr1 & QStr2 & QStr3 & " AND 1=0"

	Set M10T = Doc.RunSQLQuery("MPS_HMD_LIST1", QStr)

	Call F2leriDegistir()
End Sub
Sub cmdMPSHammaddeListesiHazirla()
	Set ggg = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MPS_KRITER")
	Set M10T = Doc.GetTableObject("MPS_HMD_LIST1")
	M10T.SetRowAID 1,1,1
	M10T.Empty


	Call GetQStr123(QStr1, QStr2, QStr3)
	if trim(MTable.B_EVRAKNO) = trim(MTable.E_EVRAKNO) and trim(MTable.B_EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
		QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T.EVRAKNO IN (SELECT EVRAKNO FROM TABLE(BAGLANTI.GetBaglantiliMPSList('UP', '" & MTable.B_EVRAKNO & "',' ')))"
	Else
		QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T.EVRAKNO>='" & MTable.B_EVRAKNO & "'"
		if trim(MTable.E_EVRAKNO) <> "" Then
			QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T.EVRAKNO<='" & MTable.E_EVRAKNO & "'"
		End if
	End if
	
	QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T.MUSTERISIPINO>='" & MTable.B_MUSTERISIPINO & "'"
	if trim(MTable.E_MUSTERISIPINO) <> "" Then
		QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T.MUSTERISIPINO<='" & MTable.E_MUSTERISIPINO & "'"
	End if

	QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T.MUSTERIKODU>='" & MTable.B_MUSTERIKODU & "'"
	if trim(MTable.E_MUSTERIKODU) <> "" Then
		QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T.MUSTERIKODU<='" & MTable.E_MUSTERIKODU & "'"
	End if

	QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T.R_KAYNAKKODU>='" & MTable.B_R_KAYNAKKODU & "'"
	if trim(MTable.E_R_KAYNAKKODU) <> "" Then
		QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T.R_KAYNAKKODU<='" & MTable.E_R_KAYNAKKODU & "'"
	End if
	
	QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH>='" & MTable.B_R_LATEST_B_TARIH & "'"
	if trim(MTable.E_R_LATEST_B_TARIH) <> "" Then
		QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH<='" & MTable.E_R_LATEST_B_TARIH & "'"
	End if

	QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T.R_MAMULKODU>='" & MTable.B_R_MAMULKODU & "'"
	if trim(MTable.E_R_MAMULKODU) <> "" Then
		QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND T.R_MAMULKODU<='" & MTable.E_R_MAMULKODU & "'"
	End if

	InStr1 = ""
	For i = 1 to 3
		KartTuru = MTable.GetFieldValue("KART_TURU_" & i, 1)
		if trim(KartTuru) <> "" Then 
			if trim(InStr1) <> "" Then InStr1 = InStr1 & ","
			InStr1 = InStr1 & "'" & KartTuru & "'"
		End if
	Next
	if trim(InStr1) <> "" Then
		QStr2 = QStr2 & " AND S00.KART_TIPI IN (" & InStr1 & ")"
	End if

	InStr1 = ""
	For i = 1 to 3
		Statu = MTable.GetFieldValue("R_STATUS" & i, 1)
		if trim(Statu) <> "" Then 
			if trim(InStr1) <> "" Then InStr1 = InStr1 & ","
			InStr1 = InStr1 & "'" & Statu & "'"
		End if
	Next
	if trim(InStr1) <> "" Then
		QStr3 = QStr3 & " AND T.R_STATUS IN (" & InStr1 & ")"
	End if

	Set MTSTable = Doc.RunSQLQuery("fgsdfjsj", QStr1 & QStr2 & QStr3)

	if UCase(trim(MTable.IHTIYAC)) = "E" Then
		Set MTSTable = Doc.Select2("sdfdsd", MTSTable.GetAppName, "IHTIYAC_MIKTARI>0", "", -1)
	End if
	if UCase(trim(MTable.IHTIYAC)) = "E" Then
		Set MTSTable = Doc.Select2("sdfffdsd", MTSTable.GetAppName, "TALEP_MIKTARI=0", "", -1)
	End if

	M10T.AppendTable(MTSTable)

	M10T.SetRowAID 0,0,0
End Sub
Sub GetQStr123(QStr1, QStr2, QStr3)
	Set MTable = Doc.GetTableObject("MPS_KRITER")
	MMPS10TStr = "SELECT 'UP' ET, T.EVRAKNO, TM.MUSTERISIPINO, TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO, TM.MUSTERIKODU, T.R_HM_ODONEMNET, T.R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN, "
	MMPS10TStr = MMPS10TStr & " T.R_MAMULKODU, T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH, T.R_KAYNAKKODU, T.R_MIKTAR0, T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK"
	MMPS10TStr = MMPS10TStr & " FROM MMPS10T T"
	MMPS10TStr = MMPS10TStr & " LEFT JOIN MMPS10TM TM ON TM.EVRAKNO=T.EVRAKNO"
	MMPS10TStr = MMPS10TStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S ON S.KOD=T.R_KAYNAKKODU"
	MMPS10TStr = MMPS10TStr & " WHERE R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'"
	MMPS10TStr = MMPS10TStr & " AND T.ACIK_KAPALI=' '"
	MMPS10TStr = MMPS10TStr & " AND S.GK_1='01'"
	MMPS10TStr = MMPS10TStr & " UNION ALL"
	MMPS10TStr = MMPS10TStr & " SELECT 'F-' ET, T.EVRAKNO, TM.SIPNO40 MUSTERISIPINO, TM.SIPARTNO40 MUSTERISIPIARTNO, S40T.MUSTERIKODU, T.R_HM_ODONEMNET, T.R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN, "
	MMPS10TStr = MMPS10TStr & " T.R_MAMULKODU, T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH, T.R_KAYNAKKODU, T.R_MIKTAR0, T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK"
	MMPS10TStr = MMPS10TStr & " FROM STOK46T3 T"
	MMPS10TStr = MMPS10TStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK46T TM ON TM.EVRAKNO=T.EVRAKNO AND T.OR_TRNUM=TM.TRNUM"
	MMPS10TStr = MMPS10TStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK40T S40T ON S40T.ARTNO=TM.SIPARTNO40"
	MMPS10TStr = MMPS10TStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S ON S.KOD=T.R_KAYNAKKODU"
	MMPS10TStr = MMPS10TStr & " WHERE R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'"
	MMPS10TStr = MMPS10TStr & " AND T.ACIK_KAPALI=' '"
	MMPS10TStr = MMPS10TStr & " AND S.GK_1='01'"
	'MMPS10TStr = MMPS10TStr & " LEFT JOIN KTA_S46T KTA ON KTA.KTA_KEY=TM.ARTNO"

	QStr1 = "SELECT 'H' SELECTED, T.MUSTERISIPINO, T.MUSTERISIPIARTNO, T.MUSTERIKODU, C.AD MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD, T.EVRAKNO, "
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " T.R_MAMULKODU, S00M.AD R_MAMULKODU_STOK00_AD, T.R_KAYNAKKODU, S00.AD HAMMADDE_AD, T.R_MIKTAR0, "
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " (T.R_MIKTAR0 - T.R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN - T.R_HM_ODONEMNET) BAKIYE_HMD, T.R_BAKIYEKAYNAK, S00.IUNIT HMD_UNIT, S00.NAME2 HMD_AD2,"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " (T.R_HM_ODONEMNET + T.R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN) R_HM_TRANSFER_EDILEN, T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH, "
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " S47T.EVRAKNO TALEPNO, S47T.TARIH TALEP_TARIHI, S47T.RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH TALEP_TESTARIH, S47T.SF_MIKTAR TALEP_MIKTARI,"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " S46T.REV_RTESTARIH ST_TESTARIH, S46T.SF_MIKTAR ST_SIP_MIKTAR, S46T.SF_NETKAPANANMIK ST_SIP_KAPANANMIK, S46T.ACIKKAPALI ST_SIP_ACIKKAPALI,"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " S46T.EVRAKNO ST_SIP_NO, S46T.TARIH ST_SIP_TARIHI, S46T.TEDARIKCI, S00S.AD SIP_MAMUL_STOK00_AD,"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " (T.R_MIKTAR0 - COALESCE(S46T.SF_MIKTAR,0)) IHTIYAC_MIKTARI"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " FROM (" & MMPS10TStr & ") T"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S00 ON S00.KOD=T.R_KAYNAKKODU"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S00M ON S00M.KOD=T.R_MAMULKODU"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN STOK40T S40T ON S40T.ARTNO=T.MUSTERISIPIARTNO"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN CARI00 C ON C.KOD=S40T.MUSTERIKODU"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S00S ON S00S.KOD=S40T.KOD"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN ("
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 	SELECT KTA.SIPARTNO, T.KOD, KTA.MPSNO, CAST(LISTAGG(TRIM(T.EVRAKNO), ',') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY T.EVRAKNO) AS CHAR(100)) EVRAKNO, "
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 	CAST(LISTAGG(TRIM(T.TARIH), ',') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY T.EVRAKNO) AS CHAR(100)) TARIH, "
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 	CAST(LISTAGG(TRIM(T.RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH), ',') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY T.EVRAKNO) AS CHAR(100)) RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH, "
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 	SUM(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 	FROM STOK47T T"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 	INNER JOIN KTA_S47T KTA ON KTA.EVRAKNO=T.EVRAKNO AND KTA.OR_TRNUM=T.TRNUM"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 	GROUP BY KTA.SIPARTNO, T.KOD, KTA.MPSNO"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " ) S47T ON S47T.SIPARTNO=T.MUSTERISIPIARTNO AND T.R_KAYNAKKODU=S47T.KOD AND S47T.MPSNO=T.EVRAKNO"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " LEFT JOIN ("
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 	SELECT T.SIPARTNO40, T.KOD, KTA.MPSNO, CAST(LISTAGG(TRIM(T.EVRAKNO), ',') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY T.EVRAKNO) AS CHAR(100)) EVRAKNO, "
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 	CAST(LISTAGG(TRIM(T.TARIH), ',') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY T.EVRAKNO) AS CHAR(100)) TARIH, "
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 	CAST(LISTAGG(TRIM(T.REV_RTESTARIH), ',') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY T.EVRAKNO) AS CHAR(100)) REV_RTESTARIH, "
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 	CAST(LISTAGG(SUBSTR(C.AD, 1, 15), ',') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY T.EVRAKNO) AS CHAR(100)) TEDARIKCI, "
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 	CAST(LISTAGG(ACIKKAPALI, ',') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY T.EVRAKNO) AS CHAR(10)) ACIKKAPALI, "
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 	SUM(SF_MIKTAR) SF_MIKTAR, Sum(SF_NETKAPANANMIK) SF_NETKAPANANMIK"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 	FROM STOK46T T"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 	INNER JOIN KTA_S46T KTA ON KTA.EVRAKNO=T.EVRAKNO AND KTA.OR_TRNUM=T.TRNUM"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 	LEFT JOIN CARI00 C ON C.KOD=T.CH_KODU"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 	WHERE TRIM(T.SIPARTNO40) IS NOT NULL"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " 	GROUP BY T.SIPARTNO40, T.KOD, KTA.MPSNO"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " ) S46T ON S46T.SIPARTNO40=T.MUSTERISIPIARTNO AND T.R_KAYNAKKODU=S47T.KOD AND S46T.MPSNO=T.EVRAKNO"
	QStr1 = QStr1 & " WHERE 1=1"

'	QStr1 = QStr1 & " AND (S00.KART_TIPI IN ('S','F') OR T.R_KAYNAKKODU IN (SELECT KOD FROM STOK46T WHERE ACIKKAPALI=' '))"
End Sub
Sub cmdTalepAc()
	Set MPSList1 = Doc.Select2("sdgsdfhsjs", "MPS_HMD_LIST1", "SELECTED='E'", "", -1)
	if MPSList1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7msgBox "Hiçbir satýr seçilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set MPSList1 = Doc.Select2("sddddgsdfhsjs", MPSList1.GetAppName, "IHTIYAC_MIKTARI>0", "R_KAYNAKKODU", -1)
	if MPSList1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7msgBox "Seçilen satýrlarda ihtiyaç sýfýr", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentUI("STOK47", "")
	Set S47E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK47E")
	Set S47T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STOK47T")

	Doc1.New_Voucher
	S47T.Empty
	S47E.CODEDKISI = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("UDEF", "PERSONELKODU", Doc.Getd7UserName, "")
	For i = 1 to MPSList1.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "Aktarýlýyor : " & i & " / " & MPSList1.GetRowCount()
		satirNo = S47T.AddRow
		S47T.SetCurrentRow(satirNo)
		S47T.KOD(satirNo) = MPSList1.R_KAYNAKKODU(i)
		S47T.SF_MIKTAR(satirNo) = MPSList1.IHTIYAC_MIKTARI(i)
		S47T.RTALEPEDILENTESTARIH(satirNo) = MPSList1.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(i)
		S47T.KTA_SIPNO(satirNo) = MPSList1.MUSTERISIPINO(i)
		S47T.KTA_SIPARTNO(satirNo) = MPSList1.MUSTERISIPIARTNO(i)
		S47T.KTA_MPSNO(satirNo) = MPSList1.EVRAKNO(i)
	Next
	S47T.SetCurrentRow(1)
End Sub
Sub cmdSecilenMPSleriYazdir()
	Set UTY = Doc.Select1("asdfasdg", "UDEFTY", "KOD='" & Doc.Getd7UserName & "'&YAZICITURU='A4'", "", -1)
	if UTY.GetRowCount = 0 Then
		Doc.d7msgbox Doc.Getd7UserName & " kullanýcýsý için kullanýcý tanýmlarýnda A4 yazýcýsý seçilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set UTY = Doc.Select1("asdfasdg", "UDEFTY", "KOD='" & Doc.Getd7UserName & "'&YAZICITURU='BARKOD'", "", -1)
	if UTY.GetRowCount = 0 Then
		Doc.d7msgbox Doc.Getd7UserName & " kullanýcýsý için kullanýcý tanýmlarýnda BARKOD yazýcýsý seçilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set MPSList1 = Doc.Select2G("sdgsdfhsjs", "MPS_HMD_LIST1", "SELECTED='E'", "", "MPS", -1)
	if MPSList1.GetRowCount() = 0 Then
		Doc.d7msgBox "Hiçbir MPS seçilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set kkkk = Doc.CreateSObject("BATCHERRORMODE")
	
	Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("MMPS10", "")
	Set M10E = Doc1.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")

	For i = 1 to MPSList1.GetRowCount()
		Doc.StatusBarMessage "MPS 'ler yazdýrýlýyor : " & i & " / " & MPSList1.GetRowCount()
		Doc1.Load_Voucher MPSList1.MPS(i)
		M10E.STATUS = "R"
		M10E.NOTLAR_1 = GetMPSNotu(trim(MPSList1.MAMUL_MPS_LIST(i)))
		if Doc1.Save_Voucher = 0 Then
			Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.MMPS10", "PrintForm"
			Doc1.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S.ETK-YM", "PrintLabel"
		Else
			Doc.d7MsgBox trim(MPSList1.MPS(i)) & " no.lu MPS kaydedilemedi", "", MB_OK
		End if
	Next
End Sub
Function GetMPSNotu(MPSList)
	GetMPSNotu = ""
	MPSArr = Split(MPSList, ",")
	if uBound(MPSArr) < 0 Then Exit Function
	QStr = "SELECT C.AD MUSTERI, S.AD MAMUL"
	QStr = QStr & " FROM MMPS10TM TM"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN CARI00 C ON C.KOD=TM.MUSTERIKODU"
	QStr = QStr & " LEFT JOIN STOK00 S ON S.KOD=TM.MAMULSTOKKODU"
	QStr = QStr & " WHERE TM.EVRAKNO='" & Doc.RightJustify(trim(MPSArr(0)),10) & "'"
	Set M10TM = Doc.RunSQLQuery("dfgsdfsj", QStr)
	if M10TM.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Function
	GetMPSNotu = trim(Left(M10TM.MUSTERI(1), 20)) & " - " & trim(M10TM.MAMUL(1))
End Function
Sub F2leriDegistir()
	Call F2Degistir("MPS_HMD_LIST1", "EVRAKNO", "MMPS10E")
	'Call F2Degistir("MPS_HMD_LIST1", "TALEPNO", "STOK47E")
	'Call F2Degistir("MPS_HMD_LIST1", "ST_SIP_NO", "STOK46E")
	Call F2Degistir("MPS_HMD_LIST1", "R_KAYNAKKODU", "STOK00")
	Call F2Degistir("MPS_HMD_LIST1", "R_MAMULKODU", "STOK00")
End Sub
Sub F2Degistir(TabloStr, AlanAdi, ByVal BrwTable)
	BrwTable = "'" & BrwTable & "'"
	Set tbl=doc.GetRowsetObject(TabloStr)
	Set fd=tbl.GetFieldDef_E(AlanAdi)
	fd.F2Fnc="DO_BRW(" & Replace(BrwTable,"'", """") & ")"
	tbl.SetFieldProperties1 AlanAdi,fd 
End Sub
Sub cmdSec()
	Set M10T = Doc.GetTableObject("MPS_HMD_LIST1")
	For i = 1 to M10T.GetRowCount()
		if i = 1 Then
			if M10T.SELECTED(i) = "H" Then
				Secim = "E"
			Else
				Secim = "H"
			End if
		End if
		M10T.SELECTED(i) = Secim
	Next
	M10T.Refresh
End Sub
